*بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*


*Da sunan  Allah  mai  rahama  mai  jin qai*



*1—2*



Dajine  mai  dogayen  bishiyoyi  , wanda  suka  rufe  hasken  ranar  dazai  haska  cikin  dajin 

Wasu  tawaga  ne  sunkai  su  dari  biyu " suke  tunkaro,, dajin  wani  ne  yace  



Ranka  ya  dade " wannan  dajin  fa  ba'a  shigarsa   saboda  abubuwan  firgitarwar  shi,  akwai  namukan  daji  sosai  aciki  amma " wata  hatsabibiyar  , aljana  ce  acikinsa  bata  bari  kashiga  ka   dauki " wani  abu  na  cikin  dajin  " idan  kuwa  kace  zaka,, kashe  wani abu  a  cikin  dajin   to  fa  kaine  zaka  zama  gawa  


Numfasawa  sarkin  yayi " yace  kai  kadena   yimin  wannan  maganar " marar  anfani  ni  za'a  fadawa   hatsabibanci  " saboda  rashin  mutumci  na  " masarautar  kasmur  na, mallak ta  zama  tawa  , na  kashe  sarkin   garin  na  kwace  matarsa  ta  zama  tawa" to  yau  saina  kasheta  " da  hannu  na  naga  mai  za'ayi  kuma   na  kama  namukan  cikin  dajin " yana  kai  karshen  maganar  tashi  yana  zare,, ido  kamar  tsohon  maye 



Madaki  yace  ,ya shugaba na  kayi  sani  cewa  wannan  aljanar  tawuce " tunanin  ka  ba  daya  suke  da  sauran  aljannu  ba 

Ita  wannan  aljanar  musulma " ce  kuma  batason  ake  daukar  hakkin  halittar   Allah  Allah  shiyasa  baa  shiga  indai,, wani  abu  zaka  yiwa  abubuwan  cikin  dajin  ,, amma  idan  ba  komai  zaka  musu  ba  zaka  wuce  cikin  ruwan  sanyi  

Sarkin " yace  wai  maiyasa  kuka  renani   , ni  sarki  safuwan  , nasha  alwashin  saina  kashe  wannan,  aljanar  da  kuke  tsoro   wai ma  yama  sunan  ta  ? 



 Madaki  , yace  kai  ranka  ya  dade  bakasan " sunan  taba  to  sunan  ta. ALJANAR JARUMAI  da  shi  ake  kiranta " tace  sai  jarumi  zata  kyautatawa  , ta  kuma  yimishi  hidima,, har  karshen  rayuwar  shi  , amma  fa  sai  adalin  mutum,, 



Sarki safuwan  yace  to  saina  kashe  ta,  nan  sukaci  gaba  da tafiya  suna  kara  kusanto,, dajin 




Tana  zaune  kan  darduma  " ta  idar  da  salla  



Tin  daga cen  nesa     take  ganinsu  suna  tunkaro  dajin" a   hankali  ta  shafa   fatiha  " ta  mike 



Kafin  kace  mai,  tuni  ta  rikide    ta  koma  wata  babbar  tsuntsuwa,,     ta  hau  sama " kan  bishiya   tana  jiran  zuwansu  ,, taga  da  wani  salo  sukazo  



Suna  shigowa , cikin  dajin " suka  fara  tafiya  a  hankali  kamar  barayi  



Wasu  manyan  halittu  ne  masu  kama  da  zakuna  suka  tsaya  a  bakin  hanya   wasu  na  tsaye  , wasu  na  kwance "  ko  wannen  su  na  jiran  ko  ta  kwana  ,  




Madaki  kuwa  tuni  yasoma  sakin  fitsari " a  kijinsa  saboda  tsananin  tsoro  ganin  wannan  halittu  ,, wanda  a  tarishin  rayuwar  shi  bai  taba  ganin  irin  suba  ,, sai  kyerma  yakeyi " yana  zare  ido  yana  wagai  ko  ina  




Sauran  dakarun   ko  wannen su  cikin  shirin  yaki  suka  gyara  takubban  su  da  kyau  




Duk  abin  suke  tana  kallon  su  daya  bayan  daya  sake  aiko  musu  zomaye  tayi  ba  kadan  ba  suna  tafo  bakin  hanya  , dan  taga  shin  sarkin  nan  mai  nuna  shi  zarumi  ne  zai  taba,  wannan  zomayen 




Ganin  farar  zomaye  suakayi  kuma  manya  manya    idonsu  nan  kamar  na  rakumi  bakinsu  irin. Na  awaki  sai  muzurai ' sukeyi  suna  kallon  su  sarki safuwan  da  tawagar,, sa  



Shi  kanshi  "    sarki  safuwan  saida  ya  firgita  saboda  tsoron  daya  kamashi  , ganin  zomaye  irin  wannan "  




Sauran"   dakarun "  suma  mamaki  ne  da  tsoro  da  fargaba  ke  ziyartar  su    kowa  yayi  shuru  babu  abinda  kake ji  sai " karar  tafiyar  dawakan  su  



Aljanar  jarumai  kam " na  kallon  su  dan  so  take  taga  sarki safuwan  ya  kama  wani  abu  a dajin  nan  yaga  yadda  ake   cin  mutumcin  mutum  ,, amma  saitaga  bai  kama  ba,,     batayi  mamaki  ba  


Dariya  ta  kama  yi  kamar  zata  fasa  dajin  saboda  amsa kowar  da  dajin  keyi  



Tuni  su  sarki  safuwan " suka  rikice  dawakansu    sai  karo  suke  da  juna  saboda  suma  ba  karamin  firgita  sukayi  ba    



Haka  suka  ringa  tafiya  har  sukabar  dajin

  

Hamdala  sukayi  saboda  sun  fito  daga  dajin  



Madaki  ne  ya  kwashe  da  dariya,  yace  ranka  ya  dade  bana  fada  maka  ba    wannan  dajin  yafi  karfin  mutum  



Sarki  safuwan  yace " kabari  muje  garin   daqasus  zamu  yaketa " ai  yanzu  banzo  da  shiri  ba  ne  shiyasa  kaga  nayi  shuru "  banyi  komai ba  yana  hade  rai       dan  bayason  reni,,  




Madaki ya  danne " dariyar   shi   saboda  ganin  shugaban  nashi  yakoma  a  sarkin  shi  




Ita  kuwa  aljana  safiya  saukowa  tayi,, ta  koma  suffar  mace  budurwa  nan  namukan  dajin  sukayo wajan  ta  , zama  tayi   suka  kewaye  ta,   


Kallon  su  tayi  tace  insha Allah  bazan  bari  wani  abu  yasame  kuba  ko  mutum  ko  jinsina  aljani    bazan  bari  a  cutar  daku  ba  indai  ina  tare  daku  ,  ta  ringa  binsu  tana  shafa  jikinsu  kafin  ta  bace  ta  lula  sama  ta  ringa ,, tafiya  cikin  sararin "  masaniya "  



GARIN   KADUNA  


wata  yarinya  ce  yar  karama  wacce  bata  fi  shekara  goma  ba  ,,  tana  bakin  kofar  wani  gida " sai  kuka  takeyi  kamar  ranta  zai  fiti  


Aljana  safiya  ce  tin  kafin  ta  iso  taga  yarinyar  na  kuka  tausayin  yarinyar  ne  ya  kama  ta  saita  sauka  ta  zama  tantabara "  ta  koma   gefen  yarinyar  " taga  ina  ne  gidan  su  kuma  maiyasa  take  kuma  



Baa  jima  ba  , wata  " mata  ta  fito   tana  lekowa,, ganin  yarinyar  yasa  tace  to  tsohuwar  munafuka "  wato  nan  kikazo  kika  zauna  na  aikeki  tin  dazun  amma  baki  kawomin  aiken  dana  miki  ba  " shine  kika  zo  kika  kama  kukan  munafurci  ko  to  yau  zakici  ubanki   tana  dangware  mata  kai 



Sautin  kukan  ta  ta  kara  ta  fara  fadin  mama  kiyi  hakuri  wllh  naje  shagon " ne  zan  siya  abinda  kika  aikeni  , ina  dubawa  naga  babu  kudin  shiyasa  ban  shiga  ba  



Mama tace  iyee,, mai  kika  ce  husna  kudin  nawa  zaki  yarmin " ai  wllh  yau  saikin  fadamin  inda  kika  kai min  kudi  na  wato  kintafi  yawon  iskancin ,  naki  acen  kuka  kashe  kudin " keda  su  shine  kice  wai  sun  fadi  to  zo  muje  gida  " yau  naga  uban  wa  ya  yatsaya  miki  da  kika  renani   ja  kama  hannun  husna  tana  janta  " a  kasa  har  cikin  tsakar  gidan  




Ita  kuwa  husna  baiwar  Allah  ,, sai  rokon  mama  take  tana  kuka  fadi  take " mama  wllh  babu  inda  naje  kiyi  hakuri " bazan  kuma  ba  na  tuba  mama  karki  dakeni  yanzu  haka  zazzabi  ne  nakeji  kiyi  hakuri  mama  




Ko  kallon  ta  batayi  ba  ta  shige  daki  dan  nemo  mabigin  da  zata  daki  husna  dashi  



Aljana  safiya  na  kallon  su  tabisu  har " gida  tana  tunanin  anya  wannan  matar  ita  ta  haifi  yarinyar  nan " kuwa  gaskiya  da  ayar  tambaya  gashi  tana  son  taimaka  " mata  amma  dole  saita yi  bincike  ta  gano  asalin  husna  



Mama  ta  fito   da  wata  wayar  wuta,, tana  fadin  dan  uban  ki  gobe  ma  idan  na  aikeki,  zaki  zomin  hannu  rabbana   nan  tasoma  dukan  husna  kamar  Allah  ya  aikota  



Husna  inba  ihu  da  neman  taimako  ba  babu  abinda  takeyi  saboda  mama  ta  riketa  sosai  tana  zaneta  




Aljana safiya  wani  tukugin  bakin  cikine  ya  rufe,  ta   har  bakinta  tururi  yake  saboda  tsananin  bacin  rai   tabbas  tasan  wannan  muguwar  matar  da  wuya   ta  zama  uwar " husna    




Wani dattijo  ne  yashigo " gidan  da  sauri  yana  fadin  ke  talatu  anya  kina  da  hankali   kuwa    mai  husna  tayi  miki  " haka  kike  zaneta  kalli  fa  yadda  kika  fasa  mata  jiki  wllh  bakida  tunani " kenan  yanzu  duk  fadan  da  nake  miki  baki jishi  ba,, ganin  taki dena dukanta  kuma  tamishi  banza " yasashi " kama  wayar  yana  fisgewa  daga  hannun  ta  




Kallon  shi  mama  tayi  tana  huci  tace " ka  kyaleni  na  zane  wannan  yar  iskar  yarinyar  " 




Yace  to  mai  tamiki " haka  a  ganin  duk  lefin  data  miki  bai  kamata " kike  mata  irin  wannan  dukan  ba   wannan  ai " saiki  , kasheta "  haka  akeyin  rayuwa  



Mama  tace  kaga  malam  baka  isaba   taya  zaa  dakeni  ahanani  kuka " akan  me  na  aiki  yarinyar  da  kudi  taje  shago  ta  siyomin " omo  amma,, taje  wajan  yan  iskan  kawayen  nata,,, suka  kashe  kudin  shine  yanzu  tazo  tana  fadamin " bataga  kudin  ba  ta  yadasu " to  bazan  yadda  ba  konaci  gaba  da  zeneta  ko  ka  biyani  ehe  dan ' bazan  yi  asara  ba   tana  jijjiga  jiki  saboda  tsabar  masifa 





Mlm  yace  to  ba  gyara  kice  na  biya  kiba  da  kike  dukan  min  diya  kodan  bakisan " ciwon  haihuwa  ba  shiyasa  kike  mata  wannan  rashin  mutumcin   




Hannu  biyu  talatu  ta  daura  aka" tana  fadin  shikenan  nashiga  uku  yanzu  mlm  ni  kakewa  gorin  haihuwa  " dan  Allah  bai  bani  ba  shiyasa  kake  nuna  min  matsayina  akan  husna  duk  ' kyautatawar  da  nake  mata  baka  gani ta  sharce  majina " da  sharen  zaninta  tana  goge  hawayen  karya 




Mlm  yace  niba  wani  gori  ai  gaskiya  na  fada  miki  idan  kinga  dama  ki  gyara  idan  baki, gani  ba  kiyita  zama " a  haka  nan  ya   tashi  husna  wadda,  tayi  laushi  saboda " dukan  da  tasha  duk  bayanta  ya  fashe " saboda  duka  




Dago  da  kanta  tayi  tace  baba  kabani  magani  nasha " jikina  ciwo  yakeyi " hawayen  fuskar  shi  ya  share   yana  tausayawa  yar  tashi  yadda  take  fuskantar  taskun  rayuwar  ,, shi kuma  ya  kasa  komai  akai,  daya  ce  zai  saki  " talatu  sai  mahaifiyar  shi  tace  inhar  yasaki  talatu  saita  tsine  mishi " shiyasa  yakasa  komai  




Ita  dai  aljana  safiya  tana  ganin  ikon  Allah  ganin  lokaci  ta  tafiya  gashi  kuma  tana  son  zuwa  wajan  yan uwanta  takai  musu  ziyara  yasa  ta  nufi  hayar  fita  tana  shan  alwashin "  saita  ,,  ji  tarishin  rayuwar  wannan  gida " saboda  ta  ganno  inda  matsalar  take " nan  ta  share  hawayen  tausayin  husna  ta  fice  a  gidan  yana  alkawarin  da  zarar  tadawo  zata  zo  domin  jin  manene,, abinda  yasa  talatu  ke  azabtar  da  wannan  yarinyar




Husna  baba  ya  mata " ruwan  zafi  ta  cire  kayanta  da  kyer  yagasa  mata  jikinta "" yabata  abinci  tana  gamawa  ya  bata  maganin  ,tsire ne  dayawa  yasiyo  mata  da  fura " yace  husna  kiyi  hakuri  kinji  wata  rana  sai  labari  " na  kasa  aiwatar  da  komai   akan  talatu  ina  jin  kunyar  ban  rikewa " mahaifiyar  ki  amanar  ki  data  bani  ba  kiyi  hakuri  insha Allah  Allah komai  zazo  dai, dai   ga  nama  nan  idan  kintashi  ", daga  baccin  kici  ga  fura  nan  da  ruwa  " kada  ki  kuskura  " kifita  inda  ba  ji  kikayi  dawo  ba  ki  rufe  dakin  ki  ku  kulle  kinji  husna  baba  




Kallon  baban  nata  tayi  tace  to  baba  Allah  yakiyaye  hanya  adawo lfy  



Baba  ya  amsa  da  ameen  husna baba   nan  tafita 



Talatu  na  nan  waje  tayi  ture  kaga  tsiya " sai  cika  take  tana  batsewa  



Shi  kuwa  baba  ko  kallon  ta  baiyi " yayi  tafiyar shi 



Ganin  yatafi  yasa  ta  shige  daki  ta  dauko  muciya " ta  nufi  dakin  husna  dan  yau  so  take " ta  nakasa  husna  tana  taba " kofar  tayi  a  kulle  tace  wllh  duk  lokacin  da  kika  fito  dana  nakada  miki  dan  banzan  kashi  " mai  kama  da  aljanu  nan  tayi  fuu  ta  koma  dakin  ta  wayar  ta  ta  dauko  



Kunna  wayar  tayi  tana  duba  wayar  wata  nember  ta,, ta  kira " ringing  uku  aka  dauka  talatu  tace  hello  tabawa  kinsan w.....

*5—6*




Aljana  safiya  kallansu  har  suka  shiga,, cikin  dakin  inna  ,, dariya  tayi  tace  yau  zanga  mai  cetonku,  yan  iska  saina  " koya  muku  hankali  " nan  itama  tayi dakin 


Acen  cikin , dakin  kuwa,, siyama  ce  ta  makale,, jikin  inna " kamar  zata  koma  cikinta  " sai  kuka  take  da  ihu,  tace  waiyo  Allah inna  , ki  boyeni  ga  bilkisu  nan  tazama  " fatalwa,, bata  mutu  ba  take  datalwa " gata  nan  inna  karki  Bari  ta  tabani 


Inna  ma  kankame,, siyama  tayi  " tana  fadin  shikenan  munshiga  uku  yau  ga  mu  a  hannun  fatalwa, Allah  ka  taimake mu  



Zomayen  suka  karaso  suna  nufar,, wajansu  inna  suna  zare  ido bude  baki  manyan  hakora  ne  bakinsu  masu tsini  , kaifi  


Ihu  siyama " ta  kumayi  ganin  wannan  zomayen  tunu  tasake  sakin  fitsari  a  karo  na  sau  ba  adadi " ta  fita  hayyacinta,, sai  kara  matse  inna  take  tana  boye  fuskar  ta  


Inna  kuwa  tunu,, tayi  sumar  zaune  saboda  tsananin  birgita  datayi  ga  matsar  da  siyama  tayi  mata  



Aljana  safiya  ce, shigo  dakin  tana  shigowa " ta  kashe  wutar  dakin "tunu  dakin  ya  gauraye " da  mugun  duhu " 


Aikuwa  siyama  ta kuma  ,, kidimewa,, ganin  ko  tafin  hannun  ta  ", bata  gani  kuka  takara  sakawa  tana  fadin  shikenan  yau  mun  mutu  inna  kitashi  dan Allah 


Wata  magigiciyar,, dariya  aljana  safiya  tayi  marar  dadin  ji tuni,, dakin  yadau amsawa  kowar,, dariyar  ta  ,  yana  girgiza  



Ita dai  inna  dama  tuni  ta  sume,, sai  siyama  ke  kuka  da  neman  taimako " tana  rike  inna  


Maido  , wutar  dakin  aljana safiya  tayi  ,, tana    sakin  wata  dariyar  kafin  ta  rikide  dakoma  wani  shirgegen  ,, zaki  mai  girma  sosai  kamar   sa  yake,, 


Boye  fuskar  ta,  Siyama  tayi  dan  tsoran  kara  nunkuwa  yakeyi,, duk  lokacin  data  kalli  aljana  safiya  


Wajansu  zakin " yayi  yana  kukan  zakuna,, ai  tuni  siyama  ta  saki  zayo  a  jikinta,, sai  zami  takeyi  itada,, inna  


Alajana safiya,, na  zuwa  ta  koma  suffar  mace  , ta  damki  wuyan  siyama,, ta  zaro  bulalar ta  tasoma  " zane  siyama  tako  ina,, sai  rokon  ta siyama,,, keyi " akan  ta  kyaleta,  zata  mata  duk  abinda  takeso 


Ita  kuwa  aljana safiya  batama,  San  tana  yiba , idan  ta  tunano  yadda  taga  jikin  bilkisu  ,, sai  ranta  yakara  baci  sosai,, saida  taga  siyama  ta  dena  motsi  kafin  ta  kyaleta  ta  wugata  gefe,, tafafi  tim  a  kasa 



Kallon  hafeez da  amina  tayi  , tace  hafeez  maza  zokata  shi  wannan  tsohuwar,, najadun  , da  sauri  yazo  ,  bulalar  tamika  mishi  " ya  amsa  ,,, bai  buga  mata  bulala,, saiya  matso  kusan  inna  


Ya  wanka  mata,, lafiyayyun  maruka,, biyu  tana  buka  mata  harara  yace  tashi  muguwa


A  firgice,, Inna  ta  fado  daga  kan   gadon,,  tana  faduwa  kasa,, tasaka  kuka  tace  , 

Fadin,,    waiyo Allah babata  ta  kabari,, waiyo Allah  babana " na  lahira,, yau  munyi  gamo  ga " fatalwa   ta  hanamu  bacci  a  daren  nan " dan Allah  baiwar  Allah  mai muka  miki " kike  azabtar  damu  haka  , kiyi hakuri  " duk  abinda,  kikeso  zanyi  miki  kinji " dan Allah dan  ,Muhammed  rasulullahi  

s.a.w.  aljana safiya ta  fada 



Tsawa  , aljana safiya  tayiwa  inna  tace  rufemin  baki  munafuka " muguwa  azzalumai  ,, ke  harda  kinsan  da  labari  da  ranar  lahira " din  mugaye  kawai,, ke  har  kina  da  imani  ne  , tsohuwar  najadu  " mai  baiwar  Allah  tamuku " kuke gana  mata  azaba kamar,  kun  samu  , baiwarku"  to  yau  zanga  mai  cetonku " , saikun  gwanmace  ,, kun tsabi " mutuwa  akan  rayuwar,, yanzu  saura  azaba   takwas ""  saina  shayar  daku   nau'ikan  azaba  ,,  kala  kala  , banzaye,, kawai  


Da sauri  inna  ,, ta  durkusa  tana  dafa  kafar  ,, aljana  safiya  tana fadin,, dan Allah  kiyi  hakuri  " wllh  namiki  alkawarin  bazan    sakeyiwa,,  Bilkisu  komai  ba   namiki  wannan  alkawarin " har  abada  insha  Allah   komai  na  gidan  nan  muzamuke,, yi   insha Allah  kiyi  min  rai  mama  ta  karki ,, kasheni  




Bugeta  safiya  tayi  tace  ,, tashi  akusa  na  , abinda  nafada " saina  aikata,,  idan  kinga  ,, na  fasa  aiwatar  da  abinda,, nace  sai  in  bilkisu   tace  ta  yafe " muku  

Kallon , Hafeez   tayi  tace  maza  kakai  minsu  cikin daji  inda  zaa  koya  musu  hankali  ,, nan  yayi  wajan  su  inna  ya  kamasu,, suka  bace 



Aljana safiya,, tace  amina  kije  dakin  garbati " ki  bashi  tsoro,  na  musamman  " ki  gargade  shi  akan  shan  giwar  da  yakeyi,  ganinan  ,, nima  zuwa  

Risinawa,, tayi  tana  fadin  angama" shugabata  , nan  ta  bace  daga  dakin  

Aljana safiya  ta  kalli  zomayen,,, tace  kuma  kutafi " gida  kuce  ina  gaishe  da  sauran  ,, zanyi  kewarku " inason  gobe  zanje  yankinmu,, insha Allah  " ban  saniba  kozan  dade  dayawa  ko,, zan  dawo  da  wuri "   Allah  masanin  gaibu " ku  tsare  dajin  nan  nasan  ,, sarki  safuwan  " zaice  ya  dawo  daji  saboda  su  daukeku   duk  lokacin  ,, da  kukaji  kamshin  su,, to  maza  ku  kirani  " komai  nakeyi  zan  bari  nazo,, 



Dan  karamin,, yazo  kusan  ta,, yace  to  muma,, muna  kewar  ku  mama,, idan  kinje  kidawo  da  wuri " bamuso  kike  nisa  damu  ki  gaishe  da  koya  da  koya  


Shafa  kanshi  tayi  tace  insha Allah  yaron  kirki  ,, maza  kutafi  inason  kafin  gari  yawaye  nabar  wannan  nahiyar,,,, zuwa  sukayi  kowa,  ya  na  shafa  fuskar  ta  ,, kafin  suka  bace 


Acen dakin  bilkisu  ,, kuwa  amina  ta  bayyana " acikinsa  ganin  garbati  , tayi  cikin  bargo  sai  kerma  yakeyi  sosai  dan  har  yanzu  tsoron  bai  sakeshiba  


Yar dariya  tayi  ,,tace  da  kyau  aini dama  nafison  abani,  irin  wannan  aikin,  na  casa  mutum  ko  aljani,   nan  ta  hau  gadon,  bayanshi  ta  shafa  tana  mai,, tafiyar  tsutsa  


A  razane  garbati  a  bude  idon  shi  , yana  juyawa,, da  mugun sauri  yadiro,, akan  gadon,, yana  haki  ganin  wata  katuwa  macijiya,, mai  kan  kura,, yana  neman  , sareshi    


Rikidewa  , amina  tayi  takoma,, budurwa  mai  kyau  ,, sosai   saidai  kafarta  'irin  na  doki  ne  

Sosai  garbati  , ya  tsorata " shakarshi  tayi  tanayin,, sama  dashi  , zaneshi  tasomayi,, tin  kafin  suyo  kasa,  suna  kasa " ta   bugashi  jikin  bango  ,, 



Tace  wato,, gakai  , dan  iska  ko  mashayin  ,, giya  to  yau  zanga  inda  zaka  sake  shan  giyar,,   idan  na  kasheka  mugu  azzalimi,, kawai  


Da  kyer,, yatashi  " yace,  dan Allah  baba  fatalwa,, kiyi  hakuri kidawomin  da matata wllh  inasonta,, ina  kaunar  ta,,  zan  gyara  kuskure  na  insha Allah,, namiki alkawarin bazan  sake  ,  koda  kallon  banza  ne  agidan,, nan  bazan  mata  ba  " kiyi  hakuri  dan Allah,, ki  kidawomin da  matata  ,, 


Dariya  amina tayi tace,, iyee  dama  kasan " darajar  ne   har  kake  wannan  abubuwan  ,, kamar  kunsamu,, baiwarku " mashayi,, idan  ka  sake  shan  giyar  nan,, saina  farka  maka,, ciki  dan  iska,  


Da sauri  yace,, insha Allah  nadena " wllh  bazan  sake  shaba  namiki alkawari  har  abada 


Aljana safiya ce  takaraso "   tace  kyaleshi  haka  ,, amina  ,, inason  kujera  maza  kawomin  ,, mikewa  amina  tayi   tana  mika  hannun  ta  " sama  ta  lumshe  ido  ,, ko  minti  biyu  ba'ayi  ba,  kujeru  biyu  suka  bayyana " a  hannun  ta,,  nan  ta  , ajiye  suka  zauna 



Aljana safiya  ,, tayi  gyaran  murya  tace,, munason  muje  shin  kaida  bilkisu  ,, auren  soyayya  ,, aka  muku  ko  kuwa  hadaku,, akayi   




Gyara,, zama  yace,  nida  bilkisu,  auren  soyayya  ,  mukayi   babanta  abokin  babana  ne  ,, tin  kafin  " a  haifeta  ina  yaro,,  gidansu  nakoma  dazama " acen  nake  komai  maman  bilkisu  mutuniyar  kirki  ce  kamar  ita  , haifeni  haka  takeyi  min  


Har  lokacin  da  tasamu  cikin,, Bilkisu  sosai  nake  murna,, zanyi  kani  ko  kanwa "   idan  naje  makaranta   naga  yaya  da  kanwa  zasu,  tafi  gida  sainake  cewa  nima  , ankusa  yimin  kanwa haka  nayita  bawa  yan  ajin mu  mama  na  tana  da  ciki,, shesuyi  tamin  dariya  


Lokacin  ,, da  aka  haifi  bilkisu  ni  ke  mata  komai " wanka  wanki  goyata "   har  lokacin  data  isa  zuwa  school  nike  kaita,, sosai  nake  sonta  ,, itama  tinda  ta  taso  babu  wanda  takeso  fiye  dani,  haka  muke  rayuwar  ,, mai  dadi,  


Inna  kuwa  sallamawa,, umma  ni  tayi  dan  komai  nawa  agudanta  yake  , saida  yawo  muke  zuwa  gidan  inna  nida  bilkisu  ,, lokacin  inna  nason  bilkisu " saboda,, tana  girmama  ta  sosai   hakan  namin  dadi  matuka  


Har  lokacin  data  , girma  inda  bilkisu  tagane  , ba umma ta haifeni  "  ba   lokacin  danace  mata  inasonta,, sosai  ta  shiga  rudu  saboda  wannan  kalmar  ,, saida  taje  wajan  umma  tamata,, tambaya  umma  tace  eh  hakane,, tukun  ta  karbi  soyayya  ta  


Soyayya  muke  mai  tsabta  nida  bilkisu  ,, tana  bani  girmana " ga  hankali  da  nutsuwa  shiyasa   nake  wa  Allah  godiya  ga Allah  danasamu " Bilkisu  a  matsayin  matar  dazan  aura  , babu  bata  lokaci  aka  ,, saka  lokacin  bikin  mu  nida  bilkisu  sati biyu  kacal  


Farin ciki,  mukeyi  sosai  nida  bilkisu  


Baba  nayiwa  inna  zancen  ansaka  ranar  bikin  mu" nida  bilkisu  ,, nan  tasoma  fada  ta  ringa  masifa  



Inna  tacewa, baba  bazai  yiyuba  na  auri  bilkisu,, ita  yar  kanwarta,, zan  aura   ziyada,, kuma  dole  na  aure  inba  haka  ba  za'a  yi  tashin  hankali  a  gidan  nan,   


Ganin  tana neman  tadawa  baba  hankali  yasa  yamata  kashedi,  yace  idan  har  tace,,, sai  na  auri  ziyada  ba  bilkisu  ba  tofa  saidai " itama  tabar  mishi  gida  jin  hakan  yasa,  ta  hakura,,amma  batason " Bilkisu  dole  tasa  tabari  akayi  auren  



Anyi  bikin mu  lfy  muna  zaman  lfy  nida bilkisu  bayan bikin mu  da  sati  , aka  cenjawa   su  umma  da  abba  wajan  aiki  suka  koma  jahar  abuja  da  aikin  ,, lokacin  munasha  kuka  nifa  bilkisu  ganin zasu  ,, tafi  su  barmu,  musamman  ma  bilkisu  tafini shiga  damuwa " har  zazzabi  tayi  ina  rarrashin  ta  tukun ta  hakura,  


Muna  cika  wata,, biyat  da  auren mu  ranar  wata  laraba  wanda  bazan  taba  manta  wa  ba,, acikin  tarishin  rayuwar  ta  , baba  yarasu  , nasha  kuka  alokacin  
 Har  kwanciya  nayi  ,, a  asibiti  


Inna  kuwa  bazance  tadamu  sosai,  ba   Dan  harkokin  , gabanta  takama,, yi  


Tin  daga  lokacin  inna  ke  ganawa " bilkisu  azaba  idan  nayi  magana  saitace  zata  tsine  min,, haka  nake  hakura  bakin  cikin,, hakanne  yasa  na  shiga  harka  shaye  shaye    saina  bugu,, inna  tace  bilkisu  ta  zageta,, ina  cikin  maye  nake  zane  bilkisu " haka  tayita  fuskar  taskun  rayuwa  , har  tasamu  ciki  


Aikuwa  inna  ta,,  kafe  cewa  wannan cikin banawa  bane " Bilkisu  maza  takebi  zina takeyi  idan  nafita  yawon  ta  take  tafiya  ,, na  nuna  ban  yadda  ba  saboda  nasan  bilkisu  tana  da  kamun  kai,  wannan  bama  halinta  bane   nace  bansan  zancen  ba  cikina  ne  ninawa,, bilkisu  " ganin  na  rikice  yasa  ta  hakura 




Haka  suke  gana  mata " azaba  har  wannan  lokacin  danake  muku  bayani  ,  amma  munkasa,, sanin  wani  gari  su  umma  suka  koma,   har  abuja  nace  nasha  tambaya  ,, amma  bansamesu  ,, ba    haka  muka  hakura,,  yakai  karshen  maganar  tana  ,, sauke  numfashi  



Ajiyar zuciya  aljana  safiya  tasauke,, tace  to  yanzu  tinda,, kagane  gaskiya,, ka  zauna  da  matar  ka  lfy  kagane  ko  yanzu  zan  maidau  maka  ita  


Garbati  yace,, nagode  sosai  Allah  yasaka  da  alkhairi   

Murmishi  safiya  tayi     tace  ba komai  fatana  dai  ku  zauna  lfy  nan  ta  kalli,,  amina  tace  kikawo  bilkisu  


Bacewa  ,, tayi  kafin " minti  uku  sai  gata  ta  bayyana  rungume  ,, da  bilkisu  tana  bacci 


Aljana  safiya  ,, ta    amsheta  taba  fuskar  ta,,    tayi,  tana fadin  kanwata  tashi,  ga  mijin ki  


Bude  idonta  tayi  ta  kalli  safiya  tace  anty  ,, murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  ga  mijin ki "" juyawa  tayi  taga  garbati  a  zaune,    


Da sauri  tashige  jikin " Safiya  tana  fadin  anty  dan Allah  kada  kibari  yazo  kusana  , giya  yakesha  kuma  dukana  yakeyi,, yana  jikin  raunika  ajikina  


Da sauri garbati  ya  matso  kusanta,, yana  hawaye  yace  dan Allah  bilkisu  kiyi  hakuri  ki  yafemin " natuba  insha  Allah  bazan  sake  bata  miki  rai  ba   nayi  miki  alkawari,  kinji ", matata  inasonki  


Baki  a  sake  bilkisu  ke  kallon  garbati,, tana  tunanin  " anya  wannan  mijin  tane  kuwa  yake  wannan  kalaman,, ,, Allah  yagani  tin  kafin  tasan  kanta  take  son  garbati  , tana  kaunarshi "   ada  shima  yana  sonta " amma  inna  ta  sakasu  a  gaba   amma,, yanzu  shine  yake  bata  hakuri  


Safiya tace kanwata,,  kidena  wannan tunanin  kije,, wajan  mijin  insha Allah  ,, yadena  komai  wanda  zai  bata  miki  rai  


Mikewa,,     tayi  daga  jikin  safiya  ta  nufi  mijin  nata,, tana  murmishi  


 Bude  , mata  hannu  yayi  da  sauri  ta  fada  jikinsa,   tana  saka kuka  , tace  Alhamdulillah  ,,  Allah  nagode  maka  daka  dawomin  da  mijina  " hankalin  shi  



Murmishi  safiya da  amina  sukayi,  suna  farin cikin  daidai  ta,, ma'aurata  ,, safiya  tace  kanwata,, baa  ko  jin  kunyar ", muma  anwani  makale  miji  


Boye  fuskar  ta  bilkisu  tayi  tana dariya  tadawo  wajan  safiya  ,, tace  anty  Allah  yasa  miki da  gidan  aljannar firdausi,  ina  godiya  agareki " anty  inane  garinku " wata  rana  zanje  miki  ziyara  

Murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  niba  bil'adam  bace,, aljana,  ce  




Da sauri  bilkisu  ta  kalli  ta,  ,, Safiya  tace  kona  nuna  miki  kigani,  ne 


Bilkisu  tace  a a  anty  amma  kedin  ko  ajinsunku,  ke  mutuniyar kirki  ce  


Wani  zobe  tafito  dashi  daga,, hannun  ta  tasawa  bilkisu  tace  wannan  zoben  " karkiyi  wasa  dashi  ,, idan  na  zo  yankinku ", ko  nan  garin  ko  kusan  garinku,,  zakiga  tayi  haske  sosai  sai  wani  lokacin  ,, zamu  tafi  ki  kula  da  kanki,  kanwata,  tajuya  ta  kama  hannun  " Amina  


Da sauri  bilkisu  ta  rungume  safiya  tasa  kuka  tana  fadin  ,Dan Allah  kidawo  wani  lokacin  nakara  ,,ganinki   anty 


Juyawa,, tayi  tana " share  mata  hawayen fuskar  ta  ,, tace  kiyi hakuri  insha Allah  zanzo  naganki  idan  kika  haihu  kinji " inason  zuwa  yankin  mune  domin  na  kai " musu  ziyara  

Sakin bilkisu  tayi  tana  murmishi  tace to anty  , nan  su  aljana  safiya  suka,, bace  adakin,, 


Wajan ta  garbati " ya  matso  ya  rungume  ta  yana  bata  hakuri,  kafin  suka  nufi  gado  suka  kwanta   


 
Yaune,, tabawa  ta  antayo  gidan,,  talatu,, bako  sallama  tashigo    tana  fadin  anty  talatu  ko  baki  nan  kardai  ace  nayi,, zuwan  banza  to Allah  innatafi  bazan  sake  dawowa  ba 

Fitowa  talatu  tayi  daga  daki,, tana  washe  baki  tace  sannu  da  zuwa  tabawa  " muje  ciki  nan  sukayi  dakin  talatu  

Husna  najinsu " tana  daga  daki   sai  share  hawayen  ta  takeyi  abin tausayi,, rabonta  da  abinci  " tin jiya  da  dare  shima  wunin " ranar  talatu  bata  bata  abinci  ba  saida  baba  yazo  yakawo  mata ", taci  shine  acikinta  har  yanzu  gashi,, anyi  sallar magarib  baba  baizo  ba  ,,tana  tausayin  kanta  gata  karama, babu  wanda  yake  kaunar  ta "   daga,, kakarta  har ", yan  uwan  baban  ta  gashi  batasan  su  waye " dangin  maman  taba,,,,, kuka  tasake  sakawa  tana  fadin  mamana " mamana  kizo  kitafi  dani " basa   sona,, baba  kadai  ke  sona " mama  talatu  bata  bani  abinci,, waiyo Allah,, ni  husna Allah  ka  taimake ni  ka  kawomin  mai  taimako  na,  koda  ma  waye " ya  rabbi  ka  nunamin  mama  na, , naganta  naje  wajan  ta  nima  na  huta,, da  wannan  cin  zarafin  da  akemin  ,, tana  durkushewa  ,, saboda  yunwar  data  cin  mata  ciki   sai  kukun  yunwa  cikinta " kemata  " takasa  ,,   mikewa  tana  tsoron  fitowa  ,, mama  talatu  ta  mata  dukan " mutuwa,, a  hankali  ta  mike  ta  bude  dakin   ganin  ba  kowa  yasa,, tayi  ahankali  ,, ta  dauki  takalmin  ,,ta  ahannu  ta  gudu  waje,, zataje  gidan  kawarta " ko  zata  samu  abunda  zata  saka  a cikin  ta  nan , tabar  gidan 



Su  talatu  na  shiga  tabawa  tabude,, wata  leda  ta  dauko  magani  kala  uku  , ta  mikawa " talatu  daya  tace  wannan  acikin  abinci  zaki  zuba  mishi  yaci  idan , yaci  har  abada  " yadena  son  husna  zai  tsaneta  fiye  da  kima,, ita  da  kanta  zata  bar  gidan  tashiga  duniya  


Ta  dauko  dayan  tace  wannan  kuma   awajan  dayafi  zama  "zaki  zuba  mishi  idan  ya  zauna  ,, to  sai  abunda  kikace  mishi " bazai  tsalleke  umarnin  kiba "  komai  kikace,, mishi  



Ta  mika  Mata  dayan " tace,  wannan  kuma  idan  kika  zuba  mishi  a  ruwa  to  zai  manta  kowa  sai  ke  kadai,, zaike  gani,  



Cikin farin ciki  talatu  ta  rungume  kanwar  ta  ta  tace,, kai  amma  tabawa  ke  yar  uwa  ce  tajini,, mai  son  cigabana ,, aikuwa  zanyi  komai  ", bazanyi  kuskure  ba  


Dariya  itama,, tabawa  tayi  tace    ai  babu  abinda  bazan  miki  ba  yar  uwata,, nan  sukaci  gaba  da  hira,, kafin  tabawa  ta  tafi 

Fitowa  tayi  da  nufin  jibgar  ,,Husna  saidai  taga  ba  kowa  adakin,, muciyar  ta  rike  a  hannun ta  tace,, iyee  lalle  ma  wannan,  yarinyar  wato  yawon  iskancin,  kika  tafi  zakizo  kisameni  wllh  na  lahira  saiya  fiki  jin  dadi  , yar  iska  tambadaddiya,, zazo  gidan ai,, 


*muleka  wajan su sarki safuwan*

Sarki  safuwan  , tinda  sukaje,  wata  nahiya,  dake  yamma  da  kasar  masar,  nan  suka " nufi  gidan  sarki  da  murna  sarkin  " ya  taresu  nan  aka  kaisu  masauki "  aka  kawo  musu  abinci,  sukaci  sukasha  kayan  marmare,, kafin  sukazo  ganawa  da  sarkin 


Sarki safuwan yace  ranka  yadade,, Susana  sarki safuwan   mai  masarautar,  kasmuri,,  inason  na  zama,  shaharerren  sarki  " kamar  kai  mai  dumbun  tarin  dukiya "  

Amma  saidai  wata  aljana  ta  hanamu,, karbar  dukiyoyi  a  wajan  talakawa  da  kudin  haraji " mai  yawa  da  kayan  abinci  , wannan  zuwan  dazamuyi ,, saida  muka  biyo " har  cikin  dajin  da  take  rayuwa  ,, inason  mu  hada  karfi  , mu  kasheta   mu  kwashe,, dukiyar  ta  da  saura  ababen  jin  dadin  rayuwa 


Sarki  ya  dago  yana  kallon,, sarki safuwan  da  murmishi,, yace  ni  sunana  sarki shamsu " kuma  naji  dadin  wannan  shawara  taka  yanzu  haka  zancen  danake  maka  ta  tafi  yankinsu " ziyara  to  muyi  sauri  muje  dajin  mu  kwashi  rabonmu,,kafin  ta  dawo,,, kaga  idan  muka " gama  kwashe  komai  saimu  kashe  ta  ,,, yanzu  haka  bokana  ya  tura  aljannu  sunje  yankinsu " su  karkashe  wasu  daga  cikin  yan  garinsu  


Dariya  mugunta  sarki  safuwan  yayi  yace, , haka  nakeson  jarumi,, yanzu  maza  muhada  kayan  yaki  mutafi   ,, 


Sarki  shamsu  yabada  umarnin  ahada  kayan  yaki  za'a  tafi  yaki,, nan  da  nan  aka  hada  kayan  yaki  da  dakaru  sunfi  dubu,  suka  kama  hanya  



 
Aljana safiya,, kuwa  tana  zuwa  garinsu,, ta  iske  tashin hankali  , da  gudu  ta  yi  dakin  baban  ta  wani  razanannan  kara  hade  da,, ihu  aljana  safiya  tayi,, wanda  yasa  duk,, yan  gari  suka  birgita  ,, suna  fadin  safiya  ce  tazo  lalle  tazo  akan  gabar  da  zata  taimake  mu,, gyara  da  Allah  yazo  da  ita 



*ni  kuma  nafece  domin  na  dauko  kayan  yakina nabi  aljanar jarumai*

Daga  cen  bangaren  ,,Tabawa  tace  kutumar " dumadu,  lalle  malam,  yataro  mash    dole  mukoma  wajan  bokan  nan  dan  bazai,  haba  yiyuba  acin  miki  mutumci  saboda  wannan  yarinyar  ,, mai  kama  da  suffar  aljannu  " shi  har  ya  isama " ki  kamata  ki  mata  tsinan  duka  ki  hanata  " abinci   kuma  ki  ja kunnen  ta  idan " fadawa  babanta  zaki  hanya  ta " dan  ubanta   shegiya " uwar  tama  munga  bayanta  bare  ita,, kamar  kwaruwa

Tabawa  tace,, ai  ki  kwantar  " hankalin  ki  ni  indai  ina  raye  bazan  bari  wani abu   ya  takura  miki,  ba  koda  zanyi  " yawo  tsirara  saina  faranta " miki  rai tinda  kika  shawo  min " kan   mijina " harya  aureni   yanzu  gamu  da  yara  dan  haka  kibar  komai  a  hannuna " ni  zanje  gidan  boka,  da  kaina  idan  nadawo  zan  zo  gidan  naki

Aljana  safiya  tinda,  ta  tashi  sama  take  tunani,, kala  kala,, tana  aiyanawa " a  ranta  da  zarar  taje,  wajan  yan  uwan  ta,, sati  biyu  zatayi  ,, idan  da  hali  in  kuma  sun  cenja  halayen  su,, dan  bazata  zauna  da  " azzalumai  a  inuwa  daya  ba  tana  gani,, suna  cutar,  bayin  Allah  ,, rikidewa  tayi takoma,, tsuntsuwa,, fara  sal  mai  mugun  kyau,, kasa  kasa  " tasoma  yi  ganin  tazo  wani,, gari  ,


Tin  kafin  tasauka  take  jiyo  sautin  kukan  wata,, mata   da  sauri  tayo  wajan  da  takejin  kukan  , a  razane  take  kallon  mutunan " su    uku  , wani  namiji  tagani   tana  ,, dukan  wata  mace  mai  ciki  ,, amma  sauran  mutanen  basu  hanashi,  ba  saima  dariya  dasuke  yi  ,da  sauri  ta  shiga  gidan "

Wani  kallon  yan  giya,, ya  aika  mata  yace,,  munafuka,, hadda  wani  yin  fuskar  " tausayi  to  baki  isaba  na  kyaleki " yarinya  maza  zo  nan  ki  ciremin  kayana " mu  kwanta  yana  yin  langi  



Danne  fushin  ta  aljana  safiya  tayi  ,, tana  tunkarar " sa  tana  isa  ta  kifeshi  da  maruka  har  shida  ko  wani  share  uku  



Sumar  tsaye,  garbati  yayi , ganinshi  yadauke,, yasoma  ganin  duhu  saboda,, marukan  sun  shigeshi,, sai  cen  yace  bilkisu  " ni  kika  mara  haka,, to  yau  zanga  uban  daya  daurawa,, uwarki  aure  ya  mike  tsaye  




 Aljana  safiya  tace  an  mareka  din  azzalumi  macuci,, maha'inci  ,,  maci  amanar  da  Allah  yabasa,, dan  iska  kawai dan  giya  " tona  gaji  yau kakaini  bango,, nace  na  gaji  tafada  ta  karfi  kamar  dakin  zai  rushe  saboda  amon  muryata,  



Ai  ba  shiri  garbati  yaji  ,, cikinsa  ya  murda  miki,, saboda  tsananin  birgitar  dayayi,,  saboda  taurin  kai  irin na  garbati,,  yasake  ,, yowa  kan  ,, Bilkisu  


Shakarshi  tayi,,ta  daga  sama  ta  bugashi  ,, da  kasa  tana  buci 


Da  kyer  ya  mike  yace  wai  bilkisu  lfyr  ki  kuwa  ni  kika,, nunawa  Allah  kika  sakoni  kasa,, kuma  ma  ai  nasan,,  bakida  karfin  dagani,,  saboda  cikin  ki  nan  


Banza  ,, tamishi  ta  juya  baya  dan  tasoma  cenja  kamanni,, janyota  yayi,  zuwa  jikinsa,, ta  fada  


Wani  dauyi  ,, da  tsami  taji  yana  yi   matse  mishi  ciki  tayi  sosai  tana  fadin  kara  dukana  ,zaka  sake  zagarmin  iyaye  ko  zaka  sake  wulakanta  ni 


Ai  bari  shiri  garbati  yasaki,, zayo  ajikinsa  yana  fadin  bazan  karaba  bilkisu  wllh  inasonki,,  ina  kunarki  inna  ce  take  sakani " na  dakeki  idan  na  kiya  sai  tace  ,zata  tsinemin,, kiyimin  rai  bilkisu  



Sakinsa  tayi  yafadi  tim,  yana  haki  ga  duk  yabata  jikinsa  da  zayo  saboda  matsar  taya  sha

Tin  karosa  tasomayi,, tana  fadin  azzalimi,, kai  ubanka  ,, haka  yarike  uwarka  ,, kana  azabtar  da  yarinyar  mutane  dan  kaga  danginta,, basa  kusa da  ita  she,, kuke  cutar  da  ita  yanzu , ko  tausaya  mata  bakayi,, ga  tsohon  ciki  ga,, dukan  da  kake   mata,, kullin    to  yau,, saina  kashe  ka  dan  iska,  



Da  sauri  ya durkusa,,   yana  fadin wllh  ina  son  bilkisu  innata  ce  take  cewa  zata,, tsinemin  inban  zaneta  ba  amma,, wllh  ina  son  matata  kiyimin  rai  , zan  gyara  halina  namiki  alkawari  zan  gyara " idan ban  gyara  ba,, ki  kasheni  ,, waiyo Allah  nashiga  uku  ni  garbati,,  kuyi  hakuri  ku  kuke  ganin  mu  bake  ganinku  ba    tana  rawar  sanyi  duk  ya  kidime " daga  yadago  ya  kalli  safiya  saita  zare  miki  ido  ,, aikuwa  hantar,,   cikinsa  ta  kada  yama  rasa,, inda  zai  saka  kanshi

Taba  wuyan  ta  tayi  taga,, ashe ta mutu,, wani  kururuwa  tayi  ta  zubai  akasa  tana  kuka  kamar,  zata  hadiye  zuciyar  ta,, saida  tayi  mai  isarta  " kafin  tazo  wajan  baban  ta  ,, tana fadin   abbana  dan Allah  ka  taimakeni " katashi  karka  tafi  ka  barni  kamar  yadda  mama  ta  tafi,  na  rokeka  baba  kada " ku  maidani  marainiya    babana  ,

Ajiyar zuciya yasauke,, yasoma  bude  idonshi ,, ahankali  yake  budewa  har  yagama  budesu  akan  yar  tashi " da  sauri  yamike  yana  fadin,, Safiya  ta ce  nake  gani  ,, yaushe  kikazo  y'ata  kinzo  alokacin  da  nakeson  ,, ganinki  jarumar  y'ata

 ganin  , tafiya  

Da sauri  ,, sukayo  wajan  ta  ,, kallon  su  take,  tuni  tadena  kuka  saboda  kar  sugano  rauninta,, tace  musu  , lfy  


Wata  mata  ta  zo  kusan  tazube a  kasa  dauke  da  wata  yarinya,, tana  kuka,, tace  safiya  dan Allah  kitaimake " mu  yarinya  ta  bata  da  lfy,, wai  ance  mayu  ne,, tun  jiya " wasu  mugaye  suka  kawo  mana  hari,, bamu  ankara  ba  suke  ta  kashemu " yanzu  haka  sunkashe  yan uwan  mu  sunkai,   dari biyu  "" kasucen  a  kwance,   sunce  wai  idan  kin  isa  kidauki  mataki,, dajin  naki ma  shesun  tarwatsa  miki ,, shi   dan Allah  ,, safiya  kiyi  wani  abu  , kinji  yar  albarka  


Wani  tukuk'in  bakin,, cikine  yarufe,, aljana safiya  har  idonta,, ya  sauya  launi  ,, tace  karki  damu  ,, insha Allah  saina  yi  maganin  su,  saina  nuna  musu  su  kananan  yan  iska  ne " yanzu  mazan  kuzo  akai  mama  na  makwancin  ta,  da  sauran  wanda,, suka  mutu,  kafin  musan  abinyi,  


Kowa na  wajan  jikinsa  a sanyaye,, yake  wasu  na  kuka  wasu  na " hawaye  abundai  abin  tausayi  , 

Kutsayawa,, tayi  cikin  mutanen " wajan  wanda  suka  mutu  tanufa " durkusawa  tayi ganin  aminiyar  ta  kwance  cikin  jini,, an  mata  kisan  gilla, ,,  ji  take  kamar  zuciyar  ta  zata  fito waje  dan  tsananin  zafin , da  take  mata " ganin  aminiyar ta    da mahaifiyar  ta  da  sauran,, yan  garinsu  duk  kwanci  cikin,, jini   duk  anmusu,, kusan  wulakanci,,,, ahankali  ta  taba  fuskar  kawar  ta  ta  tace " Allah  yajikanki,, khubura,, Allah  yasa  aljanna  ce  bakomarku " insha Allah  jinin ku  bazai   tafi,, a  banza  ba  saina  dauki " fansar  abinda  aka  muku,  ,,, wannan  alkawarina  ne  


Mikewa  tayi tadawo,, wajan  baban ta,  suka  shiga  ciki  tuni   aka  shirya  " wanda  suka  mutu  aka  kaisu  makwancin  su "  kafin  suka  dawo  ,, gida  ana  jajantawa  



Aljana safiya  tashiga  tsananin  damuwa,,  marar musaltuwa,,, tana  tunanin  wasu  bakaken  aljanu  ne  suka  " zo  yankinsu,,   dakinsu  takoma,  tana  duba " inda  maman  ta  ta  kwanta " lokacin  datake  a  mace  wajan  ,, ta  shafa,  tana  rintse  idon  ta  " cen  ajima  kafin  ta  bude   da sauri  " tana  fadin  boka  shaddar " kaine  katuro  wa,, yankina  wannan  mugayen  ,, dansu  kashe  su


To  ka  dauko  ruwan  dafa  kanka  ni  zaka  ciwa  mutumci  mashirkin  banza,, kafiri  kawai to  , wannan  karon  bazan  barka  ba " saina  kasheka  daga  kai  har" wanda  suka  sakaka  aikin,  , zanyi  maganinku  insha Allah,  da  izinin  sa,, nan  ta  mike  ta,, fito  daga  dakin  tana  fadin " abba !,abba! abba,  tana  nufar  wata  bishiyar,  mangwaro,, dake,  bayan  dakinsu  


Amsawa  yayi  ,, yace  lfy  safiya  kike  kirana  ,, haka    karasowa  tayi  ta  zauna  kusanshi,, tana  kwantowa  ta  bayanshi,, tace  abba,  kasan  dacewa  boka  shaddar  ne  ya  " turo  wannan  bakaken  aljanun,,, sukayi  ta'addanci  anan  garin 


Da sauri  ya  juyo  yace  dama  shine,, yaturosu  to  inaso  kinuna  musu  ,, cewa  ke  jinina  ce  kin  isa  , na  baki  kwana biyu  ,, ki  shirya  tsab  kije,, inda  yake  ki  kashe  dan   banza  mushiriki,,,, saboda,,cen  shekarun  baya  ma  shi  yaturo su,, fada  miki  ne  banyiba,, dan  alokacin  " kina  cikin  bacin  ran  fadan  ,, dakukayi  keda  yan  uwan  ki,, yanzu  nabaki  izinin  kije  ki,, tarwatsa min  dan  iska 


Numfashi  safiya  tasauke  tace  ,, insha Allah  babana  zanyi  haka  zai  shirya  sosai,  naje  narama  abinda  yamana    


Abban  nata  yace  Allah  yamiki  albarka,  ,, tace  ameen  abbana  ,, nan  sukaci  gaba da  hirar  maman  ta  wani  wajan  tayi  dariya,, wani  wajan  tayi kuka  





Bangaren,  husna  na  fita  gidan,, kawarta  taje,  sailuba  taje,  nan  suka  rungume  juna, ta  karasa  wajan  maman  sailuba  ta  durkusa,  kamar  babba  tace,  mama,, ina  yini  


Da  murmishi  ta  amsa  da  lfy lau  husna,  ya  baban  ki  da  talatu,  a  hankali  tace  lfy lau  mama  , 

Kallon  ta  maman  sailuba  tayi  taga  yadda,, tarame,  tausayin  tane  yacika  mata  ciki,, tashi  tayi  ta  dauko  mata,  shinkafa  da  miya,  da  lemon mai  sanyi,,da  ruwa  da  kunun  aya,, tace  zo  husna  maza  zauna  kici,, kinji  karki  kara  " nan  ma  gidanku  ne  

Mikewa  husna  tayi,, ta zo  ta  zauna  ganin  abinci  mai rai da  lfy  ,, yasa  tasoma  share  hawayen  tausayin kanta,,, rabon da  taci  wannan,, harta  manta  "amma  gashi  yau  maman  sailuba  tabata,  nan  ta  soma cin abinci  , kamar  bata  taba,, cin  abinci  ba  haka  takeyi,, idan  ta saka  wani  abaki  kafin  ta  hadiye,, ta  kuma  zuba wani  abakin  


Mikewa,, maman sailuba  tayi  ,tashige  daki,  tana  zuwa,, tasoma  kuka  kasa  kasa  "tace  kai  duniya  wannan  wacce  iriyar  matar  uba  ce,, dibi  husna  duk  ta  fita  haiyacinta,, kamar  va  itaba,,  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un  Allah  ya  yakawo  miki  dauki  husna,, ya Allah  Allah yakawo  wanda  zaiyi  sanadiyar  ,, rabaki  da  wannan  bakin  cikin,, na  gidanku,, tana  fadin haka tasake  saka kuka,, jin  sailuba  na  buga kofar  tana,   fadin  mommy  , kibude " Husna  zata  tafi  gida,, share  hawayen fuskar ta  tayi  " ta  wanke  fuskar  kafin tabude 


Sailuba  tagani  da  husna   tace  ,husna  har  kin  koshine,, murmishin  jin  dadi  husna  tayi,,tace  nakoshi  mama  harnayi  saura  ma    

Mama tace,  a a maiyasa " baki  cinye  dukaba  , Sailuba  dauko,, leda  na  juye  mata,, idan  dare  yakara  yi   saiki  ci  kinji  


Fashewa  da  kuka  husna tayi  ,tace  mama  nagode  sosai  Allah  yasaka da alkhairi  wllh  yau  kwanana  biyu,  bana  samun abinci  naci,, sai  ragowar  biskota na  nake  ci  da  ruwa,, mama  a  duniya  ku  uku  ke  sona  daga  baba,   sai ke  sai  sailuba  , amma  kowa  ya  tsaneni,, saboda  banida  gatan  uwa,  


Itama  maman  sailuba  hawaye  tagoge  tace,, ba  komai  husna  kici  gaba  da hakuri  insha Allah  , zaki  fita  daga  wannan  kuncin  rayuwar  in Allah  ya  yarda  


Rungume  mama  husna  tayi kafin  suka  fita  sailuba  ta  rakata  ,, har  kofar  gida  kafin  sukayi  sallama,  


Tana  zuwa,  bakin  kofar  gidansu  ,,cire  takalmin  ta  tayi  tana  tafiya  a  hankali,, taci  sa'a  talatu  tashiga  daki,  da  sauri  tashige  , dakin  ta  ta  rufo,  , tunawa  tayi  da  takalmin  ta  a  kofar  dakin, nan  ta tashi da sauri  " tafito,  zata  dauki  takalmin  kenan,, saiga  " talatu tafito " aikuwa  da  sauri  ta  dauko  muciyar ta,  ta  nufo  husna  

Husna  bata  ankara  ba  taji  saukar,, muciya  abayan  ta,  kafin  ta  dago  tuni  talatu , ta  rufeta  da  duka  tako  ina,, 

Ihu husna  keyi  tana fadin amma  kiyi hakuri,  dan Allah  maina miki kike  duka  na,,,  talatu  ko  kulata  batayi,, taringa  jibgarya,, kamar  ta  samu  wani  babban  mutum,, saida  taga  husna  bata  motsi " tukun  tabar  husna  ,, yashe  a  bakin  kofar  daki  tana  maida  numfashi  


Kama  kugu  talatu  tayi,, tana  fadin  yar  iska  tambadaddiya,, ni  zaki  hada  sa  malam  wato  ,, shine  yanzu kika  tafi  yawon  karuwancin  naki,, wannan inkika,, kai  shekara  ashirin  ai  mun  shiga uku,, banza  jaka  mai  suffar  aljanu   to wllh,, kadan  ma  kika  gani  indai  nice  uban  naki  da  kike  takama "  dashi  saina  nabaku  naga,, wazakike  fadawa ,, ta  fice  dakinta,  



Ahankali  husna  taja, jikinta  shiga  dakin,, kan  katifar  ta  ta  zube  tana  share  hawaye dan kukan  ma  ya  gagareta " saboda  mugun  dukan  dataci,, 


Hannun  ta  daya,  zata  miki  taji  yayi  mata " dauyi  sosai  harya  kumbura,, zare ido  tayi  ta  saka  kuka  tana  fadin  ,,  waiyo Allah  baba  shikenan  ta  karya min  hannu,, waiyo  hannuna  baba  kazo  mama ta  karya  ni  ,,,, saida  taga  ciwon  dai,, karuwa  yakeyi  sosai  yasa  tamike  " tana  dingisawa,, ahankali  ta   tashiga  toilet,   , ruwan  zafi  tahada  ta  shiga  , ciki  dan  karamin towel  dinta  , na  janyo,  ta  tsoma  shi  a  ruwan 

A  hankali  ta  daura  a  hankali,  wani  azabar  zafi,, taji yashige  ta " kara  tasaki,  tana  yarfa  hannu,,  wata  zuciya  tace  haba  , husna  kizama  jaruma  mana " ki  gasa  zai  wake,  dauke,, wa  tayi  yasaka,  saka  towel  din " a  ruwa  ta matse  kadan,, ta  daura  s  hannun " runtse  ido  tayi  ,, ta  cije  baki  ta  ringa  danna  wa,, duk  jikinta " da  hannun  ta  daya  samu  targade,  ,, haka  tayi  ta  gasa  kanta  saida  " ruwan  ya  huce  tasake  zuba  wani,  ta  cigaba  da  gasawa " saida  taji,  jikinta  yasaki ", kafin  ta  bari  ta  fito,, daga  toilet  din 



Jitayi,, jikinta,, baya   mata  ciwo,  sai  hannu  ta  sakeyi " kadan  kadan  zama  tayi  tana  dubo  maganin  da  baba  yasayo  mata " da  kyer  tasamesa " cikin  kayanta,  da  suke  a  tabge  a  share  daya,, biyu  ta  balla  tasha,  ta  koma  ta  zauna bakin  katifar 


Kuka take,  , tana  tunanin  rayuwar  ta,, ita  batasan  maitawa   mama talatu  ba  " take  azabtar  da  ita  haka, ,, yanzu  ita  haka  zatayi  rayuwar  ta  har ta  mutu,, gashi  mama  talatu " ta  hana  ta  zuwa  school  da  islamiya  ,, tana  fitowa  zata  ringa  " dukanta,    


Tana  tuna  ranar,  da  ,, mama  talatu  taje  islamiyar  su,, ta  kamata,, tace  wai  malamin  su  husna  " yana  nuna  mata  gabanshi,, zai  lalata  mata  diya " to  ita  bazata  yadda  wani  sangamemen " banza  ya  lalata  mata  yarinyar  da  batafi  yar  shekara  goma  sha  ba,

Shi  kuwa  malamin  " datawa  kazafi  ,, bawan Allah  yaringa  rantsuwa " akan  baisan  zancen  ba  Sheri  talatu  ke  mishi,, wllh  bai  taba  nunawa  wata  mace  iskanci  ba  bare " Husna  yar  karamar  yarinya,, yana  kukan  takaici  takuma " sherin  da  talatu  tamishi  ,,  amma  haka  malaman  suka  ki  yadda  dashi  suka  koreshi,, hadda   hadamishi,, yara  suna  binshi  , da  ihu  ,, suna  fadin  kwarto  dan  iska,  malam  yazama  dan  iska  " har  gidanshi  suka  kaishi,, suna  mishi  ihu  kwarto  wannan  abin  yayi  dalilin  rabuwar,, aurenshi  da  matarshi " tace  bazata  zauna  da  fasiki  ba,  tabar  mishi  gida ", kamar  yayi  hauka, haka  yaringa  ji  alokacin,  haka  ya  hakura  " ya  rungumi  yaranshi  biyu 


Talatu  kuwa  tace,, tacire  husna  daga  islamiyar  nan  babban  malamin  yaringa  bata,, hakuri  amma  tace  sam  bazai  yiyuba  a  bata  mata  yarinya  ba,, taja  husna  sukabar  islamiyar  ,, wannan  shine  dalilin  fidda  husna  daga  islamiya,, tadena  zuwa  



Bayan kwana biyu  ,, hallau  talatu  tashirya takama  husna  sukaje  school  dinsu  husna,, tana  kukan  karya,, ta  nufin  office  din,, principal  , tana  zuwa  bakin  kofa  ta  daura  hannu  biyu  aka  ta  rusa  kuka  " tana  fadin  na  shiga  uku  ni  talatu  dama  haka  makarantar ku  take,   to  wllh  bazan  yadda  ba  sai  anceremin  diyata  kona  kaiku " kara  kotu,, fitowa  principal   din  tayi  tace  , lfy  hajiya  

Hawayen  karya  talatu  ta  goge,, tace  , babu  lft  tinda  kika  ganni  ai  ba  lfy  takawo  niba  , kin  ,, wannan  yarinyar  tawa,, tin  jiya  take  zabura  , tana  kuka,,, tana  nuna  husna  da  itama  kukan  takeyi  " ganin  talatu  tana  mata sheri,  


Principal  tace,, maiyasamu  husna  din  

Talatu  tace,, mayya  ce  takama , kuma tana  wannan  makarantar  ,, bayan  nan  ma  tace  wata  , na  sa  mata  hannu  a  gabanta,,, shine  tafada  min  ni  kuma  bazan  yadda " a  maidamin  yarinya  karuwa  ba  dama  kuna  da  yan  madigo  a  makarantar  nan,, bansani  ba  nakawo  diyata  to  daga  yau  nacireta " gyara  na  cenja  mata  wata  sabuwa,,  


A  rikice  principal  din  ke  kallon  talatu  tana  bata  hakura  ,, amma  talatu  tayi  kunan  uwar  shegu  ta  firta  kwallin  rashin mutumci  ,ta  zazzagi  principal,, ta  mata  tass  tukun  ta  kama  hannun  husna  suka  dawo  gida  , 


Tin  kafin  su karasa ciki,, Talatu  ta  hankada  husna  ciki  tafadi,,  tace  munafuka  ,, yanzu  sainaga  inda  zaki  fita  ,, bake  ba  karatu  har  abada,, fice  ni  kibani  waje 

Da gudu  husna  tashiga  dakinta,, tana  kuka  tana fadin wai  baba  mama,, tacireni  a  school  da  islamiya  ,, shikenan  nadena  karatu  , haka  ta  ringa  kuka  saida  kanta yasoma  mata  ciwo  tukun,,  tayi  shuru  ,

Ajiyar zuciya tasauke  lokacin data  dawo  daga  tunanin  da  takeyi,, share  hawayen  ta  tayi,, ta  mike  a  hankali  ta  janyo  ledar  ,, abinci  da  maman  sailuba  ta  zubo  bata " sauran  abincin  data  yi  tasomaci,, tana  jan  zuciya   tana  fara   cin abinci,, takasa ci  saboda  "  zafin  da  kirjinta,, kemata,  ajiyawa  tayi  ta  wanke  hannun  ta  ta  dauro alwala  tayi   shafa'i  da  wutir, kafin  ta  kwanta,  



Talatu  ta  dauko  maganin  da  tabawa  takawo  mata,, tazuba   wa  baba  acikin abinci  dayan  kuma  acikin  ruwa  dayan,  a  zuba,, a  inda  yake  zama  ,,,, jin  yana  sallama  da  sauri  ta  kade  hannun  ta  ta  tana  amsa  sallamar  shi,, da  mike  ta  taroshi  

Tace  sannu da zuwa  malam,, tin safe  sai  yanzu,, wajan  karfe  takwas  da rabi,, Allah  yasa  dai lfy  ,,  babu  abinda  yasa  meka  


Baki  asake  baba  ke  kallon,, ta  ganin  tana  mishi  magana  a  taushe  kuma  tana  murmishi "

 sai  yaji  sanyi  aranshi  yana  fadin Alhamdulillah  kila  Allah  yanufi  talatu  da  shiriya 
Dan  tabashi  tayi  tace  ,, a a  malam  tunanin  mai  kakeyi,, ne  haka  muje  ciki  


Murmishi  yamata  yace  to  ina  husna  ko  tayi harta  ci  abinci  ta  kwanta ko,,, danne  bacin  rai talatu  tayi  tana  yar dariyar  karya tace  malam  aikasan " husna  ta  son  bacci  hartaci  abinci ta  kwanta,, 


Baba yace  masha Allah nan  suka  nufi  dakin,,  abinci  ta  gabatar  mishi  da  ruwa  , zai  zauna  yace  bismillahi  tukun  ,, yazauna  ya  janyo  ,, abinci  shima  bismillah,, yayi  kafin  yasa  hannu,,har  yakai  bakinshi " yaji  wani  irin  wari "  yace  kai  abincin  nan  nakoshi,  gaskiya  nan  yatashi  ya  wanke  hannu,,, daga  cen  yasha ruwa " yadawo  dakin, 


Talatu  kamar  ta  fasa  ihu  ganin  baba  yakicin  ,, abinci  da  ruwan ma  duka  baici  komai, ba  hade  rai  tayi,, tace  haba  mlm  bazaayi  na  hadama " abinci  kace  baka  ci  haba  dan Allah,, tayi  kamar  zatayi  kuka,, zama  yayi  kusanta  ya  kamo  hannun  ta  ,, yace  talatu  kiyi hakuri  wllh  naci abinci  abakin  masallaci " aka  fiddo  mukaci  amma  ki  ajiye  min da  safe  ki dumama  min ' karkiyi  hushi  kinji,, 


Wani sanyi  taji,, sai  tace  to  mlm  shikenan  bari " na  ajiye a  kitchen,, nadawo  mu  kwanta,, yace  to,, 

Tana fita,  kamar  ance  kalle  cen  yaga  wata  leda,  daukowa  yayi  ,, yaga  maganine " har  kala uku,,   daukewa  yayi  yasaka  a  jakar  zuwa  kasuwar shi,,, yace  haba  koda  naji fa " ashe  da  cutace  zanci to Alhamdulillah,,, nan  ya  kwanta 

Yana  ganin lokacin  da  take,  , neman  maganin  amma  yayi  shuru  yana  kallon ta,, tabbas,,talatu  batada  mutumci " yanzu  bayan  azabar  da  takewa  husna  ,, shine  takeson  cutar  dashi,, lalle  Allah  yakare  min  husna,, ta  nan  yayi addu'a  ya  kwana  



Yau  ne  aljana  safiya  ,, zataje  inda  boka  saddar  yake,,  tafito  cikin  shirin  sosai,, duka  yan  garin,, nasu  ta  hada  kansu  tace  kowa,, yahado,, yaranshi  kar  abar  mutum,,, koda  daya  ne,, zata  kaisu   dajin ta " saboda  su  samu  tsaro,, sosai  idan  tace  tafiya  zatayi"" tabarsu,,  to  za'a  iya  kawo  musu,, wani  harin,, 


Kowa  yayi  na'am  da  shawarar  ta  shiyasa  suka fito  kwainsu  da  kwarkwatar su,,     ta  kama  hannun,, baban  ta  tace  kowa  yabiyo  ta,,, nan  suka  bacce,, cikin  dajin  takaisu " kowa  tanuna  mishi  inda  zai  zauna"  nan  tafito  da  baban ta  dakinta " takai  shi  tace  baba  zauna  anan  karka  fita  zanje  dajin  boka  shaddar  ,, saina  kashe  shi " tinda  yakashe  min  yan uwa  da  mahaifiya "  saina  mishi  kisan  gilla,


Abban  nata  ya  kalle  ta,, hannun  ta  ,, yakama  yaciro  wata  zobe  mai  tsananin  kyau  yasa  mata,, yace  wannan  zobe  kiriketa  " da  kyau,,  gadonta  nayi,, kinji  insha Allah  zakiyi " nasara  


Durkusawa,,, Safiya  tayi  abban  ta  ya  dafa  kanta,, yayi  ta  jero  mata  ,, addu'a  saida  yagama  kafin,, yace  fatan  nasara  yar albarka,,,, rungume  shi  tayi  ,, tace  nagode  abbana  inasonka  


Fuskar  ta  yashafa,, yamata  murmishi  yace,, adawo lfy nima  ina  kaunarki,, nan  tafito  



Tana  fitowa  , namukan " dajin  sukayo  kanta  da  murna,, kallon  su  tayi  tana  yar dariya  tace,, ga  amana  nan  ku  kulamin  da  abbana  da  yan uwa  na,, zanje  farautar,, wani  mushiriki,  ,  suka  ce  mata to,, tace  idan  kukaji  wata  matsala  kimin  kiran,, gaggawa " nan  ta  bace  a  dajin  



Su  sarki safuwan,, da  karfin  tsafin  sarki  shamsu  ,, suke  kutsawa,, cikin  dajijjikan,, sarki  shamsu yace  , mun  kusa  zuwa  , aljanar  takawo  danginta,  ,, ta  tafi,, amma  na  kasa  gano  inda  tanufa,  


Da  murna  sarki  safuwan  , yace  mukara  sauri  ranka yadade,,   sarki shamsu,  yace to  nan  suka  kara  karfin  gudu  ,, kowa  kagani  da  manyan  kayan  yaki  


Aljana safiya  ce  ta  dira  akan  wani  tsubirin,, dutse,,  tana  neman  koda  da  bokan  yake,,,  cen  ta  hangi,, ya  turo  mata bakaken  aljanun  da sukaje  yankinsu,  ,, murmishi  tayi  tace,, dama,, kunaso " na  fara  kashewa,,, banzaye  nan  ta  gyara  tsayuwar  ta  tana  jiran  suzo  


Boka shaddar,, kuwa  ganin,, aljana safiya  yasashi  hada  su  tsafinshi  a  waje  daya,, yana  hada  kayan  yaki  kafin  takaraso  


Da gudu,, Safiya   tayo  kansu,,, suma  sa  gudu  sukayo  kanta,, nan  suka  kama  aazababben,, yaki"" sosai  take  yakarsu  saboda  wannan  zoben  da  baban ta  yabata " yakara  mata  karfi,, nunkin  banunkin,, 


Yaki  akeyi  sosai  bakajin  komai  sai  karan  takubba " da  kururuwar  wanda  in  an  kashe  shi,, sun  shafe  , awa  biyu  suna  yakar  juna, kafin  wasu  mintocin  ,, tuni  ,, aljana safiya ta  kashe su  tass  ,, tsayawa   tayi  tana  maida  numfashi " tana  kallon  boka shaddar,  

Tace  kai  shedani,, yau  karya  ka  takare "zanga  wanda  ke  saka,, ka  aika  akashe min  dangi  jinsina  ma,, nafi  karfinsa  bare  kai  dan  adam  



Wata mahaukaciyar dariya  boka shaddar,, yasaki  tana  fadin  saidai  na  kasheki " dama  wannan  ranar  nake  jira  tazo  yau  gaki  kinzo  dan  haka  yai  ,, zaki  mutu  yake  aljana  mai  taurin kai

Fada  akeyi,, tsakanin  aljana safiya  da  boka  shaddar   kamar  bazasu  gajiba, ,, sai  ya  zo  zai  soketa,  saita  bace  


Shammacer  sa  tayi  , ta  daba  mishi  takofin  ta  mai  tsananin  kaifi  da  tsini  a cinyarsa  


Wani  ihu  boka  shaddar yayi,  wanda  ya  gauraye   dajin   dabbobin,, cikin  dajin  suka  soma  tsare,, saboda  kidimewa  dasuka  


Zubewa  yayi  a kasa   yana  haki " amma  dayake  taurin  kai  garesa,,  ta  mike   yana  jan  kafa   ya  dauki  takofinsa   yana  nufar  safiya  


Murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace,, kai azzalimi  ,, yau  karshenka  yazo  munafiki  " saina  kashe  ka  nan  tayo  kanshe  gadan  gadan  , 


Shima  da  gudu,   yayo  wajan  ta,,  nan  suka  rikice  da  azababben  yaki  sosai  


Aljana  safiya  kuwa  so  take  taga  iya  gudun  ruwan  boka  shaddar  ,, da  yake  wannan  abu  kamar  wani  jarumi,, saida  ya  gaji  sosai,, da  kyer  yake  kare  kanshi  amma  saboda  tsabar  taurin  kai  irin  na  boka  shaddar  ,, bai  dena  fadan  ba 


Aljana  safiya  ita  ko  dukan  ba  bata  kaimishi,, ita  abin  dariya  ma  yakebata  wai  wannan  yakine  shiyasa  kawai  ta  maida  abin  kamar  wasa    idan  yakawo  mata  suka  saita  bace  shima  bacewa  yakeyi  da  karfin  tsafinsa  


Samar  kan  dutse  suka  koma  da  fadan  ,


Aljana  safiya  ,, cen  tasoma  tunanin  yadda  zata  karya  tsafin  zoben  hannun  ta  ta  murza  nan  takawo  haske  rufe  idanta  tayi  tana  kallon  duk  abinda  zoben  ke  nuna  mata  , da  sauri  ta  bude  idonta  tana  kallon  boka  shaddar  da  yake  kokarin,, daba  mata  wuka    da sauri  ta  hankada  shi  ya  fado  daga  kan dutsen  


Nan  ta  bace  cikin  bukkar  ta  bayyana,  tana  fuba  inda  zoben  nata  ke  haska  mata  , wata  gwarya  tagani  tana,, budewa  wani  tashin hankali ta  shiga  dan ganin  abinda  ke  cikin  gwaryar  

Kayan tsafi  ne  aciki  kala  kala  da  mugayen  aljanu  nan  taga  wani  aljanu  masu  tsare  kayan tsafin,  kamar  dai  basu  san  tagansu  ba  


Da sauri  ta  zare  takobin  ta  tana  tukarar  wata  kofa  ,, dake  cikin  dakin  , wani  feli  tagani  mai  dakuna  sunfi  goma   a filin   nan  ta  tasoma  shiga  dakin dake  kusanta  


Tana  shiga  tayi  turus  ganin  wasu       mutane  sunkai  goma,   sunyi  tsaye  kamar  gumakai  


Addu'a  taringa  yi  sosai  tana  runtse  ido  , saita  tuno  da  babanta  yace  mata  boka  shaddar  , mutum  ne  mai  mugun  hatsari  dan  haka  duk  inda  taje  take  anfani  da  wannan  zoben  zata  nuna  Mata  komai  


Mude  idonta  tayi  tana  kallon  mutanen  yanzu  sun  fadi  kasa  wani  bakin hayaki  na  fita daga  duk  ilahirin  jikinsu  

Hannun  ta  ta  daga  ,, wanda  zoben  ke  jiki  ta  saitashi  kan  mutanen  ,, nan  yabada  haske  sosai  duka  ya  haske  su   wannan  bakin  hayakin  nata  fita  da  sauri  sai  ihun  aljanun   dake  jikinsu  yake  fita  saboda  azabar  ta  sukeji  tunu suka  kone  


Saida  taga  sun  fara  motsawa  , kafin  ta   sauke  hannun  ta  , kowa  yasoma  motsawa  ,, kafin  su  bude idon su  aljana  safiya  tayi saurin  canza  halittar  jikinta   " zuwa  wata  mace  mai kyau  ,, tayi  lullubi,, sosai  ta  tsaya  tana  jiran  su  bude  ido  


Daya  daga  cikinsu  ta  bude idonta,, da sauri   ta  mike  tana  fadin  innalillahi  wa inna ilaihin raju'un  ina  ne  nan  , mommy  waya  kawomu,, wannan  dakin  mai  abin tsoro 


Sauran  ma  tashi  sukayi  suna  ambaton  sunan  Allah  sai  waige  waige  sukeyi,   


Ganin  basu  lura  da  safiya  ba  yasa  tayi  gyaran  murya  , 

Da  sauri   suka  juyo  inda  safiya  take  ,tana  musu  murmishi  

Matar  tace  baiwar Allah  nan  ina  ne,  aka  kawomu,, nida  yarana  


Har  yanxu  safiya  murmishi  takeyi,     tace  kuyi hakuri  nima  bansan  mai  yakawo  kuba  nan  nazo  na   ganku  ne  kamar  gawa  ,, a  tsaye  shine  na  taimaka  muku  


Matar tace,  to  ikon Allah  yanzu fa  muna  gida,  kowa  yashirya  zamuje  wajan  bikin  dan  kanin  alhaji  yace,  muci  gaba  ga  sabon  direbanshi  nan  zai  kaimu  , shi  zai  biyomu  abaya   bamu  sake  sanin  komai  sai  yanzu 


Alhaji  shima  yace  nima  dai  hajiya  bansan  komai,, ba  ina  fitowa  naga  direba  yazo  yacemin  tuni  yakaiku  nayi  mamaki "  amma  dayake  ina  sauri  kawai  nashiga,, muka  tafi  daga  haka  bansan  komai  ba  sai  yanzu  da  nake  miki  wannan  bayanin 


Ajiyar zuciya aljana safiya  tayi  tace,  maza  kutashi,  na  kaiku  garinku   nan  wajan  hatsari gareshi  kunsan  , a  ina  kuke  ,  to  nan  gidan  boka  ne  mai  mugun  hatsabibanci   dan  haka  kutashi  maza  mutafi  kafin  wani  abun  yafaru 


A  rikice  kowa  yatashi  yana  kallon  safiya  , zasuyi  magana  tace, kurike maganar idan  nakai  gidanku  sai  kumin  bayani  ,, nan sukafito 

Mamaki  ne  yakama  su,,   ganin  dakuna  barkatai,, ga  feli  dayan  dakin  , aljana safiya  tashiga  nan  taga  wata  mata  kwance  


Suma  su  hajiya  biyota  sukayi  dan  ganewa  idonsu " ganin  mace  a  kwance  yasa  sukayi  mamaki 


Zoben  ta  ta  saita  jikin  matar  nan  tasoma  karanto  addu'o'i  ,sosai  kafin  taga  bakin  yafita daga  jikin  matar,, amma  bata  motsa  ba  mamaki  ne  yakama  aljana safiya  ganin  batayi mutse  ba  

Wajan  ta  takarasa  tana  taba  ta  nanma  taji  shuru  kara  hannun  ta  tayi  a  wuyan  matar  man  tagano  ta  mutu  ,, girgiza   safiya  tayi  ,, tace  wannan  ta  mutu  , nan  ta  saba  ta  a  kafada  ,tajuya  tace  musu  muje  ko  



Boka shaddar  kuwa  tinda  yafado,, daga  saman  dutse    bayan  shi  ya  karye  da cinyashi  amma  saboda  taurin  kai,, yasa  shi  anfani  da  tsafinsa  saidai   yakasa  gano  ina  safiya  tashige,, haka  yayi  ta  gwada  wa  kozai  ganta  amma,, saidai  ake  nuno  mishi  haske   


Haka  aljana  safiya  taringa  bin  dakunan  tana  dubawa,, wani  saitasha  fada  da  bakaken  aljanun  dake  tsaron  mutanen  kafin  ta  dauko  su  duk  sun  mutum,,masu  rai  hajiya ce  kawai  da  yaranta  da  mijinta,, 

Ganin  mutanen  sunfi  talatin  wanda  suka  mutu  , sai  kallon  su  takeyi cike  da  tausayi,, tana  fadin  yanzu  wannan  bayin Allah  tunu,, ahalinsu  sun  manta  dasu  wasu  baa  mantasuba  ", sai  takaraso  wajan   matar,, data  dauko  a  dakin  kusan  su  hajiya  

Fuskar  ta  take  kallon  tana  tunanin  a  ina  taga  mai  kama  da  wannan  matar  amma  takasa  tunawa   ganin  lokaci  na  tafiya  yasa  ta  mike,,, ta  daga  hannun  ta  sama  tace,  hafeez  amina   maza  kuzo  ina  nemanku 

Kafin  ta  sauke  hannu   suka  bayyana,  a  gabanta   suna  risinawa,  da  fadin   fada  taki  cikawa  tamu  ya  shugaba  


Su hajiya  kuwa  saboda tsoro  kowa  makale  na  kusanshi  yayi  ganin  an  bayyana  a  gabansu  tunu  tsoro  yacikasu  , 


Kallon  safiya  tayi  ,, tace  karkuji,   komai  ku  kwantar  da  hankalin  ku    
Nan  ta  kalli  su  hafeez  tace    maza  ku  kai  wannan  mutanen, , wajan  su  abba  kace  ninace  amusu  sutura " a  bukkar  boka shaddar  na  fiddosu "  kuyi  sauri  maza  kutafi  ku  kwashesu  


Da  to  suka  amsa  nan  suka  kwashesu  suka  bace  a  wajan  , sudai  su  hajiya  "   har  yanzu   jikinsu  rawa  yakeyi,  sukajin  tsoro  sosai   


Aljana safiya  tace  kuzo  mutafi  nan  suka  biyota  "   suna  kaiwa  wajan  gwaryar  nan,  wuta  ta  buso  daga  bakinta  ta  toya   gwaryar,, nan  takama  da  wuta  sai  wani  wari  da  dauyi  ke  tashi   nan  tayi  gaba  suka  bita 



Suna  fitowa  waje,, nan  suka  boka shaddar " yana  kokarin  tashi  amma  ya  kasa  nan  ta  nufi  inda  yake  

Ganin  aljana  safiya  na  tunkaro  shi  yasashi  kokarin  janyo   takobinsa  amma  yakasa  isa  inda  takobin take,  sai  nishi  yake  

Murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  dama  na  fada  maka  kada  ka  yarda,  fushina  ya  hau  kanka " idan  nazo  to  mutuwar  kace  tazo  azzalimi  kafirin  Allah  mugu  ,, marar  imani,  ban  taba  ganin  ,, marar  mutumci  irin  kaga,, nasha  zaga  duniyar  bil'adam  amma  banga  wanda  yakai  kaba  " karaba  miji  da  mata   ka kashe  mace  dan kishiryata  tabaka  abin  duniya  ka  raba  kan  uwa  da  danta   ka  hana  ma'aurata  haihuwa   ka  hana  budurwa  aure  kana  tura  mata  aljani  ashaq  na  faduwa  da  ita,   ka tarwatsa  kan  family  ,, taya  kake  ganin  zan  barka  a  dauron  kasa  kaci  gaba  da  rayuwa  irin ku  masifa  ce  ga  al"ummar  duniya  dan  haka,, dole  katafi   dan  iska  

Boka shaddar  kam  tsananin  ciwo  yahashi  magana  bai  ankare  ba  yaji  ta  soke  shi  aciki   kara  yasaki  da  azaba  

Murmishi  tayi   tace  saina  wulakanta  ka  kafin  na  kashe  ka  nan  tayi  ta  sukar  shi  da  wuka  saida  ta  mishi  yagal  gayal    da,  jikinsa  kafin  ta  caka  mishi  takobi  a  saitin  zuciya  " a  take  ya  mutu  


Su  hajiya  ko  ganin  wannan  kazamin  kisan  yasa  wasu  sakin  fitsari  wasu  sun  fita  daga  haiyacinsu   alhaji  da  hajiya  kuwa  bayan  makale  juna  dasukayi " saboda  tsoro   lokacin  da  sukaga  ta  sokeshi  a  zuciya  ai  tuni  suka  suma  , a  tare  suka  zube  kasa  



Ruwa  safiya  tazuba  musu  bude  idonsu  sukayi  a  tsorace  suke  kallon  aljana safiya  


Ganin  haka  yasa  safiya  fadin  karku  damufa  wannan  shine  yakawo  ku  wannan  wajan  mugune  shiyasa  na  kashe  shi  


Kallon  hajiya  da  alhaji  sukayi  kafin  suka  tashi,, 



Tace  meye  sunan  garinku  ,Alhaji  yace  kano  sunan  anguwar  mu  rijiyar  zaki  nan  ne  gidana  yake  nida  ahalina  


Aljana safiya  tace  to  kurufe  idonku "  nan  suka  rufe  idonsu. 


Bacewa  tayi dasu  , sai  gasu  sun  bayyana  a  anguwar  rijiyar zaki   


Mamaki  ne  yakama  su  alhaji  ganinsu  agida  

Aljana safiya  tace  to  ina  ne  gidanku,, da  hannu  alhaji  yanuna   mata wani  gida   nan  tace  kuzo  muje  , binta  sukayi  kamar  rakumi  da  akala 


 Husna  baiwar Allah  yauma,  tana  zaune  tayi  tagumi  a  kofar  gidansu  tana  son  shiga  amma  tana  tsoro  , haka  dai  ta  daure  ta  shiga,  ciki  da  sallama  dauke  abakin  ta  


Talatu  na  zaune  dama  jiran  dawowar  ta  takeyi,, harta  danadi  bulalar  ta  wanda  zata  zaneta  jin  sallamar  ta  yasatayi  murmishin  mugunta  tace  a a  husna  lfy  na  ganki  haka  ko  wani  ya  tabaki  ne  

Abin  talatu  ke  fadawa,, yasa  husna  sakin  ranta  ta  karaso  tace  mama  yunwa  nakeji, , naje  gidan  na  anty  tabawa  ance  bata  nan  tafi  tafiya,, sai  wani  satin zata  dawo  


Talatu  tace  yayi  kyau  kitchen  tashiga,  kamar  abinci  zata  daukowa  husna  " tana  shiga  ta  dauko  ruwan  zafi  a  cikin  tukunya  saida  ta  taba  taji  yayi  zafi  sosai   , nan  tadauko  tayo  inda  husna  ke  zaune  kanta  a  duke 

Husna  bata  ankare  ba  taji  ruwan  zafi  ajikinta,, ihu tasa  tana  tashi  da  sauri  


Talatu tace  dan  ubanki,, bazakici  abinci  ba  shine  hadda  wani  zomin  sumi  sumi  kamar  wata  mage  wato  ke  abaki  abinci  toko  ubanki  ne  yazo  bazan  bayarba  inda  ba  uwarki  ta  kabari  ta  dafamin  ba  


Husna  ko  kuka  takeyi  sosai  saboda  zafin  dake  shigarta  da  gudu  tayi dakinta  tashiga  toilet  ruwa  mai  sanyi  sosai  ta  tara  , ta ringa  zubawa  kanta,  tana  kuka  sosai  


Talatu  ko  tabawa  ta  kira  tace  yadai  kanwata,, wai  har  yanxu  baki  dawo  daga  wajan  bokan  ba  

Daga  cen  bangaren  ,,Tabawa  tace  nakusa  dawowa  kinsan  mu  bakine  baisanmu  sosai  kuma  yace  saina  kwana biyu  a  wakanshi  kafin  yamana  maganin  da  zaki  mallake  mlm  da  rabashi  da  husna  dan  uwar  ta  


Dariya  talatu  tayi tace  yauwa  kanwata  komai  yace  kibi  doka,  dan  musamu  cikar  burinmu  kinji  

Tabawa tace to  yaya  sai anjima  layi  yaxo  kaina  saikin  jini  , nan  ta  kashe  wayar 


Kallon  kofar  dakin husna  talatu  tayi  tace,, tsinanniya  mai  kama da aljana  nakusa  rabaki  da  uban  naki  dayake sonki  kamar  tsoka  daya  amiya  nan  ta  kwafa  ta  shige  dakinta  


Husna  ko  tana  fitowa   zumar  da  baban ta  yake  kawo  mata  ta  dauka  ta  shafa  aduk  inda  ruwan zafin ya  taba  ta  tana  kuka mai  cin rai,  batasan  wani  abu  tawa  mama  talatu  ba  take  mata  wannan  azabar   gashi  ita  basan  kowa ba  masu  jiya  mata  dadi  daga  baba  sai  sailu  da  maman  ta,, su  kadai  kesonta  a  duniya,   saida  tayi  kuka  mai  isarta  har  bacci  yayi  gaba  da  ita  


Bangaren su  aljana safiya kuwa"   bakin  kofar  get  din  suka  tsaya  
Safiya  tace ku  buga  

Da to  alhaji  ya  amsa  " nan  yabuga  

Wani  dattijo  ne  yabude  kofar  yana  kallon  waye  ,, a  rude  yake  nunasu  da  yatsa  tana  fadin  al..al...alha...alh... alhaji,, hajiya   kune  nake  gani  kodai  gizon  ne  da  kuka  saba  min,, na  tsawon   shekara  uku  


Mamaki  ne  ya  kama  su  hajiya  tace  mlm  hayatu  kamar  yaya  shekara  uku " kana  nufin  munkai  shekara  uku  bama  nan  


Mlm hayatu  ya  kallesu  yana  , share  hawaye yace  haka  ne  hajiya  kushigo,, nan suka  shiga   ciki


Ciki  yamusu  jagora   da  sallama  suka  shiga  ciki  


Ihu wata  mata  ta  saka  tana  fadin   mai  gida  fito  kagani  su  Alhaji  da  hajiya  da  yaransu 


Fitowa  yayi  yana  fadin  kefa  khubura  , inaga  alama  kin  samu  hauka  a  kanki  mutanen  da  sukayi sheka..kafin  yakai  karshen maganar  ya  tsaya  cak , yana  fadin  ya...yay..yaya  kaine  nake  gani  ko  kuwa  



Murmishi  safiya tayi saboda  tagano  rashin  gaskiya  a  tare  dashi,  nan  tace  sune  ba  kowa  ba  ,, munafikin  Allah  dan uwan  naka   da  iyalinsa  yake  neman  salwantarwa,, 


Wani  yawu  mukut  ,, ya  hadiye  yana  kallon  safiya  ,, karfi  hali  yayi  yace  ke  yarinya  ki  iya  bakinki "  ni  kike  jifa  da  wannan  kalmar  


Murmishi  tayi  tace  , kai  kashiga " hankalin  ka  kasan  dawa  kake  magana  karamin  dan  iska  

Yace  ni  kike  fadawa,, wannan  kalaman  bari  kigani " mari  yakai  mata  saiyaji  hannun  shi  yarike  


Nan  aljana  safiya  ta  wanka  mishi  bari  tuni  yasoma  ihu,, 
 Kallon  su  Alhaji  tayi  tace   kushiga  dakunan  ku  banida lokaci  ,, amma  zan  tafi  da  wannan  munafikin  saina  koya  mishi  hankali 


Alhaji  yace  muna godiya sosai  yarinya  wllh  nima  sai  yanzu  , nake  tuno  kalaman  kanina  lokacin  dazan  tafi  harcewa  yakeyi  meyasa,, direba  baizo  yadaukeni ba  amma  yarinya  yasunan  ki  


Kallon  alhaji  tayi tace  ni  sunana  safiya  ,, amma  anfi  sanina  da  ,*aljanar jarumai*  yanxu  banida  lokaci  akwai  aiki  gabana  



Amma  bari  nadan  huta  kafin  natafi  ta  zauna  

A  tsorace  suke  kallon  ta  wai  yau  aljana  ce  agidansu  amma  gata  kamar  mutum 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  kudena  tunanin  komai  niba  macuciya  bace " kai  kuma  dan  iska  maza  kafi  agidan  kai  da  wannan  jakar  matar  taka,, ba  yanzu  zanmaka  hukunci ba 


Aguje  kanin  alhaji  ya  fita  daga gidan  matarshi  ta  mara  mishi  baya



Aljana safiya ta mike tace  zan  tafi  amma  idan  komai  yalafa  zanzo  naji  dalilin  dayasa  dan  uwanka  keson  kasheka  ,, tin  kafin  suyi  magana  , aljana safiya  ta  bace 


Baki sake  suke  kallon  juna " nan  suka  shiga  dakunan  su  sunason  suga  ranar  da  aljana safiya  zata dawo  



Su  sarki safuwan  kuwa tafiya suke,   babu  tsayawa  har  suka  tunkaro  dajin  aljana  safiya  ,, cike  da  murna  sarki shamsu  yace  kaga  munkusa  kara  sawa  ,, mukara  sauri    dan  mu  isa  sarki  safuwan  " yace   gaskiya  ni  zan  kaishe  ta  da  kaina,  


Madaki kam  sai  zare  ido  yakeyi   saboda  shima  a  gaskiya  inba  dole  ba  bazai  sake  bin  dajin  nan    ba  sai  kara  kankame " dokinsa  yake  kamar  ya  fasa  ihu  haka  yakeji  amma  ba  fuska 


Suna tafiya  har  sukazo  bakin  dajin   tsayawa  sukayi  suna  son  ganin wani  abu  yazo  wucewa  su  kashe  ,, suna  a  haka  saiga  wata  barewa  da  ciki  tana  tafiya  da  kyer  tana  cin  ciyawar  wajan  


Sarki shamsu  ne  ya  saita  kwari  da  bakarshi  yasakar  mata  akaci  sa'a  ya  saki  a  daidai  



Kibiyar  na  sokar  barewar  tayi  kuka  ta  fadi  

Da  sauri  hafeez  da  amina  suka  mike  amina  tace  , Hafeez  ankawo  mana  hari  fa  maza  kira  shugaba  wannan  karsu  cin  mana  dan  naga   alama  ,, dayan  da  tsafi  yake  anfani  


Hafeez yace  haka  ne  nan  ta  murza  zoben  hannun  sa  nan  tabada  haske  ,


 Aljana safiya  , ko  wani  dajin  mai  kyau  tagani saida  tsaya  tana  kallon  wajan,, cen  sai  taji  zoben  hannun  ta  tadauki  haske  ,, da  sauri  ta  kalli  zoben  ,, a  razane  tace  hafeez  kuma   lalle  akwai  matsala  a  dajin  ,, nan  ta  tashi   sama  , tana  fada  kayan  yakinta  , tin  kafin t....
