*11—12*


Sosai  aljana  safiya  take  kara  karfin  gudun  ta  dan  tuni  ta,, jiyo  kamshin  barewar  da  aka  kashe  ranta  sai  kara  baki  yake  saboda   sun  kashe  mata  barewa  


Acen  cikin dajin   sarki  shamsu  , ne  yasake  ganin  wata  giwa " da  diyanta  sai  wasa  sukeyi  kwari  da  bakar  shi  yasaita   yana  son  harbin  giwar  nan  , kuma  saboda  rashin  mutumci  uwar  yakeson  kashewa  

Madaki  ganin  ta'asar  da  sarki  shamsu  zaiyi  tuni  yasoma  karkarwa,, dan  shi  yana  mugun  tsoron  aljanar  jarumai  ,, musamman  ma  yanzu  datake  a  harzuke  

Shi  kuwa  sarki safuwan sai  dariyar  keta  yakeyi  saboda  shi  burinshi  aduniya  ace  duk  yankin  sarakuna  ace  shine  , shugaban  su  " shiyasa  yakejin  dadi  ganin  ana  yiwa  aljana safiya  barna  

Hafeez da amina  kam  ganin  uwar  dakin  nasu  bata  zoba,,hakan  yasakasu  shiga  damuwa  su  karfinsu  bazai  iya  yakar  hatsabibin  sarki  shamsu  ba,  mugun  tsabi  yake  na  fitar  hankali  shiyasa  suka  samu  damar  kashe  musu  barewar  su  

Amina  tace  hafeez ka  kara  kiran,  shugaba   mana  da  ina  jiyo  kamshin  tafiyar  ta  amma  yanzu  banaji  tabbas  akwai  abin  ya  tsayar  da  ita  ahanya  , 

Cike  da  damuwa  yace  wajan  kira  biyar  na  mata  kuma  duk  yana  isa  gareta  kuma  tana  amsamin, yanzu  abinda  zamuyi  mu,  boye  su  abba  da  sauran  ,, kafin  musan  abinyi  kan  tazo

Amina tace to   maza  ka  kaisu  dakin  kasa  na  shugaba  ka  boyesu  acen  karshen  dakin  nasan  wannan  hatsabibin  bazai  gansuba  ni  kuma  zan  dauki  abba  na  kaishi  daya  dakin  

Nan  suka  nufi  inda  suke,  shi  hafeez yanufi  inda  yan  uwansu  suke  ,, ita  amina  ta  nufi  inda  abba  yake  dakin hutun  aljana  safiya  

Tana  zuwa  taga  abba  yana  safa  da  marwa  , da  sauri  tace  abba  lfy  

Kallon  amina  yayi  yace  ina  lfy  amina  wannan  hatsabibin  sarki  yanason  yakar  mu  gashi  safiya  bata  karasoba,  kila  wani  taimakon  na  gaggawa  ta  tsaya  yi   , shiyasa  bata  zo  ba  yanzu  

Amina  tace  nima  dai  haka  nake  tunani  abba  ta  tsaya  wani  abu  ne  amma  yanzu  muje  nakaika  dakin  ta,  na  kwana  kazauna  acen  mu  soma  yakin  dasu  mugani  kafin  tazo  

Abba  yace  amina  bazaki  ganeba  , wannan  sarkin  ba  mai  iya  karawa  dashi  sai  safiya  idan  kuma  kuka  maza  zai  iya  illataku  , bashida  imani  ance  tsafi  gaskiyar  mai  shi  ,
To  bari  kiji  wannan  hatsabibin  sarki  tin  yana  dan  shekara  goma  yake  tsafi  mugun  matsafine,  da  kike  ganinshi  , abaya  yaso  kashe  safiya  amma  dayake  iya  tafishi  karfi  Allah  bai  bashi  ikon  kasheta  ba  
Amma  kinga  yasake  dawowa,  dama  sun  taba  karawa  da  safiya  tin tana  yar  shekara  20 , baici  ribaba   ,yatafi  amma  shine  yanzu  yadawo  , 
Inason  safiya  tayi  sauri  ta  dawo  kar  ya  kashe  namukan  dajin  nan  idan  kuwa  , hakan  ta  kasance  zai  hadu  da  fushin safiya  
Ni  yanzu  na  tsuba  bazan  iya  fada  dashiba  sai  ita  dan  dukkanku  , tafiku  karfi  sosai  

Amina  ta  nisa  tace  abba  yanzu dai  muje  , nakaika  sai  kara  tunkaro  mu  sukeyi  

Mikewa  yayi  suka  bace  nan  takaishi  dakin  aljana safiya,  na  bacci  ta  kulle  ta  nuna  mai  komai  kafin  ta  fito 

Hafeez  ma  yana zuwa  wajan  yan  uwan  nashi  yace  maza,  kubiyo  ni  sarki  shamsu  ya  kawo  mana  " hari  kuzo  muje  nakaiku  mabuya  kafin  shugaba  ta  karaso  

Da sauri  kowa  kebinsa  har  yakesu  wani  tanfatsetsen  daki  yace  ku  zauna   anan  , duk  abinda  zakuji  kada  waninku  yace " zai  fito   saboda  idan  shugaba  tazo  za'a  yi  yaki  na  fitar  hankali  , dan haka  kowa  ya  zauna  , nan  yafita  ya  kullesu 


Aljana safiya  ko  abinda  ya  tsaidata  , wata  mata  tagani  , zata  haihu  cikin  wani  daji  amma  babu  kowa  a  inda  take  shiyasa  ta  tsaya  saboda  ceton rai  

Matar  sai   juya  kanta  takeyi " saboda  tsananin  azaba  , sosai  take  wahaltuwa,  

Da sauri  ta  sauka  ta  rikide  ta  koma  suffar  mace  nan  tayi wajan  ta  tana  fadin  subhanallah  " sannu  baiwar Allah  

Dago  kai  tayi  tana  kallon  safiya  ,, bakinta  duk  yabushe,   bakoshinta  ma  haka  da  kyer  ta  iya  fadin  ruwa  ruwa,, zansha  ki  taimakeni  kibani  

Da sauri safiya ta  waiga  bataga,, alama  gida  ba  a  wajan  kawai  saita  runtse  ido  nan ruwan  ya  bayyana  , a  hannun  ta   da  sauri  ta  kafawa  matar  ruwan  abaki  ,nan  taringa  sha  saida  tasha  rabin  kwaryar " kafin  ta  janye  kanta  

Ajiyar zuciya tasauke  ,, nan  nakuda  tazo  gadan  gadan  , 

Durkusa  aljana safiya  tayi  tace  ki  dafani,   insha Allah  zaki  haihu    

Dafata  tayi  tana  girgiza  kai  alamar  ciwon  yagama  mata  duk  wani  sassa  na  jikinta  

Baafi  minti  uku  ba   ta  haihu  , nan  safiya  ta  dauki  nan  ta  goge  mishi  jiki " ta  lullube  shi  da  zanin  saida  tagama  gyarashi " tsab  tukun  na  dawo  kan  uwar,   

A  razane  take  kallon  ta  tace  baiwar Allah  karkimin  haka  kitashi  , kiga  danki  namiji  kika  haifa  gashi  nan  mai  kama  dake  

Tana  janyo  numfashi  ta  kama  hannun  aljana  safiya  tace,,  dan  Allah  da  Muhammed rasulullahi  S.A.W,, kirike  min  wannan  yaron  amana  " bashida  kowa  ahalin  yanzu  saike  kirike  shi  , kamar  ke  kika  haifi  shi  ki  saka  mishi  aliyu  " sunan  kaka  na  ne,  ni  sunana, Lateefa   babanshi  kuma  adam  , sunanshi  koda  Allah  zai  sa  kiga  babanshi " kice  mishi  ina  sonsu  shida  dana,  nan  tasoma  tari  mai  sarkewa,  

Share hawaye  aljana safiya  tayi  tace  to  yasunan  garinku  

Ta  daga  baki  zatayi  magana , maganar  ta  gagara  tuni  idonta  yasoma  kafewa 

Ganin haka  yasa  aljana  safiya  fadin  la'ilaha ilallahu muhammad  rasulullahi S.A.W  ta  fada  sau uku  tana  son  taji  matar  , nan  tafada 

Matar  tasoma  fadin  la'ilaha ill.. sai  takasa   


Cikin kuka  aljana safiya  tasake  maimai ta  fadin  la'ilaha ilallahu muhammad muhammad rasulullahi  S.A.W 


Wannan  karon  ma  kasa  fada  tayi   numfashin  ta  na  kara  sarkewa  

Aljana safiya bata  gajiba  tasake  fadin la'ilaha ilallahu muhammad rasulullahi S.A.W  


Sai  ana  uku  Allah  yabata  ikon  fadin  la'ilaha ilallahu muhammad rasulullahi S.A.W   tana  kai  karshe  , sai  saki  jiki  , alamar  ta  mutu 


Jaririn  aljana safiya  take  kallon  dayayi  kuka  fari  bai  sake  yiba  sai  kalle  kalle  yakeyi,,  kukan  ta  ta tsayar  ta  saba  uwar  abayan   ta  ta  rungume  jaririn " tana  mai  jin  tausayin  yaron  bacewa  tayi  


Tin  kafin  taje   takejin  kukan,, namukan  dajin  , da sauri  ta  isa  ciki  dakinta  na  bacci  tashiga  da  matar  da  jaririn  , turus  tayi  ganin  abban  ta  acikin  yakasa  tsaye  yakasa  zaune  


Da sauri yace  yauwa  yar albarka,  kinzo  kallon ta  yasake yi  yace Allah  sarki  , ni  dama  nasan  baa  banza  kika  jinkirta  zuwa  ba  akwai  dalili  nan  ya  amshi  jaririn  ,, ta  sauke  uwar 


Tace  abba  wannan  matar  na  tsaya  taimaka  a  wani  daji,, babu  kowa  a cikinsa  sai  ita  kadai  shiyasa,  saidai  kuma  ta  mutu  tana  gama  haihuwa

Abba  yace  Allahu akbar  , bari  na  kira  sauran  yan uwan  naki  sai  mu  mata  salla  mubinne  ta  a  cen  gaba  tinda  bamai  zuwa  nan,, yanzu  babu lokaci  maza  kishirya  sai  kashe  miki  namukan  nan  sukeyi  ,,sarki shamsu  ne  yazo 


Mikewa  tayi  da sauri  ,, tace  sarki  shamsu  lalle  yau  saiya  yabawa  aya  gardinta  , nan  tasaka  kayan  yaki ta  dauko  takobin  ta  ta  bace  a  dakin  



Su  sarki shamsu  kuwa,, ganin  har  yanzu  aljana safiya  bata  zoba  , yace  saina  kashe  duk  abinda  ke  dajin  nan  , kafin  tazo  har  ubanta  ma  da  sauran  yan  uwanta " duk  saina  kashe  su  ya  ringa  kashe  duk  abinda  ya  gifto  inda  suke, saiya  kasheshi  

Aljana safiya  ko  cikin  dajin  tafito  , nan  ta  hango  su  , ta  baya  ta  nufa  dan  so  take  ta  kashe  duk  wannan  takarun  kafin  tazo  kan  sarki  shamsu  da sarki safuwan  " duk  ma  tafijin  tsanar  sarki shamsu  ,, nan  ta  bayyana  a  bayansu  

Tin  lokacin  data  bayyana  madaki  " yaganta  da  wata  irin  siffa  ba  kyaun  gani  dan  ranta  amugun  bace  yake  , tuni  yaringa  rawar  jiki  yana  share  ido  , sai  kankame  dokinsa  yakeyi,  , takobin  sa  dama  tuni  yayi  wulli  da  ita  saboda  mugun  tsoron  da  yakewa " *aljanar jarumai*   ba  shiri  ya  cika  wandonsa  da  futsari  da  zayo  " sai  kerma  yake  yanason  yin  magana  " amma  daga  ya  daga  kai  saiyaga  aljana  safiya  na  zare  mishi  ido  tuni  ya  gigice  

Ita  kuwa  aljana safiya  ,, daya  bayan  daya  take  soke  su  da  takobin  ta  ta  kashe  mutane  sunkai  dari, , kisan  ma  na  wulakanci  take  musu  saboda  yau  iya  makura  ranta  " yakai  karshen  bacci  


Sarki shamsu  ne  ya  lura  da  aljana safiya  tafazo,, dalilin  ganewa  madubin  tsafinsa  ya  kalla  yaganta  daga  baya,, tana  kashe  dakarunsa  , a  fusace  ya  nufi inda  take  yana  karara  ihun  irin  na  mayaka   


Tun  kafin  yazo  dakarunsa  , suka  nufi  aljana safiya  dan  fitowa  tayi  a  suffar  mace  ,, gaba da  gaba  , nan  aka  kama  yaki  sosai 


Su  hafeez  na  ganin haka  yasa  suma  suka  saka  kayan  yaki,, nan  sukashiga  aka  ringa  yaki  na  fitar  hankali  


Husna ce  kwance  tana  bacci  " Talatu  ce  take  kiranta  , amma bataji  saboda  bacci  takeyi  

Mikewa  talatu  tayi  tace  , iyee  lalle  yarinyar  nan  wato  ma  ina,  kiranki  shine  kika  maidani  sa'ar  uwarki,  to  yau  zanga  mai  cetonki  , nan  ta  dauki  ruwa  mai  mugun sanyi  ta  nufi  dakin  husna  dashi 


Tana  baccin  ta  mai  dadi " taji saukar  ruwan  sanyi  ajikinta,  da  sauri  ta  mike  tana  fadin  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un waiyo  baba  


Maketa  talatu  tayi  tace  ,, ke  ni  baiwar  ubanki  ce  dazan  miki  aiki  agidan  nan  shi  uban  nake  mai  tare  miki  baya  nan  ,dan  haka  dole  kiyi  aiki ko  kisha  na  jaki,  ficeni  kije  ki  min  shara  da  wanke  wanke,,tin  kafin  na  nakada  mishi  dan  banzan  kashi  agidan  ,, munufika,  sai anyi  magana  take  wani  kibta  min  ido  kamar  mayya  to wllh,, kul  din  ki  nama  d'aci  gareshi,  zaki  fice  kosaina  jaki  akasa  



Da sauri  husna  tafita  ta  kama  shara  tanayi  tana  kuka " ga  kanta  dake  mata  ciwo  haka  tayi  ta  sharar  harta  gama  ,, zata  zauna   

Talatu tace  kuma shi  wanke  wanke,  da  wanki  ubanki  ne  zaiyi 

Tashi  tayi  da  kyer  tasoma  yin  wanke  wanken  ,, ta  kuasa  gamawa  bacci  yadoma  daukarta  nan  ta  kwanta  a  kasa tasoma  bacci 


 Talatu  ganin  husna  tayi  bacci  ,, yasa ta  mike ta  dauke,  wayar  wuta  ta  tafi  inda  ta  kwanta " 

Jikawai  husna  tayi  ana  dukanta  da  sauri  tamike  tana  kuka  

Talatu  tace  ke  har  kin isa nasakaki  aiki  " kikama  bacci   to  maza  ki wanke  su  

Haka husna  taci  gaba  da  aikin  talatu  na  biye  da  ita  idan  tasoma  gyangyadi " ta  sa  mata  bulalar  hannun  ta  , 


Har  yamma  husna  na  aiki  ta  gaji sosai  kafafunta  har  sun  kumbura  saboda   tsayuwa  ta  wuni  tana  aiki  kamar  jaka  


Ita  ko talatu  nata  mata  dariya

*بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

*Da  sunan Allah mai rahama mai jin qai*




*13—14*



Haka  husna  tayi  ta  , aiki  gata  gaji  sosai  amma  talatu  tace  saita  gama  wanke  mata  kayanta  , tas  tukun  ta  huta   har  kusan  magarib  " kafin  tagama  wankin   , da  rarrafe  takai  takinta,, saboda  tsabar  gajiya  " haka  taringa  rarrafawa  har  toilet  

Tana shiga,  ta  hada  ruwan  zafi  tashiga  , zama  tayi  tana  lumshe  ido  saboda  dumin  ruwan  na  ratsa  ta  " saida  tasoma  samun  saukin  gajiyar  , kafin  tayi  wanka   bayan  tagama  tayo  alwala  , tazo  tada  salla  

Tana  gamawa  , ta  fito  a  tsorace  take  sosai  a  hankali  take,  tafiya  har  ta  karasa  inda  talatu  " take 

Harara  talatu  ta  banka  mata  ' tace  to  mayya  meye  kika  wani  sakani  gaba  

Dukar  dakai  tayi  tace  mama  yunwa  nakeji  

Dariyar  yan duniya  , Talatu  tayi  tace  ,iyee  yunwa  ko  yarinya  , ban  dafa  dakeba  "  tana  aika  mata  harara  

Husna  tace  dan Allah  mama  kibani  abincin  koda  yayane  yake  ,tana  matsar  hawaye 

Mikewa  talatu  tayi  , tashiga  kitchen   kwashe  abincin  , tayi  ta  karce  kanzon  tukunya  , tazuba  a  kwano ta  dauko    tazuba  kanzon  tafito  ,,a  gaban  husna  ta  ajiye  tace  gashi  nan  

Dauka  husna  tayi  ta koma  dakin  ta,   tana  kukan  wannan  rayuwar  ta  ta , cike  da  damuwa  tasoma  cin  kanzon   da  kyer  take  " hadiyewa   haka  ta  ringa  ci  tana  korawa  da  ruwa  harta  cinye  , haka  husna  ta  kwana  tana  kuka  dan  yanzu  babu  babanta  a  kusa  da  ita  shiyasa  , take  ganin  tsanani  a  wajan  talatu 



Aljanar safiya  na  yakar  dakarun  sarki  shamsu,, sosai  take  musu  kisan  gilla  " ko  idonta  bata budewa  , saboda  zafin  da  kirjinta  ke  mata  saida  ta  k'ari  dakarun  sauran, basufi  su  dari  ba  kowa  ja  dabaya  yake  , sarki  shamsu  ya  karaso  amma  yakasa  zuwa  " kusan aljana safiya  saboda  , wani  kwarjinin  ta  " daya  gani  

Wani  irin  kallo  aljana  safiya  takewa  sarki  shamsu  ,, tace  kai  hatsabibin  sarki  ,, ni  safiya  nafi  karfinka  " na  kafin ka  kara  dani  

Barewa  ta  daka  kashe  da  giwa  ta  saina  kashe  duk  wanda  yake  wajan  nan,  a  kan  su  


Dariyar  renin  wayo  sarki shamsu  , yayi  yace  ke  safiya  kishiga  hankalin  ki ni  sarki  shamsu  nafi  karfinki " ko  dakaruna  baki  isaba  ki,  karasarsu  idan  kuma  kince   zaki  iya  ,to  gasunan  a  gabanki  , mugani  inki  isa  ki  kashe  su  , niba  sa' anki  bane   


Murmishi  safiya  tayi  , tace  hmmm  zakaga  cika  aiki  yanzu  yanzu  nan  ka gyara  tsayuwar  , ta  tana  jiran  zuwansu  

Sarki shamsu  kuwa  zama  tayi  tana  fadin  kumata  kisan  wulakanci  , nan  sukayo  kanta  kowa  na  ruri  irin  nasu  na  mayaka  

Suna  karasowa  nan  aka  kaceme  da  , kazamin  yaki  wanda  bakajin  komai  sai  karar  takofi  da  ihun  mazaje  , sosai  take  kashesu  , kafin  kace  me  tuni  ta  kashesu  tass  ,  gasunan  a  kwance  kowa  babu,  kisan  sauki  acikinsu  " dan  bata  yi  da  wasa  ba 


Madaki  kuwa  ganin  wannan  yakin  ,, yayi  suma  tafi  a  kirga  , sosai  yakidime  " da  ganin  wannan  fitinannan  yakin    sai  kerma  ,, yakeyi   

Sarki safuwan  ko  ganin  aljana safiya  na  musu  wani  mugun  kallo " yasashi  jin  tsoro  ya  dirar mishi   sai  muzurai  yake  yi  sosai  yaringa  jin  yana  jin,  tsoro  


Sarki shamsu  yace  , aikin  banza  zankin  kashe  wannan  kananan,   kwarin  ba  kokari  kikayi  ba   ,ki  kara  da  sarki  safuwan  " mana  inkin  isa,  tana  murmishin,  mugunta  


Kallon   baka  da  hankali  safiya  tamishi  , tace  ni  nafi  karfin  duk  wani  azzalimi  , a  dauran  kasar  ko mutum  " ko  aljani  duk  saina  naga  bayanku " shi  sarki  safuwan  , din  yaxo  mu  gwabza  mana  

Sarki safuwan,  jin  abinda  safiya  tafada "    yasa  yaji  wani  karfi  na  shigarsa  , nan  ya  mike  yana  zare  takobin  sa  ,, wajan  safiya  yanufa  da  gudu  

Su  madaki,  dai  anyi  laushi  , ko  motsin  kirki  baya  iyayi  ,  sai  kudundunewa,,  yakeyi    kamar  kayan  wanki  , 


Yaki  aka fara  tsakanin  sarki safuwan da aljana safiya  , ,,tin  baa  je  ko  ina  ba  yagaji  dama  cika  bakine  yakeyi  "   sosai  ya  gaji  

Har  kasa  aljana safiya  takai  sarki   safuwan  " takobin  ta  tadaga  sai  kuma  ta  tsaya  , kallon  shi   tayi  tace  ,, munafiki  , to  yau  karyar  ka  ta  kare  ,, saboda  rashin  imani  gari  guda  katasa  sauran  ,, wanda  baka  kasheba " ka  maidasu  bayinka  kazama  sarki to  yau  , zan  kasheka  naga  sarautar  



Cike  da  tsoro  sarki  safuwan  yace  waiyo Allah natuba "  dan Allah  karki  kasheni  ,, zan  baki  duk  duniyar  dana  tara  kibarni  da  raina  


Harara  tamishi  tace,  ashe  kasan  da  Allah  , kake    cutar  bayinsa  "   har  kana  da,  bakin  dazaka  rokeni  , macuci  koka  zata  bansan  tarishin  rayuwar  kaba,  ne  yadda  sarkin  kasmuri  yabaka  amana  ,, amma  shine  kamishi  " zagon  kasa  kake  yakarshi  har  ka  kashe  shi  , ka  koma  kan  karagar  mulkinsa " to  yau  karyar  ka  ta  kare  ,, takobin ta  tadaga  ta  soke  shi  a  ciki  

Ihu  , sarki safuwan  ya  kwala  saboda  tsananin  azabar  data  shigesa  " kafin  minti  uku  tuni  ya  mutu  

Dagowa  aljana safiya  tayi  , da  mamaki  take  kallon  wajan  da,  sarki  shamsu  yazauna  bashi  , sai  madaki  da  yake,, kerma  

Wajan  sa  ta  nufa  , tace  kai  ina  sarkin  naku  

A  razane  yadago  yana  fadin  wllh  ba  dashi  nake  tare  ba3 hasalima " nace  musu  bazan  zo  nan  ba  nidin  dan  kasar  kasmuri  ne " bayan  sarki safuwan  ,, yakaishe  sarkinmu  ya  maidamu  bayinsa,  


Sauke  takobin  ta  tayi  , tace  eh  ainaga  kai  bada  fada  , kazoba  amma  ya  akayi  sarki shamsu  nabar,  nan  wajan  

Madaki  yace  lokacin  da  kikewa,  sarki safuwan  " magana  alokacin  ya  kalli  madubin  , tsafin  nashi  kawai  naga  ya  bace  kamar  walkiya  


Ajiyar zuciya tasauke tace  ,, wato  sarki  shamsu  , ya  shammaceni   to  wllh  yaci  bashi  komin  daren  dadewa  saina  kashe  shi na  lalata  , tsafinsa,,  mugu  azzalimi  ,,yanzu  kai  idan  na  kaika  hanyar  kasarku  ,zaka  iya  ganewa 

Da sauri,  yace  eh  zan  gane  ranki  yadade   
Safiya  tace,  runtse  idonka  , nan  yarintse  


A  wata  makekiyar,, hanya  suka  bayyana  suna  sauka  safiya  tace  zaka  gane  garinku  daga  nan  

Bude  idon  shi  yayi  yace  eh  nan  hanyar  garinmu  ce  zan  iya  zuwa  da  kaina

Aljana safiya  tace  to  , maza  katafi  

Da sauri  ya  durkusa  yana  mata godiya  kafin  yakama  hanya  


Tana  kallon  shi  harya yi  nisa sosai  bata  tafiba  saida  taga  yakai  bakin  kofar  garinsu  tukun ta  bace  


Cikin  dakin  da  abban  ta  ,yake  nan  ta  bayyana,  tana  huci  ran  nan  a  hade  

 Babanta  , yazo  kusan  ta  ,  kayan  yakin  yacire  mata  ta  cenja  wasu  tukun ta  zauna  tana, kallon  aliyu  dake  bacci  


Abba  yace  yadai  ina  fatan,  kin  gama  dashi  

Iska  ta  furzar mai  zafi,, tace  wllh  baba  wannan  Karon  ma  ya  shammaceni  ,, ya  bace  na  duba  ko  ina  amma  , banga  ta  inda  yabiba  


Girgiza  kai  abba  yayi  yace  , ai  wannan  sarkin  mugun  hatsabibi  , ne  ki  tari  gaba  " insha Allah  saikin kashe  shi  , yanzu  ki  kula  da  amanar,, da  aka  baki    na  yaron  nan  kireneshi  kamar  ke  kika  haifeshi,, kinji  yar albarka  


Jinjina kai  safiya  tayi  tace  to  abba  na  duk  abinda  kace  , shizanyi    insha Allah  zan  kula  dashi  " har  lokacin  dazanga  ahalinsa  ,  ta  mika  hannu  ta  daukeshi,  , rungume  shi  tayi   tana  jin  tausayin  jaririn  


Haka  aljana  safiya  , taci  gaba  da  kula  da  aliyu  wani bangaren  kuma,, tana   shan  alwashin  " duk  randa  sarki shamsu  yashiga  hannun  ta,, saita  mishi  kisan  da  bata  tabawa  wata  halitta  ba ,  kullin  cikin  fushi  take,  sosai  take  hada  kayan  yakin ta  tana  shirin  kota  kwana  




*bayan shekara hudu*


Rayuwa  na  tafiya,  da  lokaci  awanni  na  komawa  kwana  , kwanaki  na  komawa " sati   satittika na  komawa  wata  , watanni  na  komawa  shekara  


Aljana safiya har  wannan  lokacin  bata  manta  da  husna  ba  , tana  cikin  ranta  saita  yi  yukurin  " zuwa  kawai  sai  wani  uzirin  yahanata  zuwa   , ga  kuma  aliyu  take  makale  , daa  ita  kamar  cingam  " baya  yadda  da  kowa  sai  ita  inbaya  , wajan  ta  to  yana  wajan  abba,, sosai  yake  samun kulawa  kamannin  sa  da  mamansa  na  kara  fitowa  "  kullin  idan  safiya  na  kallon  shi  yana  zuba  mata  surutu  " sai  take  tunano  mahaifiyar  sa  

Shi ko aliyu  bashida  damuwa,, shekarar  sa  hudu  amma  inka  ganshi  kamar  dan  6 years  haka  yake  da  saurin  girma   



Su husna  anzama  yan  mata  ,,saidai  har  yanzu  talatu  bata  cenja  ba  saima  abinda  yayi  gaba  " dan  yanzu  ko  tana  dukan  husna  idan  baba  yayi,  magana  " gidansu  take  zuwa  tafadawa " mamanshi  , daga  yaje  gaisheta  ta  zageshi  tass  ta  mishi  rashin mutumci  , shi  har  abin  yadena  damunsa  dan  yariga  da  yazamar  masa  jiki  zagin  da  kaka  ke  mishi  ,saidai  yabata  hakuri  , ta  yabar  gidan  


Yanzu  husna  takoma  makaranta,,  saidai  koxa  zata  tafi  saita  gamawa  talatu  aikin  gida  da  girki  tukun  take  tafiya,, kullin  sai  tayi  last  a  zuwa  school,  



Yauma  tana  tafiya  a  kasa  dan  bata  samu    , abin  hawaba    gashi  sailuba " yau  bataxoba  shiyasa  ta  fito  take  takawa  akafa  


Wata mota  ce  ta  tsaya  agaban  ta  , tsayawa  husna  tayi  " wani  mutum  ne  cikin  motar  zaikai  shekara  50 years  , da  murmishi  yace  a  a  yan  mata  ina  zuwa  


Murtuke  fuska  husna  tayi  tace  to,  baba  ina  ruwanka  da  inda  zani  kayi  tafiyar  ka  mana  kawani  tsareni  a  hanya  


Murmishi  yamata  mai  kyau  yana  k'are  mata  kallon  , yace  haba  yan mata  kizo  muje  na  rage  miki  hanya  mana  naga  kamar ke,,bai  dace  ace  kina  tafiya  a  rana  ba  ga  zafi  



Ficewa  husna  tayi  dan ta  lura  wannan  mutunin  bashida  kunya  ,, binta  yaringa  yi  yana  rokonta  tashiga  yakaita  gida  amma  takiya  


Shi  kuma  bai  gaji  da  binta  ba,  ganin  ya  nace  gashi  mutanan  bakin  layi  na  kallon  ta  yasa  tashiga  


Da  kwatan  ce  yakaita  har  bakin  kofar  gida  sauka  tayi  tace  ngd  Allah  yasaka  da  alkhairi  


Murmishi  yayi  ya  dauko  rafar yan  dubu  dubu  ya  mika  mata  dauri  biyu  yace,  ga  wannan  yan  Mata  , 


Kin  karba  husna  tayi  ,, aikuwa  saiga  talatu  ta  fito  ganin  ana  bawa  husna  kudi  taki  karba  , yasa  ta  fadin  

Husna  kakar  uwarki  yar iska  , mutum  sa  girmashi  da  komai  yana  miki  magiya  ki  amsa  amma  kinki  karba  " baa  maida  hannun  kyauta  baya  


Kallon talatu tayi tace  mama  kudin  fa  dayawa,  ne  dubu dari biyu  ne  


Da sauri  talatu  tace,  eh  naji amso  ,, karba  husna  tayi  tamishi  godiya  , yana  murmishi  yafito  yanufi  inda  talatu  take 


Tsugunawa  yayi,  yake  gaishe  da  talatu,  , baki  a  washe  talatu  ke  masawa  ,dan  ganin  babban  mutum  irin  wannan  yana  dukawa  ya  gaisheta  


 Mutumin  yace  ni  sunana  alhaji  abdallah  , naga  husna  ina  sonta  , Allah  yasa  dai  mama  baku  mata  miji  ba  


Da sauri  talatu  tace  a a  bamu   mata  miji  ba  gaskiya  kazo  a  kan  sa'a  


Mikewa  yayi  yazaro  wani  kudin  bandir  daya  yabawa  talatu  " karba  tayi  tana  mishi godiya  ,,nan  yadawo  wajan  motarshi  " Husna  yawa  sallama  ,yatafi  


Da gudu  husna  tashiga  gida  tana  rusa kuka  ,,jin  wai  wannan  shirgegen  yace  yana  sonta  zai  aureta,  tace n...

Dakin  ta  husna  tashiga  , tana  kuka  sosai  dan  ganin  wannan  ikon Allah  bayan  matsalar  datake  fuskanta   yanzu  ace  hallau    wannan,, mai  kama  da  basamuden  zaa hadata  aure  ", haka  kawai  daga  rage  mata  hanya  wannan  ma  ai  wukanci  ne,, 


Hajiya talatu  ko  , tinda  taga  wannan  alhaji  mai  dubun  nerori  ta  haukace  akan  saiya  auri  husna  ", saboda  suma  suhuta   sosai  suke  bancaka  da  kudi  ,,tabawa ta  kira  a  waya  , 

Tana  dauka  ko  sallama  , babu  tace  yar uwa ta  " abin  arziki  yasameni  ni  talatu   ,, kinga  yau  husna  wani  amshakin  mai  kudine  " yazo  yace  da  aure  yakeson   kuma  baya  son  bata  lokaci  ,, nace  mishi  ya  bari  zan  sanar  da  mlm  komai  ,, yanzu  ya  kike  ganin  za'ayi  ,,yadda  zan fadawa  malam  " kinga  kudin  dayabani  dubu  dari  fa  " ita  kuma  husna  dubu dari biyu  yabata  " ke ni  gaskiya  naga  alama  wannan  ,, shaharerren  mai  kudi  ne " kyautar  dubu  dari  uku  a  lokaci  daya  ,, ai  dole  ma  husna  ta  aureshi  koda  tsiya  koda  arziki  ,, dan  muke  samun  na  zuwa  gun  boka  


Wani  bakin  ,cikine  yatokare  bakoshin  tabawa  wato  ma  ,  Husna  babban  mai  kudi  zata  aura  " ita  diyanta  gasunan  agabanta  , bako  mashinshini   shine  wai  talatu  take  fadin  daga  zuwanshi  tabata  dubu  dari  " gaskiya  dole  ne  , nasan  abinyi  kafin  auren  nan  ya  tabbata 

Talatu tace  hello  tabawa  bakijina  ne  ,, inata  magana  ko  ba  network  ne  


Da sauri  tabawa  tadawo  daga  ,, tunanin  datakeyi  ,, marar  anfani  , tace  eh  yaya  talatu  network  ne  yadauke  min  


Ok to  yanzu  ya  kike  ganin zan  bullowa  da  malam  wannan  maganar  " ne

Tabawa  tace   kudin  zaki  nuna  mishi  ,wanda  yaba  husna  kuma  ki  amshe  dauri  daya  kibar  mata  daya  ,  dan  take  kwalliya  ,, kar  alhajin  yagane  " an  amshe  kudin   


Talatu  tace   yauwa  kanwata  shiyasa  nakesonki  amma  idan  malam  yaki  amincewa  fa ??    


Tabawa  tace  saiki  kaiwa   mahaifiyar  shi  kudin  ki  samu  ,, ki  tsara  ta   kinga  dole  yabi  umarnin  babarsa  

Talatu tace  ,,to shikenan  yanzu  gobe  ki  turo  khubura  tazo  ta  amshi  , naki  kason  

Cike da farin ciki  tabawa  tace  ba  damuwa  zan  turota  ,nagode  sosai  ,,
Talatu  tace  haba  tabawa  ai  kinfi  karfin  haka  awajena  ,indai  inadashi  babu  abinda  bazan  mikiba  yar  uwa  ta  , Tabawa  tace  to  shikenan  sai  anjima  


 Talatu  na  gama  ,, yanke  kiran  ta nufi  dakin  husna  

Husna  tasoma  bacci  ko  kayan  school  dinta,, bata  samu   tacireba  ,,  saboda  kukan da  takeyi  har  bacci  dayayi  gaba  da  ita  


Talatu  akace  ,iyee  ba  shakka  yarinya  ,, harma  bacci  kikayi  , bakiyi  girkin  ranava  ,, sai  kuma  ta  tuna  idan  fa  ta  takurawa,, Husna  alhajin  nan  bazai  kebata  kudi  ba  idan  yazo  dan  haka  zata  ,, sassautawa  husna  take  sakata  tana  kawo  mata  kudi  wajan  alhaji  ,,, murmishi  tayi  data  dawo  daga  tunanin  nata  


Ahankali  tace  husna  yar  kirki  tashi  mana,, kinga  ko  cire  kayanki  bakiyi  ba  kika  kama  bacci  ga  girki  bakiyi  ba,,  tashi  mana  

Bude  idonta  tasomayi  tana  saukeshi  akan  talatu  ,, ai  a  razane  ta  tashi  tana  fadin  dan  Allah  mama  kiyi  hakuri  wllh  bansan  lokacin  da  bacci  yayi  gaba  dani " ban  daura  girkin  ba  har shara  da  wankin  banyi  ,, ki  gafarceni   mama  dan  Allah  karki  dakeni  wllh  nagaji  kiyi  hakuri  tana  mai  kama  kafar  talatu  

Murmishin  mugunta  talatu  tayi  ,, tace  ba  komai  ai  nasan  bada  son  ranki  kikayi  ba   yanzu  tashi  muyi  wata  magana  


Cike  da  mamaki  husna  ke  kallon  talatu  , maganar  xuci  takeyi  tace  anya  kuwa  wannan  mama  ce  kemin  magana  a  tausashe   

Tabata  ,, talatu  tayi  tace   me  kike  tunani  yarinya  ta    zauna  mana  lokaci  na  tafiya  muyi  magana  kinji 


Da sauri  ta  dawo  daga  duniyar  tunani  ,, tace  to  to  mama  inajinki  

Gyara  zama  talatu  tayi  tace  , yauwa  dama   inason  na   fada  miki  wannan  alhajin  , yace  yanasonki  ,,amma  da  aure  yake  sonki  yace   , zai  dawo  bayan  sati  daya  zaizo  ganin  babanki   yace  zai  sayo  miki  wayar  nan  ,, ta  zamani  maima  sunan  ta  ,takai  karshen maganar  da  tambayar  husna  , 


Kan  husna  a  kasa  " tace  iPhone 11 ko 

Talatu  tace  yauwa  yar  kirki  ita  yace  zai  kawo  miki  ,, kema  kizama   yar  zamani  kina  cin  kwalliya  " kudin  daya  baki  kibani  dauri  daya   na  hada  dana  wajena  na  nunawa  babanki  " na  fada  miki  ,, abinda  alhaji  yace  


Husna  ko  wani bakin  cikine  ya  tunkare  ta  ,, kamar  ta  kurma  ihun  jin  wai  alhajin  yace  yana  sonta  da  aure  ita  ya  zatayi  da   rayuwar  ta  ,, yaushe  ne  zata  samu  farin ciki  a  cikin  kudin  rayuwar  ta  ,,  wannan  wata  irin  uwa  ce  " daga  ganin mutum  sai  kice  wai  saina  aureshi  



Talatu  tace  husna  ,, kidena  tunanin  komai  na  tabbata  Alhaji  yana  sonki  kuma  zai  ke  miki  duk  abinda  ,, kikeso muma  , zamu       huta  


Husna tace  to  mama  na  amince  da  daduk   maganar  ki  ga  kudin  ,, tana  dauko dauri  daya  taba  ta  


Yar dariya  talatu  tayi  tace  yau  Allah  miki  albarka  tashi  ki  cire  kayan  kishiga  kitchen  ki  mana  girki  kinga  bayan  la'asar  babanki  ,, zai  dawo   


Husna  ko  baiwar Allah  har  sanyi  taji  jin  maman  nata  tasaka  ,mata  albarka  , tace  to  mama  yanzu  zan  daura  nan  ta  cire  kayan  tasaka  doguwar,, riga  da  daura  dan  kwalin  rigar,  zata  fito


Talatu  tace  , Husna  dan  Allah  kike  kwalliya  mai  kyau  kinga  kina  da  kyau   kike  kwalliya  ,, kina  haskawa  hakan  zaimin  dadi 


Cikin jin  dadi  ,  Husna  tace to  mama  zankeyi  , nan  suka  fito  husna  tayi  kitchen  , talatu  ta  shiga  dakinta  ta  rufo  



Bagaren  aljana  safiya  kuwa,,   duk  yau  cikin  damuwa  take  sosai  ga  aliyu  bashida  lfy  yayi  kwance  kamar  gawa,, sai  numfarfashi  yake  sauke wa "  shafa  kanshi  tayi  tace  sannu  yarona  Allah  Allah yabaka  lfy  

Kallon  ta  aliyu  yayi  yace  umma  na  ,, kidena  kuka  zan  samu  sauki   yana  share  Mata  hawayen  fuskar  ta   

Murmishin  yake  tayi  wanda  aka  ce  yafi  kuka  ciwo   tace  yarona  shikenan  ,, na  dena  kaji  Allah  Allah yabaka  lfy  

Aliyu  yace  ameen  umma  ,, ruwa  zansha  

Dauko  ruwan  tayi tabashi   ,,karba  yayi  yasha  sosai  kafin  ya  janye  kanshi,, yace  na  koshi  


Ajiye  sauran  ruwan  tayi  , tana  shafa  kanta  sosai  jikinshi  yayi  wani  irin  safi  abin  mamaki cikinsa  sanyi  k'arai   da  kafafunsa,   kanshi  da  wajan  cinyoyinsa  da  hannayensa  sune  masu  zafi  ,, sosai  safiya  take  mamakin  wannan  lamari,, idan  ta  runtse  ido  tana  dubashi  amma  bata  ganin  ,, komai    hakan  yakara  tsorata  ta   


Abba  ne  ya  bayyana  cikin  dakin  ,, sallama  yamata  amsawa  tayi  tana  kallon   baban  nata  tace  sannu  da  zuwa  abba  

Kallon ta  yayi  yace  " yauwa  safiya  lfy  naga  kamar  kina  kuka  maiyasamu  aliyu  


Share  hawaye  safiya  tayi  tace  abba  wllh  nakasa  gane  meke  damunsa  "  na  duba  amma  banga  komai  ba  shiyasa  hankali   na  yatashi  please  ,, abba  kayi  wani  abu  mana  

Durkusawa   abba  yayi  yace,    muganshi  , 
 

Mika  mishi  aliyu tayi   nan  ya  amsa  tana  dubashi  ,  sosai  take  mamaki  dagowa  yayi  ya  kalli  safiya  yace  ,, yar albarka  ,  yaron  nan  sihiri  aka  mishi  mugun  sihirima  ,, mai  wuyar sha'ani  


Da sauri  safiya   dago  ta  kalli  baban  nata  , tace  sihiri  kuma  abba  ,, towa  yamishi  yaron  dabai  san  kowa  ba  a  duniyar  nan  bayan  mu 

Abba  yace,  hmmm  safiya  Keenan  bakisan  , yanzu  daga  bil'Adam  har  junnu  ,, basuda  mutumci  , to  wannan  yaron  sarki  shamsu  ne  yamishi  mugun  sihiri  ,na  gefen  na  ko  wani  mutum ne  ,,amma  bansan  sunan  shiba  


Tuni  safiya  tasoma  cenja  suffar  ta  na  mace,, zuwa  surart  ta  asali  

Da sauri  abba  yarike  Mata  hannu  yace  haba  safiya  wannan  bacin  ran  bashi  zakiyi  ba   neman  hanyar  da  aliyu  zai  warke  zakiyi  ,, kibata  sihirin  da  aka  mishi  


Ajiyar zuciya  take  saukewa  ta  lumshe  ido saboda  ,, bacin  ran  da  take  ciki   tace  abba  yazanyi  na  lalata  wannan  sihirin  da  akawa  dana  


 Abba  yace  cen  cikin  wani  daji  zakije  ,,amma  fa  dajin  akwai  hadarirrika  acikinsa   ' akwai  wata  babbar  bishiya  acikin  dajin  ,, to  ganyen  ta  zaki   ciro  ki  kawo  a  tafasa  , yasha  a  shafe  ,, mishi  jikinsa  " wannan  shine  abinda  zaa  mishi  ya  warke   ,, amma  ba  bishiyar  akwai  mugayen  aljanu  " akanta  arnan  aljanu  , suke  tsare  da  wannan  bishiya  duk  wanda  yazo  kasheshi  sukeyi  



Aljana safiya  ko  cike  da  jarumta da    kwarin  gwiwa  ,, tace  karka  damu  abba  insha Allah  babu  abinda  zai  sameni  sai  alkhairi  insha Allah  ,,kuma  saina  kawo  wannan  gayen  domin  warakar  , yarona  koda  kuwa  zan  rasa  raina  ne  , sainaje  na  kawo  

Abba  yace  to shikenan  ,,amma  yanzu  bazaki  iya  zuwa  ba  zamuke  mishi  addu'a  har  na  tsayin  sati biyu  tukun  kitafi  , lokacin  insha Allah zaiji   sauki  


Kwantar  , da  kanta  tayi  a  kafadar  babanta  tace  , insha Allah  komai  zaitafi  lfy  Allah  yakaimu lokacin  insha Allah,       Abba  ya  amsa  da  ameen  

Su  amina  na  kallon  duk  abinda  sukeyi  , sosai  uwar  dakin nasu  tabasu,   tausayi  jin  dajin  da  zataje  

Amina tace  gaskiya  shugaba  tana  hidima  da  bil'Adam  dama  jinsunmu  ,  ga  taimako  insha Allah  ,, zatayi  nasara  saita  dauko  wannan  ganyen  na  bishiyar  

Hafeez  yace   ,   insha Allah  ,, nasan  bazata  yadda  mubita  ba  zatace  " mu  zauna  mu  tsare  dajin  nan  

Amina tace  kamar  kasani  haka  zatace  tana  son  ,Aliyu  sosai  kamar  ita  ta  haifeshi  ,, nima  gaskiya  yaron  yana  burgeni  akwai  hankali  ga  surutu  " da  abin dariya  , amma  yanzu  kaga  yayi  luf  bawan Allah  shiyasa  aka  ce  lfy  uwar  jiki  , inba  ita  komai  ya  tsaya  

Hafeez  yace  hakane   amina  yaron  akwai  shiga  rai  sosai  wllh  yanzu  yazama  abun  tausayi  dubi  kiga  yadda  aka  maidashi  ,, duk  yarame  fatanmu  dai  Allah  yakareta  daga  duk  sharrin  bil'adam  , da  ma  jinsinmu  ,, Amina  tace  ameen  ya  Allah  amina  , nan  sukaci  gaba  da  hirarsu  



 talatu  ko ganin  ta  shawo  , kan  husna  saita  cike  da  farin ciki  ,,sosai  tace  ai  yarinya  dole  kike  tatso  mana  alhaji  muyi  mushasha  da   kudi   ,,nima  nake  saka  kaya  irin na  manya  ,, ko  agidan  biki  bamai  rena  ni  


Baba  na  zuwa  gida  murna  talatu  ta  tareshi  , tamai  sannu  da  zuwa  , ta  amshe  ledar  hannu  sa  ta  ajiye  sai  rawar  kai  take  ,, tayi  kitchen  ta  dauko mai  ruwa  ,, yau su   talatu  hadda  durkusa  ta  bawa  baba  ruwa  mai  sanyi  

Murmishi  yayi  yace  kai  matar  nan  makira ce  yanzu  haka  kudine  takeson  na  bata,, shiyasa   take  wannan  iyayin  , 


Talatu  tace  malam  azubo  ma  abinci  ko  wanka  zakayi  tukun  kaci  abincin  , 

Kallon ta  yayi  ya  girgiza  kai  yace  nadai  fara  wankan  dai  ,,     tukun  naci  abinci  , ko  

Mikewa  talatu  tayi  tace  to  bari  na  hadama  , ruwan  wanka  nan  tayi  gaba  ,  

Baba  ko  tunani  yake  yace  kai  gaskiya  abanza  ruwa  , baya  tsami  , wllh  wannan  matar  masifaffiya  dole  akwai  wani  abun  da  takeson  na  mata  ,   shiyasa  take  wannan  abun

 
Husna  ko  gidan  su  sailuba  tanufa  wanda  take  , ganinsu   kamar  danginta  ,, shiyasa  duk  damuwar  take  kaiwa  maman  sailuba  kawarta  ,,  tabata  shawara  ,, da  sallama  tashiga  ,, gidan  amsa  mata  sukayi   sailuba  " da  maman  ta  da  babanta  


Ciki  ta  karasa  ta  durkushe  tana  sakin  kuka  mai  taba  zuciya  ,, cikin  tashin      mama  tayo  kanta  , tace  lfy  husna  mai  aka  miki  " bakida  lfy  ne  wani  wajan  ke  miki  ciwone  ,, duk  alokaci  daya  , mama  ke  jero  mata  wannan  tambayoyi  


Dagowa  tayi  tana  kallon  mama  tace  ,, nashiga  uku    mama  kitaimake  ni  wllh  mutuwa  zanyi  idan  suka  hadani  da  wannan  basamude   


Mama  ta  dagota  tace  waye  kuma  basamude  husna  , ki  fadamin  meke  faruwa  ne  

Cikin  kuka   husna  ta  bawa  maman  labari    komai  


Sailuba  ko  murna  takamyi  tace  Alhamdulillah  kila  husna  karshen  wahalar  kice  ,,tazo  tinda  har  wannan  alhajin   yazo  zai  aureki  " nasan   baba  zaiyi  bincike  akanshi  " idan  na  kirkine   kinga  kin  huta  

Dan Allah  husna  ki  kwantar  da  hankalin  ki  kidena  kara  , saka  kanki  cikin  damuwa  ,, insha Allah  kema  wata  zaki  samu  y'anci  ,, idan  kika  yarda  ,,da  auren  wannan  bawan  Allah  "  zakiji  dadi  kinji  ,, ki  amince  mishi  ,, kema  zaki  soshi  kamar  yadda  ,,yace  yana  sonki  ,,kinga  saiki  cemishi  idan  kunyi aure  zakici  gaba  da  karatunki  ,, kinga  saiya  cenja  miki  school  ma  ,, kicika  burinki  na  zama  barrister  koba  haka  ba  mama  mai  kikace  ,,akan  maganar  danayi 


Ajiyar zuciya  tasauke  tace  ,, hakane  maganar  ki  ,, sailuba  ki  amince  da  auren  nashi  ko  zaki  samu  y'anci acikin  rayuwar  ki  , kema  kiyi  farin  ciki  kamar  yadda  kowa  keyi  ,, kinji   nasan  baban  ki  zaiyi  bincike  akanshi  ,, idan  ya  yaba  da  tarbiyar  shi  ,,zai  yabashi  ke      kinji  husna  ,, kidena  sakawa  kanki  wata  damuwar  ,, kiyita  addu'a  idan  alkhairi  Allah  yabaku  zaman lfy  idan  ba  alkhairi  bane  Allah  ya  cenja  miki  da  mafi  alkhairi  


Wani  sanyi  husna  taji  na  ratsata  ,   gaskiya  babu   abinda  zata  cewa  , iyayen  kawar  tata  ,, share  hawayen  ta  tayi  ,, tace  insha Allah  mama  zanyi  abinda  kuka  ce  ,,zanbi  shawarar  ku  ina  alfahari  daku  ,,  Allah  yasaka da  alkhairi  "  kune  dangi  a  duniyar  nan  ,, nagode sosai  ta  kara  saka  wani  kukan  


Baban sailuba  yace  kiyi  shuru  husna  insha Allah  ,, babu  abunda  zai  faru  sai  alkhairi  ,, nan  sukaci  gaba  da  mata  nasiha  da  bata  shawara  ,, suna kwantar  mata  ,,da  hankali  , saida  sukaga  ta  saki  ranta  sosai  tukun  ta  tafi  gida  


Tana  komawa  gida  wajan  baba  taje  tamishi  barka  da  zuwa  ,,lokacin  yana  cin  abinci  

Kallon  yayi  cike  da  so  da  kauna  irin  na  uba  da  diyarsa  ,, yace  husna  baba  , ina  kikaje  talatu  ,, take  kiranki  baki  nan  


Sunkuyar  dakai  tayi  tace  naje  gidansu  sailuba  ne  ,,nakai  mata wani  littafi  ne   


Baba  yace  masha Allah  to  yayi kyau  ,, nan  ta  mike  ta  shige  dakinta  tana  sakawa  a  ranta    insha Allah  zata  auri  alhaji  koda  bata  sonshi  dan  tabar  gidan  nan  


Talatu  ta  kalli  baba  tace,  yauwa  mlm   dama  inason  muyi  wata  magana  ,   

Baba  yace  inajinki  

Nan  tamika  mishi  kudin  


Kallon  ta  yayi  yace  wannan  fa  

Sunkuyar  takai  talatu  tayi  kamar  wata  ta  Allah  tace  ,,dama  wani  Alhaji  ne  yazo  yaga  husna  tin  daga  makarantar,,  yabiyo  ta   shine  akaci  sa'a  , na  fito  zanje  gidan  barka  shine  yazo  ya  gaisheni  yabani  wannan  ,, ni  kuma  naki  karba  saboda  kasan  masu  kudin  yanzu,  ba  abin  yarda  bane  ,,  yayi  tamin  bakiya  ,, ganin  babban  mutum  ya  durkusa  har  kasa,, yana  gaisheni  yana  mikamin  kudi  ,, naki  karba  , ganin  mutane  na  kallon  mu  idan  zasu  wuce  shiyasa  na  karba  


Na  tabbayeshi  , sunanshi  yace  sunanshi  alhaji  abdallah  ,,  shi  dan  kasuwane  , kuma  yanason  husna  tsakaninsa  da  Allah  shi  aurenta  zaiyi     bada  wasaba  yazo  ,, yace  zaiyo  gabatar  da  kanshi  gareka  da  neman  izinin  auren  husna    a  wajan  ,, yace  na  sanar  maka  


Shaushi  ne  yakama  baba  ,, ya  kalli  talatu   yace  amma  talatu  bakida  ,, hankali  husna  duka  nawa  take  da  kike  zancen  amata  aure  duka  duka  bayafi  shekara  15years  ba  ,, kike  zancen  aure  "  to  ni  bada  niba  za'ayi  wannan  shirmen  ba  , inason  ya'ta   tayi  karatu  sosai   tacika  burinta  ,, amma  babu  zancen  ,,na  mata  aure  yanzu  ,,ki  tashi  kibani  waje  mai  tunanin  tinkiya  kawai  ,, 


Talatu  ko  batace  komai  ba  ta  dauki kudin  ta  fice  a  falon,,  tana  fadin  inkasan  wani  bakasan  wataba  ,, mlm   yanzu  zance  wajan  kaka  na  sanar  da  ita    komai  dole  ka  yarda  ,, ni  zakawa  bakin  cikin  cin  arziki  ,, to  wllh  baka  isaba kayi  kadan  , nice  fa  talatu  kowa  yaci,   tuwo  dani  miya  yasha  in  fada  maka  mlm  ,, nan  tashige  daki 



Washe gari  talatu  taje  gidan  ,, kaka  da  sallama  tashiga  

Kaka  na  zaune  tace  a  a  lale  barhabin  da  talatu  yau  , kice  a  gidan  namu "  sunnu da  zuwa  ,, 


Zama  talatu  tayi  suka  gaisa  da  , kaka  ta  kawo  mata  ruwa  ,

Kaka  tace  talatu  nasan  dai  abanza  bazakizo  ,, gidan  ba  saida  dalilin  nasan  kin  gumtso  fesomin  , yar gari  


Talatu  ta  karkace  tawa  kaka  bayani  ,,har  abinda   baba  yafada  , mata  ta  kara  da  nata  karya  


Tafa  hannu  kaka  take  tace  a  ni  niyar  nan  yanzu  shi  mai  sunan  manya  ,, haka  yazama  mahaukaci  ban  sani  ba  yarinya  tasamu  bai  kudi  ,, amma  kace  bazata  tayi  aureba  ,, watoma  , bakin  cikin   cin  arziki  yake  mana  to  bazai  yiyuba  , zaizo  yasameni  , kikoma  gida  zakiji  labari  mai  dadi  


Cike da farin ciki  talatu  ta  wa  kaka  sallama  ta  tafi  gida  ,, tana  zuwa  gida  takira  ,,tabawa  tafada  mata  duk  abinda  ,, sukayi  da  kaka  



Bayan kwana biyu  baba  , yaje  gaishe  da   kaka   ko  amsa  gaisuwar  batayi ,, ta  rufeshi  da  fada  , kuma  tace  dole  awa  husna  aure  inkuma  ,, yace  ba  haka  ba zata  tsine  mishi   


Da sauri  baba  yace  na  amince  dan  Allah  karki  tsinemin  

Kaka  tace  ka  nemawa  kanka  mafita  dan  banza  kawai  tashi  kabani  guri  


Haka baba  yanaji  yana  gani  ya  amince  da  auren  husna  badan  ranshi  yasoba  ,, dadinshi  dayama  baban  sailuba  ,yace  ya  fada  mishi  cewa  idan  yabari husna  tayi  aure  ,,zata  samu  y'anci   tadena  ganin  taskun  rayuwa,, , Baba  yace  dadi  sosai  da  shawarar  baban  sailuba  ,, dan  haka  yacire  damuwa  a  ranshi  yazauna  jiran  ,  alhajin  yazo  yaji  


Aljana  safiya  kullin  cikin  damuwa  take  na  rashin  lfyr  "  Aliyu  abu  sai  kara  gaba  yakeyi  ,,ba  sauki  ko  kadan  , ga  tunanin  husna  da  takeyi  ,, tayi  mafarkin  ta  , tazo  ta  taimaka  mata  ,, amma  hakan  bazai  yiyuba  ,, saboda  bazata  iya  zuwa   wani  wajan  ba  tabar  aliyu  cikin  wannan  tsananin  ciwon  , shiyasa  duk  abu  goma  da  ashirin  ya  hade  mata  ,, Allah  Allah  takeyi  , lokacin  zuwa  dajin  nan  yayi  taje  takawo  ganyen  nan  ko  aliyu  yasamu  lfy  ,  takoma  ga  husna  ,, tana  tausayawa  yarinyar  kullin  cikin  tunanin  ta  takeyi  ,, haka  ta  hakura  ta  fauwalawa  Allah  komai  nata  , tana  zaman  jiran  lokaci  yayi 



Yaune  Alhaji  yasake  dawowa  ,, husna  ta  saukeshi  ,, a  dakin  baki  , takai  mishi  ruwa  da  aminci   


Taci  kwalliya  kamar  yadda  talatu  tace  mata  takeyi ,  idan  yazo  


Shi  kuwa  Alhaji  abdallah  kurawa  , husna  ido  yayi    wutar  sonta  na ruruwa, a  zuciyar  sa  sosai  takejin  kaunar  ta   


Kunya  ce  takama  husna  tace  am  Alhaji  ga   abinci  bismillah  ,  


Da sauri  yadawo  daga  tunanin  dayake   ,   yace  ok  nagode  sosai   , nan  yafara  cin  abinci   shinkafa  ce  da  miya  tasha  nama  sai  zuba  kamshi  take  ,,dayar  kular  kuma  taliya  ce  shajjage  , da  mai  da  yajinta  , gefe  ga  lemuka  masu  sanyi  


Sosai  yaci  abinci  bayan  yagama  , yayi  hamdalah  yace  ,    gaskiya  kin  iya  girki  Allah  yamiki  albarka  

Murmishi  husna  tayi  tace  nagode  


Gyara zama  yayi  yace  to husna  da  farko dai  sunana  Alhaji abdallah  sulaiman  ,,ni  dan  kasuwane  yarana  uku  maza  biyu  mace  daya  ,, matata  ta  rasu  tin lokacin  da ta  haifi  yarta  ta  ukun  wajan  haihuwa  ta  rasu  ,, tin daga  nan  bansake  " aure  ba  saboda  nakasa  samun  wadda  zata  kulamin  da  yarana  ,, sai  yanzu  Allah  yahadani  dake  ,, in  fatan  dai  zaki  karfi  soyayya  husna  wllh  ,tsananina  da  Allah  nake  sonki  da  kaunar  ki  " ki  tausayi  min  kema  kice  kinasona  " kinji  husna  baby  ,, nasan  ke kina  da  hankali  da  nutsuwa  " yarana  zasuji  , dadin  Zama  dake  ,, 


Husna  dagowa  tayi  tana  kallon  Alhaji  sosai  take  kare  mishi  kallon   wankan  tarwada  ne   yana  tsayi  dai-dai  misali  , yanada  kyakyawar  ,, fuska   sosai  yabata   tausayi  ,, jin  matarshi  ta  rasu  , lokaci  daya  taji  tausayin  sa  na  ratsata  


Gyaran murya  yayi  


Da sauri  husna  ta  sunkuyar  dakai  


Musulinci  yayi  yace  husna  kodai  ban  miki  ba  dan Allah  jiki  qai  na 


Husna  tace  na  amince  zan  aureka  , amma  zaka  barni  naci  gaba  da  karatuna  


Alhaji  abdallah  yace  bakida  matsala  ,indai  karatu  ne  ko  karshen  Barrow  kikeson  , gani   zan  goya  miki  baya  


Husna  tace  nagode  sosai  bari  na  kira  maka  baba  nan  tafita  



Alhaji abdallah  ko  cewa  yake  Alhamdulillah  , Husna  ta  amince  zata  aureni  yarinya  mai  hankali  insha Allah  zan  cika  miki  burinki  



Baba  ne  yashigo  ,,  nan  Alhaji  abdallah  yazube  a  kasa  yana  kwasar  gaisuwa  ,,, amsawa  baba  yayi  cike  da  fara'a  


Alhaji abdallah  yayiwa  baba  bayanin  komai  ,,  yace  insha Allah  zaibar  husna  taci  gaba  da  karatun ta  ,don  cikar  burinta  



Sosai  baba  ya  yaba  da  hankalin  alhaji  abdallah  ,, ya  amince  yake  zuwa  zance  wajan  husna   har  suyi  exam  , bayan  sun  gaba  sai  asaka  ranar  bikin  su  



Sosai  Alhaji abdallah  yakewa  baba  godiya  , nan  ya  dauko  kudi  yabawa  baba  amma  baba  yace  a  a  babu  yadda  baiyi  ba  ,,amma  baba  yace  bazai  amsaba      nan  baba  yafita  , yace  zai  turo  mishi  husna. 


Bayan husna tazo  har  bakin  motarshu  ta  rakashi  saida  taga  tafiyarshi  tukun  ta  koma  gida



Yaune  ranar  da  aljana safiya  zataje  dajin  biyoyi  ,, domin  ciro  wannan  ganye  awa  aliyu  magani  ,

Fitowa  sukayi  su  duka  aljana safiya  tayi  shigar  kayan yaki  da  takobin  ta  har  biyu  ,, 


Kallon  tayi  tace  to  ni  zantafi  sai  Allah  Allah yadawo  dani  ,, hafeez  amina   ga  amana  nan  ,, 


Cike  da  girmamawa  suka  amsa  da  to  shugaba  Allah  yakaiki  lfy  yadawo  dake lfy  , ta  amsa  da  ameen  ,,, wajan  da  aliyu  yake  kwance  tazo  ta  sumbaceshi  tace  yarona  insha Allah zaka  warke  da  yardar  mai  duka  ,, jin  kuka  na  neman  kwacemata  " yasa  ta  sakeshi ta  mike  ta  kalli  kowa  tace  natafi  nan  suka  mata  addu'a  sosai  " har  runtse ido  zata  bace  ,, aliyu  yace  umma  na  zanbiki  mutafi   banason  na  zauna  anan  nike  zanbi  ,,  share hawaye tayi  takasa  juyowa  saida  abba  yakare  aliyu  tukun   wata  tsuntsuwa  mai  girman  gaske  zato  gabanta  waha  tayi  suka  lula  sararin  samaniya ,,,

*Da  sunan Allah mai rahama mai jin qai*



*17—18*



Aljana safiya  tinda   suka  lula  sararin  samaniya ,, take  share  hawaye  na tausayin  dan  nata  wanda  takejin  kamar  ita  ta  haifeshi  , tana  kaunarshi  fiye  da  misali  , shiyasa  duk  ta  kasa  samun  nutsuwa  ,  duk  a sayaye  take  tafiyar  nan  ,,cen  kuma  saita  tuna  da  nasihar  da  abba  yayi  mata  yace  


Ya  ke  safiya  kiyi sani  cewa  idan  kika  nuna  karaya  a  wannan  tafiyar  taki  za'a  samu  gagarumar  matsala  ,,   idan  har  kina  yawon  tunanuka  ,, kefa  jarumar  jarumai ce ko  kin  manta  laq'abin  da  bil'adam   suka  mishi  wato  ,*aljanar jarumai* to  kike  gasance  wa  kullin  ahaka  indai  aliyu  ne zai  samu  lfy  insha Allah  ,, fatana  dai  kije  lfy  kidawo  lfy  ,, zakiyi  tafiyar  shekara  daya  kafin  kije  dajin  bishiyoyi  ,, saboda  tsananin  nisanshi  , yan  adam  ko sukanfi  , shekara arba'in   kafi  suke  wannan  daji  ,mu  aljannu  ne  muke  shekara  daya  a  hanya  kafin  muje 


Ya'ta  kiyi  sani cewa  sarki  shamsu   mugun  mutum  ne  hatsabibin  sarki  ne  ,, inason  kafin  lokacin  mutuwa  na  yazo  inga  mutuwar  sarki shamsu  , fatan alkhairi  agareki  yar albarka  


Ajiyar zuciya  tasauke  bayan  tadawo  daga  karanto  nasihar  da  baban  ta  yamata  ,, share  hawayen  fuskar  ta  tayi  ,,tace  dole  na  kasheka  azzalimi  ,, yadda  ka  maidamin  dan  amana  ta  , cikin  wannan  halin  saina  nuna  maka  ni  safiya  ba  kanwar  lasa  bace,,  wannan  karon ,  bazaka  tsira  ba  sarki  shamsu  ,, ina  dawowa  daga  dajin  bishiyoyi  ,, zan  dura  kanka  mugu  kawai  ,,  wani  karfine  taji  yazo  mata  nan  taci  gaba  da  tafiya  bakinta  dauke  addu'o'i  da  babanta  ya  koya  mata  su  yabata  wani  littafin  addu'o'i  ,  take  karanta  shi  ako  ina  ,, yana  da  mashimmanci  , sosai  , dayan  kuma  wanda  zai  nuna  mata  hanya  ne  , haka  tacigaba  da  tafiyar  ta  har  dare yayi  salla  kawai  ke  tsayar  ,  da  ita  ,,  


Tana  tafiya   cikin  duhu  ganin  tazo  wani  daji  mai  kyau  sai  taji  hankalin  ta  ya  kwanta  da  dajin  ,, nan  ta  yada  zango  don  ta  huta  inyaso  da  safe  saita  kara  gaba  ,,,tanti  ta  kafa  , tayi  shinfida  


Kayan  abincin  ta  ta  dauko  taci  takoshi  , tukun  tayi  alwala  ta  tada  sallar  nafila  ,, bayan  tagama  nan  tayi  addu'a  sosai  tukun  ta  kwanta   hannun  ta  , mai  zoben  ta  jumke  tana  saita  ta  yadda  koda  akwai  matsala  , taji       



Bangaren sarki  shamsu   lokacin  daya  bace  daga  , dajin  aljana  safiya  ,, bai  tsaya  ko  ina  ba  sai  wani  gida  mai  kyau  sosai  ya  ,bayyana  a  wani  daki  ,, yana  bayyana  ya  kwashe  da  wata  ,, mahaukaciyar  dariya  ,, yace  lalle  safiya  kina  wasa  da  sarki shamsu  mai  zamani  kayi  naka  kayi  na  wasu ,, ni  har  kin  isa  kece  zaki  kasheni   a  lokaci  daya  " ina  kallon  ki  to  wllh  nafi  karfinki   ,saina  lalata  wannan  zoben  da  kike  takama  dashi  , naga  abinda  zaki  iya  



Madubin  tsafinsa  ya  shafa  nan  safiya  ta  bayyana  ,a  jikin  sa  dauke  da  wani  jariri  sai  share  hawaye  take  ,,, murmishin  mugunta  yayi  yace  yar  wahalar  ai  nasamu  lagonki  ,,tinda  naga  kina  kuka  akan  wannan  jaririn  to  saina  lalata  , mishi  rayuwa  daga  nan  idan  kika  karaya  ,,sai  na  kasheki  , kara  saki  wata dariyar  yayi  yana  fadin  ,ni  sarki  shamsu  naci  dubu  sai  ceto    duk  wanda  yatabani  ,sai  yayi  kwanan  lahira  


Lokacin  sarki  shamsu  ,, yayi  yayi  yawa  aliyu  wani abu  amma  yakasa  saboda  koda  yaushe  , yana  wajan  baban  safiya  shi  kuma  , baya  ganinsa  ,, hakan  yasa  sarki  shamsu  yacika  da  bakin  cikin  na  kasa  cimma  ,,abinda  yakeso  


Haka  yasa sarki  shamsu  ,bin  duk  wani  tantirin  boka  ko  matsafi  tinda  aljana  safiya  ta  kashe  boka  shaddar  ,, shiyasa  ya  bazama  duniya  ,waje  waje  yake  kasashe  daban  daban  ,Sarki  shamsu  yaje  ,,amma  ko  ina  abu  daya  ne  bazasu  iya  , cutar  da  aliyu  ba  ,, wannan  kalmar  tana  kona  zuciyar  sarki shamsu  


Saboda  bakin  hali  irin  na  sarki  shamsu  ,,  saida  ya  rufe  kanshi  a  dakin  tsafinsa  na tsayin  shekara  biyu  ,, yana  hada  kayan  tsafi  , sosai  yake  shirin  ganin  bayan  aljana  tafiya   tako  ,wani  hali  

Lokacin  da  aliyu  tacika  shekara  hudu  ,,a lokacin  sarki  shamsu  yaci  galaba  akan  aljana safiya  ,, wata  rana  aliyu  yafito  baiga  safiya  ba  saiya  kutsa  wani  lungu  ,, yana  kiran  sunanta  amma  shuru  hakan  yasa  aliyu  kara  shiga  wajan  yana  kukan  umman  shi  


Sarki shamsu  na  hasko  aliyu  a  madubin  tsafi  yaganshi  shidaya  ,, tsalllen  murna  yayi  nan  ya  hada  tsafi  mai  wahalar  magani  ya  turawa  aliyu  


Lokacin  aliyu  yazauna  a  wani  guri  mai  inuwa  tana  rera  kuka  ,sai  kawai  aka  bugeshi  nan  yafadi  sumanme  , 


Aljana safiya  tafito  daga  wajan  babanta  taga  aliyu baya  nan hankali  a  tashe  take  nemansa  tofiwa  tayi  tana  kiransa  ,, cen  ta  hangosa  a  kwance  

Karasowa  tayi  , tace  yi hakuri  yarona  , ina  wajan  abba  ne  tashi  mutafi  , amma  taga  ko  motsi  baiyiba  ,, tabshi  tayi  taga  yasaki  jiki  a razane  ta daukoshi  ,,tana  kiran  yan  uwanta  ,, basuga  abinda  yasa  aliyu  suma  ba  amma  kowa  yace  bai  gani  ba  haka  akayi  tazubawa  aliyu ruwan  sanyi  amma  bai gama  farfadowa  ba  ,saida  yayi  kwana  biyu  tukun  ya  farfado  da  tsananin  ciwo  yatashi  (wannan  shine dalilin  cutan  aliyu  dan  aljana safiya)



Shi  kuwa  sarki shamsu  na  ganin , tugunsa  , yakama  aliyu  yacika  da farin ciki  ,, nan  yaci  gaba  da  azabtar  dashi  ta  hanyar  tsafi   yana  mai  dariyar  mugunta  ,, yace  aina  fada  saina  kashe  *ALJANAR JARUMAI*   ni  zan  zama  jarumin   jarumai  ,  na  duniya  baki  daya  



Husna  yanzu  hankalin  ta  ya  kwanta  da  Alhaji  abdallah  ,,ganin  yadda  yake  nuna  mata  tararraya  ,,da  kulawa  ,sosai  takejin  dadin  zatabar  gidansu,, lokacin jin  dadin  tane  yace,  


Yauma  kamar  kullin  , an  gama  musu  lecture   tafito  , waje  ita  da  sailuba  sai  ,murmishi  takeyi  

Sailuba  tace  kai  kawata,  yadai  kodai  alhajin  nakine  ,,yafada  miki  kalamai  masu  dadi ne   

Harara husna  ta  aikawa  ,, Sailuba  tace   kefa  yar  iska  ce  sailuba  ke  komai  kalaman  soyayya  , kamar  wata  mayya   wai  kisan  ma  me


Dariya  sailuba  tayi  tace  ,a  a  saikin  fada  ta  alhaji  ikon Allah  


Juya  baya  husna  tayi  tace  ,nadena  baki  labarin  ma  

Sailuba  tace  Allah  yabaki  hakuri   kawalliya  roman  zabuwa  ,



Dukan  wasa  husna  takaiwa  ,Sailuba  tace  ,wllh  sailuba  kina  da  matsala  sai  kiyita  rattabo  magana  haka  ,, nifa  ba  wani  abu  bane  dama  Alhaji  ne  yace  zai  siyomin  ,waya  iPhone  11 ,  nan  da  one week,,  ni  kuma  nacemai  yabari  in  aka  mana  auren  ,,idan  na  tare  saiya  siyamin  ,,kinga  yanzu  idan  yabani  nasan  mama  zata  kwace  ta  siyar  ,,itada  anty  tabawa  
Amma  mai  kikace  akan  abinda  na  fada  



Numfashi  sailuba  taja  tace,, gaskiya  kinyi  tunani  mai  kyau  , kinga  yanzu  idan  yabaki  mama  zata  amshe  kobata  da  niyar  karba   tabawa  zata  ,,sakata  ta  amshe  ,, wannan  tunani  ne  mai  kyau  


Husna   zatayi  magana  saiga  kiran  alhaji  yashigo  , wayarta  ,kallon  sailuba  tayi  tace  ,kinga  Alhaji  

Sailuba  tace  dauka  muji  dame  yace  ,,dagwa  tayi  tace  ,assalam alaikum 



Daga  bangaren  Alhaji  , saida  a  lumshe  ido  saboda  jin  muryar  husna  ,a  kunan  shi  ,,sai  cen  tukun  yace  amin wa'alaikum salam  ya  sahibaty  na  ina  fatan  kina  lfy  



Husna  ko  rufe  fuska  tayi  kamar  yana  ganinta  tace  lfy  lau  alhaji  na  yagida  ya  yarana  ,suke  ina  fatan  suna  lfy 


Murmishin  jin dadi  yayi  yace  Alhamdulillah  sunce  na  gaisheki  ,gobe  zasuzo  ganin mommy  dinsu  ,,sun  takurani  wai  saina  kawosu  sunganki  


Husna  na  yar dariya  tace  ayya Allah sarki  ,to  Allah  yakaimu  sai  kuzo  na  gansu 

Yace ameen  fito  nakaiku  gida  gani  a  bakin  get  din  school  dinku 



Da mamaki  husna tace  to  ya  akayi  kasan  banzo  gida  ba  


Alhaji  yace  kedai  kufito  , nakaiku  gida  nan  ya  kashe  wayar  


Dauke  wayar  husna  tayi  daga  kunnanta  ,,ta  kalli  sailuba  tace  muje  Alhaji  yana  waje  ,zaikaimu gida  ,,


Sailuba tace  to  muje  nan  suka  fita  waje  daga  nesa  suka  hangosa  


Tun  kafin su  karaso  , Alhaji  ya  kurawa  husna  na  mujiya  ,, yana  kare  mata  kallo  sosai  yake  " jin  kaunar  ta  a  ranshi  kai  gaskiya  husna  ta  dabance  wai  ahaka  ma  bata  gama  girma  ba  ,,gaskiya  kwashi  gara  a  wajan  husna  ,, ga  hankali  ga  nutsuwa  ga  kyau  uwa  uba  biyayya  ,, ga  kunya  ,,shiyasa  yake  kara  sonta  ,gashi  tanason  diyanshi  tin  ,,kafin  tajema  gidan  nashi  kai shidai  " yayi  dacen  mata  ,,da  sauri  yadawo  daga  tunanin  da  yakeyi  jin  ana  kwankwasa  , glass  din 


Budewa  husna  gaba  yayi  tashiga  , sailuba  tashiga  baya  ,suna  tafiya  suna  hira  sosai  Alhaji  yakejin  dadin  kasancewa  da  husna  


Ita  kuwa  duk  kunya  ta  rufeta  jin  yana  fada  mata  kalaman  soyayya  ,ga  sailuba  abaya  na  mata  dariya  ,,wai  bata  iya  kalaman  soyayya  ba  a  haka  sukazo  kofar  gidan  su  sailuba  tasauka  ya  nufi  gidan  su  husna 


A  kofar  gidansu  yayi  faking  ,ya  juyo  ya  kalli  husna  , cike  da  so da kauna  , yace  husna  ta  kicemin i love you,  mana  ko  naji  sanyi  araina  


Sunkuyar  da  kai  tayi  tana  murmishi  tace  ,, kunya  nakeji  ai  

Yar dariya  yayi  yace  kai  husna  nafa  kusa  zama  angonki  insha Allah  nan  da  sati  za'a  saka  ranar  bikin  mu  nidake,, ai  gyara kike  kula  dani  


Husna  tace  to  kawai  tayi  shuru  

Girgiza kai  Alhaji  yayi  yasan  bazata  kuma  magana  ba   , nan  ya  dauko  waya  sabuwa  kirar  Samsung Galaxy 12 , yabata  yace  gashi  tinda  kince  karna  siyo  miki  iPhone 11 


Dagowa  tayi  ta  kalli  wayar  ,  kallon  shi  tayi  , tayi  kamar  ,zatayi  kuka  cikin  shagwaba  tace  , nidai  gaskiya  Alhaji  wannan  tamin  ,girma  ai  


Wani  kayataccen  murmishi  yayi  yace  , Husna  Keenan  kina  rikitani  da  wannan  shagwabar  taki  inason  mace  mai  shagwaba  ,  sosai   


Rufe fuska tayi  tana   dariya  dan  yanzu  itama  ,, tana  sonshi  sosai  saboda  ya  nuna  mata  soyayya  wadda  bamai  mata  irin  ta  sai  babanta  da  ahalin kawarta  sailuba  tace  nifa  ban  iya  shagwaba  , banma  san  yadda  akeyinta  ba  ,kawai  dai  tsokana  ta  kake  


Murmishi yayi yace  to  shikenan  ai  tinda  kince  haka  nan  gaba  zaki  gane  ,yanzu  sai  gobe  insha Allah  zamuyi  waya  


Husna  na  kama  Barfun  motar  tace  to   Allah  yakaimu  fita  tayi 


Harta  juya  zata  shiga ciki  yace  am  husna  ta  bakiji  ba 


Dawowa  tayi  inda  yake  tace  gani  ,kudi  yabata  yace  kikaiwa  mama  , kinji  bake  nabawa  ba  yanzu  sai  kice  bazaki  amsa ba 


Kallon shi  tayi  tace  to  ,ai  yayi  yawa  din  ne  gaskiya  kake  ragewa  , tana  kumbura  baki 


Dariya kafin  yace  wannan  kumbura  bakin  fa  husna ta  , shikenan  ki  kai  mata  kawai  


Husna tace  to  Allah  Allah yasaka  da alkhairi  Allah  yakara  bude  


Yace  ameen  husna  ta  i love you  sosai  da  sosai  


Ai  kafin  ya  idar  ma  ta  shiga  gida  aguje   tana  waiwayo  shi  


Girgiza  kai  yayi  yace  , kai  husna kenan  shiyasa  nake  sonki  kin  banban  ta da  sauran  mata,, nan  yashiga  motar  yabar  wajan  




Aljana safiya  na  bacci  sama  sama,, sai  taji  zobenta  na  haske  mata  fuska  , kamar  bazata  tashi  saida  taji  haske  ya  soma  zafi  a  fuskar  ta  ,,da  sauri  ta  tashi  tana  salati  , 


Kallon  hannun  nata  tayi  mai  zoben  tace  , lalle  akwai  matsala  ,a  dajin  nan   mikewa  tayi  tasoma  lekawa  da  baya  ta  ,,dawo  da  sauri   tana  dafe  kirji  ,,, da  sauri  ta  dauki  littafin  ta  wanda   yake  nuna  mata  hanya  ta  bude  daga  farko  , 


Taga  ta  wuce  daji  na  farko  ,, ta  wuce  na  biyu  yanzu  tana  daji  na  uku  , mai  suna  dajin  macijiya  ,, 


Aljana safiya  tace  lalle  kafin  gari  yawaye  ,, saina  kashe  wannan  macijiyar  " data  addabi  mutane  da  aljanu   ,, nan  ta  mike  ta  dauki  takobinta  ,, da  kwari  da  bakar ta  , hadda  kananan  wukake    sunfi  , ashirin  , nan  ta  nema  musu  guri  ajikinta  

A  kwabrin  ta  jera  hudu  daya  kafan  ma  haka  ta  jerasu    


Sai  wasu  ta  jerasu  a  tsintsiyar  hannun  ta  ta  maida  hannun  rigar  ta  rufe   a k'ugunta  ma  haka  ta  jera  su  ta  dauki  kibiyoyin  harbi  ta  rataya  ,, su  a  kafadar ta   wata  hula  ta  saka  a  kanta  ta  safe  jikinta da  wasu   irin  kaya  ,, sosai  ta  shirya  tana  jiran  zuwan  macijiyar  ,, ta  duba  taga  sai  macijiyar  ta  kwana  tana  tafiya  kafin  tazo  gareta  



Amma  aljana  safiya  tasha  alwashin  saitaga  bayan  wannan  macijiya  mai  hadarin  gaske  nan  ta  zauna  tana  ,, karanto  addu'o'i  ta  taki  komawa  baccin  



 Yau   husna  bata  da  lecture  , bataje  school  ba   sai  aikin  gidan  data  , yi  kamar  wata  jaka  , talatu  ko  sannu  bata  mata  , yanzu da  sauki  , bata  dukanta  kullin  indai  takawo  mata  kudi  ,saidai  tayi  ta  aiki  kamar  wadda  akawa  wahayin  , sa   yanzu  girki  takewa  alhaji  shida  yarabshi  ,,dazai  kawo  mata  suganta  




Tana  gama  girkin  taje  tayi  kwalliya  sosai  tayi  matukar  kyau  , nan  tashiga  falon  talatu  , durkusa  wa  tayi  tace  mama  Alhaji  yazo  shida  yaran  nashi  ,, zanje  na  shigo  dasu  


Baki a  washe  talatu  tace  a  a  harsunzo  Keenan  to  kice  ina  gaishe  da  alhajin  kinji  ,,kuma  kike  , dan  mishi  dadin  baki  dan  yake  baki  kudi  dayawa  ,, kinga  so  nake  na  siya  miki  kayan  daki na  gani  na  fada  maza  jeki  kar  manta  sakona  fa 



Husna  tace  to  mama  nan  tafita  tana  murmishin  takaici  wai  ko  yaushe  maman  ,, nata  zatayi  hankali  wato  ita  kullin  kudi  shine  a  ranta  , nan  ta  kira  Alhaji  tace  ku  shigo  dakin  baki  , nan  ta  kaishe  kira

Kitchen  ta  nufa  ta  dauko  abinci  da  ruwa  ta  nufi  dakin  baki  


Da  sallama  tashiga  , yaran  suka  tashi  da  sauri  suka  amshe  kayan  suka  ajiye  , 


Yar  karamar  tace  oyoyo  mommy  sannu  da  zuwa   daddy  yace  dama  zamuzo  mu  ganki  ,, kuma  kin  kusan  komawa  ,gidanmu  , mommy  mutafi  , gidanmu  anjima  ,, tana  rungume  husna  


Murmishi  husna  tayi  tace  ,  yarinya  ta  ,, ai  karki  damu  ,,nakusa  komawa  gidanku  kinji  , amma  ba  yau  ba 


Yarinyar  ta  dago  tace  to  yaushe  mommy  zamu  tafi  gidan  namu  


Tace  sai  angama  exam  sainaje 


Tsalle  yarinyar  takamayi  ,, tana  fadin  yee  mommy  zata  koma  gidanmu  ,, 


Dariya  husna  tayi  ta  kalli  sauran  yaran  maza  su  biyu  , kowa  yayi  kamar  yayi  fushi  , alhaji  ta  kalla


Murmishi  yayi  yace  wai  sun  fushi  ta  baki  musu  magana  ba  


Murmishin  itama  tayi  , tace  ayya  yaran  albarka  kuzo  nan  


Tashi  sukayi  sukazo  wajan  ta  , rungume  su  tayi  tace  am so sorry   kunji  

Dariya  sukayi  tukun  suka  ce  mun  hakura  mommy  ,,, itadai  husna  wani  banbarakwai  takeji  idan  suna  ce  mata  mommy  


Alhaji  ko  wani  farin ciki  ke  ratsashi  ganin  yadda  husna  ta  tarbi  yaranshi  hannu  biyu  ,, sosai  take  kana  son  husna  

Gyaran  murya  yayi  yace  , wannan  su  yan biyu  ne   Yusuf da  Mustafa  sunan  su  ,sai  karamar  su    aina  muna  ce  mata  mimee  ,,, su  shekara   su  hudu  , ita  kuma  kanwarsu  ,, shekara biyu da rabi  , gareta  



Husna  tace  masha Allah Allah  yaraya  mana  su  akan  sunna  insha Allah  nakama  alkwari  zan  zamo  uwa  agaresu  bazasu  , taba  kukan  uwa  ba indai  ina  raye  agidan  


Sosai  alhaji  yaji dadin  kalamai  godiya  ya  ringa  mata  ,, kamar  ba  gobe  ,ita  husna  kunya  ne  ya  kamata  ,,tace  haba  wannan  godiyar  ta  isa 



Alhaji  yace  bakisan  yadda  nakeji  bane  husna  saboda  yaran  nan  nakiyin  aure  tinda  mahaifiyar  su ta  rasu  ,  saboda  ina  tsoron  kar  na  auro  mace  tazo  ta  gana  musu  azaba  shiyasa  ,,kullin  addu'a  nake  Allah  yabani  mace da  gari  wadda  zata  rikemin  yarana  da  amana  to Alhamdulillah  Allah  yaji  rokona  yabani  ke  


Husna tace  ba komai  ai  haka  Allah  yake  lamarinsa  Allah  yajikan  mamansu   , yace  ameen  """ nan  sukayi  ta  hira  sosai  kafin  suka  tafi  gida  ,,yauma  yabada  kudin  abai talatu  


Husna  nazuwa  tasamu  talatu  da  tabawa  tace  mama  gashi  inji  Alhaji   tana  mikawa  talatu  ,yan  rafa  na dubu  dari  uku  ,,dan  yau  Alhaji  yaji  dadin  yadda  husna  tawa  yaranshi  shiyasa  yabata  dubu  dari  uku


Da sauri  talatu  ta  karba  , tace  a  a  ai  yau  dayawa  ne  to  amshi  dauri  daya  kije  kisiyo  kayan  kwalliya  masu  kyau  ki  kara  mishi  kwalliya  mai  kyau  ,, ta  mikawa husna  dauri  daya  , 


Amsa  husna tayi  harta  juya  talatu  tace  ,,ke  husna  kayana  yana  nan  ki  wankemin  kuma  ga  girki  cen  shara  wanke  wanke  duk  bakiyi  maza  kije  ki  gamamin  kafin  mlm  yadawo  ,, da  to  husna  ta  amsa  ta  fita  


Ganin  kayan  wanki  yabata  mamaki  yanzu  duk  uban aikin  dataci  a  gidannan  baa  tausaya  mata  shine  aka  tilo  mata,, kayan  wankin  nan  yafi  kamfilat  ashirin  ga  girki  ga  shara  wanke  wanke  ,, kai  wannan  rayuwa  da  wuya  take  ,,,share  hawayen  ta  tayi  ,,nan  takama  aikin  ta 


Acen  daki  kuwa  talatu  dauri  daya  ta  mikawa  tabawa  ,, amsa  tayi  tana  godiya  kafin  tace  ,,zata  tafi  gida  nan  sukayi  sallama  ,ta  fita 


Tabawa  ko  bakin ciki  ne  yarufeta  ,, ganin  talatu  tasoma  sakarwa  husna fuska  gashi  ,,hallau  husna  zata  auri  mai  kudi   , ita  nata  diyan  sunan  babu  ko  mashinshini  ,, duk  sun  girma  ,,ina  bazai  yiyuba  dole  ne  taje  wajan  boka  mai  gobe  da  nisa  ,, ya  lalata  wannan  aure  ,,dan  alhaji  ya  auri   yarta  , dan  haka  wannan  ,,  kudin  dashi  zata  je  wajan  boka  dole  araba  husna  da  Alhaji  dan  bada  ita  yadace  ba  da diyarta  ,, yadace  haka  kawai  tana  ji  tana  gani  husna  tasamu  jin  dadi  ita  da  talatu  ,,ita  tana  nan  a  gefe  dole  ,,tayi  asiri  taraba  su  ,,da wannan  makahon  tunanin  ta  nufi  gida


Aljanar safiya  ko  na  zaune  tana  ce  addu'o'i  , tana  jiran  macijiyar  nan  tazo  kamar  daga  sama  taji  karar  tahowar  ta  tana  tunkaro  tantinta  ,, hakan  yasa  aljana safiya  ta  ,,mike  ta  hada  kayanta  ta  ajiye  , sai  tafito waje  ,, tun  daga  nesa  take  gano  wannan  shirgegiyar  ,macijiyar  na  kallon  ta  gyara  tsayuwar  ta  tayi  ,, tana  saita  takobin  ta  , 


Macijiyar  ko  tinda  dare  take  jin  kamshin  safiya  shiyasa  ta  tunkaro  inda  take  da  gudu  take  tafowa  ga  aljana safiya  


Itama  aljana safiya  ganin  haka  yasa  ta  tuna  da  maganar  babanta  ,  yace  idan  kika  hadu  da  macijiyar  nan  , kifara  aiki  ta  kwari da  bakar  tin  daga  nesa  zakike  harbinta  ,  kafin  tazo  gareki 


Da sauri  safiya  ta  zaro  kibiya  guda  ta  saka  ta  ta  dana  taja ta  saitin  kan  macijiyar

*19—20*




Macijiyar  ,,nan  ta  tsaya  cak  ta  kurawa  , aljana safiya  ,ido   tana  nazarin  tayama   akayi  wannan  yarinyar  mai  taurin  kai  tazo  ,, wannan  dajin  babu  wani  mutum  ko  aljanu  dake  iya  tsayawa  anan  komin  dare  ,, wucewa  yakeyi   saboda  tsoron  ta  



Aljana safiya  ko  duba  inda  zata  kashe  macijiyar  nan  take  songani  ajikinta  ,, cen  taga  wani  bakin  abu  kamar  kusa  ,,a  manne  a  kasan  kanta  "  jinjina  kai  safiya  tayi  ,tace  lalle  wannan  shine  wajan  makasar  ki  kuma  sainga  bayanki ,, macuciya  kawai  



Macijiya  , tayo  sufa  nan  suka  hade  itada  aljana  safiya  , idan  ta  je  zata  kai  mata  sara  sai  aljana safiya   ta  bace  ,, duk  yadda  taso  ta  sari  safiya  amma  ta  kasa  


Ita  kuwa safiya  Allah  takeyi  , ta  soketa  a  wannan  wajan  data  gani  ajikin  macijiyar  ,, sai  ta  bayyana  a  wajan  kafin ta  soketa  sai  macijiyar  ta  , juya  kanta  


Sosai  aka  ringa  fada  tsakanin  aljana safiya   , da  macijiya  , kowa  yakasa  kai  dan  uwansa  , kasa  kowa  gwani  ne  wajan  fada  , hakan  yasa  aka  kasa  samun  wanda  yafi  wani  karfi  a  tsakanin  su  


Aljana safiya  ko  taci  alwashin  ko  yini  zasuyi  suna  fada  saita  kashe  ,wannan  macijiyar   dan  tinda  ta  hada  ido  da  ita  bazata  tsiraba  


Itama  macijiyar  ,,  hakan  take  daga  gareta  tana  mamakin  karfi  irin  na  aljana  safiya  ,, ko  a  mayaka  da  duniya  bataga  ,, wanda  yakai  wannan  yarinyar  karfi  ba  ta  kasa  samun  galaba  akanta  so  take  ta  sareta  , amma  ta  kasa  , wani  tunanin  tayi  tace  toko  wannan  itace  aljanar  da  ake  kira  da  *aljanar jarumai*   inkuwa  , itace   lalle  yau  mutuwa  ce  ko  kuwa  ni  na  kasheta  ,, bata  gama  nazarin  ba  taji  sukar  takofi  a  samar  kanta  ,, wani  gigitaccen  kara  macijiyar  tasaki  ,  ta  fadi  kasa  



Aljana safiya  ce  ta  soketa  a  samar  kai  ,   ganin  bata  soki  inda  ,,takeso  ba  yasa  tace  kai  dole  na  kuma   kafin  tadawo  ,, haiyacin  ta  nan  tasake  yowa  kanta  



Tabawa   yau  takasa  tsaye  takasa  zaune  ,,   neman  hanya  takeyi  wadda  zata  lalata  auren  husna  da  Alhaji  ,, sar  zarya  takeyi  ,, cen  sai  ta  tuna  da  kawar  ta  ladi  ,da  sauri  ta  dauko  wayar  ta  ta  danna  wa  ladi  kira  


Jin  andaga  tace  hello  ladi  , kina  ina  

Daga  cen  bangaren  ladi  tace  ,, ina  gidana   kinsan  bana  zuwa   gidan  wani  daiko  ,,da  kike  tambaya  ta  dan  uwarki  tana  kwashewa  da  dariya   


Tabawa tace  bafa  nason  shirme kefa  shegiya  ce  yar  iska  , kawai  mutum  yana  nemanki  ,, da  samu  sai  kike  ,mishi  dabanci  to nafiki  daba  ,, ke  kanki  kisanni  ,  banida  mutumci  "    wllh  kinsanni  kamar  yunwar  cikinki  


Dariya  ladi  tasake  shekewa  da  ita  tace  kai  , tabawa  ni  kuwa  nasan  bakida  mutum  ci  yar  iska  tambadaddiya  , to  yanzu  dai  fesomin  nasan  kin  gumtso  to  bani  ,nasha  



Taabwa  tace  hmm  bari  kedai  kawata  , ina  yayata  talatu  kin  ganeta  ok


Ladi tace  eh  nasan  talatu  kanwar  shedan  


Tabawa  tace  ke  bana  son  wannan  haukan  maganar  taki   


Farr  ladi  tayi  da  ido  tana  fadin  to  naji  bani  nasha 


Tabawa  tace    to  ai  kinsan  akwai  niyar  kishiyar  ta  da  take  wajanta  ,, to  infada  miki   yarinyar  shegiya  tasamu  wani  shaharerren  mai  kudi  ,, kinsan  alhaji  abdallah  mai  dala  


Dafe  kirji  tabawa  tayi  tana  zare  ido  tace  Alhaji  abdallah  mai  dala  ,, shine  zata  aura  wannan  fa  kaf  garin  kaduna  ,  ba  mai  kudinsa  matar  shi  ta  rasu  wajan  haihuwa  ,, bai  kuma  aure  ba  shine  ya  mak'alewa  yarinya  yar  shekara  shabiyar  zuwa  sha  shida  ,, lalle  mutumin  " nan  mayye  ne  ,,toke  ai  harka  tabude  muku  tabawa  sai  kusha  bushasha   da  nerori  


Tsoki  tabawa  tayi  ,tace  kefa  gara  ce  ana  nuna  miki  annabi s.a.w. kina  rintse  ido 


Ladi  tace  ahayye  cass  ai  baki  nuna  min  ba  tasss  zan  bude  ido  na  ras  na  kalla  ,, kedai  fadi  , wani  abun  ki  fadi  abin  yake  xuciyarki   malama  ehee



Tabawa  tace  yauwa  ,,Ladi  to  so  nakeyi  a  bata  auren   nan tsakanin  husna  da   Alhaji  , yadawo  kan  ya'ta  babbar kinga  da  arziki  agarin wasu  gyara  a  garinku  anan  dinma  agidan  a  nan  ma  a  dakinku  ,, kinga  zamufi   bancaka  da  dukiya  idan  ya  auri  rukayya  ,, ko  ya  kikace,   kuma  naga  kamar  talatu  ta  rage  bata  aiyuka  saboda  yanzu  tana  kawo  mata kudi 


Ladi tace  gaskiya  kinyi  tunani  mai  kyau  kibari  bayan  kwana biyu  zamuje,,wajan  boka  mai  gobe  da  nisa  ,, shi zai  miki  aiki  kamar  yankan  wuka  


Murmishi  tabawa  tayi  tace  ,yauwa  kawata  nagode  sosai  sainaji  ki  nan  sukayi  sallama   


Dariyar mugunta  tabawa  tayi  tace  , haba  yaya  talatu  ai  wannan  jin  dadi  baki  isaba  kice  ke  zakisha  bazai  yiyuba  ,, dole  na  raba  husna da  Alhaji  ,, yadawo  suriki  na  nan  tamike  tayi  kitchen  



  Lamarin su  aljana safiya  da  macijiya  ko  abun  ba  sauki  sosai  ake  gwabzawa     amma  ba  wanda  yaci  nasarar  kashe  wani  daga  cikinsu  

Ita  kuma  safiya  karfi  take  karawa  daga  taji  ta  soma  gajiya  zata  murza  zoben  hannun  ta  , nan  da  nan  karfi  zaizo  mata  , dan  yau  ko  mai  za'a  yi  saita  kashe  ,  macijiyar  nan  ,dan  haka  sukaci  gaba  da  fafatawa  ,  ba  k'akkautawa  ,   sosai  ake  yaki  


Macijiyar  takai wa  tafiya  sara  aikuwa  tasameta  a  kafada  , 


Da sauri  safiya  ta  ambaci  sunan  ya  rabbi  , nan  ta  sari  macijiyar  da  karamar  wukar  ta  ,ai  da  sauri  ta  sakin  mata  kafadar  ,  ta  koma  nesa  da  ita  " duba  wajan  safiya tayi  taga  dafin  nason  shigar  bata  jiki  ,  da  sauri  ta  saita  hannun  , ta  mai  zoben  ta  tana  kaiwa  wajan  , sarar  da  ta  mata  , zoben  yayi  haske  sosai  ,, wani  zafi  safiya  taji amma  ta  daure  ,  tana  kara  jumke  hannun  " sai  cen  tukun  wajan  yadena  ciwo  dauke  hannun  ta  tayi  tana  kallon  wajan  nan  taga  kamar  , ba'ayi  wani  ciwo  a  wajan  ba   hamdala  tayi  tana  gyara  tsayuwar  ta 


Macijiya  ko  ganin  safiya  bata  ganinta  , yasa  ta  lallaba  tana  tafiya  a  hankali  tana karasowa  wajan      safiya  


Duk  abinda  takeyi  , safiya  na  lura  da  ita  ,,ganin  ta  daga  baki  zata  sareta  ta  sauri  ta  juya  tana  sakar  mata  ruwan  kibiyoyi  ,  har  biyar  abakin  ta  ta  shiga  cikinta  



Wani kara  tayi  tana  wuliliya  a  kasa  saboda  azabar  data  hadiya  da  sauri  aljana  safiya  tasake  , d'ana  kwari da  bakar  ta  ,  guda  biyu  saitawa  tayi  saidai  wannan  bakin  abun  da  take  ganin nan  ne  makasar  ta  ta  saki  kibiyar  ,  a wajan  

Ai  kuwa  nan  take ta  saki  wani  kara  wanda  duk  dajin  saida  ya  amsa  kowar  wannan  karan  nata  ,, nan  ta  zube  kasa   sharaf  ta  mutu  


Ajiyar zuciya  tasauke  tana  tasbihi  ga  Allah  daya  taimaketa  , ta  kashe  wannan  muguwar  macijiyar    tace  Alhamdulillah  ,,  nasan  babana  kai  ka  tayani  wannan  yakin  da  karfin  addu'ar  ka  ,  


Durkusa  tayi  inda  macijiyar  take  ,, tana  al'ajabin  girma  da  tsayi  irin  na  ma macijiyar  nan  ,  ta  kobinta  ta  zare  ta  farke  cikin  macijiyar    


Kibiyoyinta  , ta  dauko  ta  wankesu  ,, tace  bazan  bar  kayana  ba  wannan  sunada  mahimmanci  , nan  ta  nufi  tantin  ta   

Kayan  ta  ta  hada  , ruwa  ta  dauka  tayi  alwala  nan  ta  tada  sallar  azuhar  ,   bayan  tagama  nan  ta  hada  komai  nata  ,  tayi  kara  gaba 

Tazo  gab  da  fita  daga  wannan  dajin  sai  hanyar  tarabu  biyu  da  sauri  ,, tadauko  littafin  ganin  hanya  taduba  , nan  kasa  gane  ina  zatabi  


Zobenta  ta  murza  ta  rufe  idonta  tana  son  tambayar  babanta  inda  zatabi  


Abban  safiya  na  zaune  yana  tofawa  aliyu  addu'a  yaji   zuciyar  ta  buga  sau  uku  , dafewa  yayi  yace  ,  safiya  ", toko  takasa  gane  yan  tagwayen  hanya  ne  ,, nan  ya  shima  ya  murza  zoben  dake  hannun  shi  irin  na  safiya  ,, 



Ajiyar zuciya  tasauke  kafin  tace  abbana  wacce  hanya  zanbi    nakasa  ganewa  


Abba  ya  nuna  mata  inda  zatabi  nan  ta  bude  idonta  , kallon  inda  yafada  mata  tayi ,, nan  ta  kalli  tsuntsuwar  ta  suka  kama  hanya  sukaci  gaba  da  tafiya  


Husna  da  Alhaji  ta  soyayya  suke  sosai  , , ga  yaran  alhaji duk  week  shesunzo  ,,  wajanta  sosai take  jin  dadi  intaga  yaran  ta  saba  dasu  ,,, saidai  har  yanzu  babu  abinda  ya  cenja  da  aikin gidansu  ,, sosai  talatu  take  azabtar  da  ita  komai  na  gida  ita  keyi  ,, koda  wani  lokaci  zaka  kai  a  school  ko  islamiya  tofa  ,,babu  abinda  talatu  zatayi   harsai  tazo  zatayi  girki   shara  da sauran  aiyuka  


Baba  bashida  damar  magana  dan  yanzu  talatu  ta  maidashi  mijin  tace  ta  asirceshi  ,, ta  rabashi  da  kowa   amma  takasa  shiga  tsakanin  shi  da  husna  ,, tayi  maganin  harta  gaji  tanason  ta  raba  soyayyar  dake  tsakanin  uba  da  yarsa  ,,amma  kuma  Allah  bai  bata  damar  hakan  ,ba  " ganin  ta  rabashi  da  kowa  yasa  ta  hakura  da  rabashi  da  husna   


Iyayen alhaji abdallah  sunzo  ,, wajan  baba  ansaka  ranar  bikin  8 months    aka  saka  bayan  husna  tagama exam  za'ayi  bikin  


Su  talatu  ko  sai  rawar  kai  akeyi  ana  wani  yauki  ita  ga  yarta  zata  auri  mai kudi  , ,,ko  gidan  biki  taje  bata   kula  talakawa  , tana  bude  hanci  ,, mutanen  anguwar  ko  sunsan  hakin  talatu  batada  kirki  shiyasa  basa  shiga  harkar  ta  



Husna  ko  sosai  take  farin ciki  ta  matse  tabar  gidan  su  saboda  tsananin  da  take  fuskanta  


Washe  gari   tabawa  da  sassafe  , ta,  nufi  gidan  ladi  ,tana  zuwa  tasamu  harta  shirya  " nan  suka  kama  hanyar  wani  kauye   , bayan  sun  isa  cikin  daji  suka  kutsa  ,  nan  sukayi  wata  bishiya  babba  ,, tin  kafin  suzo  ladi tace  , su  cire  ta  kalmin  su  ,,nan  suka  cire   suka  nufi     bishiyar 



Wani  mutum  ne   mai  tsayi  ya  rufe  jikinsa  da  wata  fata  ,, tin  kafin  su  karaso  yace  nasan  maike  tafe  daku  ,, tsinannu  


Da sauri  ladi  tace  nagaishe  da  boka  mai  gobe  da  nisa  ,, bokanmu  mai  share  kukanmu  

Wata  mahaukaciyar dariya  yayi  ,, yace  wannan  haka  yake  sahiba  ta  ,, amma  kusake  min  bayani

Wata  kwarya  yadauko  da  ruwa  , nan  yayi  wasu  kalaman  tsafi  saiga  husna  ta  bayyana  akan   darduma  ,, tana  karatun  alqur"ani  mai girma  , bokan  ya  daga  kanshi  yace  leko  kigani  


Lekowa  ,, sukayi  nan  sukaga  husna  na  karanta  alqur"ani
