Da  mamaki  aljana  safiya  take  kallon  abin  suka  zagayeta  sai  kallon  su  take  daya  bayan  daya  dan  ita  abin  ma  har  tama  rasa  mai  zata  ce  dan  al'ajabi  ,gyara  tsayuwar  ta  tayi  tace  yaku  wannan  mugayen  dodani  kuyi  sani  cewa  shi  wanda   ya  turokun  maiyasa  baizo  da  kanshi  bane  ya  turoku  kodan  kuma  irin  halinsa  agareku  aduniyar  ku  ta  dodanni  duk  abinda  kukeyi  ina  sane  ko  anfada  muku  cewa  ni  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  mai  tsoro  ce to  bana  tsoron  kowa  a  duniyar  nan  sai ubangiji  na  wanda  ya  halicceni  ya  halicceku    ku  kusan  daga  jin  sunana  ma  kunsan  niba  sa'ar  yaro  bace  dan  haka  ku  facemin  anan  tin  kafin  nayi  yagal  gayal  da  sassan  jikinku  ba  takai  karshen maganar  tana  zare  takobin  ta  karama  daga  k'ugunta 

Wani  gurnani  sukayi  suna  bude  kofar  hanci  
Wani  daga  cikinsu  yace  yake  aljana safiya  kisani  cewa  mu  dama  kin takura  mana  kin  hanamu  jin  dadi  kin  hanamu  cin  naman mutane  sai  gashi  anturo  mu  mukashe  ki  dan  haka  yau  saikin  yabawa  aya  zakinta  

Wata  magigiciyar dariya  aljana safiya  tayi  nan  da  nan  daji  ya  amsa  sautin muryar  ta  tasoma  magana  da  kakkausar  murya   wadda  duk  mai  sauraron  muryar   saiya  gigice  koya  kurumce  
Aljana safiya tace  ni  ai  bana  zalinci  kuma  na  tsani  azzalumi  da  munafiki  da  maci  amana  idan  kuka  yimin  taurin  kai  to  zakuga  yadda  karshenku  zai  kasance  a  dajin  nan  saina  gama  daku  tukun  nadawo  kan  uban  gidan  naku  da  yake  baku  umarni  kuna  binsa  kamar  jakuna  

Jin  haka  babban  cikinsu  yace  karya  kikeyi  ke  baki  isa  ki  kashe  muba  saidai  mu  kashe ki  muma  aljanun  dodanni  ne  ba  irin  dodannin  daji  da  kika  sani  bane  ko  bacewa  kikayi  to  muma  zamu  bace  yau  karyar  ki  takare  sai  mukaiwa  shugaba  sarki shamsu  ke  a  daure  ya  kasheki  musha  jininki  mu  cinye  daman  ki  


Murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  to  a  gwabza  mugani  nidaku  wanda  zaiyi  nasara  

Babban  dodon  saida  ya  dab'a  kirjinsa  yana  nuna  shi  mai  karfi  ne  yace  hahaha  lalle  kin  kawo  kanki  makasa   maza  ku  afka  mata  aikuwa  kamar jira  sukeyi  suka  nufi  inda  aljana safiya  take

Ita ko  safiya  kallon  tsuntsuwar  ta  tayi  tana  mata  murmishi  tace  masoyiya  kiyi  kallo  ki  more  abinki  

Fiffiken  ta tsuntsuwar  ta  daga  alamar taji  dadi  

Dawo da dubanta tayi   akansu    tana  kallon  yadda  suke  gudu  suna  son  karasowa  gareta  ganin  kafin  suzo  gareta  da  yar  tazara  yasa  ta  rintse  ido  da  sauri  
Zoben  hannun ta  ta  murza  tana  fadin  babana  kana  kusa  ka  ban  sirrin  dodannin  nan  mai  kasani  akansu  

Abban  nata    yana  zaune  yana  addu'a  yaji  saitin  zuciyar  yabuga  da  karfi  mikewa  yayi  yace  safiya  yanzu  Allah  yasa  dai  lfy  
Shima  rintse ido  yayi  yaji  abin  ta  fada  miki  ,murmishi  yayi  

Aljana safiya  na  sauraron  shi  

Abban nata  yace  safiya  wannan  dodannin  daga  kasar  masar  suke  sarki  shamsu  yaturo  miki  su  dodannin  baa  kashesu  da sauri  saboda  makasarsu  , tana  da  wahala 
Su  wannan  akwai  inda  zaki  yankesu  su  mutu  ,wajan  shine  
Idan  sukazo  kusa  dake  ki  duba  tsakiyar  kansu  zakiga  wani  shatin  baki  a  kansu  to  nan  shine  makasar  su  ko  kuma  kiduba  cinyarsu  zakiga  wani  bakin  shima  a  jikin  cinyarsu  to  nan  ma  makasar  sune   kiyi sauri  yarinya  ta  nasara  takice  da  izinin  Allah  

Da sauri  aljana safiya  tabude  idonta  tana  kallon  su  baifi  takun  kafa  goma  su  karaso  inda  take ba kallo  take  kare  musu  daya  bayan  daya  kuma  taga  duk  abinda  babanta  ya  fada   murmishi  tayi  tace yau  karyarku  da  zalincin  ku  yazo  karshe  nan  ta  ciro  kibiyar  ta  ta  d'ana  a  jikin  bakar  tana  saita  na  gabar  satin cinyarshi  take  kaiwa  hari  

Suna  kara  kusanto  ta  ta  sakarwa  na  gabar  
Faduwa  yayi  yasaki  wani  firgitaccen  kara  nan  ya  mutu 

Turus sukayi sauran  ganin  an  kashe  babban  nasu   kowa  yakasa  motsawa  ba  wanda  yakara  yunk'urin isaga  aljana safiya  


Murmishin  samun  nasara  aljana safiya  tayi  tace  kuzo  mana  aina  fada  muku  ba'a  ja  da  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞   idan  kunyi  muso  to  mai  karar  kwana  yazo  


Daya  daga  cikin  su  yacika  da  bakin ciki nan  ya  nufeta  da  mugun  gudu  

Aljana safiya  dama  ta  d'ana  kwari da bakar  ta  kawai  jira  take  aikuwa  tana  ganin   wani  ya  nufota  cikin  zafin  nama  ta  daga  kwari da bakar  da  saita  tsakiyar  kanshi  ganin  zai  goce  mata  da  sauri  ta  sakar  masa  

Wani  ihu  ihu  yayi  kafin  yafadi  kasa  ji  kake  tim  sai  yaringa  juye  juye  tukun  ya  mutu  


Dariya  aljana safiya  tayi  wadda  yasake  karade  dajin  tace  akwai  wanda  yake  bukatar  zuwa  barzagu  nan  da  minti  daya  


Shuru  sukayi  suna  tsoron  akashesu  , basu  gaji  da  duniya  ba  wasuma  da  matansu  dan  haka  sukayi  cirko  cirko  ba  wanda  yayi  yunkurin  zuwa  ga  aljana  safiya  


Itama  aljana safiya  tsayawa  tayi  tana  kallon  su  ,tace  yaufa  tinda  kuka  shigo  gonata  to  kun  sayi  mutuwa  da  kanku  dan  bana  sassauci  bana  yafiya  bana uziri bana  jin  roko  ga  irin  azzalumai  masu  zuciyar  fir'auna   ku  gamasu  son  kasheni   ku  ji  kukeyi  bansan  kune  sarki shamsu  ya  tura  kuka  sakawa  aliyu  na  cuta  ba 

Ko  ance  muku  ban  san  kune  kuka  kwashi  bakaken  aljanun  kuka  kaisu  nahiyar mu  suka  kashe  min  rabin  yakina  ba 
Duk  kun  dauka  bansan  komai  ba  to  kunyi  kuskure  tinda  kuka  shigo  hannuna  dama  kobaku  zoba  nizan  nemeku  na  kasheku  azzalumai  
Sai  gashi  kunzo  da  kanku  kun  kawo  kanku  da  kanku  gareni  Alhamdulillah  nagodewa  Allah  dan  yau  zan  kashe  makiya  na  makiyan  Allah  

Kuma  ko  kwana  zamuyi  a  haka  nidaku  saidai  mu  kwana  dan  babu  wanda  zai  tsira  daga  farko  na ni  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  sainaga  bayanku   nan  ta  zauna  tana  kara  samun  kwarin  gwiwa  ko  kwana  zasuyi  to  saidai  su  kwana  dan  saitaga  bayansu  



Husna  na  bacci  a  dakin  umma   cen  cikin baccin  tayi  mafarki  headmaster  ya  kamata  yana  kissing  din  ta  tako  ina  tanason kwacewa  amma  ta  kasa  da  karfi  tace  a  a  sir  

A firgice ta  tace  tana  bude idon ta  ganinta  akan  gado  sai  waige  waige  takeyi   ji  tayi  kamar  ana  shafa  bayanta  ai  da  gudu   hantsilo  daga  gadon  tana  fadin waiyo Allah  babana  gashi  nan  zaimin  fady  ta  fito  daga  dakin  tana  kurma  ihun  gashi  nan  


Umma  da  alhaji  abdallah  suna  zaune  , suna  hira  zuwansa  kenan  gidan  yazo  cin  abinci yana  jiran  a  dauko  mishi  yaran  shi  a  school  suci  abinci   sukaji  ihun  husna tana  taka  stam  biyu  biyu  saboda  tsananin  birgita  data  yi  

Da sauri  Alhaji abdallah  yamike  yana fadin  subhanallah  umma  kalli  husna. Itama  umma  mikewa tayi  tana  tarar  husna  tace  ya'ta  lfy  maiya faru ? 


Husna  na  saukowa  da  gudu  ta  shige  jikin  umma  tana fadin  gashi  nan  zaimin fady  yana  kissing  dina  waiyo Allah  babana  

Rungume ta umma  tayi  tace  kiyi  shuru husna  babu  abinda  zai  miki  kina  nan  tare  damu  babu  wanda  zai  tabaki kinji.


Husna  bata  dena  ihun  a  taimaketa  ba  tana  kuka 

Alhaji abdallah  da  damuwa  ta  bayyana  akan  fuskar  shi  ya  matso  kusansu  yace  husna ta  kiyi  hakuri  kinji  ina  tare  dake  bamai  cutar  dake  insha Allah  kinji  husna ta 

Husna najin maganar  Alhaji abdallah da sauri  ta  saki  umma  ta  shige jikin alhaji abdallah ta  rungume shi 


Shima alhaji abdallah babu  yadda  ya  iya  saiya  rungume  ta  yana  mai  jin  kunyar  mahaifiyar  shi   dan  yana  da  kunya  sosai. 

Murmishi umma tayi tace  abdallah  ka  rarrashe ta  tayi  shuru  ka  kuma  tofa  mata  addu'a  naga  har yanzu abinda  yafaru  bata  manta  ba  da  alama  ma  farkin  abun  ne  tayi  shiyasa  ta  fito  aguje  

Alhaji abdallah ya  dago husna  yace  husna ta  bude  idonki  kiganni  nine  ba  wani  bane. Kara rungume  shi  tayi  tana  girgiza kai  tama  kasa  magana  don  tsoro 

Ganin bazatayi  magana  ba  alhaji abdallah ya  jasu  kan kujera  suka zauna

Ita dai husna  tayi  lamo  ajikin sa  sau  sauke  ajiyar zuciya  takeyi. Alhaji abdallah  kuwa  addu'a  yake  mata  yana  fatan  tadawo  hayyacinta  tadena  kwanciya  ajikinsa  saboda  yana  cutuwa ba  kadan  ba    yadda  husna  ke  manna  mata  na  fulanin  ta  sai  yaji  kamar  yake  shafata  amma  sanin  bata  zama  halas  dinsa  ba  yake  kiyaye   dokar  Allah  

Suna  a  haka  sukaji  sallamar  yaran   suna  shigowa  da  gudu, ai  ihun  murna  suka  kwasa  ganin  husna  ajikin  baban  nasu  

da gudu sukayo  kansu  husna  mimee tace  mommy  oyoyo  ga  mommy  tadawo  gidanmu  nan suka  k'arasa  suka  rungume  husna  da baban nasu 

Murmishi  alhaji  yayi yace  yaran  albarka  kunxo ? Yusuf  ne  yace  eh  daddy  ance  zamuyi  exams  nan  da  week  

Alhaji abdallah yace  to  masha Allah   kuje  ayimuku  wanka  daiku  dawo 

Mustafa yace  daddy maiya  samu  mommy mu  naga hawaye  a  fuskar  ta  

Husna  ta bude  idonta  ganin  yaran  tayi  agabanta  da  sauri  ta  mike  ta  sauka  akan  alhaji  durkusawa  tayi  tana  fadin  oyoyo  yarana tana murmishi. Da sauri  suka  rungume ta  suka  dariya  itama  dariyar  take  ta  biye  musu  

Umma ko  baki  ta  kama  tace  a  ikon  Allah  abdallah  kaga  yarinyar  nan  da  tadamemu  da rigima  kalli  dasu  mimee  sukazo  shikenan   tadena 

Yar dariya alhaji  abdallah  yayi  yace  ai  husna  tana  son  yaran  nan  shiyasa  nake  kaunar ta  

Umma tace  gaskiya  dai  wanda  yaso  danka  ka  kaunace  shi. Alhaji  yace  hakane  umma  


Husna  ta  dagosu  tace  yauwa  muje  namuku  wanka  sai  kuci  abinci.  Mimee  tace  yee  mommy  nizaki  farawa  ko, yusuf  yace  a  a  nidai  zatawa  bake  ba   mustafa yace  dukkan ku  narigaku  , ai  nan  suka  kama  muso  a  tsakanin  su  mimee  harta  fara  kuka 

Husna tace shut  mana  dukkanku  zan  muku  muje  inane  dakinku  

Dena  muson  sukayi  mustafa  yace  mommy  dakinmu  a  sama  yake  muje ,mikewa  husna tayi  tace  to  muje  ko  ,nan  sukayi sama 

Dariya umma  tayi  tace  gaskiya  abdallah  yarinyar  nan  nada  hankali  kawai  dan  a  tsorace  takene  shiyasa  

Daddy  yace  sosai makuwa  wllh  umma  kinga  yadda  take  girmama ni  abin  yana  burgeni 

Umma tace  idan  baban husna  yadawo  zan  tura  kawunka   wajansa  ya  rage  lokacin  auren  nan  gara  tayi  exams din  a  gidan  ta  

Alhaji abdallah  yace  kai  amma  sanaji  dadi  sosai  wllh  umma  

Umma tace  insha Allah  haka  zaayi  , nan  sukaci  gaba  da  hirarsu  suna  jiran  su  husna  su  fito 



Bangaren aljana safiya  ko  har  yanzu dodannin  nan  ba  Wanda  yayi  yunk'urin  zuwa  wajan  ta 


Cen  wani  acikinsu  yace  ku  wannan  fa  kurine  take  mana kawai  mu  afka  mata  ,gaba daya  shawarar  shi  suka  dauka  nan  suka  nufota  da  gudu  

Aljana safiya  na  ganinsu  gaba daya  tace  ya  Allah  kabani  mafita  naci  galaba  akan  makiya  na  ,kibiya  wajan  goma  ta  d'ana  tana  saita  ta   tasakar  musu  

Aikuwa  ko  wacce  saida  tasame  su  nan  suka  zube  a  kasa  suka  mutu. Sauran  wajanta  sukayo  ita  kuwa  tana  sakar  musu  ruwan kibiyoyi  idan  suka  kusantota  saita  bace  ta  kara  tazara  dasu 

Sosai  take  kashesu  dena  harbinsu  tayi  ta  soma  musu  ruwan  wukake  kanana   tana  wulla  musu  saboda  dafin  wukar  duk  inda  ta  samesu  sai  wajan  ya  d'aye  kamar  wuta  

Bayyana tayi  a  kusa dasu  takobin  a  hannun ta  nan  suka  rufu  akanta  aka  kama  yaki  baji  bagani  

Aljana safiya  na  kasarsu  itama  saita  rikide  tazama  wani  shirgegen  zaki  wanda  yafi  dodannin  girma  nan  taringa  yagasu  tana  kashesu  

Yaki  ake  fafatawa  a  tsakanin  su  bakajin  komai  sai  kururuwar  dodannin  nan  wanda  ake  kashewa.
An  shafe  fiye  a  awa biyu  ana  yaki  tsakanin aljana safiya  da  dodannin  nan  
Duk ta  kashe  su  sai  kadan  wanda  suma  sun  jigata  

Ganin  sunyi laushi  aljana safiya  tayi saurin  zaro  kibiyar ta  ta  sakar  musu  ,nan  take  suka  mutu .
Ajiyar zuciya  tasauke  ganin  ta  kashesu  

Tsuntsuwar ta  ta  tashi  tana  nufota  samar  kan aljana safiya  tazo  tana  shawayi  tana  zagayeta  

Murmishi safiya tayi  tace  munyi  nasara   masoyiya  yanzu  muje  gaba  dajin nan  bana  zama  bane. Nan  tsuntsuwar  tayo kasa  aljana  safiya  taho  suka  lula  sararin  samaniya sukaci  gaba  da  tafiya  gudu  sosai  tsuntsuwar  keyi  



Tabawa  ce  zaune  sai  sak'a da  warwara  takeyi  tana  karanta  wasikar  jaki  , mikewa  tayi  tace ina  sam  bazai  yiyuba  dole  mukoma  gidan  boka  mai gobe  da nisa  mayafinta  ta  yafa  tafice  agidan , gidan  ladi  ta  nufa  tana  zuwa  ta  sameta  a  falo  tana  zuk'ar  shisha 

Bako sallama  tashigo   

Hararar  ta  ladi  tayi  tace  kefa  garace  tabawa  zakizo  gidana   bako  sanarwa 

Zama tabawa  tayi  tace  ai  dole  ki  ganni  nifa  na  kasa hakuri  har  lokacin  auren  nan  kawai  mukoma  gidan  boka  mai  gobe da nisa   musake  jin  ko  akwai  mafita 


Ladi tace  to  shikenan  bari  na  shiga  daki  na fito nan tayi dakinta  ,tana  zuwa   ta  dauko mayafinta  tafito  nan  suka  kama  hanyar  gidan  boka  

Suna  zuwa  boka  mai gobe da nisa ya  kece  da dariya   yana fadin  nasan  me kuka  dawo  yi  

Da sauri  suka  zube  agabanshi  suna  kadade  kayi  k'arko  boka  mai  gobe  da  nisa  

Sake dariyar  yayi  yace  to  yanzu  nesa  tazo  kusa  bayan  Kwana  biyu  za'a  rage  lokacin  auren  husna  itada  alhaji  abdallah mai dala  dan  haka  zamu  baki  magani  wanda  zaki  zubawa  husna  a  cikin  abinci  idan  har  taci  itada  kanta  zatace  batason  alhaji  


Shi  kuma  alhaji  zaa  zuba  mishi  a  ruwa  idan  yasha  shima  ,zai  tsani  husna  yaji  yarki  yakeso  

Cike da farin ciki  tabawa  tace  godiya  nake  boka mai gobe da nisa  nawa  zan  biya  kudin  magani 

Boka yace  bama  amsar  kudi  harsai  aikinmu  yaci  dan  haka  kije  ciki  za'a  miki  wanka  na  biyu  

Da sauri  tabawa  tace  to  boka  tana  mikewa  tanufi  dakin 

Kallon ladi  boka  yayi  yace  wannan  matar  gaskiya  ta  hada  komai  ranar  na  kwashi  ganima  a  jikinta   har  mafarkinta  nakeyi  bari  naje  na  kashe  k'ishirwa  na   dama  a  matse  nake 

Dariyar  yan  bariki  ladi  tayi  tace  aikinka  na  kyau  bokana  kaje  kaji  dadi  kayi  duk abinda  kakeso  

Dakin  yanufa kamar  kullin  dakin duhu  gareshi   da  alube  ya  isaga  tabawa   dan  yanzu  bataji  tsoro  irin  na  farko data  shigo  , yanzu  bata  damuba  ko  mai  zataji  yau zata  daure  

Boka ya  afka  mata   abinda  yafi  son  a  jikin  tabawa  na  fulanin ta shi  mamaki  ma  yake  wai  tana  da  budurwa  amma  gata  nan  cass  da  ita  ,, saida  dirjeta  son  ranshi  ,yau  yafi  dadewa  sai  ihu  yake  

Tabawa  ma  yau  dadin  abinda  ake  mata  takeji  sai  ta  rufe  bakinta  tana  jin farin ciki  gurinta  zai cika   akan  husna  


Saida  suka shafe  awa daya  tukun boka  yasamu  nutsuwa  ya  tashi  a  kanta  yayi  saurin  komawa  inda  ladi  take  , dariya  ladi  tayi  tace  kana  wuta  bokana  yanaji  yau  kun  dade  dayawa  , kaji  komai  zamzam  ko  

Shima dariyar  yayi  yace  gaskiya  ne  ladi  tawa  kin  burgeni  ai  irin  wannan  zakike  kawomin  na  biya  musu  bukata  suma  su  biyamin  tawa  bukatar  

Murmishi ladi  tayi  tace  zanci  gaba  da  kawo  maka  irin  su 

Tabawa na  fitowa  ranta  fas  ta  karaso  inda  suke  tace  sungama  boka  abamu  maganin 

Dariyar  bakida matsala  yayi  yace  to  gashi  namiki  bayanin  komai  

Tabawa tayi godiya  nan  suka  tafi  gida

*21-22*



Haka  tabawa  take  zuwa  gidan  talatu  ,tana  fada  mata  karya  da  gaskiya   nanar  da  tazo  bata  jima  ba  husna  ta  kawowa  talatu  kudin  da  alhaji yabata  a  ba  talatu  saboda  yanzu  shiyake  dauko  ta  a  school  koda  yaushe  yace  karta  kuma  hawa  motar  haya  tana  matsayin  matar  dazai  aura   sosai  husna  taji  dadi  itada  sailuba  yake  kawosu  


Bayan  tafita  tabawa  ta  kalli  talatu  tace  yaya  talatu  dama  nazo  miki  da  wata  shawara  ne  kozaki yadda  bansani  

Talatu  ta  kalli  tabawa  tace  inajinki  fadamin 

Tabawa  tace  yanzu  kina  ganin  idan  husna  ta  auri  Alhaji  zakici  dukiya  kuwa  bayan  sunyi  aure  

Talatu  tace  sosai makuwa  ai  husna  na  tsorona  dama  ubanta  ne  yake  shigar  mata  yanzu  kuma  sai  yadda  nayi  dashi  kinga  kuwa  dole  tayimin  duk  abinda  nakeso  koda  tayi  aure  bazai  hanani  zane  yar  iska  ba  idan  tayimin  lefi  

Tabawa  da  tacika  da  bakin ciki  saita  danne  tace  to  yanzu  yaya  ni  a  ganina  gyara  mu   lalata  wannan  auren  yadawo  kan  rukayya  kinga  ,, zamufi  yin  bushasha  da  naira  koya  kikace  kinga  idan  kaka  taga  husna  ts  auri  alhaji  to   itama  fa  zatace  sai  anbata  nata  rabon  amma  in  rukayya  ce  babu  abinda  zamu  bata  idan  aka  kawo  

Talatu ko  bankawa  tabawa  harara  tayi  tana  , bude hanci  tace  amma  tabawa  baki  hankali  inbanda  ke  kidahuma  ce  taya  kike  tunanin  alhaji zai  fasa  auren  husna  ya  auri  yarki  rukayya  da  bata  son  mutane  su  rabeta  to  bari  kiji  infada  miki  inma  baki  saniba  kisani  alhaji   yaranshi  uku  maza  biyu  mace  daya 
Saboda  su  yakiyin  aure  bayan  mutuwar  matarshi  dan  kar  yakawo  mata  mai  mugun  hali irin  rukayya  wadda  zata  azabar  mishi  da  yara  , kina  ganin  rukayya  zata  iya  rike  yaran  wani  har  uku  take  musu  girki  wanka  ta  shiryasu  suje  school  kinsan  rukayya  ko  daya  daga  cikin  wannan  abubuwan  bazata  iya  ba   yarinyar  dako  gyaran  shimfidar  gado  mata  iyaba  girki  mata  iyaba  bare  shara  itace  kika  tunanin  akai  gidan  alhaji  abdallah  mai dala  a  matsayin  matarshi  kema  kisan  bazai  yiyuba  
Kuma  duk  abinda  mukace  ta  dauko  takawo  mana  wllh  ko  rantsuwa  nayi  bazanyi  kaffara  ba  bazata  dauko  ba  ko  gidan muke  zuwa   wata  rana  cewa  zatayi  muntakura  mata  to  kima  cenja  tunanin ki  akan  wannan  auren   husna  itace  mai  jin  tsoron  mu  duk  abinda  kikeso  zatayi  kuma  zatake  kula  mishi  da  yaranshi  kinga  ta  haka  zamuke  samun  kudi  daga  gareta  

Amma  kin  wani  ce  wai  rukayya  to  kibari  wata  auren  kiga  mezatayi  sakinsha  mamaki  wllh  


Wani  abu  n  ya  tokarewa  tabawa  kirji  saboda  jin  ana  kushe  diyar  ta  amma  ba  komai , cikin  zuciyar  ta  tace  na  rantse  ko  zanyi  yawo  tsirara  bazan  bar  husna  taji  dadi  a  duniya  ba  dole  rukayya  ta  auri  Alhaji  kota  halin  yaya  ne  ni  har  a  nunamin  duniyanci  hmmm  yaya  talatu  bakisan  tuggu  nabane  shiyasa  duk  makircinki  na  dameki  na  shanye   saina  wulakanta  rayuwar  husna  indai  akan  kudi  ne  babu  abinda  bazan  yiba   ga  boka  mai gobe da nisa  yacemin  bayan  auren  su  zai  rabasu  dan  haka  zakiga  yadda  ake  tijara  wllh  

Talatu ko  ganin  tabawa  tayi  shuru  kamar  mai  tunani  saita  taba  ta  tace  ke  mahaukaciya  tunanin  mai  kike  yi 



Da sauri  tabawa  tadawo  daga  duniyar  tunanin  ta  tace  ba  komai  yaya  ai  hakan  ma  yayi  husna  zatafi  mana  biyayya  akan  rukayya  wannan  bakin  an  dauki  lokaci  mai  tsayi  gaskiya  ina  lefin  wata  uku  ko  hudu  amma  har  takwas  wajan  shekara  guda  baki  tunanin  kar  yaje  ya  shafe  ta  kafin  auren  yazo  kilama  yamata  ciki  kinga  in  hakan  tafaru  akwai  matsala  


Talatu  tace  babu  abinda  zai  mata  ai  husna  tsoro  gareta  , kuma naji  babanta  yana  fadin  karta  kuskure  koda  hannun  ta  ne  namiji  yakama  kinko  babu  wata  matsala  


Tabawa  tacika  tayi  fam  ganin  duk  shawarar  data  kawo  akan  lalata  auren  nan  sai  talatu  ta  hana  cen  kuma  tace  to  shikenan  yaya  nima  banason  hakan  tafaru  bari naje  gida   idan  lokacin  bikin  yayi  samuje  wajan  boka  yabamu  maganin  da  za'a  mallake  alhaji  kinga  shikenan  komai  saimunce 


Talatu  ta  washe  baki  tace  yauwa  yar uwa ta gari  yanzu  kika  kawo  shawara  Allah  yakaimu  lokacin  nan  tabawa  ta  tafi  gida  cike  da  bakin ciki 



Lamarin  aljana safiya  tinda  tabi  wannan  hanya  take  tafiya  akan  tsuntsuwa  ta  suna  falfala  gudu  burinta  baifi  ace  takai  dajin  bishiyoyi  ba  tinda  baban ta  yace  shekara  daya  tazayi  tace  insha  kamar  wata  dayane  zanyi  nadawo

Sosai  kukan  abin  ke  tunkaro   ta   jin abun daga  bayanta  yake  tafowa  tayi saurin  juyawa  zare  ido  tayi  ganin  abun  ke  kusanto  ta  tace  n.....

[18/10, 17:56] MRS. MU'TASS: *𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧  𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞*

*25—26*



Suna  dawowa  ta  adana  maganin  tason  taji  talatu  takira  ta  tafada  mata  an  rage  lokacin  bikin  husna  da  alhaji   sai  farin ciki  takeyi  dan  bazata  yadda  samarta  ta  kubuce  mata  ba  


Lamarin  aljana  safiya    tinda  suka  lula  sararin samaniya  tsuntsuwar  ta  take   sharara  gudu. Aljana  safiya  nata  tunanin  aliyu  ta  tana  tausaya  mishi  cen  kuma  sai  tace,Allah  sarki husna  kema  ban  manta  dakeba  ina  nan  zuwa  gareki  da  zarar  nadawo  aliyu  yasamu  lfy  zanje  naga  wani  hali  kike  a  cikin gidanku  inason  nasan  tarihin  ki  dana  hajiya  amma  saina  dawo  insha Allah  komai  zai  daidai  ta  da  izinin  Allah , share  hawayen ta  tayi  sukaci  gaba  da  tafiya  tana  kallon  hanyar tafiyar  ta  a  littafin  dake  hannun  ta  shike  nuna  mata  inda  zatabi  sosai  suke  tafiya  ba  tsaya  salla  kawai  take  tsayar  dasu  


Sarki shamsu  na  zaune  a  gaban  madubin  tsafinsa  yana  yaren  tsafi  cen  kuma saita  bude  idon shi  ya  saukesu  akan  madubin tsafinsa  a razane  ya  mike  yana  ja  dabaya  saboda tsananin mamaki. Yace  kutirin  bala'i  lalle  aljana safiya  yanzu  duk  yawan  wannan  dodannin  kin  kararsu  aikuwa  bazaki  tsira  daga  wannan  tarkon dazan  d'ana  bikiba  saina  tagayyara  rayuwar  ki  kafin  kije  dajin  bishiyoyi  saina  makantar  dake  ta  yadda  zaki  dena  ganin  haske  a  idonki  ni  zaki  rena  zakisan  waye  sarki shamsu  yafice  daga  daki  fuuu  kamar  mahaukaci  haka  yanufi  dakin daya 

Yana zuwa  ya dodonsa  ransa  yayi  mugun b'aci  har  wani  cenja  hala  yake  saboda  b'acin rai  
Durkusawa  sarki shamsu  yayi  yana  fadin  kagafarceni  yakai  shugaba  na  naga  duk  abinda  yafaru  tsakanin  aljana safiya  da  dodannin  mu  yazama  dole  mudauki  mataki  akanta  kafin  yakai  ga  isa  dajin  bishiyoyi  

Wani irin  kallo  dodon  ke  mishi  sai  cen  tukun  yayi  wata  kururuwa  wadda  tabawa  sarki shamsu  tsoro  da  al'ajabi  da  mamaki  dan  bai  taba  ganin  dodon  nashiba  a  irin  wannan  yanayin  na  yanzu  da  sauri  ya  mak'ale  ajikin  bangon  dakin  jin  dakin  ya  dauki  girgiza  sai zare ido  yakeyi  yana  kallon  dodon  nashi  daya  tak'ark'are  iya  karfinsa  yake  wannan  kururwar  

Saida  aka  kwashi  mintuna  kusan  goma  tukun  dodon  yadena  wannan  kururuwar  , yana  kallon  sarki shamsu  sa  jajayen  idonshi  masu  firgitarwa  da  tsoratarwa. Sarki shamsu  yace  shugaba  tuba  nake  a min  aikin  gafara  

Kallon  dodon  yasakeyi  kafin  yace  ya  kai  sarki shamsu  shin  kasan  mainakewa  wannan  kururuwar ? Girgiza kai sarki shamsu yayi  yace  gaskiya  bansani  ba  shugabana  saika  fada  dan  bantaba  ganinka  acikin  wannan  yanayin  ba. Dodon yace  ina  takaicin  inda  wannan  yarinyar  tasamu  takobin  da  na  dade  ina  nemansu  da  zoben  dake  hannun ta  tin  ina  karami  nake  neman  wannan  abun  biyu  amma  saigashi  agun  makiyar  mu kuma  dasu  take  anfani  tana  kashe  duk  abinda  yaso  cutar  da  ita  dan haka  saina  sake  tura  mata  da  bataliyar  kada  macizai  kuraye  zakuna  duk  abinda  nasan  iya  cutar  daya  bayan  daya  saina  tura  mata  su  tukun  ni  na  afka  mata  daga  karshe  na  kasheta  na  dauke  zoben  da  takobin   nine  zan  yaketa  idan  har  abinda  na  tura  mata  suka  kasa 

Farin ciki  ne  yacika  sarki shamsu  yace  wannan  shawara  tayi  shugaba  duk yadda  kayi  dai-dai  ne  

Dodon yace  bari kagani  nasan  yanzu  ta  kusa  sauka  kasa domin  hutawa  zanyi  maganin ta  , nan  ya  shafa  gefen  bangon  yana  kallon  wajan  yace  dukkanku  na  turaku  maza  kuje  kuyi  maganin  wacce  mai  taurin  kan, cen  kuma  saiya  shafa  bangon  kafin yadawo  dubanshi  ga  sarki shamsu  dayake  kallon  shi 

Sarki shamsu  yace  nasan  angama  bari  ninaje  fada  inada  baki  yanzu  an  kawai  mutanen  goma  dakace  kanason  jini  suna  dakin  madubin tsafina  kashiga  shanye  jininsu  konawa  kakeso  zan  kara  maka  dodona  


Wata  girgiza  dodon  yayi yace  lalle  k kasance  mai  biya  min  bukata  ta  don  haka  babu  abinda  zaka  nema  karasa  daga  gare. Sarki shamsu  yayi  murmishi  yafice  daga  dakin  ya  nufi  cikin masarautar  


Husna ce  tafito da  gudu  tana dariya  mimee  ce  ta  biyo  bayan  sai  su  Yusuf da  Mustafa  suna  take  mata  baya  suma  dariyar  sukeyi    mimee  tace  mommy  kitsaya  mana  nizaki  bawa  aini  autarki ko ?  Husna  ta  tsaya  tana fadin  ai  bazan  baku  ba  tinda  kuna  fada  sainayi  fushi  ma  tinda  bazaku  dena  fada  a  tsakanin  kuba  tana  daure  fuska  ita  wai  irin  mahaifiya  din  nan  tana  wa  diyanta  fada 


Kama kunansu  sukayi  mustafa  yace  am so sorry  my  sweetheart mommy  bazamu  sakeba  kinji, murmishi husna  tayi  tace  to  shikenan  yanzu  muyi hira  bari  na  zauna  saina  baku  labari  mai  dadi  amma  bari  nazo  zan  dauko  littafin  hikaya  sainake  karanta  muku,  to  sukace  suna  tsallan  murna  

Duk  abinda  sukeyi  alhaji abdallah  yana  kallon su  sai  murmishi  yakeyi  yana  kara  kaunar  husna  a  zuciyar  shi  dama  umma  bata  fitoba  

Husna  ko  da sauri  ta  hau  kan  stam tana  gudu  gudu     takai kafarta  zata  ajiye  akan  matak'alar  kawai  ta  goce  tayi  baya  tana  gangara 

Da gudu Alhaji abdallah ya nufi  wajan  yana fadin  husna  da sauri  yakarasa  kafin  tazo  kasa  yayi tare  ta  ta fado  a  jikinshi  

Jijjiga  ta  yasoma  yi  yana husna  please  kitashi  ,mimee  tuni  tasoma  kuka  su  Yusuf  samar sukayi  dakin  umma  suka  kirata  da sauri  umma  tazo  falon  tana fadin  lfy abdallah maiyasami  husna  ne 

Kamar zaiyi kuka  yace  zamewa  tayi  akan stam. Umma  tace  kawo  ruwa  mustafa  da gudu mustafa  ya  je  dauko  runwan  sanyi  yayi  yakawo  ,amsa  umma  tayi  tana  zubawa  husna  a  fuska  

Ajiyar zuciya tasauke  tana  rufe  idonta  ,Umma  tace  sannu husna  ,bude  idonta husna tayi  tana  kallon  umma  tace  yauwa  umma inasu  mimee  suke 

Da sauri  mimee  ta  fada  jikin  husna  tana  kuka  tace  mommy  waye  ya fado  dake  daga  kan  stam  ,shafa  kanta husna  tayi tace  zamewa  nayi  ina  sauri  zan  dauko  littafin  hikaya 

Yusuf yace  mommy  babu inda  yake  miki  ciwo  , Husna na  murmishi  tace  eh  babu  kudena  kukan  kunji  tana  share  musu  hawaye 


Murmishi  umma  tayi tace  to  kowa  yazo  ya  zauna  tashi  sukayi  kowa  ya  zauna  kusa da husna  

Alhaji abdallah yace  umma  yaunefa  husna  zata  koma  gida  munyi  waya  da  baba  yacemin  yazo  gida  nakai  husna  

Umma tace  ayya  Allah  sarki  ai  na  manta  kwana biyu  ashe  zatayi  

Dagowa husna  tayi  tana  kallon  Alhaji abdallah  sai  taga  ya  mata  kyau  sosai  ba  abinda  yafi  burgeta  a  fuskar  shi  kamar  dan  karamin  bakinshi  da  sajenshi mai  kyau  yasha  gyara, shima  k'ura  mata  ido  yayi   yana  sakar  mata  da  murmishi  

Umma ko  wani  sanyi taji  tace  a  wai  abdallah  ne  yake  kallon  so  agabana  wanda  abaya  idan  aka  mishi  zancen  aure  bayasonji   sai  gashi  yana  yi  agabana  , tashi  tayi  ta  kama  hannun  su  mimee  tace  muje  mutaya  husna  hada  mata  kaya ,ba  muso  suka  bita  

Saida husna  ta  more  da  kallon  shi  tukun ta  sauke  idonta  tana  dariya  , shima  dariyar  yayi  yace  yan  mata  na  yadai  kona  miki  kyau  ne  wannan  kallon  haka  

Rufe fuska tayi  tana  murmishi  tace  eh  kayi kyau  sosai  kasan  mai  yake burgeni  a  jikinka ? Girgiza kai  yayi  yace  a  a  husna  ta  saikin  fada  

Husna tace  bakinka  da  sajenka  sunamin  kyau  kamar  na  taba  da  kuma  saita  rufe  bakin  har  fuskar  ta  tana  dariya  

Murmishi yayi yace  iyee  lalle  kice  nina  musamman  ne  wajan  husna  ta   ni  kuma  komai  naki  yana  burgeni  sosai  matata  

Jin  abinda  yafada  husna  tayi  saurin komawa  bayanshi  tana  boyewa  sai dariya  take  jin  yace  mata  komai  nata  yana  burgeshi  

Janyota yayi  tadawo  gabanshi  yace  husna  ta  kinsan  yadda  nakesonki  kuwa  shiyasa  za'a  matso  da  bikinmu  kusa   kilama  asaka  wata  daya  dan  kidawo  kusa  dani  bayan  auren  saiki  cigaba  da  karatunki  a  gidanmu  koya  kika  ce? 

Husna ko saboda  tsananin  farin cikin  zatabar  gidansu  tadawo  inda  baza'a  daketa  batasan  lokacin  data  fada  jikin  Alhaji  tana  miki  kiss a  gefen  wuyarshi  


Da sauri  ya rintse  ido  jin  wani  yanayi  ajikinsa  a  cikin zuciyar shi  yace  haba  husna  dole  na  matsa  adawo  da  bikinmu  kusa  dan  nake  tabaki  son  raina  alokacin  kinzama  muharrama  ta  yanzu kuma  cutuwa  nake  idan  kika  rungume  ni  , ajiyar zuciya  yasauke  jin  tasake  shigewa  jikinsa  bai  hanata  ba  yasake  bude  mata  ta  kara  rungume  shi  

Husna kuwa lokacin  data  rungume  Alhaji  jinta  tayi  a  sabon  yanayi  mai  dadi  shiyasa  tasake  shigewa  jikinsa  tana  lumshe ido  

Kallon ta  Alhaji  yakeyi  yadda  take  lumshe ido  shi  kuma  duk  tsikar  jikinsa  ta  tashi  , yace  اعوذ بالله من الشيطان الرجيم  ، اعوذ بالله من الشيطان الرجيم  ، اعوذ بالله من الشيطان الرجيم   ya  fada  sau  uku  tukun yadena  jin wannan  yanayin  ya  maida  kallon  shi  ga  husna  da  mamaki  yake  kallon  ta  ganin  har  bacci  yayi  gaba  da ita murmishi yayi   ya  dauketa  cak  yanufi  sama  da  ita 

Umma na  ganinsu  tace  a a  bacci  tayi  , yace  eh  umma  bari  na  kira  baba  nacema  sai  da  yamma  zan  kawota  ,Umma tace  eh  idan  ta  tashi  saikazo  ka  dauketa   , kwantar  da  ita  yayi  ya  fice  adakin 


Umma  tace to  kuzo  muje  kasa  muzauna  karku  tasheta  bacci  takeyi  , da sauri  mimee  ta  hau  kan  gadon  tana  kwanciya  a  gaban  husna  ta  rungume  ta  su  Yusuf ma  a  bayanta  suka  kwanta  , dariya umma  tayi  tace  to  ninatafi  saikuyi  bacci  ko  nan  ta  fita  


Talatu  na  gida  tana  fakon  husna  tazo  tamata  dukan  mutuwa  ,sai hura hanci take  ,saida  baba  yazo  yamata  bayana  jin  ance  tana  gidan  su  alhaji  abdallah  saita  washe baki  tana  murna tasan  dai  dole  zatazo  da  kayan  arziki dan  haka  ta  dena  fushi  amma  taci  alwashin  saita  zane  husna  


Aljana safiya  koda  wasa  bata  sauka  idan  zatayi  salla  saita  tambayi  babanta  yafada  lafiyar  dajin  tukun  ta  tsaya  tayi  salla  tana  gamawa  zata  bar  wajan  tahau  tsuntsuwar ta  suci  haba  da  gudu  


Sarki shamsu  sunkasa  kama  aljana safiya  duk  wajan  da  suka  ganota  zakunan  da  kurayen  suje  tuni  ta  bar  wajan  , hakan  yasa  bakin ciki  ya  mamayeshi  sai  magana  bayayi  inba  da  dodon saba   

Shima  kanshi  dodon abin  mamaki  yake  bashi  yadda  suka  kasa  kama  aljana safiya  nan  suka  shirya  mata  wani  sabon  tugun 

Dodon  yace  za'a  tura   bakin aljani  daya   yaje  mata  da  suffar  aliyu  ko  babanta  idan  ta  yadda  dashi  to  zasuyi  nasara 

Dariyar mugunta  sarki shamsu yayi  yace  yauwa  dodona  aini mantawa  nayi   da  wannan  amma  hakan  ma  bamu  makara  ba   

Sarki shamsu  yabar  dakin  cike  da  farin ciki yana  fadin wannan  karon  zamuci  galaba  akanki   


Husna na tashi  daga bacci  tayi wanka  tashirya   su  umma  ta  hada  mata  kayan  arziki  sukatafi  har  su  mimee   suna  zuwa  anguwar su  husna  da  kyer  suka  rabuda  yaran  tana  kuka  suma  sunayi  a  haka  suka  koma  gida  ,shima  gida  ta  nufa  


Da  sallama  tashiga  talatu  ta  tareta  da  murna  tana fadin  a  a  ashe  tsautsayi  yaso  samunki  to  muje  ciki. Itadai  husna  kallon  talatu  take  da  mamakin  wannan  sabon  halayya  data  fito  dasu  


Dakin  suka  shiga  ko  kayan  bata  ajiyeba  talatu  a  karba  tace  kawo  nagani  tana  budewa  , atamfofi  tagani  da  mayafi  sai  kayan kwalliya  hadda  takalma  da  hijab  

Talatu tace  wannan  duk  hajiyar  alhaji  tabaki  ,Husna  tace  eh  mama  ita  tabani  tace  wannan  atamfar  uku  nakine  sauran  nawane  

Harara  talatu  ta  bankawa  husna  tace  iyee  atamfa  kadai  zan  dauka  dan  uwarki  duk  wannan  kayan  to  bazan  dauka  ba  duka  nakeso 

Husna  data  soma  hawaye  tace  mama  kidauki  duka  kibani  kayan kwalliyar  da  takalmi  ,Talatu  tace  babu  abinda  zan  baki ba  wahalalliya  

Husna kuka tasaka  tana rokon talatu  tabar  mata  kayan  kwalliya  amma  ta  kiya  , data  matsama  rufeta  tayi  da  duka  saida  tamata  duka  iya  son  ranta  tukun  ta  kyaleta  har  zata  fita  tadawo  ta  kama  kunnen  husna  ta  murde  tace  wllh  idan  babanki  yadawo  kikace  na  dakeki saina  miki  wanda  yafi  wannan  yar iska  kina  jina  

Husna  tace  to  , tukun  talatu  tasakar  mata  kunne  ta  harbeta  tukun  ta  fice  adakin  


*Anan zan  dakata✍🏻*

*27—28*


Husna  kuka  taci  takoshi  tukun  ta  nufi  toilet  wanke  fuskar  ta  tayi  tana  ajiyar zuciya  jin yadda  kunenta  ke  mata  zafi   sosai  takjin  zogi  a  wajan  da  talatu  ta  murda  mata  fitowa  tayi  bakin  gado  ta  zauna  tana  lumshe ido  ,tana  tuna  yadda  umma  da  alhaji  abdallah  suke  kula  da  ita  basa  barinta  da  damuwa  kwana  biyun  datayi  a  wajansu  har  wani  kyau  da  haske  takara   
Murmishi  yayi  tunawa  da  alhajin  ta  yace  mata  za'a  matso  da  bikinsu  kusa  ,hannu  tadaga  sama  zatayi  addu'a  kuka  ne  ya  subce  mata  cikin kuka  tace  

Ya Allah  ka  kawomin  sauki  acikin  rayuwa  ta  Allah  ka  nunamin  mahaifiya  ta  a  yanzu  nagane  mama  ba  ita  bace  mahaifiya ta  shiyasa  take  azabtar dani  Allah  kasa  a  adawo  da  bikin  nan  sati  biyu  nima  na  huta  da  wannan  wahalar  da  takaici  ya hayyu ya  k'ayyum  ka  taimakeni  nasan  kasan  dani  kuma  ba  sona  bane  bakayi  ka  jarabceni  da matar  uba muguwa  ya  rabbi  kasa  aurena  shine  karshen  wahalata  nima  nasamu  y'anci  irin  na  sauran  mutane  naji  sanyi  azuciya  ta   tana  kaiwa  nan  cikin addu'ar  ta  ,ta  shafa  a  fuskar ta   kwanciya  tayi  bacci  yayi  gaba  da  ita  


Aljana  safiya  suna  ta  gudu  yunwa  takeji  amma  taki  cewa  su  tsaya  taci abinci  burinta  bai  wuce  ace  gata  a  dajin  bishiyoyi  ta  ciro  maganin  ta  dawo  gida  tana  son  ganin  husna  yanzu  awani  hali take   nisawa  tayi  tace  Allah sarki  husna  ta  ina  nan  tafe  gareki  


Wani  daji ta  hanga  ganin  yunwa  na  kara  karuwa  tacewa  tsuntsuwar  masoyiya  tsaya  na  tambayi  babana  mu  tsaya  anan  dajin  mu  huta  
Tsayawa  tsuntsuwar  tayi. Aljana safiya  ta  murza zoben ta  tana  rufe  idon  , tace  abbana  kaduba  min  dajin  nan  bashida  matsala  

Daga  bangaren  abba  yace  kai  masha Allah   yanzu  kinyi  rabi  a  tafiyar  ki  gaskiya  kinyi  sauri  sosai  , wannan  daji  bashida  matsala  ku  sauka  a  cikinshi  kuhuta 

Murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  nagode abbana  tukun  ta  bude  idonta  , umarni  tayiwa  tsuntsuwar  dasu  sauka  kasa  , sauka  sukayi  kasa  

Aljana safiya ta  shimfida  dardumar  ta   tukun  tazauna  jakarta    ta  bude  tafito  da  abinci  tana  ci  take  jin  karar  tafiya  ta  gabanta  amma  abin  mamakin  data  dago  bataga  komai  ba. Cigaba  tayi  dacin  abincin  ta  amma  wannan  karar  tafiyar  yana  nan  abin  na  kara  tinkaro  ta  bata  sake  dagowa  ba  saida  ta  koshi  tayi  hamdala  wanke  hannun  ta  tayi  tukun  ta  kalli  inda  ake  tafowa , batayi  mamakin  ganinsu  ba  dan  haka  ta  gyara  zamanta  tana  murmishin  yadda  suke  bibiyarta  

Janyewa  tayi daga  kallon  su  tana tunanin  wani  irin kisa  ya  dace  dasu  , jin  suna  tafowa  da  sauri  , yasata  mike  tana  gyara  takobin ta  

Tsayawa  sukayi  daga  dan  nesa  da  ita  

Wani  mugun kallon  take  binsu  dashi  kafin  tace  bansan  wani  abu  na  tsare  muke  ba  kuke  takura  min  to  komin  yawanku  tinda  bakuji  tsoron  yadda  na  kashe  yan uwanku  to   zaku  je  inda  suka  tafi  

Daga  gefenta  taji  murya  aliyu  yana  fadin  ummina  ummi   a  mamakance  take  kallon  inda  take  jiyo  muryar  aliyu  , ganinshi  tayi  yana  mata  murmishi   da  gora  ahannun shi  ta  ruwa  , Aljana safiya  tace  yarona  waye  yazo  dakai  nan  kuma  dakai  dawa  kukazo  

Aliyu yace da  abba  mukazo  nan  amma  narigashi  zuwa  inda  kike  danna  kawo  miki  wannan  ruwan  da  abba  yace  kisha 

Aljana safiya  tace  to zo  nan  tana  nuna  mishi  gefen ta , mai  suffar  ta  aliyu  ya  kallon  bataliyar  zakuna  da  macizai  da  kada  dasu  gaban  aljana  safiya  kowa  yayi  shirin  kota  kwana  , murmishi  yamusu  tukun ya  nufeta 

Aljana safiya  tana  kallon  duk  abunda  sukayi  tasan  wannan  ba  aliyun  tabane  sharine  irin  nasu  dan  haka  ta  dubi  yar  karamar  wukar  dake  jere  a  hannun  ta  ta  dan  turo da  guda  biyu  cikin dubara  , tana  kallon  shi  yana  zuwa  gareta 

Yana  matsowa  kusan  kafin  ya  mika  mata  gorar  ta sakar  mishi  wuk'aken  biyu  daya  a  sharen  ciki  daya  a  kirji   aida  sauri  yasaki  gorar  hannun  yasaka  wani  k'ara  mai  cika  kunne a  take  yafadi  nan  yasoma  cenjawa  yana  komawa  wani  k'aton  maciji  yana  gama  zama ya  mutu 

Kallon  su  tayi tace  anfada  musu  ni  sakarya  ce  irinku  ko  ance  muku  bansan  aliyu   ba  za zan  kasa  bambamce  shi  to  kunyi kuskure  a  rayuwar ku  sainaga  bayanku  tana  aika  musu  kallon  bazaku  tsira  ba 

Wani  zakine  acikinsu  yayi  amimiya  irin  nazakuna  ya  tunkareta  a  guje  , itama  aljana safiya   nufosa  tayi  da  gudu  sosai  tana  gudun  ta  zare  kwari da bakar  ta  ta  saitashi  , tin  kafin  yazo  kusa  ta  sakar  masa  da  kibiyar  har  uku  

Ai  jikake  tim  yafadi  kasa  macecce , sauran  na  ganin  haka  sukayo  kanta  gadan—gadan  , itama  aljana safiya  tayo  kansu  tanajin sabon  karfi  na  shigarta  sosai   suna  hadewa  aka  kama  yaki  baji  bagani  sai  kara  sa  ihun  su  kakeji  a  cikin  dajin ana  ta  zubar  dasu  kasa  sosai  take  yakarsu  idan  kaga  fuskar  aljana  safiya  saika  razana  yadda  ranta  yabace  sosai  haka  fuskar  ta  tanuna  



Husna !husna!  Bazaki  fitoba  dan  badaurin  auren  uwarki  koni  baiwarki  ce  dazaki  barni  da  aiki  zaki  fito  ko  saina  je  dakin  na  lallasaki  yar iska  tambadaddiya kifito  kona  gyara  miki  zama  agidan   talatu  ce  ke  wannan  masifar  har  bakinta  kunfa  yake  saboda  tsananin  masifa  sai  hura  hanci  takeyi  jin  bata  amsaba  kuma  bata  fitoba  wani  bakin  yarufe  talatu tace  lalle  yarinyar  nan  dan  kinga  ina  raga  miki  shine  har  kika  samu  damar  renani  to  zakiga  yadda  zanmiki   

Kitchen  tanufa  tana  dube  dube  bataga  mabugi  ba  wanda  zata  zane  husna  dashi  cigaba  tayi  da  nema  ganin  tana  batawa  kanta  lokacin  ta  zari  muciyar  da  take  tuka  tuwo  hadda  abin  tuwon  ajiki  yana  turiri  haka  ta  dauko  muciyar  ta  nufi  dakin  husna  

Husna  baiwar Allah  batama  san  talatu  na  kiranta  ba  dan  bacci  takeyi  sosai  kuma  tanajin  dadin  baccin  ta  sai  munshari  take  a  hankali. A haka  talatu  tazo  taganta tayi  dai–dai  tana  sharar  bacci  

Talatu tace  iyee  ba  shakka  yarinya  kinsamu  guri  to  zakici  ubanki  yanzu   muciyar hannun  ta  tasoma  jubgar  husna  dashi  tana  fadin  ni  zaki  rena  ina  miki  magana  kina  bacci  to  yau  zanga  mai  kwatarki  a  gidan  nan  tacigaba  da  dukanta 

Husna ko  kamar  a  mafarki  taji saukar  duka  ga  zafi  taya  shigeta  tana  son  motsawa  amma  talatu  ta  danneta  tahanata  tashi  sai  jubgarta  takeyi  tako  ina. Kuka  husna  keyi  tana  rokon  talatu  tayi  hakuri  amma  ko  kulata  batayiba  tacigaba  da  dukanta  

Saida  ta  gaji  tukun  ta  kyaleta  tana  huci  tace  dan  uwarki  ni  zaki  bari  da  aikin  gida  kizo  kina  bacci  a  daki wato  kega  yar  gwal  ko saboda  kin  daukeni  sa'arki  shiyasa  kika  soma  renani  ko  zaki  tashi  kije  ki  min  wanki  da  gyaran  gida  kosaina  kara  miki  dukanne  bai  ishekiba  takai  karshen maganar  tana  banka  mata  harara  

Da sauri  husna  ta  tashi  da  kyer  tafita  tana  kuka  sosai  zuciyar ta  kemata  zafi  bata taba  jin  shaushin  zagin  da  talatu  take  mata  ba sai  yau ta  kara  tabbatar wa   da  talatu  ba  mahaifiyar  ta  bace  dan haka  yau  taji  ciwon  zagin  da  akai  wa  mahaifiyar  ta  haka  ta  kama  shara  tanayi  tana  kuka  bayan  tagama  tayi  wanke  wanke  ,shima  tagama  ta  fida  tuwon  tazuba  a  kuloli  ta  gyara  ko  ina  yayi  tsaf  dashi  saida  ta  tabbatar  , babu  wani  datti  a  kitchen  din  tukun  ta  fito  falon  

Inda  talatu  take  zaune  ta  isa  tace  mama  ina  kayan  naki  yake  wanda  zan  wanke? Saida  talatu  ta  aika  mata  tukun  tace  suna  cen  jikin  fanfo  nakai  miki  shi  kiyi  sauri  kafin  malam  yadawo   ta  maketa  , gocewa  husna  tayi  amma  saida  dukan  yasameta  tashi  tayi  tana  share  hawaye  ,ta  nufi  comper   din  gidan gindin  fanfon  tayi  nan  tasoma  wankin  tana  tunanin  rayuwar  ta  



Bangaren aljana safiya   suna  nan  sai  yaki  akeyi  baji  ba  gani  duk  ta  kushe  su  saura  kadan  yarage  , ja da baya sukayi  ganin  basufi  su  goma  ba  suma  tana  kai  musu  sara  da  suka  tako  ina    tsayawa  itama  tayi  tana  sauke  numfashi  

Aljana safiya ta  kallesu  tace  dama  aina  fada  muku   duk  wanda  yashigo  gonata  tobashi  bafita  da  ransa  kamar  yadda  nayi  alwashin  saina  kashe  shugaban  naku   kuzo  mana  , tana  gyara  tsayuwar  ta  

A tare  suka  yo  kanta  ,itama  kansu  tayo  aka  hade  duk  wanda  ta  soka  da  takobin  ta  saidai  wani  bashiba   kafin  wani  lokacin  tuni  ta  kashe  su  ,tsayawa  tayi  tana  maida  numfashi  tace  Alhamdulillah  Allah  nagode  maka  da  kake  bani  nasara  akan  bakiyana  , Allah  ka  kaini  inda  nakeson  zuwa  cikin  k'ank'anin  lokacin  dan  nasan  yarona  yana  cen  yanajin  jiki   kayanta  ta  hada  tana  rataye  jakarta  tsuntsuwar  ta  tazo  hawa  tayi  suka  cigaba  da  tafiya  
Tana  addu'ar  Allah  yakaita  dajin bishiyoyi  a  karamin  lokaci  basai  takai  lokacin  da  babanta  yafada  yanzu  ma  Alhamdulillah  yace  ta  wuce  rabi  a  tafiyar  ta  shiyasa  yanzu  tsuntsuwar  ta  takara  k'aimin  gudu  


Yau  kawun nan  Alhaji  abdallah  sukazo  domin  arage  lokacin  auren  baba  ya  tarbesu  da  fara'arsa  yana  musu  sannu  dazuwa  shida  baban  sailuba  ,zama  sukayi  aka  gaisa  tukun  kawon  alhaji abdallah  yace  malam  musbahu  munzo da  wata  magana  Allah  yasa  za'a  fashimcemu  


Murmishi  baba  yayi  yace  to  ai  anzama  daya  baban sailuba  yace  maike  tafe  daku  Alhaji  nura  , gyara zama  yayi  wanda  aka  kira  da  alhaji  nura , yace  dama  munzo  ne  akan  arage  lokacin  bikin  nan  yadawo  wani  biyu  ko  uku  saboda  abdallah  wani  aiki  yasameshi  a  abuja  nan  da  wata  hudu  zai  soma  to  shine  yace  muzo  muroki  alfarma  adawo  da  bikin  wata  biyu  ko  uku   yanason  kafin  lokacin  an  gama  auren  su  da  husna  saiya  dauketa  sutafi  cen  

Baban sailuba  yace  a  wannan  ai  ba  matsala  bane  , indai  wannan  maganar  ce  sai  abarshi  a  wata  uku  yanzu  Keenan  saura  wata  biyu  tinda  anci  wata  daya  da  saka  ranar  bikin  , koya  kace  malam musbahu  murmishi  baba  yayi  yace  inbanda  abunka  salisu  ai  husna  yarka  ce  basai  na  saka  baki  ba  duk  abinda  kace  haka  za'a  ,

Baban sailuba  yace  hakane  kuma  to  yanzu  nan  da  wata  biyu  za'a  daura  aurensu  insha Allah  hakan  yayi  ko? Yana  juya  zancen  zuwa  ga  su  alhaji  nura  , 

Alhaji nura yace  gaskiya  mungode sosai  Allah  yabar  zuminci  wannan  yayi kyau  yanzu  saimu  je  afadawa  mahaifiyar  shi  yadda  mukayi  mungode  malam  salisu   

Baban sailuba yace  ba komai  Allah  yakaimu lokacin  , suka  amsa  da  ameen  nan  suka  tashi  su  baba  suka  rakasu  har  bakin  mota  , bayan  sun  tafi  baba  ya  kalli  baban  sailuba  yace  salisu wllh  naji  dadin  rage  lokacin  auren  nan  da  akayi  , saboda  husna  tasamu  y'anci  a  rayuwar  ta  

Jan numfashi  baban sailuba  yayi  yace  gaskiya  hakan  yayi  sosai  saitaci  gaba  da  karatun  ta  gidanta  kaima  hankalin  ka  zaifi  kwanciya  da  hakan 

Baba yace  hakane  insha  Allah  nan suka  sallama  kowa  ya  shiga  gidanshi  

Baba nazuwa  yafadawa  talatu  komai  , dagowa  yayi  dan  yaga  wani yanayi  fuskar  ta  zatayi  sai  yaga  tana  murmishi  fuskar  fal  farin ciki  

Talatu tace  a  gaskiya  wannan  abun  yayi sosai  malam  kace  munkusa  shan  biki  nan  da  wata  biyu  to  yanzu  saina  fadawa  wanda  na  gayyata  cewar  an  dawo  da  bikin  nan  da  wata biyu. Baba yace  eh  kifada  musu  andawo  dashi  baya  ,yana  fadin  hakan  yafita  zuwa  kasuwa 

Talatu ko tabawa  ta  kira  tafada  mata  duk  abinda  baba  yafada  mata  tukun takashe  kiran  haka  taringa  kiran  kawayenta  da  yan uwanta  tana  fada  musu an  janye  lokacin  bikin  husna  yadawo  wata  biyu 

Bayan tagama  tace  ahayye  ni  talatu  matar  musbahu  ba  gaba  da  gaba  koda  ta  baya  sai  anshirya  nakusa  yin  fancaka  da  naira  diyata  zatayi  aure  gidan masu  kudi  ai  dole  nashana  


Tabawa  na  zaune  sai  dariya  take  tana  shewa  kamar  wata  zararriya  rukayya  ce  tafito tace  waini  innar mu  lfy  kike  wannan  abu  koda  kun  haukacene  bamu  sani  ba  tana  wurgawa  tabawa  harara.  Tabawa  ta  kama  hannun  ta  tace  je  banza  gata  nake  kokari  nemo  miki  

Da  mamaki rukayya  ke  kallon  tabawa  tace  inna  wani  gata  kuma  dacen  kinsan  da  gata  da  kika  barmu  muke  yawo  mun  girma  amma  ba  mai  sonmu  sa  aure  kowa  badan Allah  yake  sonmuba  saidai  biyan  bukatar shi   tana  fusge  hannun  ta  daga  cikin na  tabawa 

Ita kuwa  bata  damuba  tace  gata  dai  rukayya  nan  tabata  labarin  Alhaji abdallah mai dala  da  auren  dazaiyi  na  husna. Zaro ido  rukayya  tayi tace  bahun ubancen  wai  dama  husna  ce  zata  auri  alhaji abdallah mai dala  ,

Tabawa  tace  sosai makuwa  shine  nakeson  auren  yadawo  kanki  yanzu  wata  biyu  yarage  ana  kaita  gidanta  zai  saketa  ya  aureki 

Rukayya  tace  lalle  inna  ai  nice  dama  na  dace  da  auren  alhaji  abdallah  ba  husna  ba  kawai  duk  yadda  kikayi  daidai  ne  innata  ina  bayanki 


Dariyar mugunta  tabawa  tayi tace  ban  mukashe  yar  gari  tafawa  sukayi  tukun  sukaci  gaba  da  hirar su 


Sarki shamsu  da  dodon shine. Zaune  suna  bude  madubin  tsafinsa  , shafawa  sarki  shamsu  yayi  nan  suka  soma  kallon  duk  abinda  yafaru  da  karfi  , sarki shamsu  yace  a  a......

*29—30*




Sarki shamsu  sai  zare  ido  yake  ganin  b'arnar  da  aljana  safiya  tamusu  duk  ta  kashe abun  suka  tura  mata  babu wanda  yayi  saura. Dodon  ya kalli  sarki  shamsu  yace  lalle  wannan  yarinyar  ta  gama  damu  anya  kuwa  ita  kadai  take  wannan  yak'in ba wani  yake  tayata ba  inkuwa  hakane  to  karfinta  bashida  iyaka. Sarki shamsu  yace  nima  abinda  nake  tunani  dan  babu  wanda  za'a  turawa  wannan  bataliyar  ya  tsira in mutum ne  abu  daya  ya  isa  ya  kasheshi  amma  ita  tana  aljana  mun  tura  mata  aljannu  irin  ta  gashi  ta  kashesu  yanzu  wani  abu  zamuyi  mata  mu  kasheta  kagafa  yanzu  kadan  yarage  taje  dajin  bishiyoyi ? 

Dodo yace  gaskiya  nayi  mamakin  yadda  aljana safiya  tayi  sauri  a  tafiyar ta  ko  a  jinsin  aljannu  sai  anyi  shekara  ana  tafiya  tukun  ake  zuwa  dajin bishiyoyi  saboda  nisanshi  da  abubuwan hadarin dake  hanyar  , amma  sai  gashi ita  batayi  rabin  shekara  ba  ta  kusan shuwa   kuma  yanzu  haka  tana  falfala  gudu  sosai  bata  tsayawa  a  hanya  saidai  in  yunwa  takeji  lalle  ta  shammacemu  sosai  dole  musake  sabon  shiri  inba  haka  ba  nan  da  wata  biyar zata  iya  zuwa  dajin bishiyoyi 


Da  mamaki  sarki shamsu  yake kallon  yake kallon dodon  yace  ya  shugaba dama  aljannu  zasu  iya  zuwa  dajin bishiyoyi  kafin kafin shekara  naji  kace  aljana safiya  takusa  zuwa  to  ita  a  aljannun ma  ita  ta  dabance  ko  tafi  kowa  baiwa  acikinsu  wannan  yarinyar  hatsabibiya  ce  

Dodo yace  sosai makuwa  zata  iya  zuwa  a  wata  shida ko  bakwai  kagani  da  tafiyar ta  batafi  wata  uku  ba  amma  gashi  nan  ta  tafi  rabin tafiyar  ina  da  tabbacin  babanta  ne  ke  taimaka  mata  shiyasa  ta sauri  sosai  

Sarki shamsu yace  inkuwa  hakane  yasa  tayi  saurin tafiya  to  mu  afkawa dajin  ta  mushe  babanta  kaga  shikenan  itama  bazata  samu k'warin  gwiwar  tafiyar  ba  daga  nan  samu  kasheta  nina  dauki  zoben  kai  ka  dauki  takobin 

Dodo yace to  karma  kafara  wannan  zoben  da  take hannun  aljana safiya  da  takobin  wajanta  duka  mallakina  ce  tin  kafin  a  haifi  kakan  ka  nake  nemanta  yanzu  kuma  na  ganta  dole  sudawo  hannuna  

Sarki shamsu yace  to  shugaba  badamu  duk  abinda kace  haka  za'ayi  bari  ,kuma  ya  batun  kashe  baban  nata  ?Dodo yace  kai  har  kana  da  karfin  da  zaka  kashe aljani malik baban aljana safiya ? Toko  ni  nan nasha  gwadawa  na  kashesa  amma  na  kasa  babu  wanda  yakaishi  karfi  da  jarumta shiyasa  tin safiya  na  yar  shekara  shida  yake  koya  mata  yaki  sosai  yanzu  kuma  ta  gajeshi  dan  haka  kadena  ma  tunanin  zuwa  dajin 𝗮𝗹𝗷𝗮𝗻𝗮𝗿 𝗷𝗮𝗿𝗶𝗺𝗮𝗶  dan  yafi  karfinka  idan  kuma  kace  zuwa  zakayi  toga  hanya  nan  ,yakai karshen maganar  yana  nuna  miki  hanyar fita

Sarki shamsu yace  ya  shugaba  ai  tinda  kace  karnaje  to  bazan  jeba  musamu  wata  mafitar  dai 

Dodo yace to shikenan kabari zanyi tunani  nan da  kwana biyu. Sarki shamsu yace to  shikenan  yanzu  bari  natafi  nan yafice  adakin 


Wata mahaukaciyar dariya dodo  yayi  yace  yaro  baisan  wutaba  saiya  taka  sarki  shamsu  kana  tunanin  zanci  gaba  da  zama  ne  a  inda  kake  kasa  samun biyan  buk'atunka   dani  hmmm  ai  ka  kuka da  kanka  duk ranar  dana  amshi takobi da zobe to a  lokacin nizan  mulki  jinsin aljannu da  namun daji dama  mutane baki daya  , zaka gane  baka da  wayo   



Sarki shamsu ma  na fita  yayi murmishin  mugunta  yace  shugaba  kenan  kana tunanin  zanbar  maka  wannan  zoben da  takobi  to  kayi  kuskure  a  rayuwar  ka  muna  kashe  aljana safiya kaima  zan kasheka  na  dauke  komai nazama  sarkin  aljannu da mutane  hahahaha yayii  gaba abinshi



Husna  zaune  tana  karatun  alqur"ani mai girma  sai taji wayarta  na  neman  agaji  da  kamar  bazata  dauka  ba  saita  duba  ganin  mai  kiran  yasa  tayi  murmishi  , daga  kiran tayi tace  assalam alaikum  ya  Alhaji na

Saida  yasauke  ajiyar zuciya  tukun  yace  amin wa'alaikum salam ya  husna  ta  matata  abin  alfahari  na  muradin  raina  hasken  zuciya ta 

Dariya husna tayi  sai  taji  duk  wani b'acin  ranta  ya  fita   tace  duk  ni  kadai  Alhaji  na ? Alhaji abdallah yace  sosai makuwa  ai  kinfi  haka  ma yanzu dai  albishirinki 

Murmishi husna tayi tace  goro  fari  tass  fesomin  nasan  ka  gumtso  magana ne  to  abani  insha 

Yar dariya  yayi  yace  kamar kuwa  kin  sani  husna  ta  nakusa  hutawa  da  zuwa  gidan  umma  cin  abinci  da  sauransu  nakusan   dena  share  dakina  da  kaina 

Kamar  yana  ganinta  ta  rufe  fuska tace  nifa  alhaji  kunya  nakeji  idan  kana  wannan  maganar  nifa  yarinya ce.  Dariya yayi yace  to  ai  zaki  girma  insha Allah  kuma  daga  yau  kidena  cemin  alhajin  nan  kamar  wani  surukin ki  


Turo baki gaba husna tayi tace  to  mai  kakeson  na  fada  bakaje  makkah  dinba 

 
Alhaji abdallah yace naje sauba adadi  husna  amma  please  a dan  sauyamin  suna  mana  ko,  yayi fuskar tausayi  kamar  tana  ganinshi  

Murmishi husna tayi tace to  ka  zab'a ko 

Alhaji abdallah yace to ni zanke kiranki da  ,Sahibaty  kefa  mai  za kike  cemin

Husna jin yace mata  ,Sahibaty wani  dadi  taji  ya  mamaye  zuciyar  ta   saida  takai  second kamar  goma  tukun  tace  ni  kuna  zanke  cemaka  sahiby  yayi  dadi ko

Alhaji abdallah yace  wow sunan yayi  dadi  gaskiya  to  yanzu  bari  na  barki  zanje  gidan  umma  su  mimee  nason  ganina 

Husna tace  to shikenan  ka  gaishemin dasu  kace  nayi kewarsu 


Murmishi  yakuma  yi  yace  to beyy  love you sahibaty 

Boye fuskar ta tayi tace love you so much  tana  kashe wayar  dan  taji  kunya  

Kwanciya tayi tana  murmishi tana  tuna  masoyin  nata tace  Allah  yabarmu  tare  



Aljana safiya  tafiya  take  ba  kamar  hannun yaro  tana  karanta  littafin  ta na  addu'o'i  wani  sansani  ta  shigo  inba  taga  wasu  sun  yada  zango  da  alama  matafiya ne  suka  yada  zango  anan  ,kasa  suka  nesa  dasu  tana  bin  wajan  da  kallo  sosai  dajin  ya  burgeta  , kallon  tsuntsuwar ta tayi  tace  masoyiya ki  canja  halitta  zakije  wajan  matanan  cen  ki  ganomin  suwaye  ,a  take  tsuntsuwar  ta  rikide  tadawo  saurayi dogo kyakkyawan gaske  , murmishi aljana safiya tayi  tace  kije kizagasu  kiga wasu  k'abila ne  ya  yanayin rayuwar  yake  idan  kingama  kizo  kimin  bayani , jinjina kai  saurayin  yayi  tukun  ya  tunkari  ayarin  


Da sallama  ya  isa  garesu  mazan  suka  amsa  mishi  kowa  sai  kallon  shi  yakeyi  saboda  tsananin kyaun shi  , wani  acikinsu  ya  kasa  daurewa  yace  malam  daga ina  gaka  kyakkyawa  dakai  

Murmishi  saurayi yayi  yace  muna  cikin tafiyane  sai  muka  yanko  dajin  nan  nida  yar  uwata  gatacen  a  gindin wata  bishiya  tagaji  , shine  muka  tsaya  anan  da  muka  ganku  muma  mu  huta  sai  muyi  gaba  


Wani ne  acikinsu  ya  tashi  yana  zare  takobi  yace  kai  karka  rena mana  hankali  , kilama  kuba  mugaye  bane  kamar  sarki  shamsu  to  ka  koma  inda  kafito konayi  filla filla  da  naman  jikinka 

Murmishi saurayin yayi yace  a a  ni  bana  fada  yar uwata  ta  turoni  mugaisa  ne  kuma  muba  mugaye  bane  kamar yadda  kake  tunani  zamuje  dajin bishiyoyi  ne  nida  yar uwata  , gajjya ce  tasa muka  tsaya  anan  ganin mutane  shiyasa  amma  kayi hakuri  nibana  fada  da  kowa


Hararar shi  yayi  ya  matso  kusa dashi  yace  kai  dan  saurayi  daga  ganinka  kai  azzalimi  ne  shine  kake  mana  karya  danmu  yarda  dakai  takobin  ya  daga  zai  rasa mishi  , shikuwa  saurayin ko  motsawa  baiyiba  bare  yanemi  hanyar  kare  kanshi  


Da karfi  aka  taka  mishi  tsawa  yace  dakata  khalid  karka  yanke hukunci  , tsaywa  yayi wanda aka  kira  da  khalid  ya  kalli  mutumin yace   Allah  ya  taimaki  sarki  ayimin afuwa  bazan  karaba 

Ran  sarkin  abace  yake  kallon shi  yace  daga  yau  karna  kuma  ganin hakan  ta  faru  dan  zaka  iya  daukar  hakkin  wani , Khalid yace  afuwan  ranka  yadade  bazan  kuma  ba  

Numfashi sarkin  yaja  yace  dan saurayi  daga  ina  , Saurayin  ya  kalli  sarkin  yace  mu  mata fiyane  nida  yar uwata  gata cen  mungajine ganinku yasa  muka  tsaya  muma  mu  huta  tukun  muyi  gaba  

Murmishi sarkin  yayi yace  to  badamuwa  kaje  kace  wa  yayar  taka  tazo  nan  cikin  mata  yan  uwanta  , yace to  nan  ya  juya 


Duk abinda  sukeyi aljana safiya najinsu  kuma  tana  kallon  su  lokacin  da  taga  khalid  zai  sari  tsuntsuwar ta  data  koma  saurayi  har  ranta  yasoma  baci  amma  jin  maganar  sarkin  yasa  ta   sauke  ajiyar zuciya  


Yana zuwa  yace  nasan  kinsan maisuka  fada  muje  , murmishi  tayi  tace  masoyiya  nakuwaji  dakyau  muje  nan  takayo  inda  mutanen  suke 


Sallama  tamusu  ds  murya  siririya  mai  dadin  ji  , da sauri  suka  amsa  mata  jin  daddad'ar murya  

Gaisawa  sukayi sarkin   yace  ina  fatan zaku  zauna  damu  kafin  kutafi  , murmishi  safiya tayi  tana  sadda  kanta  kasa  tace  insha Allah  , dagowa tayi  ta  kalleshi  , mamaki ne  yakamata  ganin  kamar  ta  taba  ganin shi  girgiza kai tayi  tace  to  koba  shi  nataba  ganiba  na  taba  ganin  mai  kama  ds  wannan  sarkin , gyaran murya  yayi  yana  aika  mata  ta  tattausan  murmishi  , yace  bismillah  ga  tanti  daya  kushiga  tinda  kince  k'aninki  ne  


Godiya  tamusu  tukun  suka  shiga  ciki.  Suna  shiga  tsuntsuwar takoma  yadda  take  suffar  ta  na  tsuntsuwa  murmishi  safiya  tayi  tace  masoyiya  wannan  ayarin  sunada  kirki  da  alama  mutanan  kirkine  , tsuntsuwar  tace  nima  haka  nagani  nan  sukaci  gaba  da  hirarsu  


Sarki  tinda  aljana safiya  suka  bar  wajan  yake  jinjina  kyau  irin nata  da  kunya  murmishi yayi  yace  Allah sarki  gaskiya  yarinyar ta  hadu  , kwanciya yayi  a  masaukinsa  



Yau  tabawa tazo  gidan  talatu  zama  sukayi  ana  hira  sai  tabawa  tace  yaya talatu  kince  andawo  da  bikin  wata  uku  yanzu  yarage  saura  wata  biyu  , to  gyara  asamu  maganin  da  duk  abinda  mukacewa  Alhaji  yabawa  husna  takawo  mana  bazai  mana  muso  ba 

Talatu tace  dama  kiran  danamiki  kenan  yanzu  kije  wajan  bokan  naki  yabaki  maganin  da  husna  zatake  mana  biyayya  kamar  kare  da  uban  gidansa  

Tabawa  tace  ainama  amso  gashi  tana  kuntowa  daga  hannun zaninta 


Amsa  talatu  tayi  tana  murmishi tace  yauwa  shiyasa  nake  sonki  yanzu  yaya  za'ayi  anfani  dashi  

Tabawa tace  wannan  kizuba  a lemo  idan  yazo  zance tabashi  yasha  , wannan  kuma  azubawa  husna  acikin  abinci  idan  akayi  haka  angama  komai  sai  abinda  mukace  mata  zatayi  aiki  dashi 

Washe baki talatu tayi tace  aikuwa  yana  zuwa  zan  bata  takai  mishi. Tabawa tace to shikenan  idan  komai  yayi  kiyimin  bayani  kafin  lokacin  daurin aure  zan  koma  wajan  boka mai gobe da nisa  , saiya karo  wani 


Tafawa dukayi  tukun  tabawa  tace  ni  zankoma  dama  aiki  nakeyi nabari  nazo  dan  haka  sai anjima  


Husna  take  rakub'e  tana  jin  duk  abinda  suka  fada  tayi saurin  komawa  dakinta   tana  fadin  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un Allah  nagode  maka  daka  munamin  abinda  akeson  aikata  mana  insha Allah  bazamu mushaba  , zama  tayi  tana share  hawaye  tayi  tagumi  


Cikin dare  aljana safiya suka  fara  jin  ihun  ayarin  nan  da sauri  ta  mike  tana  daukar  kayan  yakinta  ta  fito  waje  tsuntsuwar ta  takoma  saurayi  itama  da  takobi  a  hannun  ta  , da sauri  safiya  ta  tsaya  tana  kallon  abinda  yazo musu 


Wata irin  halittace  mai  girman gaske  ga tsayi  ga girma  ko  kallon  ta  kayi sai  hantar  cikinka  ta  murda  bari ta  tunkarota  


Mutanen  wajan  duka  boyewa  sukayi  sai  sarkin  ne  yafito  da  takobinsa  cikin kayan  yaki  , yana  huci  irin  mazajen  nan  

Murmishi safiya tayi tace  haka  nakeson  ganin  namiji  masoyi muje  mutaimaka  mishi  nasan  ayarinsa  babu  abinda  zasu  iya, nan  sukayi  wajansu  da  gudu,  kafin sukaraso  wannan  halittar  ta  nufi  sarkin  da  mugun gudu aljana safiya  ta  bayyana  a  gabanshi tace....

*Da sunan Allah mai rahama mai jin qai*



31—32



Aljana safiya tace  kai  dakata...ta fada  da  karfi, cak   wannan  halittar  ta  tsaya  ga  abinda  takeson  yi   sarki ko  kallon  aljana safiya  yayi ganin  ta  tsaya  agaban  wannan  halittar  sai  cin  magani  take  shi  kuma  yana  bayanta 

Aljana safiya tace daga  ina  kake  waye ya  turoka  kashe  bayin Allah  ko  kaine  katuro  kanka  kabani  amsa?

Kururuwa  halittar  tayi  tace  ke  babu  ruwanki  ni  wajan  sarki  aka  turoni  bake  ba  dan  haka  ki matsa  kibani  waje  tin  kafin  na  aikaki  lahira  , murmishi aljana safiya  tayi  tace  idan  kanason  na  matsa  ka  fadamin  waya  turoka  kashe  sarki  

Wannan halittar tace  sarki shamsu  ne  yaturoni  to  kibani  waje  kona  hada  dake  

Wani  tuk'uk'in bakin ciki ne  yarufe  aljana safiya  wato  dai  sarki  shamsu  ba  ita  kadai  yake  hari ba  to  ma  shi  wannan  sarkin maiya  miki  da  yake  son  kasheshi  jan numfashi tayi tace  to  kakoma  kacewa  sarki shamsu  ni  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  nahana  idan  kuma  kanason  zuwa  barzagu  to  ga  fili  gamai  doki  , tana  zaro  takobin ta 

Sarki tuni  yasoma  kyerma  jin  wai  wannan  tagabanshi  *aljanar jarumai*   ce  agabanshi ga  mamakin  da  yakeyi shi a rayuwar  sa  maiya  tsarewa  sarki  shamsu  da  yake  addabar  rayuwar shi  bayan  kwashe  mishi  mutane  da  dukiya  dayayi  bai  ishesaba shine  yake  binsa  da  wani  sharrin dagowa  yayi  yana  kallon  bayan  aljana safiya  yadda  tayi  tsayuwar  saiya  burgeshi  dan  haka  shima  saiya  fito  daga  bayanta  yace  niba  malalaci  bane 


Shin  wai  maina  tsaremishi  dayake  turoku  kuna  daukemin  mutanen  garina  da  yan uwana  da  dukiyata  maina  tsarewa  sarki shamsu  taya  takurawa  rayuwa ta  to  yau  kozan  mutu  bazaku  daukar min  mutane  ba 

Murmishi safiya tayi  tace  kwantar da hankalin ka  nima  ai  bina  yake  amma  yasan  ruwa  ba  sa'an  kwando  bane  ,amma saboda  bakin  jahili dak'ik'ine shi  yadena  bibiyata,  kai  kuma  ka  koma  kafada  mishi  sak'ona  

Girgiza mai  karfi  wannan  halittar  yayi  yace  babu  inda  zanje  saina  kashe  ku  

Kallon bakada  matsala  aljana safiya  tamishi  tace  to  bismillah  idan  har  zaka  iya  , kallon tsuntsuwar ta  tayi  tace  masoyiya  maza  kizama  jarumin  namiji  , ina  son  akarawa  sarki  karfi  sosai  yadda  bazai  gaji  da  yak'i ba 

Tsuntsuwar  data  zama  saurayin saita   canja  halitta  zuwa  wani  masamuden  namiji   mai  tsayi da girma  sosai  

Jinjina  aljana safiya  tamishi  alamar  yes  yayi hakan  

Juyawa  tayi  ga  sarki  da  yake  tsaye  yana  kallon  ikon Allah  an  canja  suffa agabanshi  
Aljana safiya tace  karkaji  tsoro  zamu  taimakeka  ne  yanzu  kaje  wajan  masoyiya  zai  kara  maka  karfi 

Ajiyar zuciya yasauke  yanufi  inda  yake  yana  zuwa  ya  dafa  kanshi  , kamar  na  minti uku  nan  da  nan  sarki  yaji  wani  mugun karfi  ajikinsa  , bude  idon sa  yayi  

Aljana safiya tace yakaji  ,Sarki yace  naji  karfi  marar  musaltuwa  zan  iya  karawa  dakowa  , Safiya tace  da  kyau  kashirya   yace  to  nan   gyara  tsayuwarsu  suna  jiran  wannan  halittar  tazo  garesu  aljana safiya ce  agaba sarki agefenta  sai  masoyiyar ta  a  gefenta  dayan  


Wannan halittar tanufosu  suma  nufarta  sunakayi  suna  kabbara,  ana  hadena  nan  suka  soma  kai  mishi  sara  sa  suka  tako  shima  halittar  yana  kai  musu  duk  inda  yasamu  ,sosai  aka  kacame  da  azababben yaki  baji  bagani 



Husna ce  tafito  daga  wanka  tana  shafa  mai  saiga  kiran  alhaji abdallah  yashigo  dauka  tayi  tana  murmishi  tace  assalam alaikum 

Amsa mata yayi da amin wa'alaikum salam  ya,  Sahibaty ina  fatan  kina  lfy  yagida  yasu  mama

Yar dariya tayi  tace  mama  na  dakinta  lfy lau  ka  k'araso  ne  , yace  a a  nakusa  zuwa  tare  muke  dasu  mimee  sun  dameni  shesun  ganki  shine  nace  bari  na  kira  na  fada  miki  ,,

Husna tace  to shikenan  sakun  zo  nan  sukayi sallama  ta  kashe wayar 

Dakin  baki taje  takai  mishi  komai  har abinci  lemo da  dauko  a frij ganin  duka  kala  daya  saitayi  murmishi tace  hmmm  mama  kenan  dauka  tayi  takai  tukun tadawo  ciki  dakin  talatu  tanufa  

Talatu  na  waya  daya  tabawa  tana  fada  mata  ta  zuba  maganin  a  lemo  ita  kuma  husna  kafin  tadawo  zata  zuba  mata  acikin  abinci   
Duk abinda take  fada  husna  najinta  
Sallama tayi  tana fadin  mama  Alhaji yazo  kuma  banga  lemo  ba  a ina  suke  nakai  mishi

Cike  da  farin ciki talatu  ta  mike  tafito  falon  tace  a  a  har  yazo  Alhaji  to  muje  na  dauko  miki  lemun  inason  yayi  sanyi  ne  kinsan  yan  hutu  baa  basu  lemo  ba  sanyi 

Murmishi husna tayi  tace  hakane  mama  muje  kibani  ,kitchen suka  nufa  talatu  ta  dauko  lemo  din  mai  magani  aciki  tace  gashi  idan  kun  gama  kafin  kidawo  zan  fada  miki  indomie  kinji  kina  fitowa  kishigo  ki dauka  ki  tabbatar da kinci 


Husna tace to mama  nan  ta  amshi  lemun  ta  fice a kitchen 


Husna na  fita  taje  wajan  flower din  take  shuke  a  felin  gidan  ta  zubar  da  lemon  din  tana  kuka  sosai  saida  tayi  mai  isarta  tukun ta  gyara  fuskar  ta  tace  Allah  nagode  maka  daka  bani  ikon  jin  wannan  mummunan  abun  da  za'a mana  , dakin  baki  tanufa 

Da sallama tashiga  kafin ta  shigo  yaran  suka  tare  ta  kowa  so  yake  ya  rungume  ta  sai  rige-rige  sukeyi   


Dariya husna tayi tace  a  kubi  a  hankali  mana  yaran  albarka  ,Mimee tace  mommy  nina  rigasu  ni  zaki  farawa  kiss  husna tace kowa  zan  mishi  nan  ta  subbacesu  daya  bayan  daya  tace  to  shikenan  ko  yanzu  muje  muzauna ko 

Duk abinda sukeyi alhaji abdallah na  kallon  su  sai  murmishi  yake  musu sosai  suka  burgeshi  

Gaisawa sukayi  tukun husna ta  zuba  musu  abinci   nan ma  fada  aka  soma  mimee tace  ita  abaki  husna  zata  bata  ganin  haka  yasa suma  sukace  saita  basu  ,Yusuf harya fara kuka  yace mommy  nizaki  fara  bawa  abaki ko  yana  rike  hannun  husna 


Shafa kanshi  husna tayi  tace  duka  zan  baku  kuzauna  saina  nake  baku  one by one 

Zama sukayi suna  murna  nan  tasoma  basu  sunaci  kowa  na  bata  labarin  school  ,dena  basu  tayi tace  ba'a  magana  idan  anacin abinci  kunji ko  karna  kumaji  kunyi  magana  sai  kun  koshi ok? Ta  fada  tana  dan  hade rai 

Ganin ta  hade rai  mustafa yace to  mommy  bazamu  sakeba  munyi shuru. Husna tace  yauwa  yaran albarka  haka  nakesonku nan  taci  gaba  tabasu  abincin 


Alhaji abdallah ko  kawai  al'ajabi da  mamakin  husna  yake  ganin  basu  da  karamin  yaro  agidansu  kuma amma  tasan  yadda  zata  yima  yaro  magana  yabi  gata  karamar  yarinya  amma  hankalin  babba  gareta  

Saida kowa  ya  koshi  tukun  Yusuf yace mommy kema  zauna  mubaki  abaki  kinji,  dariya tamusu  tace  ni  ai  babba  ce  zanci  dakaina  kusha ruwa  ko 

Kwabe fuska mimee tayi tace  haba  mommy  please  kici  zamu  baki  kema  ,,Husna ganin  zasuyi kuka  tace  to  kubani  amma  banda  fada  daya  bayan  daya  zakuban 

Yusuf tsalle yaringa yi  suka  soma  bata  itama  abaki  idan  wannan  yabata  wannan  yabata  layi  sukebi  

Alhaji abdallah shikam  yasamu  abun  kallo  sai  dariya  yake  musu  ganin  ana  bata  abaki sai  wani  bata fuska  takeyi  abincin  har  a  kumatunta  da  hab'arta  zuwa  hancinta  duk  sun  bata  mata  fuska  kowa  sai  kaimata  yakeyi   ganin tana jenye  kai  yayi saurin fadin  kibar mommy  takoshi  

Mimee tace to  wai kin koshi mommy  da sauri  husna tace  eh  tana  goge  fuskar  ta  

Kwashewa da dariya Alhaji abdallah yayi  ganin  yadda  fuskar  husna  takoma  yace  kai  amma  sahibaty kinsha  girki  kinganki  kuwa 

Dama  dai husna  kamar  jira  take  ai  kawai  ta  fashe da  kuka  da sauri yaran  sukayo  wajanta  kowa  na  rungume  ta  

Mimee tace mommy waye  ta  tabaki  daddy ne ko  Yusuf  tambayar ta  yakeyi  kowa  ya  rude  ganin bazata  kyaleba  suma  suka  kwanta  a  jikinta  suna  kuka 

Husna ko  bata san  suna  yiba  kukanta takeyi. Alhaji abdallah  na  musu  dariya  danba  karamin  burgeshi  sukayi  ba  ganin sun  dunk'ule a  guri  daya  kowa  ya  bude  baki  sai  kuka  yakeyi

Bayan yagama dariyar yace mimee kuzo kuyi shuru nizan  rarrashe ta  kunji  kyalewa  sukayi kowa  yatashi  akanta  suka  zauna  a  gefe  

Kallon ta  Alhaji abdallah yayi yace  sahibaty zo nan  naji  waye  yataba ki  nasan  ba  wannan  maganar  bace  ta  saka ki  kuka  

Rarrafawa  tayi  ta  karasa  wajansa  tana  ci  gaba  da  kukanta  


Mik'ar da ita yayi amma  tak'iya ta  tsaya  saima  kara  sautin  kukan  ta  tayi  , ganin  hakan  yasa  alhaji yacewa  su  Yusuf  kuje  waje  kuzauna  a  falo  zan  rarrashi  mommy  da  to  suka  amsa  mishi  nan  sukabar  dakin 


Yana ganin sun tafi  ya  janyota jikinsa ya rungume yana  bubbuga bayanta  alamar  tayi shuru  , saida  tayi  mai  isarta  tukun  tayi  shuru  


Dagota  yayi yace  husna wai  meyafaru  kike  wannan  kukan  kinsan bana  son  ganinki  cikin  damuwa  

Kallanshi tayi  tace  sahiby kayimin alkawarin  bazata  dena  sonaba  ko  awani  hali  nake  ciki

Alhaji abdallah yace  namiki  alkawarin  bazan  taba  gudunki ba  kinmaza  jinin jikina  husna  fadamin  abinda  yafaru 


Husna tafada mishi  abinda talatu  da  tabawa  suka  k'ulla musu  tana  kai karshen maganar  ta  kuma  rungume shi  tace  banason  rasaka  a rayuwa ta  inasonka  da  kaunarka  banaso  wani  abu  yahana  aurenmu  banida  kowa  daga  kai  sai  babana  da  kawata  ku  kadaine  ke  sona  a duniyar nan  bansan  ina  mahaifiya ta  takeba  bansan  danginta  ba  dangin  babana  sun tsaneni  har  kakata  itama  bata  sona 


Wani zafi Alhaji abdallah yakeji  acikin zuciyar jin  kalar  azabar  da  akewa husna  tace  kiyi hakuri  husna insha Allah  ni  zan  tambayi baba  ina  dangin  mamanki  suke  dakuma  dalilin  dayasa  danginsa  suka  tsaneki  da  inda  mamanki  take  tana  raye  ko  ta  mutu 


Ajiyar zuciya tasauke tace  nima  inason  nasan  hakan  amma  baba  saiyacemin  saina  girma  zai  fadamin  bansani ba  ko  inkaine  zai  fadama 


Alhaji abdallah yace  insha Allah ma  idan  nasake  dawowa  yana  gari  zan  tambayeshi  insha Allah  kici  gaba da hakuri  komai  zaizo  daidai  insha Allah  


Sanyi taji  ya  ziyarci  zuciyar ta  tace to  , nan  suka  fito  tamusu  rakiya  har  suka  tafi tukun  takoma  ciki  , kitchen tanufa  daga  indomie  dauka  tayi  ta  juye  a  leda  tayi  gina  a  waje  abinne  , tana  mikewa  tabar wajan  takoma  dakinta 

Zama tayi  bakin gadonta  tace  istagafurullah  Allah  kayafemini  na  zubar  da  abinci  nima  bada  son  raina  na  zubar  ba  Allah  ka  gafarta min  tukun ta  kwanta 


Acen  cikin daji  kuwa  yaki ake sosai  saida  suka  galabaita  tukun  suka  kashe  wannan  halittar   shima  da  taimakon  aljana safiya  ta  gano  makasar  halittar  tukun  suka  kasheta  , bayan  suka  kasheta  zama  sukayi  suna  hutawa 


Sarki ya  kallesu  yace  gaskiya  ina godiya agareku  masu taimako  Allah yasaka muku  da  alkhairi  lalle  kece 𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  mai  hana  zalinci  munatajin  labarinki  amma  bamu  taba  ganinki  ba  sai  gashi  kinxo  inda  muke  kin  taomakemu  gaskiya  naji  dadi  godiya  mai  tarin  yawa  yagareki 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace ba komai wannan  ai  aikin mu  taimakon  bayin Allah   mu  zamu  yuce  saboda dama  akan  hanya  muke 

Sarki yace to  shikenan  aljanar jarumai  Allah  yakareki  daga  duk  wani  sharri  , aljana safiya tace ameen  nagode  sai  wata  rana  nan  suka  juya  


Yana  tsaye  tsuntsuwar ta  tadawo  suffar  ta  tahau  har  tasoma  tashi  sama  , ta  kalloshi  da  murmishi  akan fuskar ta  tace  na  manta  ya  sunanka  


Da karfi  yace  sunana  sarki  adam sulaiman   nagode  sai  wata  rana  yana  daga  musu  hannu  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  to  insha Allah  , sai  kuma  tace  adam sulaiman  barin  zancen  tayi  kawai  sukaci  gaba da tafiya 


Tabawa  ma  yau  anzo  da  gulma   gyara zama  tayi  tace  ,yaua  talatu  wannan  maganin  kinbasu  kuwa 

Dariya talatu tayi tace  sosai makuwa  bari  kiji  na  kira  husnar 


Husna! Husna!  Jin ta  masa  tace  zo  nan  maza 


Shigowa tayi da sallama  amsawa  sukayi  da  zauna  akasa  tace gani  mama  


Talatu tace  wannan lemon kinkaiwa  Alhaji yasha  kema  kinci  indomie  dana  fada  miki 


Kan husna akasa tace  eh mama  duka na  cinye  shima  yasha  , cike da  farin ciki tabawa  tace  to  tashi  jeki  , fita  husna tayi  tabar dakin


Tabawa tace to  yanzu  tinda  yace  zankoma  gobe  na  karbo  sauran  ki  kawo  dubu  hamsin,  kudin talatu tabata  nan  sukayi sallama  ta  tafi  gida

A hanya sai dariyar mugunta  takeyi tace  hmmm  wai  ni  su  yaya  zakiga  yadda  dukiyar  Alhaji abdallah mai dala  zata  dawo  mallakin  nida  rukayya  ,,a  haka  taje  gidan  ta  tace  gobe  sanmako  zatayi  suke  wajan  boka...
