*33—34*



Yau  tabawa  ko  baccin  kirki  takasa  yi  saboda  mugun  k'udurinta  akan  husna  Allah Allah  takeyi   gari ya  waye  ta  tafi....da  sassafe  ta  shirya  ko  abinci  bataciba  ta  kama  hanyar  gidan  ladi  

Knocking tayi  a  kofar  gidan  ladi  , Ladi  na  bacci  taji  ana  knocking da  haushi  haushi  tace  wai  uban  waye  yake  damuna  da  wannan  safiyar  tabawa  tace  ubanki  yar iska  ni  budemin  kofa  kona  buge  da  kaina  nashigo  , Ladi tace  tsuwwww kefa  tabawa  bakida  burki  kefa koda  yake  dama  k'usa  guda  ta  kunce  akanki  in  banda  jaraba  irin  taki  da  bak'ar  zuciya ki  kasa  bari  goma  tayi  tin kafin  karfe  7:00 AM tayima  zakizo min  gida  


Tabawa tace  ai  duk hanya  daya  muke  nidai  budemun  nashigo  ki  shirya  mutafi  , bude  kofar  ladi  tayi  tashigo  suka  wuce  a  falo  suka  yada  zango  

Ladi tace to yanzu  shiga  kitchen kimana  breakfast ni  zan  shiga  nayi wanka kafin  nafito  kingama  tabawa tace  to  shikenan  saikin  fito  nan  tayi  kitchen  din  don  daura musu  abinci 

Dankali ta  fek'e  ta  yayyanka  tukun ta  soya  k'wai  ta   shayi tamusu  tana  gamawa    ladi  tafito  zama  tayi  sukaci  suna  gamawa  suka  kama  hanyar  gidan  boka 



Aljana safiya  fuskar ta dauke da  murmishi  tana  kara  godiya  ga  Allah  dayake  bata  nasara  akan  makiyanta  gashi  takusa  k'arasawa  dajin bishiyoyi  dan  haka  ta  kara  karfin  gudun  ta  tana  addu'ar  Allah  karya  sake  hadata  da  abinda  zai  tsaidata  a  hanya  inba  salla  ba  ko  cin  abinci  , kallon tsuntsuwar ta tayi tace masoyiya  tsaya mu  tambayi  abba  yanzu  muna  wani  yanki  , tsayawa  tayi  

Aljana safiya ta murza  zoben ta  rufe idonta tayi  saita  soma  magana  tace  ya  abbana  ina  fatan  kana  cikin  amincin Allah da manzonsa  dafatan  kana lfy  ya  jikin  aliyu na  


Abba na  zaune  da  wani littafin addu'o'i a hannun shi  yaji  maganar  safiya  murmishi  yayi  yace  ya ya'ta  muna nan lfy  aliyu jikinsa  da  sauki  sosai  yana  samun  kulawa sosai  daga  wajan  amina  da  hafeez  


Murmishi aljana safiya tayi tace  abbana  yanzu  muna  wani  bangare  yau  ban  tsaya  ba  salla  kadai  da  cin abinci suke  tsaidani  amma  inba  suba  ban  tsayaba  yin  wani  abu ba  muna  ta  tafiya  sosai  


Abba yace  ya  ya'ta   bari  na  duba  miki   shuru  yayi  na  wani  lokaci  tukun  yace  masha Allah  Alhamdulillah  yar albarka   gaskiya  nayi  mamakin  yadda  kika  kai  wannan  wurin  a  k'ank'anin lokaci  haka   kinsan  kuwa  yanzu  kina  kusa  da  dajin  bishiyoyi  sosai  kin  kusantu  shi   bakiji  kamshinsa  ba  da  kina  tsaya ?

murmishin  farin ciki sosai safiya keyi tace  gaskiya  abba  naji dadi  wllh  yanzu  kenan  kina  kusa  da  dajin  bishiyoyi  kai Alhamdulillah 

Shima murmishi  yayi  yace  eh  hakane  amma  fa  kafin  ki  karisa  shine  wahalar  saboda  mugayen  halittun  dake  bakin  dajin  da  kewayanshi  amma  zan  tayaki  da  addu'a  Allah  yakareki  daga  sharrin  su  insha Allah   rabin  shekara  zai  kaiki  , indai  har  kinje  kin  ciro  maganin  to  a lokaci  daya  zaki  ganki  a  gida  haka,  QA'idar dajin  bishiyoyi  take  

Indai  kajeshi  to  aljnnun cikinsa  ke  kawoka  gida  ina  fatan  kin  fashimci  bayani na  yar albarka  yanzu  ki  shirya  da  shirin kota  kwana  saboda  akoda  yaushe  zaki  iya  haduwa  da  wannan  halittun  na  kusan  dajin bishiyoyi  sune  zasu  hanaki  shiga  cikin dajin  da  wuri   zakiyi  ta  fama  kafin  kishiga  , ina  miki  fatan alkhairi  ya'ta  nasara  ta  lullubeki  aduk  inda  kike  


Cike da  kwarin gwiwa aljana safiya  tace ameen  abbana  inasonka  sosai  na  matsu  nadawo  aliyu  ya  samu lfy  kona  waiwayi  husna  

Abba yace wacece  husna  safiya? 

Aljana safiya  tace  wannan  maganar  saina  dawo  zan  fada  maka  inason  taimaka  mata  ne  yanzu dai  mubar komai  zan  maka  bayani  idan  nadawo  gida 

Abba yace to shikenan  Allah yamiki albarka adawo lfy  


Aljana safiya tace ameen  tukun  ta  soma  tafiya   murmishi  tayi  tace  Allah sarki husna  kina  cikin  raina  da  zuciya  ta  keda  su  Alhaji da  hajiya  ga  kuma  bilkisu  ta  garbati  , dariya  tayi  tunowa  da  su  siyama  yadda  take  sakin  fitsari hadda  zawo   tace  insha Allah  zankai  muku  ziyara  nan  bada  dadewa ba   , 



Husna  na  shara  taji  kanta  namata  ciwo  sosai  ga  zazzabin  daya  rufeta  duk  ta  kasa  gama  aikin  gidan  ga  wankin ta  a  gefe  sai  rawar  sanyi  takeyi  hakorin ta  har  hadewa  sukeyi  saboda  tsananin zazzabi  

Talatu ce  ta  fito   daga daki  tana  hamma  ganin  husna  a  durkushe  tana  kyerma  , Talatu tace  ke  husna lfy kike  rawar  sanyi  ba lokacin  saba  

Da kyer  husna  tace  mama  zazzabi  nakeyi  da  ciwon  kai  shiyasa  ban  gama  aikin  da  wuriba, Talatu tace  aikin  banza  to ina  ruwana  da  zazzabin  ki  yar  iska  kila  bakinje  alhaji abdallah ya  lallabaki  ya  dirka  miki  ciki  shine  kike  zazzabi  zo  ingani  wllh  indai  ciki  ne  dake  saina  yankaki  agidan nan  , matsowa  husna tayi  talatu ta  duba  hannun ta  tukun tace bude idon ki  nagani  budewa  husna tayi  gabanta  na  dukan  uku  uku  , bataga  komai sakinta  tayi  tace  nidai  banga  komai  ba  amma  maza  ki  gama  min  aikina  ki  kira  Alhaji  yakaiki  asibiti  kuma  dani za'aje  inma  cikin  shege  kika  yo  ai  naji  


A hankali husna tace  to  mama  tukun ta  kama  shara  tana  tana  kukan  rashin  gatan  da  batadashi  wai  talatu  tana  mata  sharrin  tayi  ciki   haka  taci gaba  da  aikin  gidan  tana  jin  kanta  kamar  zai  rabe  gida  biyu  hakan  takejinsa  amma  babu  yadda  ta  iya  dole  tayi  gama  aikin  koda  zata  samu  zama  



Su tabawa  sun  isa  wajan  boka mai gobe da nisa  yana  ganinsu  yace  kai  gaskiya  naji  dadin  ganinku  wannan  matar  ina  kewarta  dole  na  tsareta  na  kwana biyu ko  uku  danna  more  na  huta  naji  dadi  a  wajanta  , ajiyar zuciya yasauke ganin  sun  kusa  zuwa  gareshi


Ladi tace  sai  boka mai gobe da nisa  la'ananne  tsinanne  kayi  taka  kayi  takowa  boka  namu  maganin kjkanmu 

Kecewa yayi da wata mahaukaciyar dariya  yana  shafo  kirjin ladi  yace  mutumiya ta  nasan  da  zuwanku  shiyasa  nake  yinki  yanzu  mai  kuke  so  a  baku 

Dariya ladi tayi tace  to  kamai  bukata  nan  ita  zata  fadi  abinda  za'a  masu 

Kallon shi yamaida  ga  tabawa  da  kanta  yake  a  kasa  tinda  sukazo  taji  tashiga  sabon  yanayi  jin  muryar  boka  , a hankali boka  yace  tabawa  yanzu  me  kikkeso  a  musu 

Dagowa tayi  tana murmishi tace  yanzu  tinda  sunci  maganin  kace  sai  bayan  aure  zaiyi  aiki ajikinsu   to  yanzu  inason  husna  ta  lalace  take  bin  maza  har  inda suke  tana  basu  kanta  kaga  idan  alhaji abdallah  yagani  saiya  dena  sonta  afasa  auren  , daga nan  saika  daura  mishi  son  rukayya 


Gyara zama  boka mai gobe da nisa yayi  yace  to gaskiya   aikin  yana  da wahalar  yi  saboda  sai kinyi  kwana  biyu  anan  ana  miki  wanka  nasara  dan  burinki  yacika  


Da sauri  tabawa tace  har  kwana biyu  boka  ka  taimakeni   yarana  kanana  ne  dayar  batafi  wata  shida  da  yayewa  ba  idan  bana  nan  wazai  kulamin  da  ita 


Dariya boka  yayi  yace    ai  indai  kinason  biyan  bukatar  ki  dole  kibi  doka  idan  kuma  bakiso  toga  hanya nan  , amma  a  zuciya cewa  yake  bama  zan  bari  kitafiba  a  kwana biyun 


Ladi  ta  kalli  boka  sukayi  yar ido  tukun  tajuya  ga  tabawa  tace  kefa  kamar  kanki  bayan  ja  wllh  to  meye  kwana  biyu  yasu  watama  sukeyi  dan  biyan  bukatar su  

Tabawa tace to  shikenan  na  amince  

Boka ko  farin ciki  yacikashi sosai  yace  to  kije  dakin  da  kika  sama  zuwa  suna  jiranki  anjima  kifito  saikuyi  Allah  da  ladi  , Tabawa tace  to  nan  tashige  dakin 


Dariya ladi tayi  tace  da  kyau bokana  kana  shagalinka  wllh  karka  raga  mata  tinda  tanason  dole  sai  sunyi  kudi  kabiya  mata  burinta  kaima  tabiya  maka  naka  buri  saika  fito  ina  jiranku 

Dariya shima  bokan  yayi  yace  ladi  bazaki  ganeba  matar  nan  tahadu  sosai  shiyasa  nace  saitayi  kwana biyu  dan  nake  hutawa  da  ita  


Ladi tace  hakane fa saina jiku  nan  yayi  dakin  , yana  zuwa  ya  afka  mata  saida  ya  samu nutsuwa tukun  ya  barta  yayi  saurin  fitowa   , itama  fitowa  tayi  tana  kai  jin dadin  abinda  aka  mata  

Tace ladi  idan  rukayya  taje  gidanki  kice  sai  nan  da  kwana biyu  zan  dawo  nasan  zata  gane  , Ladi tace  ok  nina  tafi  bayan  kwana biyu  zanzo  na  daukeki  mukoma  gida , sallama  sukayi   da  ladi  takama  hanya  , tana  wa  tabawa  dariyar  batada  wayo 


Boka mai gobe da nisa  abin  nema  yasamu  dan  haka  yacewa  tabawa  ki  koma  ciki duk  abinda  kike  bukata  akwai  a ciki  , Tabawa ta  amsa  da  to  nan  tayi  cikin dakin 


Aljana safiya  nata tafiya  ba  tsayawa  kuma  bata  dena  jin  kamshin  dajin bishiyoyi ba  tana addu'ar  Allah  ya  gajarce  mata  nisa  tafiyar ta  , wani  daji  ta  biyo  mai  mugun  zafi sosai  gashi  akwai  tsaya  a  haka  suka  ringa  gudu sosai  zafin  nashigar  su  ita  tsuntsuwar  ta  da  kyer  suka  fice  suna  fita  hallau  wani  dajin  suka  kaiwa  kai  
Shi kuma  duk kunama  ce  acikinsa  manya  manya  idan ta  cijeka  saidai  wani  ba  kaiba  dan  haka  aljana  safiya  tayi  shirinta  sosai  ta  safe  jikinta  ta  yadda  koda  sun  hau  kanta  da  cizo  bazataji  zafi  , bismillah tayi   ta  kalli  tsuntsuwar ta  tace  masoyiya  yanzu  kuma  mundena  tafiya  a  sama  dole  ta  kasa  zamubi   kizama  jaruma  nasan  ke  jarumar  ce  kishirya  zamubi  cikin  kunama  nan  gabanmu  dajin  duk  itace  dan  haka  kishirya  , nan  da nan  ta  rikide  ta  dawo  mace  itama  ta  rufe  jikinta  kamar yadda  aljana safiya  ta  umarceta  , nan suka  kama  hanyar  shiga  dajin 


Husna  nata  aiki  bayan  ta  gama  tayi  girki  duk  a  adaddafe  take  komai  tana  gamawa  ta  lallaba  takishiga  dakinta  wayar  ta  ta  dauko  , ta  danna  kiran number  alhaji  , yana dauka  ta  saka  kuka  


Ai  tuni  ya  rikice  yana  tambayar  ta  sahibaty  menene  yasameki  mama  ce  tadakeki  ko  wani  abun  aka  miki  duk  ya  rude  

Da kyer husna ta  iya  tsayar  da  kukanta  tace  banda  lafiya  mutuwa  kila  zanyi  kaxo  ka  kaini  asibiti  tana  kasarawa  kukan  gudu 

Da sauri  yace subhanallah ganinan  zuwa  kinji ina  unguwar  ma  nazo  ganin  abokina ganinan zuwa  , to  amma  ina  baba  yake 


Cikin kuka husna tace  baba  baya  nan  shiyasa  mama  tayi  ta  sakani  aiki  yanzu  haka  bana  iya  gani  sosai  saboda tsananin ciwon da  kaina  yakemin  please  nidai  kazo  


Alhaji abdallah yace  fito  dama  nabiyo  hanyar gidan ku   yanzu na  karaso  fito  maza  sahibaty  ya  kashe kiran  umma  ya  kira  yafada  mata  yace  umma  husna  ba  lafiya  kisame mu  a  asibitin , Hassan   umma tace to shikenan  ganinan   nan  yakashe  wayar 


Yana  karasowa  kofar gidan su  husna ya  fito  da  sauri  sai  kallon  kofar gidan  yakeyi  inason  tafito  


Husna tana gama waya da  alhaji ta  saka hijab din ta  ta  fito  dakin talatu taje  tace  mama  alhaji yazo  mufita  zai  kaini  asibiti  da kyer take  hada  kamomin  magana  saboda  ciwon  kan  dake  addabar ta  , Talatu tace  sako  mayafi  sai  kuma  tace  yar iska  inma  cikine  yamiki  sai  kuje  azubar  dashi  nama  fasa  zuwa  ficemin  a  daki  wahalalliya  kawai  ta  hankada  husna  ta  fadi  kanta  ya  bugu  da  bango  


Da  rarrafe  husna  take  fita  daga  dakin  sosai  take  jin  numfashin  ta  na  kokarin  guduwa  , haka  ta  ringa  rarrafowa  har  bakin  kofar  gida  , ganin  alhaji abdallah  yana  safa  da marwa  magana  takeson  mishi  amma  takasa  , tana  d'ag'a  mishi  hannun  amma  ya  juya  mata  baya    a hankali  tasoma  mikewa   tana  tashi  tsaya  ta  yanke  jiki  tafadi  sumanmiya  

Karar faduwar husna  yasa  sh  juyawa  da sauri  husna yagani  kwance  ai  da  gudu  a  isa  gareta  bai  tsaya  komai  ya  dauketa  a mota  yasaka  ta  yayi  asibiti  da  ita  


Ikon Allah ne  kadai  yakai  Alhaji abdallah asibitin  duk  hankalin shi  atashe  yake   yana  zuwa  ya  daka  birki  da  karfi  ya  fito  , kowa  sai  kallon  shi  yakeyi  suna  tausaya miki  ganin  yadda  yafita  hayyacinsa  



Dauko husna yayi a  hannu  yana  nosee   hassan da  karfi  yake  kiransu  , da  gudu suka fito  ya  daurata  akan  gadon  sukayi  wani  daki  da  husna 


Sai  zarya  yake  yakasa  zama  abokinsa  hassan  ne  yafito  da  gudu  gudu  ganin Alhaji abdallah yace  ina  husna  take ? Kasa  magana  yayi  sai  nuna  mishi  yayi  inda  aka  shiga  da ita  da sauri  yashiga  dakin



Bayan awa biyu  doctor hassan  yafito  yana  sharce  zufa   da sauri  alhaji abdallah  yayo  kanshi  yana fadin  hassan  yajikin  sahibaty  na  maiyake  samunta  yanzu  awani  hali  take  kafada  min  mana  hassan  , yana  cakumar  kwalar  doctor hassan


Cire hannun shi  yayi  daga  wuyanshi   ya  zauna  dashi  a  daya  daga  cikin  kujerun  dake  wajan  yace  am  so sorry  abokina  husna  ta.....

*35—36*





Doctor hassan  yace  am so sorry  abokina  husna  tana  cikin  mawiyacin  hali  maiyasa  baku  kawota  da  wuriba  gashi   ciwon  yaci  karfinta  


Dafe  saitin zuciyar  shi  alhaji abdallah yayi  jin  yadda  take  masa  zafi  yace  hassan  kafadamin  wacce  cuta  ce  take  damun  husna ta  banason  kaboye  min  komai  


Numfashi  doctor  hassan ya  ka  kafin  yace   yarinyar  nan  zazzabi yaci karfinta  har  jinin  jikinta  ya  kare  da  ruwa  yanzu  ansaka  mata  ruwa  sai  jini  muke  bukata  wanda  za'a  saka  mata  

Idon Alhaji abdallah a  rufe  yace  da  kuma  me  


Jum  doctor hassan yayi  saboda  baisan yadda  abokin  nasa  zai  dauki  maganar  tashiba  

Jin  bai  bashi amsa  ba  ya  bude  idonshi  akan  doctor  hassan  yace  kaifa  nake  sauraro bana  son ka  boyemin  komai 


Saida  doctor hassan  ya  furzar  da  iska  mai  zafi  daga  bakinshi  tukun  yace  bansan  ya  zaka  dauki  maganar  tawaba   amma  zan  fada  maka  tinda  ka  nace  


Alhaji abdallah yace  eh  fada  kayi sauri  kuma  saboda  yafara  jin  haushin  doctor hassan  ganin  yana  jan  mishi  rai


Doctor hassan yace  to  bayan zazzabi  da  rashin  jini  da  ruwa  husna  nada  hawan  jini  a  halin  yanzu  jinin  ta  yahau  sosai  fiye  da  yadda  muke  tunani  saboda  tsananin  tunanin  da  yakeyi  shi  yakawo  karin  hawan  jinin  ta  


A razane  ya  kalli  doctor hassan  yace   has...ha....hassan  ban  gane  mai  kake  nufiba   kana  so  kacemin  husna ta  na  fama  da  hawan  jini  

Rikeshi  doctor  hassan  yace  wannan haka  yanzu  muje  a  gwadaka  in  jininku  yazo  daya  sai  adauka  idan  baizo  daya  ba  saika  saya  


Alhaji abdallah  ji  yayi  kanshi  na  kuya  mishi  sunan  Allah  yaringa  ambato  tukun yasamu   nutsuwa  yace  to  muje  a  gwada 


Dakin  gwaji  suka  nufa  ana  gwadashi  yazo  daya  dana husna  , babu  bata  lokaci  aka  zuk'i  leda  biyu  doctor hassan  yakira  wata  nosee  yace  taje  tasakawa  husna  wannan  jinin  ta  karin  ruwan  yanzu  ya  kare  , ta  amsa  da  ok  sir  tafice  a  dakin  ta  nufi  dakin  da  husna  take  


Ledar ruwan  tafidda  tasaka  mata  na  jinin  tukun  tafita 



Fitowa  sukayi  daga  dakin  gwajin  jini  suka  koma  office  din  doctor  hassan   

Zama sukayi  doctor hassan  ya  kalli  alhaji abdallah  yace  abokina  ina  da  shawara  ko  zaka  amince  da  shawarar tawa? 

Kallon shi  alhaji abdallah yayi  yace  inajinki  abokina  kafadi  shawarar ka 

Gyara zama  doctor Hassan  yayi  yace  maizai  hana  kaje  gidansu  husna  gobe  a  daura  muku  aure   saboda  ka  kula  da  ita  ina  kyatata  zaton  yarinyar  nan  wahala  takesha  agidansu   idan  aka  daura  muku  auren  inyaso  daga  baya  she  ayi  shagalin  bikin  a  dauke  ta  takoma  wajan  umma 


Ajiyar zuciya yasauke  yace  gaskiya  naji  dadin  wannan  shawarar  taka  insha Allah  zan   kira baba  nafada  mishi  komai  gobe  sai  adaura  mana  auren  dan  nima  wllh  na  k'agara  husna  tadawo  kusa  dani  


Murmishin jin dadi doctor hassan  yayi  yace  to masha Allah hakan  yayi  kyau  Allah  yakaimu  goben  

Ya  amsa  da  ameen yace  zan  iya  ganinta   


Doctor hassan  yace  eh  zaka  ita  shiga  kaganta  


Mikewa  yayi yace  ok  bari  nagani  

A  tare  suka  fita  doctor hassan  yayi  sashen  duba  marassa  lfy  shikuma  alhaji  ya  nufi  dakin  da  husna  ke  cikin 


Talatu  na  zaune  sai  dariyar  mugunta  takeyi  tace  aikin banza  yarinyar nan  ko  tantama  babu  cikin  shege  ne  taje  yayimata  ni  ba  ruwana  ko sau  goma  zai  mata  ciki  suna  zubarwa  cen  ta  matse musu  indai  anke  kawomin  kudi  shashasha  kawai  nima  gidan  zan  bari  , ta  mike  ta  dauki  mayafinta  ta  rufe  dakinta  tabar  gidan  tanufi  gidansu 


Yana shiga  yaga  husna  kwance  ta  farka  tana  kallon  sama  alamar  tunani   


Takowa  yayi  a  hankali  yazo  bakin  gadon  zama  yayi  akan  kujerar  dake  gefen  gadon  , ruk'o  hannun  ta  yayi  yace  Sahibaty  na  kintashi  sannu  Allah  Allah yabaki lfy  , jin  bata  amsaba  ya  dan  dafa  kafadar  ta 


A firgice ta  juyo  ganin  masoyin  nata  saita  soma  yunk'urin  tashi   taimaka  mata  yayi ta  tashi zaune 

K'in  zama  tayi  ta  fada jikinsa  tasaki  marayan kuka  wanda  kanajinsa  kasan  daga cikin zuciyar ta  yake  fita  


Bai  hanata  kukan  ba  ya  rungume ta  yanajin  duk  zuciyar  shi  ba  dadi  ganin  abar  son  nashi  cikin  wannan  halin  , bubbuga  bayanta  yake  saida  tayi  mai  isarta  tukun  ta  kyale  


Dagowa tayi  tana kallon shi  na  yan  dak'ik'u  tukun tace  sahiby  dan Allah ka  dauni  banason  nakoma  gida  ni  zan  zauna  awajan  umma    mama  bata  tausaya  min  komai  agidan  nan  ninakeyi 


Tausayin tane  yakuma  lullubeshi  yace  insha Allah bazata  koma  gidaba  wajan  umma  zaki  zauna  gobe  za'a  daura  auren mu  kinji  yanzu  bari  na  kira  baba   yace  anjima  zai  shigo gari 


Da sauri  husna  ta  dago  tace  da  gaske  bazan  koma  gidanmu  ba  da  umma  zan  zauna  


Alhaji abdallah yace  eh  sahibaty  insha Allah  yanzu  zan  kira  baba  saiku   gaisa  wayarshi  ya  fiddo  ya  danna  kiran  number  baba  , ringing daya ana  biyu  ya  dauka  , yace  assalam alaikum  baba  barka  da  warhaka  yakasuwa  


Daga  cen  bangaren  baba  yace  lfy lau  ya  yaran  yasu  maman  naka  


Alhaji abdallah yace  Alhamdulillah  baba  yauwa  dama  baba  inason  neman  wata  alfarma  a  gareka  Allah  yasa zaka  amince  



Murmishi baba  yayi  yace  ba  damuwa  injinka  


Alhaji abdallah yace  baba  inason  a  daura  auren  mu  da  husna  gobe  insha Allah  idan  kadawo 


Mamaki ne  yakama  baba  yace  gobe  gobe   alhaji  to  wani  abu  ne  yafaru  ?


Yace eh  baba  husna ce  ba  lfy  nan  ya  kwashe komai  yafada  mishi  yadau  da  cewa  shine  nakeson  a  daura  auren  gobe  saboda  nake  kula  da  ita  har  tasamu  lfy  kaga  haramun  ne  nake  taba  jikinta  babu  aure  a  tsananin  mu  


Cike da damuwa  baba  yace  yaushe  wannan  ciwo  yakama  min  husna  Allah  yabata  lfy  nima  yanzu  haka  ina  hanya  kuma  wannan  shawara  tayi   nagode sosai  da  kake  kulamin  da  husna  Allah  yamaka  albarka  , nasan  ko  yanzu  na  mutu  bazan  yi  kukan  halin da  husna  zata  tsinci  kanta  bayan mutuwa  ta  tinda  kaga   Allah  yabarku  har  aljantul fiddaus 


Cikin jin dadi  yace  ameen  baba  nima  nagode  da  yardar  daka  bani  Allah  yasaka  da alkhairi  Allah  yakawoka  lfy  


Baba yace  ameen  yaron kirki  sai  kajini  anjima  zanzo  sallama  sukayi  ya  kashe  kiran    


Husna  na  kallon  ya  kashe kiran  kuma  bai  nata  sun  gaisa  da  baban  ba  aiko  tasaki  kuka  

Da sauri  ya  kalleta yace  sahibaty  lfy   wani  wajan  yake  miki  ciwone  


Banka  mishi  harara  tayi  tace  ba  Kaine ba  kace  zaka  bani  mugaisa  da  baba  shine  ka  kashe  kiran  bamuyi  magana  ba 


Dafe  kai  yayi yace  oh sorry  sahibaty  wllh  na  manta  ne bari  anjima  zan  kira  miki  shi  


Da haka  yamata  wayo  tace  eh  shikenan   


Umma ce  tashigo   dakin  hankalin ta  atashe  ganin husna  a  zaune  sai  taji  sanyi  aranta   tace   sannu  husna  Allah  yabaki  lfy  


Sunkuyar da kai  husna  tayi  tana  mai  jin  kunya  umma  ta  ganta  a jikin  alhaji  , tace  ameen umma  inasu  mimee  ne  


Murmishi umma tayi  tace  anjima  zasuzo  

Kwantar da  ita  yayi  yadawo  inda  umma  take  , kallon shi  tayi  tace  meke damunta  ne


Zama yayi  yafada  mata  komai  da  maganar dasukayi  da  baba  


Sosai umma  taji  dadi  tace  to  Allah  yakaimu  goben  insha  Allah  , ya  amsa da ameen  



Acen  bangaren  aljana safiya  da  tsuntsuwar  ta  sosai  suka  ringa  gudu  saida  sukazo  bakin  dajin  tukun  ta  tsaya  ta  kalli  tsuntsuwar  ta  tace  masoyiya  kin  shirya  ?


Tsuntsuwar  data  koma  saurayi  tace  nashirya  ya  shugaba  ta  


Murmishi husna tayi  tace  to  muje  da  bismillah  suka  soma  taka  kasa  suna  tafiya   cike  da  jarumta  , saida  suka  shiga  tsakiyar  dajin  tukun  sukaga  bataliyar  kunamu  a  gabansu  suna  tunkarosu 


Tsaya  sukayi  aljana safiya  tace  masoyiya  mu  gwada  baiwarmu  mugani  ko  Allah  zai  taimakemu  mu fita  daga  cikin  wannan  dajin  


Tace  to  ranki  yadade  , saida  suka  zo  kuna  da  kunamun  shesuka  bace  


Bayyana  sukayi  a  nesa  dasu  abin mamaki  hallau  sukaga  wata  bataliyar   a  gabansu  


Aljana safiya  tace  to ikon Allah  bari  na  tambayi  abba  yazamuyi  mufita  daga  wannan  dajin  

Zoben hannun ta  ta  murza  tana  rufe  idonta  , murmishi tayi  tukun  tace  abbana  gamu  cikin  daji  mai  kunama  wanda  basuda  iyaka  yazamuyi  mufita  ya  abbana 


Abba  yace  gaskiya  wannan  dajin  yanada  hadari  sosai   mafita  dayace  ya  ya'ta  


Aljanar safiya  tace  abbana  famun  naji  ko  munfita  daga  wannan  azababben  daji  bayan  kunamu  hadda  zafi  gareshi  kamar  ana  hura  wuta  



Abba yace  to  mafitar  itace  ki yi  anfani  ta  zoben  hannun  ki  haskenta  shi zai  hana  su  ganku  sai  ku  wuce  daga  dajin  batare  da  kowa  yaji  ciwoba 



Murmishin jin dadi aljana safiya  tayi  tace  abba  to  tana  zanyi  anfani da  zoben  harya  hasken  ta  ya  hana  su  ganmu 


Abba yace  zaki  murzata  kamar  yadda  kikeyi idan  zaki  kirani  , to haske  mai  yawa  zai  fito  daga  cikinta  saiya  mamaye  dajin  idan  yayi  hakan  sakuyi  sauri  kubar  dajin 


Aljana safiya tace  to  abbana  Allah yakara  girma  ina  sonka  abbana  

Murmishi yayi yace to  shikenan  Allah  yakareku  , gabama  haka  zakiyi  har  kikai  dajin  bishiyoyi  


Aljana safiya  tace to  abba saika  jini  bude  idon  ta   tayi  tace  masoyiya  matso  kusana  ki  rikeni  abba  yabamu  mafita,  matsowa  tayi  kusan  aljana safiya  


Zoben  ta  murza  nan  take  hasken  yafito  ya mamaye  dajin   madallah  tayi  suka  b'ace   basu  bayyana ko  inaba  sai  bakin  dajin  , murmishi husna tayi  tace  Allah yabar minkai  babana  


Dajin  na  gaba  shima  haka  suka  tarar  macizai  yadda  babanta  yafada  mata  haka  tayi  suka  wuce  dajin  


Haka  suka  ringa  ratsa  dajinjika  Allah  yana  kub'utar  dasu   kuma  sosai  aljana safiya  take  addu'a  tana  jin  kamshin dajin bishiyoyi  na  kara  kusanto  su  



Sarki shamsu  yanzu  yana  dakin  da  madubin  tsafinsa  yake  yana  hada  karfi  da  tsafi  don  zuwa  yak'ar  aljana safiya   


Yana zaune  yace  aljana safiya   kenan  saina  kasheki  na  kashe  shugaba  don  na  mallaki  wannan  zoben  da  takobin  dake  gareki , nine  zan  mulki  mutane da aljanu  , karkiga  kin  kashemun  abubuwa  dayawa  to  ina  nan  zuwa  gareki  saina  lalata miki  jin  dadin  rayuwar  ki  ni  sarki shamsu  duk  wanda  yaci  tuwo  dani  miya  yasha  wllh  , yana  huce  


A  bangaren dodon  sarki shamsu  ma hakan  take  shima  yana  alwashin   saiya  kashe  aljana safiya  



Washe gari   kawun Alhaji abdallah  yazo  da  sauran  abokansa  aka  daura  auren  su   akan sadaki  naira  dubu  dari  biyar  


Da farko  baba  yace  wannan  kudin  yayi yawa  shi  subu  hamsin  ma  ya  isa  basai  an  bada  dayawa  ba  , alhaji nura  yace  ba'a  su  haka  zasu  bada  , haka  baba  ya  amsa   

Yan anguwar  su  namishi  barka  suna  nashi  hannun  bayan  kowa  ya  tafi  baba  yashiga  gida   ranshi fass  


Kallon shi  talatu tayi  tace  a  a  malam  yaudai  kadawo  da  fara'a  


Murmishin  sa  yadad'a  fadadawa  ya  mik'a  mata  kudin  hannun  shi 

Baki a  washe  talatu  tace  wannan  fa  malam  


Baba yace  sadakin  yarkine  husna  , yanzu  aka  daura  auren  ta  da  alhaji abdallah  to  wannan  ki  ajiye  awajan  ki  kafin  wani  lokaci 


Talatu  girgiza tayi  tace  ahayye  ni  talatu  yanzu  wannan  duk  sadakin  husna tane  kai  masha Allah   yanzu  malam  yaushe  zata  tare  to  


Murmishin dadi  baba  yayi  dama  dan  ya  tushe  bakin talatu  ne  shiyasa  yabata  kudin  yasan  akwaita  ta  son  kudi  kamar  ranta  yace   daga  asibiti  zata  koma  gidan  mahaifiyar  shi  ne  idan  tasamu  lfy  zata  koma  gidanshi   a lokacin  zaayi  walima  


Talatu  saboda  tsananin  farin cikin  zata  dama  mai  kudi   batasan  lokacin  tada  rungume  baba  tana  mai  kiss  a  goshinsa  , tace  kai  to  Alhamdulillah  Allah  yabata  lfy  idan  sun  koma  zanje  na  gano  ta  yasunan  anguwar  tasu  maman  alhajin 

Dariya  baba yayi yace  a  ikon Allah  duk  murnar  ce  tasa  yau  aka  rungume  ni  hadda  kissing  

Farr talatu tayiwa  baba  da  ido  tace  ces  makuwa  ai  yau  ranar  ta  musamman  ce  tinda  husna  tayi  aure  


Baba yace  to  masha Allah  sunan  unguwar  su  unguwar  dosa   nan  ne  gidansu 


Talatu tace  to shikenan  zanje  naganta  ko  gobene  

Baba yace  to shikenan  bari  nafita  kasuwa 


Da sauri  talatu  ta  dauko  mishi  jakarshi  ta  rakashi  har  kofar  gida  tana mishi  beyy  beyy 


Shi  baba  kar  mamaki  yake  yadda  talatu  take  son  kudi  yace  Allah  Allah yashirya  ki  talatu  


Talatu  na  dawowa  ciki  ta  kira  tabawa  amma  bata  sameta  ba  hakura  tayi  tace  gobe  zanje  gidanta  nafada  mata  wannan  daddad'an  labarin  



Washe gari  ladi  ta  tafi  dauko  tabawa  tana  zuwa  gidan  boka mai gobe da nisa   bata  ganshi  a  wajeba  ,dariyar  yan  bariki  tayi  tace  shege  boka  yana  cen  yana  kwasar  gara  bari  na  huta  wllh  kuyi  kugama  ina  jiranku  


Boka mai gobe da nisa  na  ciki  yana  cewa  tabawa  yau  zata  tafi  gida  dan  haka  za'a  mata  wankan  bankwana  , 

Cike da  farin ciki  tabawa  tace  to  shikenan  ayi  sauri  ayimin  ko 


Dariya boka  yayi  yace  to  shiga  dakin  duhu  ana  za'a  miki  , da sauri  tabawa  tashiga  dakin  


Tana  shiga  yabita  abaya  yana  zuwa  ya  afka mata    kullin  haka  sukeyi  ba  dare  ba  rana  

Ita kuwa  sakarya  tabawa  har  batason idan  tashiga  dakin  hudu  tafito  da  wuri   saboda  abinda  ake  mata  , yana  mata  sugar 


Sai  da  suka  kwashi  awa  biyu hayin  na  uku  tukun  boka  yafito  waje  ,suna  gama  ido  da ladi  suka  kwashe da  dariya  



Isowa yayi yace  a  mutuniyar  ai  naji  zuwanki  a lokacin  bana  kasar  ne  ina  wata  duniya  


Dariya itama  ladi  tayi  tace  ai  naga  alama  


Tabawa ce  tafito  tana  daura  zaninta  da fara'arta  ta  karaso  tace  kawata  kinxo  


Murmishi ladi  tayi  tace  eh  muje  ba  lokaci boka zai miki  komai  bashe  kin  amshe  komai ba 



Boka yace  eh  sai  bayan  wata  uku  zaki  dawo  kafin  kizo  zan  hada  miki  maganin  da  yarki  zata  mallaki  alhaji 


Farin ciki ne  ya  lullube  tabawa    kama hannun ladi tayi  suka  tafi  gida 


Suna zuwa  bata  ma  zauna  ba  ta  nufi  gidan  talatu  


Da  murna  talatu ta  tareta  suka  zauna  


Talatu yabata  labarin  auren husna da alhaji abdallah 


A razane  tabawa  take  kallon  talatu  tace  kina  nufin kicemin  an  daura  auren  


Talatu ko ajikin tace  eh  jiya  aka  daura  na  kira  wayarki  baya  shiga 


Bakin cikine  yarufe  tabawa  saita  tuna  maganar boka  murmishi tayi  a  zuciyar ta  tace  bazan  bari  kiji  dadin  aure  ba  husna  saina  raba  auren  ,kallon talatu tayi tace  to  shikenan  yanzu  saimu  jira  tasamu  lfy  ko  


Talatu tace eh  kuma  sadakin ta  dubu dari biyar  


Tabawa da  bakin ciki  yarufeta  tace  to  kibar  kudin  awajanki  boka  yace  bayan wata uku  nakoma  yanzu  zan koma  gida  idan  lokacin  yayi  zan  zo  na  amshe  kudin  


Talatu tace to shikenan  , sukayi sallama  tabawa  takoma  gida  cike  da  bakin  ciki  da  hassada  tace ko zanyi  yawo  tsirara  saina  raba  auren  nan  



Husna  na  zauna  ita  da  umma  baba  yazo  yaganta  yafada  mata  an  daura  aurenta  , kuka  taringa  yi  tana  rike  hannun  baba  

Murmishi baba yayi  yace  husna  kidena  kuka  farin ciki  yamata  kiyi ba  kuka  ba  ki  godewa  Allah  ki  dage  da  addu'a da  tsayuwar  dare  kinji  ya'ta  

Tana share hawaye tace  to  baba  , saida  yaga  tasaki ranta  yatafi  kasuwa


Doctor Hassan  yashigo  yabata  sallama  saboda  yaga   hawan jinin  nata  yasauka  sosai   


Kayansu  suka  hada  umma  takama  husna  suka  fito  har  cikin mota  tasaka  ta  a  gidan  gaba  inda  Alhaji abdallah  yake  sai  wani kallo  yake  mata     ita  kuma  husna  rufe fuska  tayi  tana  murmishi  takusan  samun  y'anci  


Gidan  umma  suka  nufa  acen  zata  zauna  saboda haka  shima Alhaji abdallah  yadawo  nan  


Umma tace  to  ikon Allah  kaima  taremin  zakayi  agida  


Sosa kanshi  yayi yace  umma  saboda  nake  tiamaka  miki  ne  


Murmishi umma tayi  tace  to  shikenan  damuwa  


Haka  sukaci  gaba  da kula da  husna sosai  take  samun  sauki  duk  bayan  sati  suna  zuwa  ganin  doctor  hassan  kuma   yana  jin  dadin  yadda  hawan  jinin  ta  ke  sauka   


𝘽𝘼𝙔𝘼𝙉   𝙒𝘼𝙏𝘼 𝙐𝙆𝙐  


Husna yanzu  tasamu  lfy  harta  koma s school  Alhaji abdallah ya  canja  mata  wata  mai  tsada  sosai  take  jin dadin  canjin  rayuwar  data  samu  


A bangaren aljana safiya  sunsha  gwagwarmaya  sosai   a  wannan  tsakanin   


Yaudai  saiga  aljana safiya  a  cikin dajin  bishiyoyi  bata  tabbatar  da  hakan  ba  saida  taji  wata  murya  nacewa  barka da  zuwa  dajin  bishiyoyi  , ki  dauki  abinda  yakawoki  mai  maidaku  gida 


Saboda tsananin farin cikin  aljana safiya  kasa  ta  zube  tana  sujjadar  godiya  ga  Allah  daya  tsakaita  mata  tafiyar  da  kuma  taimakon  babanta  


Hadda  kuka  take  tana fadin Alhamdulillah  Alhamdulillah  Alhamdulillah  Allah  nagode  maka  daka  gajarcemin  tafiyata  zuwa  wannan  guri  mai  nisa

*37—38*


Da  sauri  aljana safiya  take  tafiya  sai  zuba  murmishi  takeyi   tace  masoyiya  kawai  mu  bayyana  gasu  abba  

Tsuntsuwar ta  tsaya  aljana safiya  tahau  kai  suka  bace  a  


Abba  tinda  yatashi  yake  murmishi  haka  kawai  yakejin  farin ciki  nakara  kusantoshi  yace  da  alama  yarinya  ta  tayi  nasara  


A  tsakiyar  dajin  aljana safiya   su  amina  da  hafeez  ne  zaune  da  sauri  yamike  yace  yar uwa  wllh  kamshin  shugaba  nakeji  kodai  tadawo  


Amina  tace  to  duba  kagani  ko  tadawo  ne  , zoben  hannun  shi  ya  kalla  ya  murza zoben   haske tayi  sai  kuma  haske  ya  bace,  kallon  amina  yayi  yace  yar  uwa  wllh  shugaba  ta  rufe  kofar  dazan  ji  inda  take  


Amina tace  ina  kyautata  zaton  tayi  nasara  ajikina  nakejin  hakan 


Hafeez yace  to  shikenan  Allah yakareta  aduk  inda  take  , Amina  ta  amsa  da  ameen  


Su aljana safiya  basu  bayyana  ko  ina  ba  sai  inda  abba  yake  zaune  shida  aliyu  yana  rungume  dashi   


Cike da  mamaki  da  al'ajabi  abba  ya  mike  rike  da  aliyu  ganin  aljana safiya  na  nufosu  , murza  idonshi  yayi  danya  tabbatar  yabude  itadin  dai  yagani  tana  mishi  murmishi    abba  yace  safiya  kece  ko  gizo  ne kikemin  

Da gudu  takaraso  inda  su  abba  suke  ta  rungume  su  tana  sakin  kukan farin ciki  


Abba  na  murmishi  yace  Alhamdulillah  Allah  nagode  maka  daka  dawomin  da  yarinya  ta  lfy  itada  masoyiyar  ta  


Aljana safiya  ta  dago  tace  abba  Allah  yakaremu  da  kuma  taimakon  ka  Alhamdulillah  gashi  munje  mundawo   lfy  babu  abinda  yasamemu  saidai  fa  munsha  gwagwarmaya  sosai  abba  


Kallon aliyu tayi  saita  amshesa  daga  hannun  abba  , kara  rungume  shi  tayi  tana  jin  kaunar  yaron  har  cikin  ranta  tace  yarona  insha Allah  zaka  samu  lfy   kaji 


Dagoda  kanshi  aliyu yayi yace  ummina  sai  ya kuma  rungume  ta  yana  kuka  ganin  ummin  nashi  tana  kuka  


Murmishi safiya  tayi tace  yaron albarka  kukan  me  kakeyi  gani  nazo  babu  inda  zan  sake  tafiya  nabarka  


Murmishi abba yayi  ganin  yadda  safiya  take  lallaba  aliyu  sai  yaji  sun  burgeshi  sosai 


Sauran  yan  cikine  suka  fito  da  gudu  amina  tazo  ta  rungume  aljana safiya  tana fadin  Alhamdulillah  shugaba  kindawo  masha Allah  mungowa  Allah  daya  dawo  mana  dake  lfy  


Itama aljana safiya rungume  amina   tayi  tana  fadin  nadawo  amina  Alhamdulillah  babu  abinda  yasamemu 


Sakinta  amina tayi  , shima  hafeez  yazo  ya  rungume  aljana safiya  (kasancewar  hafeez dan  yayarta  ne  shekarar  talatin mahaifiyar sa ta  rasu shiyasa  yake  ganin aljana safiya  kamar  mahaifiyar sa  saboda  kamannin  dasuke  da  mamansa)Hafeez  yace  masha Allah  naji  dadin  sawowarki  lfy  Alhamdulillah  mungode  lfy  


Murmishi yayi  tashafa  kanshi  tace  masha Allah  mundawo  gida  lfy  

Kowa  yazo  da  farin cikin sa  yana  taya  aljana safiya  murnar  dawowa  daga  dajin  bishiyoyi  


Bayan  anhuta  ne  aljana safiya  tabasu  labarin  abubuwan  firgitarwar da  suka  tinga  haduwa  dasu  a  hanya   kowa  sai  jinjina  jarumtar  safiya  yakeyi  jin  yadda  sukasha  gwagwarmaya  


WASHE GARI 


Aljana safiya  ta  dauko  maganin  tabaiwa  abba  amsa  yayi  yadakashi  yace  a  samo  ruwa  mai  kyau  


Aljana safiya  takawo  mishi  yazuba  maganin  daya  daka   acikin  ruwan  , kallon safiya  abba  yayi  yace  wannan  maganin wankan  sati  daya  za'a  mishi  insha Allah  zai  warke  da  izinin Allah  

Murmishin jin dadi  aljana safiya  tayi tace  masha Allah  kace  nakusa  zuwa  ganin  husna  

Abba yace  yauwa  dama  kince  saikinzo  zaki  fadamin  wacece  husna  ?

Gyara  zama  tayi tace  abba  yarinyar  nan  sau  daya  nataba  ganinta  amma  tashiga  raina   yadda  naga  wata  mata  na  azabtar  da  ita  inason  jin  tarishinta  nasan  wannan  muguwar  matar  ba  mahaifiyar  ta  bace  saidai  kishiyar uwa 


Girgiza kai  abba  yace  haka  bil'adam suke   amma  yaushe  kika  ganta  


Aljana safiya  tace abba  tin ranar  danaje  daukoku  zuwa  cikin  dajin  nan  a  ranar  na  ganta  , ban  tsaya  ba  nayi gaba  ina  zuwa  wani  gari  bansan  bama  yasunan  garin  naga  wadda  tafi  husna  shan  wahalar rayuwa  dole  tasa  na  tsaya  na  taimaka  mata  ,nan  takwashe  labarin  su  bilkisu da  garbati  da  surukarta   ta  fadawa  abba  


Yar dariya  yayi  yace  hmmm  mutane  sunada  tsoro  amma  sai  mugun  nufi  akan  yan  uwansu   


Aljana safiya tace  haka  suke  duk  yawancin  jinsin  mutane  haka  suke  hassada  da  bakin ciki  k'ashi  da  zargi  zato  zalinci  tauyewa  maraya  hakki   to  idan  kasamu  jinsin mutane  kagama  , muma  a  namu  jinsi  akwaisu  dayawa  


Abba  yace  hakane  gaskiya  Allah  yakaimu  lokacin  da  aliyu zai  warke  muga  babanshi  

Aljana safiya tace ameen  abba  amma  fa  ko  baban  aliyu ya  bayyana  bazan  bashi  shiba   dan  banajin  zan  iya  rabuwa  da  aliyu  


Dafata  abba  yayi yace  to  Allah  yasa  yabar miki  amma  nasan  wata rana  inya  kai  minzalin  aure  zai  koma  duniyar  bil'adam ne  yazauna  acen  saidai  yake  kawo  mana  ziyara 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  hakane  abba  to
Allah yanuna  mana  auren  aliyu na  hmm  zaasha  biki  

Dariya abba yayi yace  to  shikenan  ai  sukaci  gaba  da  hirarsu  



Husna  ce  tadawo  daga  school   a  gajiye  tana  shigowa  da  sallama  babu  kowa  sai  mijin  ta  a  zaune  yana  zaman  jiranta  tazo  yaji  dalilin  dayasa  taki  cin  abinci  kafin  ta  tafi  school  

Amsa  mata  sallamar  yayi  ya  dauke  kanshi  

Husna  ganin  sahibyn  nata  kamar  yayi  fushi  saita  k'araso  da  sauri  tana  fadin   subhanallah  sahiby  maiya  sameka  ne  haka  

Banza  yamata  yasake  murtuke  fuska  alamar  ba wasa  

Husna duk  sai  taji  ranta  babu  dadi  jakar ta  ta  ajiye  ta  matso  kusanshi  tana  shafa  fuskarshi  ,tace  sahiby  mai  maiya faru  ne  ko  wani  yabata  maka  rai  awaje  kayimin  magana  please  tana  shafa  sajan  fuskar  shi  


Duk yana  jinta  amma  yayi shuru  saboda  ranshi  yab'aci  da  kin  cin  abincin  datayi  tashi  yayi  ya  nufi  dakinsa  yabarta  awajan 


Husna  tuni  tasoma  hawaye  tace  nashiga  uku  ni husna  maiya  batawa  mijina  rai  nima  yake  fushi  dani  waiyo Allah  babana  tashi  tayi  tashare  hawayen ta  tabi  bayanshi  


Bude  kofar  tayi  taganshi  ta  kishingid'e  akan  gado   da  sauri  ta  hau  kan  gadon  zama  tayi  kusa  dashi  ta  kuma  cewa  sahiby  wai  menene  haka  waye  ya  taba  ka 


Gyara  kwanciyar shi  yayi  mai  ce  mata  komai  ba 

Husna  na  ganin  ya  kuma  yin  banza  da  ita  kuka  tasaka  mai  ratsa  zuciyar  mai  sauraro  

Yana jin kukanta  amma  yashare  so  yake  ya  hukunta  ta  

Ganin  bai  kula  taba  saita  sauka  agadon  ta  fita  da  gudu  tana  kuka  , falo  tadawo  kwanciya  tayi  akasa  tana  rera  kuka 


Umma ce  tashigo  gidan  da sallama  jin  sautin  kukan  husna  ai  da  sauri  ta  karaso  tana  fadin  subhanallah  husna  lfy  
Karasowa umma  tayi  tana  dagota  daga  kwanciyar  datayi  


Husna  na  ganin umma  takara  sautin kukan ta  cikin kuka  tace  umma  fushi  yakeyi  dani  kuma  na  tamabayesa  yaki  kulani  dan Allah  umma  kibashi  hakuri  banason  fushinsa   agareni  hakan  zai  iya  sakani  damuwa  , bazan  jureba  umma  ki  tiamakeni  kibashi  hakuri 


Umma ko  mamaki ne  yakamata  tama  rasa  mai  zatace  sai  cen  tukun  tace  kodai  kin  mishi  lefi  baki  sanibane 

Husna tace umma  bansani  ba  na  tambayesa  yaki  bani  amsa  ko  kulani  yakiyi  


Alhaji abdallah  na  ganin husna ta  fita  kitchen  yaje  ya  daura  indomie da  kwai  , yanajin  duk abinda  sukeyi  sosai  tabashi  tausayi  amma  yadanne  zuciyar  shi  yacigaba  da  dafa  indomie  sa   bayan tadafu  yasauke  yazobe  ta  slate  dinner  ya  kawo  tukun  yayo  falo 


Umma  nata  rarrashin  husna  amma  takiyin  shuru  sai  kuka  takeyi  , juyawa  husna tayi  taganshi  tsaye  ya  harde  hannun  shi  yana  kallon  ta  


Kuka  takuma  sakawa  tana fadin  umma  kinganshi  ko  yana  harara ta  wllh  umma  bazan iya  jurewa  ba  waiyo Allah  ni  husna  nashiga uku  wai  maina  maka  kaki  kulani  


Umma  ganin  yashigo  saita  basu  waje  ta  haura  sama  tace  bazan  iya  wannan  rigimar  takuba  kunga  tafiya  ta  tashige  dakinta  


Husna  ta  sake  kallon shi  gani tayi  ya  bude  mata  hannu  alamar  tazo    ai  kamar jira  takeyi  ta  tashi  da  sauri  har  tana  hadawa  da  gudu  tana  isa  gareshi  ta  shige jikinsa  

Murmishi yayi  yace  to  kukan  ya  isa  haka  muje  kici abinci 

Sai yanzu  husna  taji  yunwa  sosai   dagowa tayi  tace  sahiby wai  maina  maka  kaki  kualani  please kafadamin 


Daukarta  yayi  cak  yayi  darner  da  ita  indomie  yabude  

Kamshin ya  daki  hancin  husna  lumshe  ido  tayi  tace  wow  sahiby  ka  iya  girki  


Murmishi yayi yace  tin  kafin  kicima  kike  yabawa 

Yar dariya tayi  tana  kallon shi  tace  eh  ai  daga  gani  zatayi  dadi  sosai  


Abinci  yasoma  bata  tanaci  tana  zuba  mishi  santi  saida  ta  jenye  kanta  tace  nafa  koshi  

Kallon ta  yayi  yace  ban  yarda ba  bude  cikin  nagani 


Doguwa rigarta  ta  tattare  har  saman  cikinta  tace  kagani  nafa  koshi  


Kallon cikin  yayi  yaganshi  ashafe  kamar  ba'a  zuba  komai  ba  yace  ahakan  ne  kika  koshi  kalli fa  yana  kai  hannun  shi  kan  shafaffan  cikin  nata  


Dariya husna tayi  tace  eh  haka  nake  ai  


Bakinta  ya  goge  mata   tukun  ya  mike  yana kallon ta  yace  nifa  bacci  nakeji  sahibaty   kitafi  wajan  umma  kuyi hira  dama  fushin  danayi  saboda  bakici  abinci  ba  kika  tafi  school  


Kuka husna  ta  saka  tana  dire-dire  tace  nidai  babu  inda  zanje  a  gunka  zan  kwanta  nima  bacci  nakeji  tana  turo baki  gaba  

Dariya  tabashi   baiyiba  sai  yace  ato  nidai  nace  kitafi  wajan  umma  bacci  zanyi 


Ihu husna  tasa  tana  kiran  umma  ! Umma!, kinga  yace  bazai  kwanta  dani  ba  wai  nazo  wajanki  


Da sauri  yarufe  mata  baki  yace  kai  haba  sahibaty  tunamin  asiri  zakiyi  umma  tafito  ta  koreni  agidan 


Murguda baki  tayi tace  to  kaje  dani  kona  kira  umma 


Dariya  yayi yace  to  shikenan  muje  ko  

Tsayawa  tayi  tace  nidai  ka  goyani  


Babu  yadda  ya  iya  ya  runkuya  ta  haye  bayanshi  tana  dariya  dakinsu  yanufa  suka  shiga  ya  ajiyeta  


Cire  uniform  dinta  tayi  tayi  zamanta  daga  gajeran wando ta  bakin  gwiwa  sai pan da yan  karamar  rigar  ciki  da  breziya 



Kallon  yakeyi  ko  k'itawa  babu  husna  tace  to  mu  kwanta  

Ajiyar zuciya yasauke  yace  ok  shima  kayanshi  yacire  yabar  kanana   yahau  gadon  


Itama  hawa  yayi  fegensa  


Janyota yayi  jikinsa  yaja  musu  blanket   babu  jimawa  bacci  yayi  gaba  da  husna  


Shi  kuma  baccin  yaki  zuwa  sai  taba  gashin  kan  husna  yake  yadda  yakeda  laushi  ga  kyau  


Ita kuwa  husna  baccin ta  take  hankali kwance  da  kyer  shima  bacci  yadauke shi


Umma  najin  lokacin  da  husna  take  kiranta  harzata  fito  sai  taji  maganar husna  ta  karshe  , murmishi  tayi tace  kai  yaran  nan Allah  yashirye  ku  



Su talatu  harka  tasamu  sai  wani  hura  hanci  takeyi  wai  ita  nan  surukinta shaharerren  mai  kudi  , kullin  sai  Alhaji abdallah  yaje  gaishe su  yabawa  talatu  kudi  dubu  hamsi 

Siyasa yanzu  ko  yan  unguwar  su  take  ganin  tafi  karfinsu  ita  mai  kudi  ce 


Baba ko  dariya  ma  take  bashi  idan  yaga  tana  wani  Abun  



Tabawa  na  shirin  zuwa  gidan boka  mai gobe da nisa  , zazzabi  yahanata  zuwa  dole  ta  hakura  tabari  saita   samu  lfy  amma  kullin  da  bakin cikin  husna ta  auri alhaji  take  kwana  tana  tashi  gashi  lokacin  komawarta  yayi  amma  zazzabi  yahanata  zuwa  
 

Sarki shamsu  yana  cen  dakin  tsafinsa  kayan  yaki  yake  hadawa  ko  fitowa  bayayi  saboda  son  yaga  bayan  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  


Shima  dodo  hakan  take  daga  bangaren  shi  kullin  saiya  turawa  aljana safiya  mugayen  halittun  da  bak'aken  aljannu  amma  daga  sun  tukari  dajin  aljana safiya  to  basa  kai  labari  ake  kashesu  abin  yanabawa  dodo  mamaki  ganin  yadda  duk  abunda  yayi  niyarwa  aljana safiya  baya  samunta   dan  haka  yanzu  yakira  sarki shamsu  su  hada  karfin  tsafinsu  waje  daya  ko  zasu  kashe  aljana safiya   


Sarki shamsu  yashigo  dakin  dodo  gaisuwar  girmamawa  yayiwa  dodo  tukun  yace  yashugaba  naga  kiranka  shine  nace  bari  nazo  ko  ansamo  mafita 


Kallon shi  dodo  yayi yace  dama  inason  mu  hada  karfin  tsafinmu  a  waje  daya  saimu  yaki  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞 saboda  naga tsafin namu  baya  zuwa  cikiin  dajin  aljana safiya 




Alhamdulillah jikin aliyu  nasamun  sauki  yauma  yana  wasa  a  gindin  wata  bishiya  aljana safiya  ce  ta fito  daga  dakinta  tana  neman  aliyu cen  taganoshi  yana  ta  surutai  sai  dariya take  da  sauri  ta  nufi  inda yake  


Tana  karasawa  taje  yarona  mai  kakeyi anan  ne  ,kallon ta  aliyu yayi yace  ummi  wasa  nakeyi  fa  wannan  abokin  nawa  yace  zaimin  yawo  shine  yakuma  cemin  mamanshi  ta  hanashi  ni  kuma  nayi fushi  dashi  tinda  yaki  zuwa  gidanmu  


Dariya aljana safiya  tayi  tana  kallon kayan  wasan  nashi  teddy ne  kuma  namiji  ga  kuma  wata  a  gefe  ya  zaunar  da  ita  tace  yarona  wannan fa  ita  wacece  


Dariya yayi  yace  ummi  wannan  itace  maman  abokina  ta  hanashi  zuwa  shine  na  maidata  gefe  


Murmishi tayi tace  zomu  shiga ciki  muci abinci  , da sauri  yatashi  yana  tsallen murna  yace  ummi  abincin  irin  na  jiya  ko  shizaki  dafa  min 

Safiya tace  eh  yarona  muje  maza  jeka  ganinan  zuwa  


Da  gudu  yabar  wajan  yana  fadin  abba! Abba! abba  ummina  tace  zata  dafa  min  abinci  mai  dadi irin  na  jiya  

Daukarshi  abba  yayi  yace  to shikenan  mushiga  ciki  kafin  tadafa  suka  shiga ciki 


Aljana safiya  tana  shirin  tafiya da  aliyu  lokacin  daya  mike  zasu  tafi  ta  hango   bataliyar  sarki  adam  sulaiman  


Kallon su  take  har  suka  kusan  zuwa  inda  take  


Shima sarki adam sulaiman  kallon  aljana safiya yake  da  mamaki  wajanta  yanufa  da sallama  ya  isa  gareta 


Murmishi safiya tayi  tace  amsa  mishi  sallamar  tace  a  a  kune  a  yankin  namu  


Murmishin shima  yayi  yace  nine  wllh  kina  dai  gani  

Gaisawa  sukayi  yace  naga  wani  yaro  yana  gudu  
Aljana safiya  tace  yarona  ne  yanzu  yashiga  ciki  muje  ku  huta  a  wajan  abba  na 


Binta  sarki adam  yayi  sauran  suka  samu  inuwa  suka  zauna  , a  gidin  wata  bishiya   ta  musu  shimfida  ta  kira  abba  ya  fito  shida  aliyu 


Abba  nazuwa  suka  gaisa  aljana safiya tace  abba  wannan  shine  wanda  muka  hadu  dashi  a  kusan  dajin  bishiyoyi  

Murmishi abba yayi yace masha Allah  

Aliyu ya  kwanta jikin abba yace  abba  wannan  su  wanene  yana nuna  sarki adam 


Murmishi yayi  yace  bak'o  ne  yazo  


Dariya  aliyu yayi yace  bako  toshi  ina  gidansu  yake  , Abba yace  gidansu  yana  cen  gaba  


Sarki  adam  na  kallon  aliyu  sai  yaji  kaunar  yaron  tashigesa  gashi  da  wayo,  yace  ya  sunanka  

Aliyu  yatashi  yazauna  yace nine  dan  ummina  

Dariya  sukayi  aljana safiya  tace  sunanka  zaka  fada  mishi  fa  , Aliyu yasake  cewa  to  ummi  sunana  aliyu ko 


Sarki adam  sosai  yaji  yanason  yaron haka  suka  cigaba  da hira  


Bayan  sun  huta  sunci abinci  sarki adam yamusu  sallama  yakira  saura  tawagar  suka  kama  hanya  


Da karfi  aliyu yace  bak'o  idan  kadawo  zaka  kaini  gidanku  nagani  


Juyowa  sarki adam  yayi yace  eh  zan  dawo  nan  tukun yajuya  , sosai  yaron yabirgeshi  yace  to  yaron nan  kamar  bil dam  nake  gani  to  koma  meye  zan  dawo  zamuyi  magana  da  aljana safiya  


Husna  tayi  shirin  bacci  zama  tayi  tana  jiran  sahibyn  nata  yazo  bata  jima  ba  yakaraso  cikin  daki 


Murmishi yayi  yace  sahibaty  na  Alhamdulillah  yanzu  dai  kinsamu  lfy  komai  nomal  saimu  koma  gidanmu  ko 


Tashi  tayi  takaraso  gabanshi  hannu  ta  d'aga  tanason  tab'a  fuskarshi 

Dariya yayi yace  kai  wllh  sahibaty  akwai ki  da  neman  rigima  yar  karama  dake  zaki  iya  tab'o fuska ta 

Mintsinin shi  ta  a  gefen  cikinshi  , da sauri  ya  rike  hannun ta yace  mugunta  ko  

Dariya tayi tace  toba  nace  ka  matso  na  taba  fuskar ka  please  

Daukarta  yayi  yakaita  kan  gado  kwantar  da  ita  yayi  shima  ya  kwanta  yaceto  yanzu  saiki  taba  ko

Hannu ta kai  ta  taba  sajan fuskar shi  wanda  yake  tsone  mata  ido  kullin  saitayi  ta  shafa  mishi  saje 


Lumshe ido yayi yace  ke  wai  yanzu  dole  sai  kin taba min  sajena  

Murmishi yayi yace eh  sahiby  yanamin  kyau  ne 


Kara rungume ta  yayi  yace  to  ayi  bacci ko gobe  zamu  koma  gidanmu 


Shuru tayi  tana  murmishi bata  ce  komai  ba 


Kallon ta  yake  bako  k'iftawa   , Husna  batayi auneba  taji    bakinshi  cikin  nata  yana  kissing dinta 

Sun  kusan  minti talatin a  haka  husna  taji  abun  yasoma  fin  kwakwalwan ta  ta  saka  mishi  kuka 


Sakinta  yayi  yana  maida  numfashi  saida  ya  saita  kanshi    tukun  ya  kalli  husna  sai  kuka takeyi 

Janyota yayi jikinsa yace  am so sorry  sahibaty  nakasa  daurewa  ne  bazan  karaba  

Jin  yace  bazan  karaba  sai  tayi  luf  ajikinsa   babu  jimawa  bacci  yayi  gaba  dasu  


Nima  nace  asuba tagari masoya 🥰🥰

 Alhaji abdallah  kam  kasa  bacci  yayi  gashi  husna  ta  bak'aleshi  daga  ya  motsa  saita  kara  rungume  shi  daya  kara  motsawa  gaba  daya  ma  kanshi  ta  kwanta  taci  gaba  da  baccin ta hankali kwance  


Kallon ta  kawai  yake  duk  ta  hanashi  motsi  haka  ya  hakura  yana  addu'a  har  shima  yasamu  bacci  yayi  gaba  dashi  


Da  asuba  shiya  fara  tashi  addu'ar  tashi  daga  bacci  yayi  yakara  da  salatin annabi s.a.w.   ya  soma  tashin  husna 

Bude  idonta  tayi akanshi , murmishi  yasakar  mata  yace  saba hul-khai  ya  sahibaty  tashi  anyi  sallar  salla 


Itama murmishin tamishi  tace  saba hul-nur  ya  sahiby  , tukun  ta  tashi  daga  kanshi  itama  addu'a tayi  tukun  tace  kafara  shiga  ko  dan  kasamu  jam'i  a  nasallaci 

Murmishi yayi  yace  to   na  shige  toilet  bai  jima  ba  yafito   itama  tashiga  ta  dauro  alwala  , tinkafin  tafito  ya  shimfida  darduma   tana  fitowa  yajasu  raka'atainun  fajur  


Bayan  suna  idar  ya tafi  masallaci  ita  kuma  ya  gabatar  da  sallar  ta   bayan ta  gama  tazauna  tana  azkarul saba 


Alhaji abdallah na  dawowa  husna  tagama  addu'o'i  ta  

Juyowa  tayi  tana  murmishi  ta  karaso  kusanshi  durkusawa  tayi  tace  barka da  safiya  sahiby  dafatan  katashi  lfy  


Dagota  yayi  yazaunar  da  ita  gefenshi  yace  barka dai  sahibaty  kintashi  lfy  


Kanta  asunkuye  tace  lfy lau 


Dariya yayi  yace  to  kuma  kunyata  kikeji  yanzu  

Rufe fuskar ta  tayi  tace  eh  mana 


Alhaji abdallah yace  hmm  tab  su  kunya  manya  kamar  bake  bace  jiya  kika  danneni  ko  motsi  hanani  kikayi nayi yanzu  duk  jikina  ciwo  yakeyi 

Da sauri  tarufe  kanta  da  bargo  tace  nidai  a a  gaskiya  dukama  nawa  nake  da  har  zakayi  ciwon  jiki


Murmishi yayi  shima  ya  hayo  kan gadon  yaja  musu blanket  yace  to shikenan  muyi  bacci  kafin  lokacin school  yayi  


Husna tace  to  


Su  tabawa  fa  jiki  yayi  tsanani  yanzu  ko ruwa  sai  an  mik'o  mata  gashi  talatu  bata  zauna  da  itaba  rukayya  ma  batako  kulata  idan  ta  tafi  yawon  barikinta   sai  kusan  asuba  ake  kawota   shiyasa  batasan  halinda  tabawa  take  ciki 

Yauma  kamar kullin  rukayya  tafito  tasha kwalliya  kamar  amarya  har  zata  wuce  saita  ji  tanason  ganin  mahaifiyar  ta ta  , cikin dakinta  tashiga  , da sauri  takarasa  inda  tabawa  take  kwance    d'agota tayi tace  mama  maiyasa meki  ne  haka  


Da  kyer tabawa  ke  hada  harrufan  kalma  tace  rukayya  kikaini  asibiti  banda  lfy  kinji


Sakinta  rukayya  tayi  da sauri  ta  dauko  wayarta  ta  danna  kiran  saurayin ta  , ringing daya  yadauka  dama  kiranta  yake  jira   baki na  rawa  rukayya  tace  hello  baby  ka  taimakeni  kazo  mukai mamana  hospital  tana  cikin tsananin  ciwo


Daga cen bangaren  saurayin  yace  ok  kufito  gani  akofar  gidanku  

Rukayya tace to  yanzu  zamu  fito  nan  takashe  kuran  , da  sauri  takoma  dakin  hijab tasakawa  tabawa  ta  taimaka mata  suka  fito  waje 

Da sauri  saurayin rukayya  yabude  gidan  baya  rukayya  tasaka  tabawa  suka  nufi  asibiti 


Suna  zuwa  aka  karbeta  likitoci  suka  rufu  akanta  domin  ceto  ranta 

Rukayya sai kuka  takeyi  duk  iskancin  rukayya  tanason  mamanta  bata  son  taga  mamanta  a  hali  rashin lfy  shiyasa  yanzu  duk  takasa  tsaye  takasa  zaune  sai  kuka  take  , shi  ko  saurayin nata  yana  rarrashin ta  amma  taki  dena  kukan 


Saurayin rukayya yace  haba  rukayya  meye  yasa  kike  mata  kuka  bafa  mutuwa  akace  tayiba  kiyi  hakuri  mujira  doctor  yaxo  mana  

Dagowa rukayya tayi  tana  kallon  sa  tace  haba  baby  bazaka  gane  bane  banason  rasa  mamana  


Ruko hannun ta  yayi yace  naji  muzauna  mujira  tukun 


Basu  jima  da  zama  wata  nosee  tafito  daga  dakin  da  sauri  rukayya  ta  karaso  inda  take  tace  doctor  dan Allah  yajikin  mama  na  

Kallon ta  doctor  din tayi tace  yanzu  dai  Alhamdulillah  jikinta  da  sauki sosai  kuma  ina  miki  albishir  da  kin  kusan  samun  k'ani  maman  nada  juna  buyi 


Da sauri  rukayya  da  rike hannun doctor  tace  da  gaske  mamana  tana  da  ciki   kuma  yanzu  babu  abinda  yake damunta  bayan  cikin  


Murmishi doctor tayi tace  eh  gaskiya  bamu  muga  komai  ba  sai  cikin shine  yasakata  wannan  ciwon 


Rukayya tace Alhamdulillah  nagode sosai  zan  iya  ganinta  ? 


Doctor tace  eh  kishiga  tana  ciki 

Wajan  saurayin ta  tadawo  tace  baby  mama  ciki ne  da  ita  ashe  shiyasa  take  wannan  ciwon  waiyo Allah dadi  kasheni zan  kuma  samun  kanwa  Allah  yasa  mama  ta  haifi  namiji  


Murmishi yayi yace  to  Alhamdulillah  yanzu  bari  koma  idan  anbaku  sallama  zanzo  nakaiku  gida  tinda  yau  ba   zuwa  wajan  party  ko  

Dariya tayi tace  eh  zan  kula  da  mama  gobe  zamuje  ai  


Yace ok to shikenan  sai  munyi  waya  yafice  abinshi 


Dakin  rukayya  tashiga  ganin tabawa  ta  farka  da sauri  ta  karaso  tace  sannu  mama  yajikin  naki  Allah  yabaki  lfy 

Dan  murmishi  tabawa  tayi  tace  ameen  rukayya  wanene  yake  samuna  

Murmishi rukayya tayi tace  ihmm  mama  ai  abin  dadine  ingaya miki  zaki  haifamin  kanwa ne  

Cike da tsoro tabawa ke  kallon rukayya tace  kamar  yaya  rukayya  ban  ganeba  kiyimin  yadda  zan  gane  


Rukayya tace  mama  to  cikine  dake  yanzu  kin fashimta 


Dake  kirji  tabawa tayi tace  ciki  kuma  rukayya  nashiga  uku  


Da mamaki rukayya ke  kallon ta  tace  eh  mama  wani  abu  ne  ko  bakison  cikin ne 


Kamar zata  fasa kuka tace  rukayya banason  cikin  nan  kalli  yadda  yaringa  bani  wahala  yanzu  me  kike  tunani  idan  nazo  haihuwa  ai  kawai  mutuwa  zanyi  ni kuma  ban  shirya  mutuwa  ba  burina  baicika  ba 


Rukayya tace  to  mama  ai  ciki  bazai  hanaki  yin  abinda  kikesoba  nidai  inason  cikin  nan dan Allah  karkice  bakiso 


Kuka tabawa  tasaka  tasan dai  wannan  ba  cikin  mijin  tabane  na  boka  ne  cen  sai  wata  dubara  ta  fado  mata  murmishin jin dadi tayi 


Tace rukayya  kisa  acire  min cikin  nan  kinji  idan  naji  sauki  inhar  kinason  na  rayu kuma  nacika  miki  burinki  na  auren  Alhaji abdallah mai dala 


Rukayya  tace to shikenan  amma  saikin  samu lfy  zankaiki 

Cikin jin dadi tabawa tace to  rukayya

Doctor ce  tashigo  tabasu  sallama    rukayya  takira  babyn  ta  yazo  yakaisu  gida   




Yau  sarki shamsu  da  dodon sa  suka  gama  kayan  yaki  na  zuwa  dajin 𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞   ba  kamaramin  kayan  yaki  suka  hadaba  suka  kama hanya


Aljana safiya  ce  zaune  itada  aliyu da  abba  amina  da  hafeez  

Abba  ya  kallesu  yace  ya'ta  maza  ku shirya  sarki shamsu  da  dodon sa  na  kan  hanyar  zuwa  gareki  , kafin  suzo  maza  kushirya 

Aljana safiya tace  abbana  ai  nasan  da  zuwansu  ina  kallon  duk  wani  shirinsu  kuma  sunkawo  kansu  dama  aliyu  nake  jira  ya  warke  nafar  musu  Alhamdulillah  aliyu  lfy  saikuma  gasu  sunkawo  kansu  da  kansu   abba  ashirye  nake  sainaga  bayansu  saina  musu  kisan  gillah  babu  wanda  zai  tsira  daga  tarko  na  

Jinjina kai  abba  yayi yace  to  masha Allah  saiki  fita  saboda  tsoro  badan  tasoba  


Sosai ran aljana safiya  yayi  matuk'ar baci  tace  na  rantse  da  ubangijin  halittu  saina  tarwatsa  sarki shamsu da  dodon sa  , tamike  tashige  daki


Abba yayi murmishi yana  jinjina  jarumta  irin  ta  safiya  , yacewa  su  amina  maza  kuma  kishirya  nasan  zasuyi  kwana biyu ko  uku  kafin  su  k'araso  


Mikewa  sukayi  amina tace  insha Allah  wannan  karon  muzamu  sa'a  akan  sarki  shamsu  azzalimi  macuci  ni  nan  zan  sokeshi  da  takobina  tana  hade rai 


Murmishi abba  yayi yace  nasan  zaki  iya  amina  Allah  yabaku  sa'a 


Hafeez yace  insha Allah  nasara  tamuce  wannan  karon  saimunga  bayan  sarki shamsu  da  yardar  mai  duka


Abba yace  Allah  yatsemin  ku  aduk  inda  kuke  , suka  amsa  da  ameen  kowa  yashige  dakinsa 


Aljana safiya  nashiga  daki  ta  zauna  rufe idonta  tayi  tana  ganin  duk  kayan  tsafin  su  sarki  shamsu  murmishi tayi  tace  yanzu  zan  bata  kayan  tsafin naku  mugaye  kuma  saina  kasheku  insha Allah  


Hannun ta  ta  hade waje  daya  tana  murzashi  ta  jima  ahaka  tukun  ta  bude idonta  dariya tayi  tace  wannan  tsafin  nagama  rugujeshi  saidai  wani  bashiba  , 


Su sarki shamsu  sunata  tafiya  sarki shamsu  yace  shugaba  ai  na  tabbata  wannan  karon  saimun  kashe  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧  𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞   mun  mamaye  komai  nasu 

Dodo yace  ai  dole  ma  mu  kasheta   , haka  sukaci  gaba  da  tafiya  suna  tattauna  yadda  zasu  kashe  aljana safiya  da  ahalinta 



Yau  husna  zasu  tare  agidansu  umma  ta  gayyato  danginta  dana  mijinta  za'a  walima  kafin  akaita  gidanta 


Su talatu  ansha  dauri  sai  wani  yauk'i  take  wai  ita  gamai  diya  

Tabawa ma  taxo  saboda  tasamu  lfy  anzubar  da  cikin  bata  sake  komawa  ba  gidan  boka  amma  jin  ance  husna  zata  tare  agidanta  yasa tace  ana  gama  walima  washe  gari  zata  koma  wajan  boka  yabata  magani  ganin  irin  gidan  su  alhaji abdallah  abin  yasake  tsaya  mata  arai  wai  husna  ce  agidan  nan  



Bayan kowa  yazo  anceka  gidan  da  yan uwa  da  makota  umma  ko  bakin  kamar  gonar  auduga  dan  farin ciki  


Wata  kanwar  umma  ce  ta  shirya  husna  cikin  lessin blu  mai  kyau  kayan  sun  amsheta  tayi  kyau  sosai  sai zuba  kamshi  takeyi   talatu  na  gefenta  sai  dauke  kai  takeyi  ta daura  kafa  daya kan daya 


Kowa  yafito  amarya  ake  jira  su  sailuba  sune  akan  gaba  sai  kawayen su  na  school  fitowa  suka  inda  ake  gabatar da  walimar  aka  zauna  da  husna  

Malama  ta  mike  tasoma  wa'azi  mai  ratsa zuciya   sosai  kowa  ya  nutsu  yana  jin  maganar  malama  na  shigarsa  amma  banda  talatu da  tabawa  


Tabawa  ta  bankawa  mai  wa'azin   harara  tace  aikin  banza  mijin  me  da  zanke  wani  mishi  biyayya  kamar  ubana  to  baza'a ayi  biyayyar ba 


Bayan angama  aka  sakewa  husna  wanka  da  canja  mata  wani  kayan  , tana  fidda  kayan  talatu  tadauke  tasaka  a  jakarta  

Girgiza kai husna tayi tace  Allah  yashirya minke  mama  kudin  data  samu  ta  raba  abiyu  tamikawa  talatu  tace  mama  ga  wannan  kudin  karna  zubar


Ai da sauri talatu ta  amshe  kudin  ta  saka a  jakarta  tana  washe baki  tace  yar albarka  zan  ajiye  miki  tinda  ba  kashesu  zakiyi ba  Allah  yabaku  zaman  lfy  ni zamu tafi  gida  bayan  kwana  biyu  zamuje  ganin gida

Bayan  anfito  da   husna  mota  aka  sakata   kawayen  ta  suka  shishiga  motocin  sai  gidan  husna.  Unguwar nasarawa  J.R.A.  anan  gidan  husna  yake  suna  shiga  unguwar  wani  tsantsareren  gida  suka  nufa  mai  gadi  yabude  musu  get  suka  shiga  

Kowa  yafito aka  shiga   ,masha Allah   Allah gidan  ya  tsaru  iya  tsaruwa  tasha  kyau  da  kayan  more  rayuwa  yan  kawo  amarya  shesuka  zama  kauyawa  a  gidan  kowa  yana  tofa  albarkacin  bakinsa  wasu  nasaka  albarka da  yaba  tsaruwa  da  kyaun  gidan. Wasu  ba  gulmar  wai  wannan  gidan  husna  ne  yar  karamar  yarinya da  ita  diyar  talaka  ,

Tabawa tace  kamar  yaya  boka 


Boka yace  da  baki  zubar  ba  nizan  dauke  cikin  a  sakawa  husna  shi  a  cikin  da  bakaken aljanu  suke  firgitar  ta  da  haka  mijinta  zai  tsaneta  yakoreta  amma  yanzu  kin  zubar  da  damarki 

Hannu biyu  tabawa  ta  daura  akai  tana  fadin  nashiga uku  nayi  wasa  da  damata  yanzu  boka  meye  abinyi  


Wani murmishin samun  nasara  boka yayi yace  saikin  sake  daukar  ciki  tukun  zamu  haka  husna  daukar  ciki  harsai  ke  kindauka   saimu  tura  mata  shi  da  mugayen halittun da  zasuke  tsoratar  da  ita  


Murmishi mugunta  ladi tayi  tace  haba  tabawa  kawai  ki  amince  sai burinki  yacika   na  son  aurawa  alhaji abdallah mai dala  rukayya 


Tabawa tace na  amince  matuk'ar burina  zai  cika  akan  husna  zanyi koma  menene  dan  naga  husna  ta  tagayyara  rayuwa  tamata  zafi  kowa  yatsaneta  


Boka yace to  kishiga  dakin wanka  ai  dama  bakaken aljanu  suka  saka  miki  cikin  saboda  su  miki  aiki  amma  da  kika  samu she  kika  zubar  dashi  yanzu  kije  zasu  sake  baki  wani  nan  da  wata  daya ko  biyu 

Da sauri  tabawa  tashiga  bukkar  boka 


Tana shigewa ladi  ta  kwashe da dariya  tace  shege  boka  nawa  kana  lamarinka  fa 

Dariyar  shima yayi yace  ba  dole  ba  wannan  tsaleliyar  mata  haka  gata  kwakwalwan  baya  fashimtar nine  nake  shagalina  da  ita  amma  bata  ganewa  , amma  yanzu  idan  namata  ciki  zan  mata  aiki  akan burinta 


Ladi tace  gaskiya  tinda  tana  maka  biyayya  kaima  kacika  mata  burinta  akan  husna  

Boka yace angama  jiramu  mudawo  , dariya tayi tace  ina  nan  kuje  kudawo,  


Yana  shiga  kamar  yadda  suka  saba  aikata  alfashan  su  haka  sukayi bayan  wasu  awanni  boka  yafara  fitowa  tukun  tabawa  tafito  tana  gyara  zaninta

Dariyar mugunta ladi tayi tace  to  muje ko 

Boka yace  kullin zakike  zuwa  ana  miki  wanka  harsai  sun  dena  fushi  dake  sun  baki  ciki 

Cike da farin ciki tabawa  tace  boka  kabasu  hakuri  bazan  sake  tsallake  umarninsu  ba  tuba  nake  kabari hakuri  subani  cikin  ko  burina  yacika 


Dariya boka yayi yace  bakida  matsala  tabawa  zan  basu  hakuri  sosai  kuje gida  sai  gobe  naganku  , suka  amsa  da  to  suka  tafi gida 

Aljana safiya na  zaune  tana  jira  karasowar  sarki shamsu da  dodon sa  duk  wani  tsafinsu  tuni  tabata  musu  shi  sosai  take  shiri  itada  amina  da  hafeez  


Tana zaune  abba  yace  yar albarka  Allah  yabaki nasara  nazo  na  fada  miki  sarki shamsu  gobe  zasu  iya  karasowa  dajin  nan  ki  shirya  komai  inason  a  wannan  karon  naga  kin  kashe  mugu azzalimi sarki shamsu  da  dodon sa  Allah  yabaki nasara


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  insha Allah abbana  nasara  tawace  na  lalata  duk  wani  tsafi  nasu  saidai  in  kafin  suzo  zasu  hada  wani  , saina  kashe  sarki shamsu  da  hannuna  dan iska marar mutumci  tana  kara  hade rai  tuno  da  yan  uwanta  daya  kashe  mata 


Abba yace insha Allah   ina aliyu yakene  banganshi ba 


Fitowa  aliyu yayi daga  inda  yabuya  yace  abba  gani  na  boye  maka  ne  


Dariya abba yayi yace  to  ai  ina  ganin  inda  kake  nafadi ne  danka  fito 

Wajan safiya aliyu yayi  ya  zauna  yace  ummi  yaushe  zaki  kaini  naga  bak'on  nan  dayazo  mana  

Shafa kanshi tayi tace  yarona  kayi hakuri  nanda  wani lokaci  zai  dawo  saika  ganshi  kaji  , tana  mamakin  yadda  kullin  sai  aliyu yace  akaishi  wajan  bak'o 

Aliyu yace to  tukun  yabar dakin



Bari  muleka  gidan  amarya husna 


Husna ce  rungume jikin alhaji abdallah  sai  saka  mata  albarka  yake  ita  kuma  tana  kuka  fuskarta  duk  ta  kumbura  saboda  kukan  dataci  

Kwantar da ita  yayi  ya  shiga toilet ya  hada ruwan zafi  ya  dauketa  yakaita  toilet  cikin  ruwan  yasaka  ta  ai  ta  sauri ta  mike  jin  azaba  na  ratsata 

Da sauri  ya  maidata  yana  lallaba ta  har  yasamu  ya  gasata  sosai  tukun mata  wanka  shima  yayi  suka  fito  


Shirya yayi  ya  gyara  dakin  ya  kwantar da ita shima  ya  kwanta  ya janyota jikinsa  da sauri husna ta  matsa  tana kuka  tace  banaso !banaso! nidai

Murmishi yayi  wanda  ya  nuna  yadda  yake  cikin farin ciki  yace  kizo  mu  kwanta  babu  abinda zan  miki  kinji  yi hakuri  da  lallami da  wayo  ta  yarda  ta  kwanta  tuni  bacci  yayi  gaba da ita  shima  baccin yayi  gaba dashi 


Talatu  ce  zaune ta  dauko  kudin da  husna  tabata  anyi  daurin  dan  kwali  ture  kaga  tsiya  tana  irga kudin sai  murmishi  take  zubawa


Baba ne  yashigo  yaganta  ahaka  girgiza kai yayi  yace  Allah  yashirya ki  talatu. Gyaran murya  yayi  yace a a  talatu  wannan  kudin  fa 

Yar dariya tayi tace malam  husna  tabani  tace  na  ajiye  mata  shine  nake lissafawa  naga  ko  nawane  saina  kirata  na  fada mata  ko  nawane  na  ajiye  mata  lokacin  daya  shirya  saitazo  ta  amsa

Murmishi baba  yayi yace  to  masha Allah  nina  tafi  kasuwa 


Mikewa  talatu tayi  tana  kwashe kudin  tace  tsaya  malam  na  rakaka  bakin  kofar gida


Dariya yayi yace  a  sabon salo kenan  har  nasamu  muk'amin  rakiya  yau  agidana  


Farr talatu  tawa  baba  da  ido  tace  eh  kobaka  sone  ,, Baba yace  a  a  muje  , jerawa  sukayi tamishi  rakiya  har  bakin  kofar gida  tukun  tadawo 


Tana  dawowa  na  lissafa  kudin  dubu  dari da  saba'in  gud'a raka  har  uku  tukun tace  lalle  zan  zama  hajiya  talatu  matar  musbahu  ikon Allah  maman  husna  ko ba'aso dan  uban  mutun  ta  dauko  shoki  ta  dire  tukun  tashiga  daki  

Tabawa takira  tace  kizo  ki  amshi  kudin  kije  wajan  bokan  dazai  mallake  mana  zuciya alhaji abdallah da husna  muke  juyasu  kamar  waina  a  tanda  


Daga cen  bangaren,,tabawa tace   ganin nan  zuwa  , dama  tabawa  so  take  taji  talatu  zata  bata  kasonta  ne  ko kuwa  sai  gashi  takirata 

Babu jimawa  tabawa  tazo  suka  kulla  makircinsu  da  talatu  tukun ta  amshi  dubu dari  tabar  mana  sauran  shima  talatu  tace  zataje  wajan  wani  malami  ance  yana  aiki  kamar  yankan  wuka,  
Da  haka  suka  rabu  tabawa  tadawo  gida  cike  da  farin cikin burinta  zai  cika 


Itama talatu  haka  take  cewa  zata  cika  burinta  akan husna  idan  suka  kwashe dukiyar  alhaji abdallah   saisu  saka  a  kashe  husna  da  alhaji  shikenan  komai  yazama  nasu  

Ni kuwa nace ayi mugani indai  tusa  zata  hura  wuta



Yau  su  sarki  shamsu  suka  karaso  bakin  dajin  aljana safiya  amma  abinda  yabasu  mamaki  sun  kasa  shiga duk  tsafin  dasuka  yi  baya  anfani 


Cike da  baccin rai  sarki shamsu  yace  wai  meye matsala  ya  shugaba na 


Dodo yace  ai  sai  yanzu  naduba  ta  lalata  mana  duk  wani  tsafinmu  ,saidai  batasan  nafita  tantiranci  ba  muna  da  sauran  kayan  tsafi  daya  kuma  dashi  zamuyi  anfani  mushiga  ciki

Farin ciki ne  ya  ziyarci zuciyar sarki shamsu  yace  to mu  gwada 

Dodon ya  karanto  wasu  kalmomin  tsafi  aikuwa  cikin  sa'a  hanyar  tabude  suka  kutsa  kai 


Duk abinda sukeyi aljana safiya na  ganinsu  kuma  ita  tabasu  dama  shigowa  ciki  dan  kar  sarki shamsu  ya  shammace ta  ya  gudu  irin  na  yakin  baya   , suna  shigowa  tarufe  ko wacce  hanya  ta  dajin 


Suna  shiga  sarki shamsu  ya  kwarara ihu  yace  ina  kike  kamar marar mutumci  nafika  rashin mutumci  to  gamu  munzo  yau  kwananki  yakare  wai  𝗮𝗹𝗷𝗮𝗻𝗮𝗿 𝗷𝗮𝗿𝘂𝗺𝗮𝗶  to  zanga  mai  kwatarki  anan 


Amina da hafeez ne  suka  fito  cikin  shirin  yaki  wajan safiya suka   nufa  

Aljana safiya  tamike  tsaye  tace  muje  ai  babu  mai  rabamu  dasu  sai Allah  

Hafeez yace wannan haka  yake  b'acewa sukayi  saigasu  sun  bayyana  a  dan  nesa  da  dasu  sarki shamsu 


Sarki shamsu yace  yau  sunanki  gawa  nizan  kasheki  da  kaina 


Murmishi aljana safiya tayi  tanajin  yadda  karfi  sosai  yake  shigarta  tace  ai  sai  an  gwada  akansan  na  kwarai  inba  tsoro ba  kazo  mana  kagani 


Wata mahaukaciyar dariya  sarki shamsu yasaki  yazaro  takobin sa  haryayi  taku  daya  , Dodo  yarikeshi  yace  ni  zan fara  duka  daya  zan  mata  na  kasheta  ,tsayawa sarki shamsu yayi  yana  jinjinawa  dodon  nashi  


Da gudu  dodon  yayo  inda  su  aljana safiya suke 


Itama aljana safiya  ta  kalli su  amina tace  karku  shiga  ni zanji dashi  ku  kula  da  sarki shamsu  karya  gudu  duk da  na  rufe  ko  ina  hatsabibi  ne  
Suka amsa  da to 


Da gudu  aljana safiya  ta nufo dodo  shima  da  gudu  yanufota  kowa  na  gyara  takobinsa
