*43—44*




Kowa da  haushin  kowa  yake  tunkaro  dan  uwansa  suna  haduwa  aka  kaure  da  azababben yaki baji  ba  gani  


Amina  ta  wugawa  sarki shamsu  harara   tana  mai  nuna  da  saita  kasheshi  

Shima  hararar  ta  yayi  ya  nuna  ta  da  takobinsa  

Hafeez yace  dan  iska  yaudai  baka  isaba  kabar  dajin  nan  samun  kasheka  munbawa  kura  namanka  tacinye  mugu  azzalimi  


Sarki shamsu yace  nine  zan  kasheku  kananan  marassa  kunya  nafi  karfinku  daga  ku  har  shugabar  taku  nine  nan  sarki  shamsu  naci  dubu  sai  ceto  wllh  kowa  yaci  tuwo  dani  miya  yasha   kuma  duk  wanda  ya  tanka  min  saidai  uwarshi  ta  haifi  wani   wllh Allah  yana  zare  ido  


Dariya amina tayi ta  tako  kusa  dashi  tace  kai  karamin  dan  iska ne kabari  shugaba  tagama  azzalimin dodon  naka  ai  yau  saidai  wani  bakaiba  


Shuru sarki shamsu yayi ganin yadda aljana amina  take  kumbura  tasoma  cenja  launi  daga  suffar mutum  tasoma  komawa  suffar ta  na  aljanu.  Tsoro ne  yasoma  kama  sarki  shamsu  , amma  saboda  karfin  hali  irin  nashi  saida  tattaru  sauran  jarumtarshi  yace  to  zamu  gani  nidaku  waye  zaici  galaba  banzaye  kawai  


Murmishi amina  tayi bata  sake  cemishi  komai  ba   taci  gaba  da  hade  rai  tana  canja  halittar  ta  



Hafeez kuwa  duk  ya  rasa nutsuwar shi  ganin  aljana safiya  sai  yaki  sukeyi  baji  baji  bagani amma  babu  wanda  yagaji  saima  kara  karfin  yakin  su  sukayi  , hakan  yabawa  hafeez  mamaki  ganin  dodon  nan  fa  yana  ji  da  karfe  ajiyar zuciya  yasauke  yace  ya rabbi  ka  taimaki  ummi na  shugaba  mai  adalci  Allah  kabata  nasara  akan  makiyanta  kar  suci  galaba  akanta   


Aljana safiya da  dodo  yaki  suke  sosai  kowa  yakasa  kai  dan  uwanshi  kasa  


Cen  dodon  ya  shammaci aljana safiya  ya  yanketa  da  karfi  a  cinyarta  


Runtse ido  tayi  tana  cije  baki   tana  kallon  wajan  a zuciyar ta tace  ya Allah  ka  kawomin  agaji  naga  bayan  wannan  mushirkan  , dagowa  tayi  tana  kallon  dodo  daya  nufota  zai  sake  caka  mata  takobin  hannun shi   jin  muryar  abba tayi acikin kannan ta  yana  fadin  yar albarka  ki  taure  kitashi  kada  kibari  yaraso gareki  idan  kika  tashi  ki soki gefen  cikinsa  tanan  kadai  zaki  iya  kashe shi  da  sauri  aljana safiya  tamike  tana  rike  cinyarta  a hankali  take  mikewa  harta  Mike  dukanta  , 


Dodon na  kawo mata  sara  tayi  saurin  b'acewa  ta  bayyana  ta  bayanshi  shima  juyawa  yayi  zai  sake  kai  mata  sara  nanma  ta  b'ace  haka  suka  ringa  yi  saida  aljana safiya  taji  ciwon datakeji  ya  ragu  tukun  ta  gyara  takobin ta  ta  bayyana ,a  gabanshi 


Kukan kura  dodo  yayi  zai  kai  mata  sara  da  sauri  tayi  kasa  karamar  wuk'ar  ta  tayi  saurin  zarowa  kafin  ya  motsa  ta  caka  mishi  agefen  hakarkarin  sa  


Wata gigitacciyar kara  da  kururuwa  azaba  dodo  yayi  saboda  ta  cokeshi  sosai  har  cikinsa  wukar  tayi shige  ya  fadi  akasa  matacce  jikake  timm  kamar  giwa  ce  ta  fadi  



Sarki shamsu  da sauri  yaja  dabaya  yana  zaro ido  ranshi  ne  yayi mugun  baci ganin  an  kashe  mishi  dodon sa 


Hamdala aljana safiya tayi  tana  kara  yiwa  Allah  kirari  

Amina da  hafeez  farin  ciki  ne  ya  bayyana  akan  fuskar  su  suna  kallon  aljana safiya  jinjina  suka  mata  


Murmishi tayi   ta  dafa  kirjinta  ta  nuna  musu  tana  sonsu  suna  cikin zuciyar ta 



Sosai sukaji  dadi  amina hadda  hawayen ta   na  farin ciki  


Aljana safiya ta  tunkari  sarki  shamsu   tace  bil'adam  suna  cewa  rana  dubu  ta  barawo  rana  daya  tak  tamai  kayace  yau  dubunka  tacika   yau  zaka  kwana  a  lahira


Babu  ko  d'igon  tsoro  a  idon  sarki shamsu  yace  banza  abaya  ma  baki  kasheni  ba  sai  yanzu  dana  rik'a  na  bunk'asa  zakice  wai  zakiga  bayana  kinyi  kadan  


Dariya aljana safiya wadda  dajin  gaba  daya  ya  amsa  sautin  dariyar  


Sarki shamsu  mugun tsorone  yashigesa  amma  saboda  shi  hatsabibi  ne  bai  nunaba  saima  cewa  yayi  wannan  sakaryar  dariyar  kuma  bazata  tsorata  niba  


Aljana safiya tace  hmm  kazo  a  gwaza  kawai  


Cike  dason  ganin  bayan  aljana safiya  sarki  shamsu  ya  zare  takobin sa  yanufeta  da  gudu  


Murmishi safiya tayi  tace  hmmm  nakashe  dodon  kama  barekai  dama  dan  kana  anfani  da  sihirine  kake  guduwa  yanzu  guwa  na  lalata  tsafin  bari  naga  iya  gudun  ruwanka  shashasha kawai   ta  gyara  tsayuwar  ta tana  jiran  zuwanshi 


Sarki shamsu  yana  karasawa  inda  aljana safiya take  yakai  mata  sara  


Kaucewa tayi  takoma  gefe 

Sake  kai  mata  wani  saran  yayi  


Nan  ma  ta  kauce  da sauri ta  kwashe  kafafunsa  yafadi  kasa  murmishi tayi  tace  kai  ka  isa  ka  hada  karfina  da  naka  kana  dan adam  ina  aljana  dama  tsafine  kai  taimakonka kake  b'acewa  kamar  jinsinmu  to  na  lalata  tsafin  kuma  kaima  na  kasheka  kowa  yahuta  da  zalincin ka 

Takobinta  ta  daga  sama  zata  caka mishi 

Amina da hafeez sukace  dakata  shugaba  


Tsayawa tayi tana  kallon su  tace  menene 


Amina tace inaso  kibamu  umarni  mukashesa  

Murmishi safiya tayi tace shikenan  nabaku  nan  takoma  gefe  tana yiwa  sarki shamsu dariyar  bey  bey  tana  daga mishi hannu  tace  sai  munhadu  a  lahira  tasake  sakin wata dariyar 


Amina da hafeez suma dariya sukewa sarki shamsu  suna  nunashi  amina  tace  to  ina  tsafin  naka  da  kake  tunk'aho dashi     yau  zaka  tafi  lahira  



A  tare  suka  caka mishi wukar  amina ta  kirjinsa  hafeez ta  cikinsa 


Wani ihu  sarki shamsu ya  kurma  saboda  tsananin azaba  nan  ya  fadi  matacce 



Aljana safiya  itama  inda take  tsaye  faduwa  tayi  saboda  tsananin  ciwo  da  zugin  da  cinyarta  take  mata 



Da sauri  suka  yo  kanta  hafeez ya  dauke  suka  b'ace  a  dakin  ta  suka  bayyana  cike da  tashin hankali 


Da sauri  abba  ya  amsheta  yace   taji  ciwo  sosai  shine  yasakata  suma  takobin  sihiri ce  ya  soketa  da  ita  shiyasa  ciwon  yayi tsanani


Amina tace  to  abba  yanzu  ya  zamuyi  


Abba yace  ku  kwantar da  hankalin ku  zata samu lfy  kifita  ki  ciro  wani  ganye  zaki  ganshi  da  furanni  ajikin  bishiyar   to  kiciro  ganyen  dashi za'a ke  tafasashi  ana  gasa  mata  wajan  ciwon  zata  samu lfy  insha Allah 


Share hawaye  amina tayi  tace  to  abba  bari  naje  naciro  nan  ta  b'ace  a  dakin  , bata  jimaba tadawo  dakin  dauke  da  ganyen  maganin  a  hannun  ta  tace  abba  gashi 


Amsa yayi  yafita  daga dakin  babu  dadewa  yadawo  dauke  da  wani  kwano  maganin ne  ya  tafaso  karasowa  yayi  inda  safiya  take  kwance  

Wajan ciwon  ya  wanke  da  zuwan  zafin  bayan ya  gama  ya  goge  yasamo  wani  dan  karamin  kyelle  ya  daure  mata  wajan  yana  gamawa  yace  kuje  kuhuta  zata  warke  ai  Alhamdulillah tinda  ankashesu  azzalumai 


Fita  sukayi  duk jikinsu a  sanyaye  suna  zuwa  wajan yan uwansu  suka  basu  lbarin komai  suna  duk sukayi sanyi  jin  shugabar tasu  ba  lfy   haka  suka  zauna  jugum jugum 





Tabawa  yanzu  kullin  saitaje  gidan  boka  anyi  mata  wanka  Allah Allah  take  subata  cikin  nan  ko  burinta  zai  cika  


Yauma  taje  wajan  bokan  yake  ce  mata  ai  anbata  ciki  

Cike da farin ciki  take  godiya  yace  mata  taje  gida  amma  yanzu  ba  cikin  zaa  sakawa  husna  ba  yanzu  aiki  zasuyi  akanta  su  hanata  nutsuwa  daga  nan  sauran  aiki  bazai  fada  mataba  zata  gani  da  kanta 


Dariyar mugunta  tabawa  tayi tace  godiya ga  mashurin boka  mai gobe da nisa  yanzu  nawane  kudin aikin 

Dariya shima yayi yace  a a  ba  yanzu ba  zaki  biya sai  aiki  yayi   kike  zuwa ne  kullin  kamar yadda  kike zuwa


Murmishin jin dadi tayi tace to shikenan  indai  wannan  ne  bakada  matsala  zanke zuwa  koda  a  rana  sau  uku  kace  zanzo  matuk'ar  bukatata  zata  biya 


Murmishin nasamun nasara  boka  yayi yace  a a  sau  daya  zakike  zuwa  tashi  kitafi  sallamanmiya  


Da sauri  tabawa ta  baro  gidan  boka  tadawo  gida  ranta  fari  tass 




Husna  da alhaji  anashan  amarci  yanzu  idan  kaga  husna  bazakace  ita  bace  yadda  ta girma  tacika  ta  kara  fari  tass  kana  ganinta  kasan  jin dadi  da  kwanciyar hankali  ya  bayyana  a  jikinta. Wata  uku  kenan  da  aurensu  amma  in ka  ganta  bazaka  gane  taba 



Yauma  kamar  suna  daki  hula  take  sakawa  sahibyn  nata  tsalle takeyi  takai  hannun ta kanshi  amma  takasa  sai  huci  takeyi  ganin  dai  bazata  isaba  tahau  kan  gadon  tukun  tasaka  mishi  hular 


Kissing din ta  yayi  yana dariya yace  sahibaty  kayan  rigima     


Hararar shi  tayi tace  eh  din  anji  


Murmishi yayi yakama hannun ta  suka  fita  tamishi  rakiya,  har  bakin mota  takai  shi



Shiga  yayi  tana  mishi beyy beyy. Mai  gadi  ya  bude  mishi  get  tafita  ,juyawa  tayi  zata  koma  ciki  kanta  taji  ya  sara  mata  da  karfi  kamar  zai  rabe  gida  biyu,  runtse ido  tayi  tace  kai  wllh  ciwon  kan  nan  yatakura min bari  naje  nasha  magani 


Tana shiga  ta  nufu  bedroom dinta  magani ta  dauko  tasha  jin  ciwon  na  kara  karuwa  saita  nufi  kan  kujera  danta  kwanta  ta  huta  anjima  saita  daura  girki,  bata  karasaba   aka  dauketa   sama  take  sakota  kasa  tafadi sumanmiya  taji  ciwo  a  goshinta 


Takai   kusan  awa uku  tukun  ta  farfado  a  hankali  ta  tashi  jikinta  duk  ciwo  yake  mata   wayar ta  tadauka  ta  kira  Alhaji  tafada  mishi  bata  da  lfy  ta  fadi  har  taji  ciwo  


Hankali a  tashe  yadawo  kafin  ya  karaso  tuni  jikin  husna  yayi tsanani  sosai  da kyer  take  janyo  numfashi 

A haka yazo  yasameta  da  sauri  ya  dauketa  sukayi  asibiti  , babu bata lokaci  suka  amsheta  aka  soma  dubata  


Alhaji abdallah duk  yashiga  damuwa  sai  safa da marwa yakeyi  


Bayan wani  awanni  doctor hassan  yafito  yana  gyara  glass din  fuskar shi 


Da sauri  Alhaji abdallah yace  hassan  ya  jikin nata   murmishi doctor hassan  yayi  yace  congratulations  madam  nada  juna biyu  harna  wata  biyu  


Alhaji abdallah yace da  gaske  kake  Hassan  sahibaty na  tanada  ciki  Alhamdulillah  Allah  nagode  maka  


Murmishi doctor Hassan yayi yace  sosai makuwa  yanzu  an  wanke  mata  ciwon  dataji  a   goshinta  zan  rubuta  muku  magunguna   saika  siyo   da  yamma  saiku  tafi  gida  

Sosai Alhaji abdallah yake  farin ciki marar musaltuwa  yace  nagode sosai  abokina  yanzu  zan  iya  ganinta  


Doctor hassan yace  a a  bacci  takeyi   muje  kasiyo  maganin  , binshi  yayi sukaje  office  dinsa  ya rubuta mishi  maganin  , fita  yayi  yasiyo  yadawo  , zama  yayi  yana  jiran  tashi  husna  su  tafi  gida  



Wata nosee tafito  tace   ranka yadade  madam  ta  farka  , da sauri  ya  mike  ya  shiga  dakin 


Ganin ta  yayi  zaune  sai  dake  kai  takeyi  karasawa  yayi  yace  sannu  sahibaty  

Kallon shi tayi tamishi murmishi  tace  yauwa   


Hannun ta yakama yace  Alhamdulillah  sahibaty  kinada  ciki  na  wata  biyu 

Da sauri  ta  kalleshi  tace  da  gaske  nima  zan  zama  uwa  

Jinjina  mata  kai  yayi  alamar eh  


Rumgume shi tayi  tana  fadin Alhamdulillah  ala ni"imatullah  


Alhaji abdallah yace  to  tashi  muje  gida   , taimaka  mata  yayi  ta  sauko  amma  har  yanzu  kanta  bai  dena  ciwo ba  ga  wani  zafi  da  yake  mata 


Doctor hassan yashigo  yanasu  takardar  sallama  yace  madam husna  Allah yaraba lfy  , rufe fuska tayi  tana  murmishi  , sallama  sukayi  dashi  suka  nufi  gidan  umma 


Da sallama  suka  shiga  ciki  , Umma  ta  amsa  musu  tana  mikewa  tsaye  tace  mai zan gani  husna  maiyasa  meki  haka 


Murmishin karfi hali husna tayi  tace  zamewa  nayi umma  ina  yini , amsawa  umma  tayi  tana  riko  husna  ta  zaunar  ta  


Murmishi Alhaji abdallah yayi yace albishirinki  umma  na 


Umma tace  goro  fari  tass  


Yace  umma  yarki  ciki ne  da  ita  


Umma ta  kwashe  baki tace  kai  haba  Alhamdulillah  y'ata   Allah  yaraba  lfy  , kuma anan  zata  zauna  saita  haihu  

Fuskar tausayi alhaji yayi yace haba umma  da  wuri  haka  

Hade rai  umma tayi tace  eh  saidai  inkaima  tarewa  zakayi  anan  din 



Ajiyar zuciya yasauke yace Alhamdulillah  da  baa  rabani  da  sahibaty  naba  , kallon umma  yayi yace  to  shikenan  umma  saimu  dawo  , bari  na  tafi  gida  nakawo  mata  kayanta  


Umma tace  to  saika  dawo  , yafita  


Haka  husna  ta  ringa  samun  kulawa  sosai  a  wajan  umma  da  mijinta  , saidai  matsalar  su  daya  daga  ansha  kwana  sai  jinin  husna  ya  k'are  an  saka  mata  wani  

Wannan  lamarin  ba  karamin  damunsu  yakeba  an  rasa  daga  ina  matsalar  take  haka  suke  ta  fama  idan  kuma  kanta  ya  sara  mata  sai  anrike  , idan baa  riketaba  da  gudu  take  fita  



Hakan  yakara  sakasu  cikin  damuwa  addu'a  kawai  suke  mata


  , Baba  kam  ji  yake  kamar  yadawo  da  cutar  jikinsa   ganin  yadda  husna  ta  kod'e  tayi  fari  sosai  idonta  ma  fari  yakara  yi  sosai  yake  tausaya mata


Husna kuwa  ita  tasan  azabar  da  takeji   zafi  jikinta  yake  mata  koda  yaushe  ga  kanta  kamar  zai  tsage  , 



Aljana safiya  jiki  Alhamdulillah  yasoma  samun  sauki  kullin addu'a  take  Allah  yabata  lfy  ko  taje  taga  husna  a  wani  hali  take  , dasu  bilkisu  matar  garbati  , gasu  alhaji da hajiya,  wannan  tunanin  dashi  take  kwana  dashi  take  tashi  , duk  tasaka  damuwa  aranta  bata  ko  walwala  kullin  ran  nan  a  hade  yake  ko  aliyu yanzu  bata  sakin  mishi  fuska  shiyasa  ma  abba  yahana  shi  zuwa  wajan  safiya 



Tana zaune  abba  yace  safiya  kidena  daga  hankalin  ki  insha Allah  zaki  samu lfy  kije  ga  husna  


Murmishi  tayi tace to  abba  nadena  , zama  yayi  suka  soma hira  , Aliyu ne  yashigo  yana  kuka  

Abba yace  lfy  yaron ummi  , wajan abba  yakaraso  yace  abba  kafadawa  ummi  takaini  wajan  wannan  bak'on  dayazo  gidanmu  , tacemin  zai  dawo  amma  yaki  zuwa  nidai  akaini  gidansu  naganshi  abba   yakara  sautin kukan shi 

Murmishi safiya tayi tace  kayi hakuri  yaron kirki  banida lfy  idan  nasamu  lfy  zan  nemo  maka  shi  aduk  inda  yake  a  fadin  duniyar  nan  kaji  kadena  kukan 


Dariya yasoma  yace  to  ummi  sannan  yafita  , Safiya tace  ni  ina  mamakin  yadda  aliyu  ya  saka  sarki  adam  aranshi  har  yake  kukan  saiya  ganshi 


Abba yace  hadin Allah  ne  kawai  


Boka mai gobe da nisa  yana  zaune  tabawa  na  gabanshi  yace  yanzu  haka  husna  mun  tura  mata  manyan  shadanun  aljanu  suna  zuk'e  mata  jinin  jikinta  daga  ansaka  shesu  hanye 


Dariya tabawa tayi tace  ai  harnaje  ganinta  ta  koma  kamar  fulawa  yanzu  kuma  asaka  mata  cikin  na  jikina 


Boka yace  ai  ansamu matsala  yanzu haka husna  nada  ciki  har  yafito  bazamu  iya  saka  mata  wani  ba 


A sanyaye tabawa tace  to  yanzu yaya  za'ayi 

Kallon ta  yayi yace  zamuyi ta  bata  wahala ne  daga  karshe  ta  mutu  , cikin jikinki  kuwa  kicewa  mijinki kina da  ciki  , zai  amsa  na  rufe  bakinsa  babu abinda  zaiyi

Murmishin jin dadi tabawa tayi tace  to shikenan  bari  nakoma  saika  jini  tafice  gida



Alhamdulillah yanzu  aljana  safiya  lafiya  tasamu  sosai  taji  dadin  jikinta  dan  haka yau  ta  shirya  zuwa  ganin  husna  , sallama  tayiwa  su  abba  tace wa  amina  hafeez  kuzauna  cikin  shiri  akowani  lokacin  zan  kiraku  ,, suka  amsa  da  to  Allah  yakiyaye  hanya  , murmishi tayi  ta  b'ace  


Yanzu  husna  batasan  wanda  yake  kantaba  , bata  gane  kowa  saidai  tayita  kallon  su  ,ko  magana  ma  yanzu  ta  gagareta  , hakan  ba  karamin damun su   umma  abin  yayi  ba  ,

Yau  umma  ta  tafita  anguwa  tabar  mai  aikinta  ta  tsere  mata  husna  bazata  jimaba  zata  dawo  , yar aikin tace  to  , Umma  tafita  


Yar aikin umma  itama  fita  tayi  zata  kaiwa  mamanta  kudin  da  aka  bata  na  wata  tabar  husna  ta  kadai  agidan  ganin  tana  bacci 



Tabawa tace boka  kawai  ka  kashe husna  kowama  ya  huta  na  tsaneta  


Boka yace angama  yanzu  haka  husna  tana  kwance  yanzu  zan  aika  mata  masu  kasheta  

Dadine  yakama  tabawa  tamishi godiya  tukun tabar  gidan  boka  takoma  gida  tana  sauraron  talatu  takira  ta  tace  mata  husna  tamutu 


Husna na  bacci  taji  an  danne  mata  kirji  yuk'urawa  tayi  zata  tashi  amma  takasa  sai  fitar  da  numfashi  take  na  azaba 



Aljana safiya  ta  bayyana  a  daidai  inda  taga husna  lokacin  tana  karama  , baba  tagani  yafito  yana  sauri   sai  talatu  tana  ce mishi  malam   gobe  zanje  unguwar dosa  ganin husna  

Baba yace  ok Allah yakaimu  nima  inna  tashi  zanje  naganta  , daga  haka  yafice 


Aljana safiya  ta  rasa  yazata  yi  wani  tunani  yafado  mata  da sauri  ta  koma  wata  budurwa  mai  kyau   saita  soma  tafiya  


Baban sailuba  ne  yafito  shima  zai  tafi  kasuwa  da sauri  safiya tace  assalam alaikum 


Tsayawa  yayi  ya  amsa  mata  sallamar  ta   gaisheshi  tayi  cike da  ladabi,, amsawa  yayi  yana murmishi 



Aljana safiya tace  dan Allah  baba  ina ne  inda  aka  kai  husna  inason  ganinta ne 


Baban sailuba yace  to  baiwar Allah  daga ina  haka  kike  neman  husna 

Aljana safiya tace  ni  kawar husna ce  tin  muna  yara  a school  muke  hadu to  anyiwa  babana  transfer ne  muka  tashi  daga  kaduna  yanzu  munzo  ziyara ne  shine  nace  bari  nazo  naga  husna  , na  leka  gidansu  amma  ba  kowa  naji  wani  yana  cewa  anmata  aure  shine  nakeso  kafadamin  gidanta  naje  mugaisa  , takai karshen maganar  tana  share  hawaye 

Tausayi  tabaiwa  baban sailuba  yace Allah sarki  baiwar Allah  husna yanzu  tana  cikin  tsananin  ciwo  yanzu  haka  bata  gane  wake  kanta  kullin  abin  sai  gaba  yakeyi  , idan  sunan  unguwar su  inda  take  yanzu  unguwar dosa  ya  mata  kwantancen  gidan  su  umma 


Godiya sosai tamishi  hadda  durkusawa  tayi  tana fadin nagode sosai  baba  Allah yasaka da alkhairi 


Murmishi baban sailuba yayi yace  ba komai  ,nan  yayi gaba  abinshi  yana  jinjina  shak'uwa irin  wannan  



Aljana safiya naganin  ya  tafi  tuni  ta b'ace  a  bakin kofar  gidan  ta  bayyana  ganin  idan  tatsaya  buga  kofar  za'a  bata  lokaci  kawai  ta  b'ace  , cikin  falon  ta  bayyana  


A razane aljana safiya  ta  juya jin  gurnani  , wa  zata  gani  husna  ce  kwance  a  kasa  sai  juya  kai  take  


Da  karfi tace  a  a  husna  ! Husna  da  sauri  takaraso  gaban  husna  wandda  take  tsakanin  mutuwa da  rayuwar  yasake  cewa  tayi  husna....

بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم 



*45—46*




Da sauri  aljana safiya  ta  dauki  husna  ta  rungume  ta  tana  kukan  ganin  yadda  taga  husna  kamar  ta  mutu  ma  jikinta tasoma  dubawa  taga  babu  jini  wani  abu taji  yazo  mata  wuyanta  ta  tsaya  mata  saboda  tsananin  bac'in  rai  , abinda  yafi  bata  mata  rai  sihirin  dake  jikin  husna  kowacce  kafa  ta  jikin  husna  wadda  iska  taza  shiga taji  dadin  jikinta   duk  anbita  da  sihiri  an  toshe  , nan  da  nan  ranta  ya  kuma  bacci  gashi  ta  rasa abinyi  ganin  batada  mafita  sai  ta  sab'a  husna  a  kafadar  ta   ta  b'ace. Bata  bayyana  ko  inaba  sai  dajinta   tana  zuwa  da  gudu  tanufi  abba  tana  fadin  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un  abbana  ka  taimakeni  ka  taimaki  marainiyar  Allah  abbana  husna  tana  cikin  halin  ciwo  banma  saniba  ta  mutu  kotana  raye  abbana  sun  cutar min  da  husna  wani  lefi  ta  aikata  musu  suka  bata  wannan  mugun  aikin  wllh  basuci  banza  bazan  taba  yafemusu  ba  saina  tarwatsa  rayuwar su  saina  hanasu  bacci   duk  wanda  yakeda  sa  hannu  akan  wannan  ciwon   wllh  saina  illatashi fiye  da  yanda  yayiwa  husna  abba  kaduba  kaga  yadda  suka  dawo  da  husna  abba  kayi  wani  abu  kar  husna  ta  mutu  wllh  inasonta  kamar  yadda  nakeson  aliyu  


Abba  kuwa  ganin  kamar  safiya  ta  fita  hayyacinta  sai  yayi  saurin  tashi  ya  karasa  wajanta  karba  husna  yayi  yakaita  dakin  safiya  ya  kwantar  


Aljana safiya kalli  abba  cike da  fargaba  tace  abba  tamutu  ko?

Girgiza kai  abba  yayi  yace  ki  nutsu  mana  safiya  kidawo  hankalin ki  danmu  samu  mafita  bata  mutu  ba  amma  tana  cikin  halin ciwo  mai  tsananin  gaske  maza  kawo  min  ruwa  


B'acewa  safiya tayi  batayi  minti  biyu  ba  ta bayyana a  dakin  dauke  da  ruwa  mai  kyau  

Karb'a abba  yayi  yazauna  kusan husna   yana  kare mata  kallo  dan  yagano  inda  matsalar  take  , ya  dauki  tsayin  lokaci  yana  kallon  husna  tukun  ya  dago  ya  kalli  safiya  


Aljana safiya  tayi  saurin  zama  kusa  da  abba  tace  abba  kamar  sihiri  aka  mata na   bak'ak'en shadanun  aljanu  ko  ? Tako  karshen maganar  da  tambayar abba 



Ajiyar zuciya yasauke  yace  hakane  safiya  wannan  yarinyar ta  jima  tana  tare  da  aljanun  nan  suna  cutar   da  ita  saboda  rashin  imani  kallifa  cikine  ajikinta  amma  haka  suke  bata  wahala  abin  dake  cikin  natama  sun  soma  tabashi  sunason  kashe  shi ne   



Runtse ido  aljana safiya  tayi  jin  wannan  bak'in zancen   dagowa  tayi  idonta  ya  cenja  launi  yakoma  ja  har  wani  fatsa-fatsan  wuta  yake  saboda  tsananin  b'acin  rai  , tace  na  rantse  da  mahaliccina  saina  daukarwa  husna  fansan  abinda  aka  mata  abba  yanzu  meye  abinyi  



Abba  yace  safiya  sai  anmata  ruk'iya  tukun  za'a  fitar  dasu   


Ajana safiya  tace  abba  ka  mata  ni  zan  riketa  tana  mikewa  daga  inda  ta  zauna  ta  dauko  husna  ta  rungume  ta  ta  zauna  a  gaban  abba  tace  bismillah  abbana  , idan  kuma  za'a  dauki  lokaci  saina  kira  amina  ta  riketa  nizan  koma  nagano  mai  wannan  barnar  kuma  naje  wajan  babanta  na  tambayesa  ina  maman  husna  take  dan  nasan  wannan  muguwar  matar  ba  ita  ta  haifi  husna  ba  wata  kilama mamanta mutuwa  tayi  shine  suke  azabtar  da  rayuwar  ta  



Abba yace  to  yar albarka  kira  amina  tazo  saiki  tafi  Allah  yakareki  daga  duk  wani  sharri  



Ameen  ta  amsa  zoben hannun ta  ta  murza  nan  takawo  haske  ,  hasken  na  fita  saiga  amina  ta  bayyana  a dakin  tace  tashugaba  ta  har kika  daw...bata  karasa  zancen  ba  ganin yadda  safiya  take  huci  ko  magana  takasa  ga  wata  yarinya  a  hannun ta 


Aljana safiya tace  amina  ki  rikemin husna  abba  zai  mata  ruk'iya  ki  kulamin  da  ita  harna  dawo  


Cike da  girmamawa  amina tace  angama  shugaba  tukun ta  amsheta  , Aljana safiya  ta  b'ace  a  dakin  



Bata  bayyana  a  ko  ina  ba  sai  cikin  falon  umma  inda  ta  dauki  husna   zama tayi  a  kan kujerar  falon  jin  za'a  shigo  falon  tayi saurin  cenjawa  takoma  kamar husna   kwanciya tayi  kamar  yadda  irin  mai  tsananin  ciwo  yakeyi  haka  tayi  tana  jiran  taga  waye  zaiyo  falon



Da sallama  talatu  da  tabawa  suka  shigo  jin  ba  kowa  sai  talatu  tace  to  basa  nan  ma  ai  kinga  husna  a  kwance 



Dariyar mugunta  tabawa  tayi tace   a  husna  ansoma  jin  sauki  tinda  har  ana  tafiya  abarta  ta  kadai  a  gida  



Talatu tace  kuma dariyar  ta  miye  ganin  marar lfy  mukazofa  ba  gidan  biki  mukazoba  kike  dariya  ni  bana  son  shirme 


Bata  rai  tabawa  tayi tace  to  yaya  talatu  dariyar  farin  ciki  ne  nakeyi  husna  tasoma  samun  sauki 


Talatu tace  to  shikenan  suka  zauna  kuda  ita  


Talatu  tace  hmm  yarinya  taji  maza  kalli  yadda  takoma  shikenan  yanzu  andena  kawomin  kudi  nifa  badan  kar  malam  yace  banzoba  to  da  wllh  babu  abinda  zaikawoni  saboda  gulmar  mutane  yasa  nazo  


Murmishin mugunta  tabawa  tayi  tace  ai  nima  haka  wllh  yaya talatu  gyarama  ta  haifi  abinda  yake  cikinta  ta  mutu  kinga  idan  kika  dauki  yaron   zamuke  samun  kudi  daga  babansa  


Yar dariya  talatu tayi tace  kumafa  kema  kin  kawo  shawara  ai  naje  gidan  wani  malami  nace  a  kurumtar  da  husna  duk  lokacin  danaje  gidan ta  zai  bude  inda  alhaji  yake  ajiye  kudi  na  kwasa  son  raina  


Dariya tabawa  tayi tace  ashe  kece  kina  kurumtar  da  ita  to  waye  ya  mata  sauran  asirin  na  jikinta 



Talatu tace  wannan  kuma  ban  sani  ba  gaskiya  inda  nasanshi  to wllh  saina  maidashi  yadda  yawa  husna   nifa  kudi  nakeso  take  kawomin  aduk  lokacin  dazata  zo  ganin gida   ba rashin  lfy  nakeso  tayiba  , in  batada  lfy  taya  zatazo  gidan  bare  harta  kawo  min  kudi 



Shuru tabawa  tayi  cen  kuma  saita  tuno  wata  dubara murmishi tayi  tace  kinga  ko  naje  na  amso  wani  magani  a  wajan  wani  malami  yanzu  dauko  ruwa  ajika  shi  yace  a  shafe  mata  jikinta  dashi  


Da sauri  talatu  ta  tashi tace  gaskiya  naji dadin  wannan  abun  dakikayi  yar  kanwata  bari  nakawo  ruwan  ta  nufi  kitchen 



Tabawa  na  ganin  talatu  tashige  kitchen  tayi  saurin  dauko  k'unllin maganin  budewa tayi  ta  dauko  ta  shafa  a fuskar  aljana safiya  ta  zuba  mata  sauran  acikin  gashin  ,,kanta  tana  gamawa  tayi  saurin  cusa  ledar  jakarta  ta ciro  dayan  maganin  ta  rike  hannun ta  , dariyar mugunta  tayi tace  yar  wahala  ai  gyara  kibar  duniyar  ko  rukayya  ta  auri  alhaji abdallah  nima  nake shagali  da  naira    , sakarya  yaya talatu  batasan  nice  nan  namiki  asiri  nasaka  ake  shanye  jinin  jikinki  daga  karshe  a  kashe  ki  na tsaneki  husna  tinda  kikafi  rukayya  kyau  da  farin jini   dama  nace  saina  lalata  miki jin dadin rayuwar ki  saikin  zabi  mutuwa  akan  rayuwa    mai  kama  da  aljanu  , 



Duk abinda  takeyi  aljana safiya  najinta  bakin ciki  na  kara  ruruwa  a  zuciyar  ta  wato  kenan  matar  baban  husna  da  kanwarta  suke  suka  mata  wannan  abun  aikuwa  sun  dauko  ruwan  dafa  kansu  



Talatu tafito  tace  kinga  abinci  nagani  mai  dadi  natsaya  naci  tukun nafito  


Murmishi  tabawa tayi tace  kawo  ruwan  mika  mata  tayi  


Tabawa ta  bude  maganin  zata  zuba  saiya  kufce  mata  ta  zube  akasa  , cike  da  bakin  ciki  tace  kash  yaya talatu  kinga  maganin ya  zube   


Talatu  tace  wannan  wani  rin  hauka ne  ina  kike  kallo  har  maganin  yazube  yanzu  gashi  anyi  asarar  shi  muna  komawa  gida  gobe  kije  ki  amso  wani  maganin  



Tabawa  da  bakin ciki  ya  gama  cikata  tace  ai  dole  ma  nakoma  na  karbo  yanzu  tashi  mutafi  tinda  basu  gidan  basa  nan  


Mikewa  talatu tayi tace  to  muje  itama  mikewa  tayi  suka  fice  a  falon  


Suna fita  aljana safiya  ta  tashi  ta  karkade  jikinta  tace  lalle  zaku  karbi  hukunci  , maganin  daya  zube  ta   kalla   da  sauri  tamike tace  boka mai gobe da nisa  lalle  ganinan  zuwa  gareka  azzalimi  macuci  karshenka  yazo   , jin  maganar  umma  tayi  saurin  komawa  ta  kwanta  ta rufe  ido 


Umma  tace  shigo da sallama  da sauri  takaraso  inda  take  kwance  dago  da  kanta  tayi ta  daura  akan  ciyarta  tace  Allah sarki  yarinya  kiyi  hakuri  najima  gashi  indo   ta  fita  tabarki  ke  kadai  tana  share  hawayen  tausayin  surukar  tata 


Wani sanyi aljana  safiya  taji  jin  yadda  matar  take  kaunar  husna   

Su  mimee  ne  suka shigo  gidan  da  sauri  sukayo wajan  su  umma  


Mustafa yace  umma  wai  yaushe  mommy  zata  tashine kullin  damun  tambayeki sai  kice   bacci  take  hala  shi  baccin  nata  baa  gajiya  dashine  mudai  muce  ta tashi  anjima  daddy  zai  kaimu  shan  scream  kinji  umma  kice  ta  tashi  


Mimee tace  eh  umma  kice  ta  tashi  tamana   girkin  abincin  ta  mai  dadi 


Danna kukan ta  umma  tayi  tana  murmishin  yak'e  tace  kuyi  hakuri  batada  lfy  ne insha Allah  idan  tamu  sauki  saita  muku  girkin 


Kukan  mimee  tasoma  yi  takaraso  inda  take  kwance  ta  kama  hannun ta  tace  mommy  please  kitashi  daga  baccin  muje  asibiti  abaki  magani  kinji  mommy  kiyi  magana 

Mustafa  da  yusuf  ma  karasowa  sukayi  suna  fadin mommy  kitashi  muje  asibiti  gaba daya  suka  soma  kuka  duk  sun  cika  falon  da  kuka 


Itama  umma  tana  kallon su  tana  yin  nata  kukan  

Alhaji abdallah ne  yashigo  yaga suna kuka   zuciyar shi ce  ta  buga  da  karfi  da  sauri  karaso  yace  umma  maiya  faru  kuke  kuka  shi  azatonshi  jiyake  husna  mutuwa  tayi  ne


Mimee ce  ta  kalleshi  tace  daddy  kace  mommy ta  tashi  daga  baccin  akaita  asibiti  kaji  daddy  umma  tace  mana  batada  lfy  


Ajiyar zuciya yasauke  jin  ba  abinda  yake  tunani  bane   yace  to  kuje  kucire  kayanku  ku  canja  wasu  saikuzo  zata  tashi  kunji 


Da to  suka  amsa  suna  murna  sosai  jin  ance  husna  zata  tashi  


Umma tace  lamarin  ciwon  nan  na  husna  yana  karyamin  zuciya  wllh  abin  tausayin  yayi  yawa  ga  ciki  ga  ciwo  Allah dai  yabata  lfy  


Alhaji abdallah yace umma  kinga  husna  tana  motsi  , da  sauri  umma  ta  kalleta  


Bude  idon ta  tayi  tana  fadin  la'ilaha ilallahu Muhammad rasulullahi S.A.W  , cike da  farin ciki  umma  ta  kamata  ta  tasheta  jingina  ta  tayi  da  jikin  kujerar  



A hankali tace  umma ruwa  zansha 


Da sauri  alhaji abdallah  yayi kitchen  ya  dauko  mata  ruwan  ya  bata  tasha  



Umma  na  fadin  Alhamdulillah  Alhamdulillah  husna  ya  jikin  naki 


Kallon umma  tayi tace  da  sauki  sosai  akaini   daki  umma  wanka  zanyi  


Alhaji abdallah  yayi saurin  mikewa  zai  dauketa  yace  sahibaty  bari  nakaiki  


Da sauri  tace  a  a  bakai  zaka  kaini  ba  umma  nakeso  takaini  


Dariya umma tayi tace  to  yi  zaman  ka  na  kaita  abdallah  


Murmishi Alhaji abdallah  yayi yace  kai  sahibaty  ni  kika  wa  haka  agaban  umma  


Murmishi tayi kawai  a  zuciyar ta  tace  taya  zan  yadda  ka  taba  jikina  alhalin  kaiba  mijina  bane  , jin  umma  ta  kama  hannun  ta  sai tayi sauri  mikewa  , suka  yi  sama  dakin  umma  suka  shiga  , Umma  da  kanta  ta  hada  mata  ruwan  wanka  tace  to  jeki  kiyi  ya'ta 


Da to  ta  amsa  ta  ahige toilet din  


Umma  tadawo  kasa  wajan  alhaji abdallah  suka  soma  hira  cike da  jin dadin  husna  ta  samu  sauki  


Aljana safiya  na  ganin umma  tabar  dakin  saita  b'ace  itama   a  dajin  boka  mai gobe da nisa  ta  bayyana  , 


Boka mai gobe  da  nisa  yana  lalata da  wata  mata  kamar  yadda  yakewa  tabawa  a  haka  tasamesu 


Bata  musu  magana  ba  sai  ranta  daya  kara  b'aci. Tace  fasik'i  mushirki  yau  karshenka  yazo  dan  iska  marar mutumci  




Bayan  boka  da  wannan  matar  sun  fito  aljana safiya  tayi  saurin  komawa  mace   mai  kyau  da  daukar  hankali   


Boka  yazo  inda  take  yace  hahaha  yarinya  maike  take  dake  tin  jiya  aka  fadamin  zuwanki  fadi  bukatar  ki  abiya miki  


Haushi ne  yakara  kama aljana safiya  tace  kaji  makaryaci  wai  an  fada  mishi  zuwana  munafikin Allah  saika yabawa  aya  zakinta  , dagowa tayi  tana  kallon shi 


A razane  boka  yaja  da  baya  yake  ke  wacece  


Tsawa  ta daka  mishi  nunashi tayi  da  yatsanta  saiga  boka  daure  jikin  bishiya  tace  wacece  kace  nice 𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞  aljana safiya  nice  nan    kaima  kasan  ba  alkhairi  yakawoni  wajanka  kaba  ajalinka  ne  yazo  mushirki 



Zazzaro ido boka  yayi yace  waiyo natuba  kiyi hakuri  karki  kasheni  ban  gaji da  duniya  ba  



Wanka  mishi maruka tayi  har  uku  tace  ni  zaka  cewa  baka  gaji  da  duniya  ba  iyee  nace  nizaka  fadawa  wannan  banzar  maganar  to  maza  ka  ciro  asirin  dake  jikin  husna  yanzu  yanzu  anan  take  nakeso  



Da sauri  ya  kalle ta  murza  hannunshi  yayi cen  kuma  sai  hayaki  yafito  daga  hannun  kara  yasaka  yana  yarfa  hannu  nashi  yace  ai  tuni anyi  ruk'iya  sun  gone  yanzu  batada  komai  jinin  tama  andawo  mata  dashi   kiyi hakuri  karki  kasheni 



Aljana safiya  taji  sanyi a  ranta  jin  ance  husna  tasamu lfy  kallon shi  tayi tace  ai  yau  sai  kwanan  lahira  bana  rantsuwar   nakasa cikata  dole  na  kashe  ka  ta zaro  wukar  ta  


Ihu boka  yasoma  yi  yace  kitaimaka  karki kasheni  banyi aikin  alkhairi  ko  dayaba  aduniya  banida  lada  kodaya  kwaya  dayane  duk  aikina  zunufaine  kiyi hakuri  karki kasheni  



Murmishi yayi yace  da  kasan  ba  daidai  kake  aikatawa  ba sai  yanzu  to  ba  wannan  alfarmar  a  tsarina  , maida  wuk'ar ta  tayi  tace  inada  aiki  agabana  zoben hannun ta murza  yana  kawo haske  tace  hafeez  maza  kazo 


Yana  bude idon ta  hafee  ya  bayyana  a  gabanta  yace  fada  taki  cikawa  nawa  shugaba  mai  adalci  , murmishi tayi tace  inason  ka  kashemin  wannan  ta  nuna mishi  boka mai gobe da nisa  tace  kisan  gilla  kisan  wulakanci  nakeso  kamishi  yasoma  d'and'anan  azaba  tin  kafin  yaje  lahira  tana  kaiwa  nan  ta  b'ace  



Hafeez  ya  ciwo  takobinsa  wani  kwalba  ya  fiddo  da  ruwa  aciki  watsawa  boka  yayi  ai  take  fatar  jikinsa  tasoma  d'ayewa 


Ihun azaba  boka  yakeyi  yana  kallon  hafeez 


Hafeez yace  bakaga  komaiba  munafiki  wata  yar wuk'a  ya  fuddo  ya  yanki  boka  a  fuskarshi  da  kafarshi  hannun  shi  ko  ina  ya  yankeshi  


Wani  magigicen  ihu  boka  keyi  yana  bawa  hafeez hakuri  


Dariya hafeez keyi yace  bakaga  komai  hallau  yaciro  kwalbar ruwan  yabi  duk  inda  ya  yankeshi  yazuba  mishi  ruwan  acikin  gurin 


Boka  azaba  tayi  azaba  yafita  hayyacinsa sa  sai  kururuwa  yake  da  ihu  



Hafeez  na  ganin  zai  suma  ya  zaro  takobin sa  ta  soka  mishi  a  cikinsa  saida  yamishi  yagal gayal  yamishi  kisan  wulakanci  , tukun  yabar  dajin  yakoma  dajinsu  ranshi  fari tass  yana  hamdala  yakashe  mashirki  



Aljana safiya  na  b'acewa  daga  wajan boka  a  dakin  umma  ta  bayyana  kaya  ta  cenja  tafito  tana  tafiya  a  hankali  irin  na  masu  rashin lfy 


Da sauri umma  ta  tarota  suka  zauna  sosai  suke  nuna  kalawarsu  gareta  


Aljana safiya  taji  dadi sosai  ganin  mijin husna  da  mamanshi  sunason  husna  hakan  yamata  dadi  saita  saki  jiki  dasu  suka  ringa  hira  



WASHE GARI 

Alhaji yazo  gaisheda  umma  ganin  ta  yayi  ta  rufe  ido  tana  murmishi  sosai  ji  yayi  ta  burgeshi  kama  hannun ta  yayi yace sahibaty  



Da sauri  ta  bude  idonta  tace  na'am  antashi lfy  


Murmishi yayi yace Alhamdulillah  ya  jikin  tace  da  sauki  


Mikewa  yayi yace  natafi  office  saina dawo  


Dama  Allah Allah  take  yasakar  mata hannu  tace  adawo lfy  Allah yatsareka  


Cikin jin dadi yace  ameen  sahibaty na  Allah  yabaki  lfy  



Murmishi tamishi ya  fita  



Haka  suka  yini  baba  yazo  ganin  ta  sosai  yaji  dadin  ganin  yartashi  tasamu  lfy  sun  jima  suna  hira  tukun  yace  zai  tafi  


Rakiya tayo  mishi  tsayawa tayi  tace  baba  wai  ina  mama ta  take  baka  taba  fadamin  inda  takeba  ko  kuma  danginta 


Nan da  nan  fuskar baba  ya  cenja  jin  abinda  tace  tuni  yasoma  hawaye  yace  husna  kiyi  hakuri  kisamu  lfy  kinji  zan  fada  miki  ko  wacece  mamanki  da  danginta  kinji   idan  kika  warke  saikizo  na  fada  miki  


Jiki a sanyaye tace  to  Allah  yakaimu   nan  sukayi  sallama  yatafi  itama  tajuya  tana  son  taji  labarin  maman  husna  da  har  baba  yake  kuka  daga  tambayar shi  aljana safiya  tace  lalle  akwai  babban  lamari  a  labarin  nan  amma  ko  menene  ma  zan  jira  saina  je  nakoyawa  waccen  tabawar  Hankali  tukun  nan  tashige  ciki



Tana  shiga  tace  umma  wai  wacce  unguwa ne  gidan  tabawa  kanwar  mama  yanzu  na  manta  wllh  


Umma bata  kawo  komai  aranta  ba  tace  suna unguwar su,TUDUN WADA  nan ne  shine  kika  manta  


Murmishi yayi  yace  eh  mantawa  ne  nayi     



Cikin  dare kowa  yayi bacci  aljana  safiya  ta  b'ace  ya  nufi  gidan  tabawa   


Tabawa  na  bacci  taji  an  kifeta  da  mari  har  biyu  agigice  ta  tashi  tana  waiyo Allah  wannan  wata  jaraba  ce  haka  kodai  mafarki  nakeyi ne  ji  tayi  hallau  an  sake  wanka  wani  marin  na  wannan  karon  yafi  shigar ta  so  ihu  tayi  , tafito  falo  ta  guje  , wutar  falon  ta  kunna   



Tana  kunnawa  aka  kashe  tsoro ne  yakama  tabawa  tace  wai  waye  ne  


Daga  cen  bakin  kofar  falon  taji ance  ubanki ne...Ai  bashiri  tabawa  taji  cikinta  yabada  k'ululu  bata  ankaraba  taji  zawo  da  fitsari  suka tasakesu  alokaci  guna   , sai  zare ido takeyi  gilmawar  farin  wani  abu  tagani  , a  gudu  tanufi  dakin ta  tana fadin m....

*47 —48*




Tabawa  duk  tarikice  saboda tsananin birgita  da  tayi  tin  kafin  tashiga  dakin  aka  bango  kofar  dakin  da  karfi  ji  kake  bamm  da  sauri  ta  tsaya  tana  maida  numfashi  sai  kyerma  take  duk  ta  rasa  nutsuwar  ta  idan  ka  ganta  kamar  wadda  aka  zamo  daga  kogi  ta  jika  jikinta da  futsari ga zawo  ga  zufa  abu  goma  da  ashirin  yataron  wa  tabawa  sai  nemanta  taimako take  amma  babu  wanda  yake  jinta  


Hasken  falon  aka  kunna  da  sauri  tabawa  tasoma  zare  ido  tana  kallon  ko  ina  wani  uban ihu  ya  kwala  tana  ja  dabaya    bakinta  na  rawar  sanyi  saboda  abinda   tagani  

Wata  yar  karamar  yarinya  tagani  gashin  kanta  har  kasa  bakinta  jini  ne  ki  zubowa  idonta  manya  manya   gasu  jajaye  kamar  garwashin  wuta  bata  kaya  ajikinta  cikinta  na  fitar  da  wani  ruwa  mai  d'auyi   kafafunta  irin  na  doki  ne  fuskar  ta  duk  kuraje  ne  suna  fitar da  ruwa  mai tsami  sosai  , 


Da sauri  tabawa  taringa  ja  dabaya  tana  fadin  waiyo Allah  Allah nashiga  uku  ni  tabawa  wannan  fatalwa  ce  ko  aljana  wai  Allah  ku  kuke  ganinmu  bake  ganinku  ba Allah  yabaki  hakuri  babata  Allah  yahuci  zuciy... bata  karasaba  taji  an  taka  mata  wata  gigitacciyar  tsawa  wadda  ta  kara  gigitata  tsoro  ya  kara  shigarya  


Aljana safiya  tace  kiyimin  shuru  munafuka  annamimiya  mashirkiya  fasik'a  marar  imani  da  kinsan  da  Allah din  iyee  tambayar ki  nakeyi  da  kinsan  da  Allah  har  daza kike. Ambatarsa   shashasha  marar mutumci  da kin  rike  Allah  ai  da  baki  je  kika  kashe  rai kika  lalata  rayuwar  marainiyar Allah  ba  gaki  mazinaciya  maci  amanar  auren sunna  maci  amanar  Allah  kinyi  asara  duniya  da  lahira  yau  zanga  mai   daure  miki  gindin  iskanci  da  kikeyi  saikin  zab'i  mutuwa  akan  rayuwar  duniya  


Cikeda  har kowa  da  murya  mai  amo  aljana safiya  take  magana  saboda  tsananin  b'acin  rai  har  turiri  yake  fita  daga  bakinta  


Tabawa  tayi  sumar  zaune  jin  muryar  aljana safiya  ta  gama  rikicewa  gabaki  daya  ma  batasan  bama  a  ina  take 



Aljana safiya  tace  yanzu  zaki  karbi  hukunci  marar  mutumci  kawai  , zoben hannun ta  murza  takawo  haske sosai  tukun  ta  rufe idonta  tace  hafeez  amina  kuna  ina  maza  kuzo  ga  aiki  yasamu,  tana  bude  idon ta suka  bayyana  agaban ta  


Amina tace  fada  taki cikawa  tamu  ya  shugaba  mai  adalci   


Murmishi safiya tayi  tace  kunga  wannan  azzalumar  ku  ladaftar  min  da  ita  sosai  


Murmishin jin dadi hafeez yayi yace  ai  koni  kadai  na  isheta shugaba  , 


Murmishi  safiya tayi  tace  ai  inason  hukuncin  ta  ya  ninka  na  boka  sau  goma  karku  kasheta  yanzu  ku  ladaftar  da  ita  yar iska kawai 


Tabawa  ko  tuni  ta  suma  zawo  da  fitsari  tayi  yafi  a  k'irga  


Amina  ta  karaso  inda  take  a  wanka  mata  mari  ko  wani  kuncin  , ai  ba  shiri  tabawa  tadawo  daga  sumar  datayi  tana  ihu  


Tana  bude  ido  taga  wasu  abu  agabanta  sun  tsura  mata  ido  suba  mutane  ba  suba  dabbobi  ba   abun  tsoro  ko  wanne  fuskar nan  ba  annuri  


Ihu takuma  sakawa  tana  neman  taimako  


Hafeez yace  amina  maza  bani  bulala ta  mu  fara  zaneta   

Amina  ta  zaro  bulala  biyu  ta  mikawa  hafeez  daya  tarike  dayar  nan  suka  ringa  zane  tabawa  tako  ina   ba  tsayawa  

Tin  tabawa  na  ihu  harta  suma   ruwa  mai  mugun  sanyi  aljana  safiya  ta  maka  mata  shi  a  fuskar ta   tana dawowa  daga  sumar  sukaci  gaba  da  zaneta  sai  ihu  take  tana  zagin  ladi  tada  kaita  gun  boka  gashi yanzu  tana  amsar  hukunci  



Saida  suka  mata  dukan  da  bata  motsi  shima  aljana safiya  tace  su  kyaleta  , dena  dukanta  sukayi  gefe  suka  koma  suna  kallon ta 



Aljana safiya  ta  mike  daga  inda  ta  zauna  ta  nufo  tabawa  tana  mata  murmishin  mugunta   


Tabawa  ba  abin takeyi  sai  zare  ido   tana  ihu  tin karfinta  amma  bamai  jinta  


Aljana safiya  na  isoga  inda  tabawa  take  shak'ar  wuyanta  tayi  ta hadata da  bango  saida  taga  ta  soma  kakarin  mutuwa  ta  wullar  da  ita  kasa  tana  huci  tace  bakiga  komai  ba  yanzu  kika  soma  amsar  hukunci  daga  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧 𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙞𝙢𝙖𝙞  bana  yafiya  bana  tausayin  wanda  bashida  tausayi  bana  bukatar  masu  sab'awa Allah  a  tare  dani  bana  son  mugu  azzalimi  maci  amanar  Allah  banason  duk  mace  ko namijin  da  sukecin   amanar  aure  dana  ubangijinsu  bana  son munafiki  mai  fuska biyu  duk  masu  halayen nan  idan  suka  shiga  hannuna  ni  safiya  zasu  gwammaci  dama  basuzo  duniya  ba  ke kuma  kin  kasance  daga cikinsu   aduk  cikin  halayen  dana  lissafo  duk  kinasu  dan  haka  dole  hukuncin  ki  yafi  na  duk  wanda  ya  cutar  min da  husna   muni  , tana  kaiwa  nan  tasake  boll  da  tabawa  ta  bugata  da  kasa  



Tabawa  kuwa saboda  tsananin azabar  da  k'ugunta  da  bayanta  suke  mata in  banda  ihu  ba  abinda  yakeyi  



Aljana safiya  ta  kalli  su  amina  tace  yaran  kirki  kuje  gida   amina ki  kulamin  da  husna  sosai  kafin  nazagayota  yanzu  asuba  ta  gabato  zan  koma  gidan  surukar  husna  , sai  da  dare  zamu hade  


Hafeez  kam  dariya  yayi yace  kidade  kiyi k'arko  ummin  aliyu  shugaba mai  adalci  ai  in  dare  yayi  akusa  zan  tsaya  ina  ganin  kiranki  nayi  saurin  zuwa  


Amina ma  murmishi tayi tace  nima haka  sai anjima  shugaba  , Safiya tace  to  saikunji  kirana  tana  fadin  haka  suka  b'ace 



Aljana safiya ta  kalli  tabawa  wanda  take  a  sume  mari sakar  mata  guda  daya  wanda  yadawo  da  ita  daga  sumar  tayi  , tana  ganin  tadawo  hayyacinta  ta  b'ace  itama  



Tabawa  da  rarrafe  ta  shiga  dakinta  ta  nufi  toilet  tahada  ruwan  zafi  sosai  jikinta  yake  mata  zogi  duk  shatin  bulala  ne  ajikinta  ga  hannun da ya  goce  k'ashin  bayanta  ma  jitake  kamar  ya  karye  yadda  yake  mata  zafi  



Aljana safiya  na  b'acewa  sai  adakin  umma ta  bayyana   ganin umma  tayi  tana  bacci  ajiyar zuciya  yasauke  tace  Alhamdulillah  da bata  farko  ba  taga  bana nan  a  hankali ta  kwanta  tana  murmishi  tace  zaku  gane  wacece  safiya  , rufe  idonta  tayi  jin  umma  na  kokarin  tashi  


Umma  natashi  tayi  addu'ar  tashi  daga bacci  tajuya  ta  kalli  aljana safiya  wadda  take  a  matsayin  husna  hannu  tasa tasoma  tashin  ta  tace  husna  tashi  ankira  salla  kinji  tashi  muyi  salla  saiko  sake  komawa  bacci 


Duk  maganar  da takeyi  aljana safiya  najinta  sai  ta  bude  idonta  a  hankali  tashi  tayi  tana  salati  tace  umma  har  anyi  kiran sallar  ne 


Murmishi umma tayi tace  eh  anyi  tashi  muje  muyi  salla  , tashi  sukayi  umma  tafara  shiga  tayo  alwala  itama  tashiga  ta  dauro  alwala,  salla  suka tada  bayan  sun  gama  umma  ta  kalle

  
Tace  husna  yajikin  naki 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  da sauki amma  yanzu  banajin  komai  sai  rashin kwarin jiki  


Itama  murmishin  tayi  tace  Allah yakara sauki  shima  zai dawo  ahankali  yanzu  kikoma ki  kwance  kafin  yaran nan su  tashi  su hanaki  bacci  


Kasa  tayi  dakanta  tace  umma  tukun  tahau  gadon  ta  kwanta  , Umma  tayi  wanka  ta  canja  kaya  tafita  adakin  


Aljana safiya  na ganin  umma  ta  fita  ta  mike  ta  b'ace  a dakin  , bata  bayyana  ko  inaba  sai  dajin  ta  

Da sallama  tashiga  dakin  


Abba  yadago yana  amsa  sallamar  yace  a  a  kece  yanzu  


Murmishi tayi tace  eh  nazo  naga  jikin  husna  ne  


Abba yace  gata nan  tana  salla  kibari  tagama  saiku  gaisa 



Bayan  husna  ta  gama  salla  ne  ta  juyo  tana  kallon  aljana  safiya  

Murmishi  safiya tayi  mata  tace  antashi lfy  husna 


Da  mamaki  take  kallon ta  tace  lfy lau   wai  nan  a  ina  nake  inasu  umma  da  mijina  da  yarana  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  nan  kina  gidan  su  safiya  ne  wato  ni  , nina  kawoki  nan  lokacin  kina  tsakanin  mutuwa  da  rayuwa  nan  ta  kwance  labari  komai  ta  fada  mata  


A razane  husna  take  kallon su  tace  dama  ku  ba  mutane  bane  

Aljana safiya tace  sosai makuwa  mu  aljanu ne  amma  masu  adalci  da  taimakon  wanda  aka cuta  kinga  wannan ta  nuna  aliyu  to  shima  daga  taimako  nasameshi  mutum  neshi  ba  aljani  ba  amma  anan  yake  rayuwar sa  tin kallon  da  yazo  duniya  mamanshi  tarasu  tabani  amanar  shi  inason  nan  gaba  nasamo  danginsa  suganshi  


Ajiyar zuciya husna  tasauke  tace  ayya Allah sarki  gaskiya  kunada  Kirki  Allah  yasaka  muku  da  alkhairi  yanzu  wani  hali  su  umma  ke  ciki  



Aljana safiya  tace  suna  nan  lfy  kuma sunji  dadin  samun  lfyr  ki  yanzu  zan  koma  idan  dare  yayi  zanzo  na  daukeki  na  maidake  gida   ki  kwana  wajan  mijin  ki  tinda  yaga  kinji sauki  shi  azatonshi  ni  kece  shiyasa  nazo  naga  jikin  naki  sai  kuma  naga  lfy  tasamu  Alhamdulillah  da  dare  zan  maidake   gida  kinji  husna   


Share hawaye  husna tayi  tace  gaskiya  bani  da  baki  yimuku  godiya  kudin  ko  a  jinsin  mutane  da  wuya  asamu  kamarku  Allah  Allah yasaka  muku  da  gidan  aljanna nagode sosai  kedin  ta  musamman  ce  kin  amsa  sunan  𝙖𝙡𝙟𝙖𝙣𝙖𝙧  𝙟𝙖𝙧𝙪𝙢𝙖𝙞   Allah  yakareki  aduk  inda  kika  nufa


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ba komai  husna  basai kinmin godiya  ba  sai  anjima  innazo  daukarki  


Abba  yace to  yar albarka  saimun  jiki  , da  to  ta  amsa nan  ta  b'ace  


Husna ta  kalli abba  tace  kaka  Allah  yasaka da alkhairi zaku  bani  aliyu? 


Murmishi abba  yayi  yace  a ai  saidai  ki  tambayi  safiya  tace  ko  babanshi  yazo  bazata  iya  rabuwa  da aliyu  dashiba 


Husna tace to  shikenan  zan  tambaye ta  daga  tadawo,  tana  rumgume  aliyu  dayayi  luf  ajikinta  yana  jinsu  suna  hirarsu  koda  aljana safiya  tazo  bai  kulataba  tayi  kwanciyar  shi  jikin  husna  



Aljana safiya nakomawa  tayi saurin  canja  kayanta  tafito  tayo  kasa  wajan  su  umma  


Da  gudu  yaran suka  karaso  gareta   suna  mommy  mu  kintashi  lfy  


Rumgume tayi  tana murmishi tace  Alhamdulillah  yakuke  


Lfy lau sukace  mimee tace  mommy  idan  muka  dawo  daga  school  zamuje  wajan wasa ko

Kuncinta safiya taja  tace  sosai makuwa  ya'ta   daga  kundawo  damuje   sakinta  sukayi  kowa  yadauki  jakarshi  na  school  sukace  beyy beyy mommy  saimun dawo


Itama hannu ta daga musu  tace  beyy  adawo lfy 


Umma na  murmishi tana  kallon  su  cike da  burgewa 


Wajan tadawo  suka  soma  hira  tagaisheda  alhaji   ,, amsawa  yayi  yana  maijin  dadin  ganinta  


Tashi  yayi  shima  yafice  wajan  aikinshi  rakiya tamishi  har  bakin  mota saida  taga  fitarshi  tukun  takoma ciki  


Da  yamma  sukaje  wajan wasa  sosai  taga  yaran  na  nuna  jin  dadin su  sai  kusan  magarib  suka  dawo  gida  


Bayan  sallar isha"i  kowa  yashiga  daki  , cen  karfe 12:00 am   na  dare  aljana safiya  ta  tashi  ahankali  tana  kallon  alhaji  abdallah  wanda  yajuya  mata  baya  saboda  ta  kiya  ya  rumgume ta  shine  yayi  fushi  har  bacci  yayi  gaba  dashi 


B'acewa tayi  taje  dajinta  tana  shiga  taga  husna  tana  addu'a  a  hankali tace  husna  maza  fito  mutafi  


Da sauri  husna  ta  mike  tasaka  hijab din  ta  taje  kusan  aljana safiya  



Aljana safiya tace rufe  idonki  ba  musu  tarufe  

Aljana safiya tace  bude  , a  hankali ta  bude  idonta  akan  alhaji  abdallah  


Aljana safiya tace  kiyi ahankali ki  kwanta  idan  natafi  saiki  tasheshi  tabashi  hakuri  fushi  yakeyi 



Husna  tace  maiyakewa  fushi  kuma??

Aljana safiya tace  rungume ni  zaiyi  na  nuna  banaso  shine  yayi  fushi  azatonshi  kice  yanzu  natafi  kibashi  hakuri  



Godiya sosai  husna tamata  tukun aljana safiya ta  b'ace  adakin 


Husna ta  tashi  Alhaji  tasoma  bashi  hakuri  tace  kayi  hakuri  sahiby  jikina ne  banajin  dadinsa  shiyasa  nakiya  ka  rumgume  ni  


Murmishi yayi yace  ba  komai  ai  nasan  bakiyimin  irin haka  ya   daukota  zuwa gabanshi  , da sauri  husna  tashige  dama  tayi  kewar  mijin  nata  , ya  rufesu  da blanket (  nima  nabi aljana safiya  nabar  masoya)



Aljana safiya nafita  ta  kira  su  amina  suna  zuwa  tace  amina  kije  gidan  talatu  kai  kuma  hafeez  kaje  gidan  tabawa  ni  kuma  zanje  gidan  wannan  yar iska  ladin  duk  da   hukuncin ta  baikai  nasuba  ,,,

Dakin  daya  tashiga  nan  taga  baba  shima  yana  bacci  da  tasbafa  a  hannun  shi   a  alama  yana  lazimi  bacci  yayi  gaba  dashi  , janyo  mishi  kofar  tayi  a  bakin  kofa  ta  watsa  wani  haske  a  bakin  kofar  ta  wuce  dakin talatu  


Tana shiga  tace  yar  banza  zaki  fadamun  dalilin  dayasa  kike  takurawa  husna  tana  fadin  haka  ta  kunna  hasken  dakin 


Talatu  cikin  bacci  taji  a  kunna  hasken  daki  da  magagin  bacci  ta  tashi  tace  kai  wllh  malam  kaidai  ankai  wahalalle  inbanda  neman  fitina  irin  naka  mutum  na  bacci  kazo  ki  kunna  mishi  hasken  da  jaraba  to  wllh  saika  fadamun  dalilin  dayasa  kake  kunna  min  haske  ni  zaka  tasa  a  bacci  to  kataro  masifa  da  bala'i  wllh  agidan  nan  dan  renin  hankali   bari  dai  natashi  naga  meye  kakeyi  bude  idon ta  tayi  tass  amma  bataga  kowa  ba  ga  dakin  kuma  a  rufe  yake 


Aljana safiya  na  kallon ta  ita  abun  dariya ma  yabata  wai  mijin  tane  dan  renin hankali  tace  tab  lalle  matar  nan  ba  iya  kan  husna  kika  tsaya  ba  har  kan  baban  husna  ashe  kin  rena  mijin  zakiga  reni  kuwa  karamar marar kunya 


Talatu ganin ba  kowa  sai  tace  hmmm  wato  toilet  kashiga  lekawa  tayi  amma  taga  babu  kowa  a ciki cike da  masifa  tace  wai  wannan  wani  irin hauka ne  


Aljana safiya tace  uwarki  ce  take  haukan  


Da sauri  talatu  ta  waiga  amma  bataga  kowa  ba  hakan  yasa  taji  tsoro  sosai  yashigeta  kafin  tajuya  taga  wutar  dakin  ta  mutu  da  sauri  ta  takure  a  waje  daya  tana  zare ido  ji  tayi  an  shafa  mata  baya  a  hankali  da  sauri  ta  jiya  amma  duhu  dakin  gareshi bataga  kowaba  



Bata  ankara  ba  taji  saukar  mati  tass tass  saida  aka  mata  takwas  ko  wani  kuncin mari  hudu  

A gigice  talatu  tasaka ihu  tana fadin  yauni  talatu  naga  jaraba  da   wannan  wani  abune  yake  barina  haka  bata  rufe  bakiga  ta  an  shak'e  mata  wuya  anyi sama  da  ita   


Aljana safiya  ranta  a  mugun b'ace  tasoma  jibgar talatu  tako  ina  ba  tsayawa  tana fadin  azzalimi  asararriya  yar  wahala  macuciya  marar  d'a'a    wadda  bata  da  tausayi  yau  zanga  mai  cetonki   yar  banza  taci  gaba  da  dukanta  ba  sassautawa  


Ihu talatu  keyi  kamar  ranta  zaifita  zafin  duka  na  shigarta  tako  ina duk  inda  aka  buga  ajikinta  wani  tsananin  azaba  takeji  


Saida  ta  mata  dukar  tsiya  tukun ta  saketa  ta  fado  kasa  tim  wutar  dakin  ta  kunna   ta  bayyana  a  suffar ta  na  aljanu  


A  firgice  talatu  taringa  karara  ihu  tana  kiran  malam  malam  kazo  ka  taimakeni  malam  musbahu  mijina  kazo  za'a  kashe  maka  matarka  musbahu  dan  Allah ka fito ka  taimakeni ina  kashiga  , tana  fadin  magana  tana  ja  dabaya  saboda  abinda  take  gani  agabanta  *(hhhh ni kuwa nace  su  talatu  yau  har  kama  sunan  baba  na  ainashi anji maza*)



Tsawa  aljana safiya  tayiwa  talatu  tace  yimin shuru  munafuka  da  kinsan  darajar  musbahu  da  kike  kiranshi  marar mutumci  da  kinsan  shi  musbahu  yana  amsa  sunan  mijinki  da  baki  daukeshi  abakin  komai  shashasha  marar  kirki  yau  zakiyi  bayani  


Talatu  kuwa inbanda  zaro ido  da  karkarwa  da jikinta  keyi  bata  iya  ko  magana  saboda tsananin birgita  da  mugun  tsoron  daya shigeta  a  hankali  tace  dan Allah  dan  Muhammed  rasulullahi  S.A.W  karki  kasheni  kiyi  hakuri  dan  Allah  namiki  alkawarin  bazan  sake  yiwa  malam  koda  kallon  banza  bare  na  zageshi  kona  fada  miki  magana  wllh  natuba  har  abada  bazan  sake  yiwa  malam  rashin  kunya  ba  ki taimakeni   wllh  ban  taba  kyautatawa  malam  ba  idan  na  mutu  yanzu  maizan tarar  a  kabarina  dan Allah  karki kash...bata  karasaba  jin  daukar  duka  a  bakin  ta 


Cike da  tsantsar  bac'in rai  aljana safiya  tace  idan  kika  kuma  magana  na  lahira  saiya  fiki  jin  dadi  marar  hankali kawai   ashe  kinsan ba  daidai  kike  aikatawa  ba  amma  kika  kasa gyara  halayenki  ashe  kinsan  akwai  mutuwa  amma  kika  kasa  aikata  alkhairi  ashe  kinsan da  kwanciyar  kabari  amma  kika  kasa  yiwa  mijin ki  biyayyar aure  dan  kisamu  salama  acikin  kabarinki  idan  kin  mutu  duk  kinsan  dasu  amma  kike  aikata  abubuwan  da  kikeyi  wato  tsabar  rashin  mutumci  ne  da  rashin tarbiya  da  imani  irin naki  ga  kuma  son zuciya  da  kwadayii duk ke  daya   dukkansu  kinsan  ba  daidai  kike  yiba  amma  kika  kasa  gyara  halayenki  to  yau  saikin fadamin  wani  dalili  yasa  kike  k'untatawa  rayuwar  marainiyar  Allah  dan  ubanki  jaka  kawai  , tana  kaiwa  nan  tasake  shak'ar  talatu  taci  gaba  da  dukanta  kamar  ba  gobe 


Talatu ihun azaba take  tana fadin  Allah  yatsine  miki  tabawa  kin  kaini  kin baro  kin  sakani  a  muguwar  hanya  yanzu  gashi  fatalwa  taxo  tana  min  hukunta  dan Allah  inna  fatalwa  kiyi  hakuri  zan  gyara  hakina  insha  Allah  zan nemi  gafarar  husna  da  babanta  wllh  bazan  sakeyin duk  abunda  zai  cutar  da  husna  ko babanta  kiyiimin rai


Aljana safiya  jin talatu tana magana  tace  iyee  ashe  baki  dakuba  dan  yar mararsa  tarbiya  shiyasa  kika  samu  bakin  magana  , bulala  ta  fiddo  taci  gaba  da  zaneta  tana fadin  ni  zakike  roko  wai  zaki  gyara  ai  saidai  duka  yasa  ki  gyara  ni  bana  yafiya  bana  afuwa  bana  sassauci  bana  jin tausayin  duk  wanda  zai cuci dan  uwanshi  ko  yaci  amanar  da  aka  bashi  , yau  zakimin  bayanin  wadda  yake  baki shawarar  ki  takurawa  rayuwar  bayin  Allah  , taci gaba  da  zaneta  saida  taga  ta  suma  tukun ta  kyaleta  tana  huci  kamar  kasa 


Waigawa  aljana safiya  tayi  hannun ta  d'aga  nan  ruwa  ya  bayyana    watsawa  talatu  tayi  tana fadin  tashi  maza  kifadamun  wanda  yasaki  kike takura musu  


Ihu talatu tasaka  jin  ruwa  mai  mugun  zafi  ajikinta  harda  gishiri  aciki  duk  inda  ruwan  yataba  ajikinta  wata  kalar  azaba  talatu  takeji  musamman ma  inda  yakeda  raunika  sai  ihun  azaba  take  tana fadin  na  tuba  kiyafemin  innata  bazan  sakeba dan Allah  kiyimin rai


Wata mahaukaciyar  dariya  aljana safiya  sai  kuma  ta  hade  rai  kamar  ba  ita  tayi  dariyar  ba  tace  afuwa  daya  zanmiki  ki  fadamin  dalilin ki  na  aikata  wannan  abubuwan  da kike  maza  ina  jinki



Talatu tace  wllh  ada  ni  inason  husna  har  babanta  ina  mishi  biyayya  tabawa ce  ta  huremin kunne  ganin   baban  husna  banasona  sai  maman  husna  yake  so  ko  magana  namiki  sai  yaga  dama  yake  amsawa  saboda  basona  yake ba  hasalima  auren  dole  aka  mishi  dani   mahaifiyar shi  ce  tahada  auren  kuma  duk abinda  nakeyi  ita  ke  goyamin  baya  


Aljana safiya tace  ok ke kuma  dake  bakida  hankali  shine  kika  bi shawarar su  kuke  bin  bokaye ko 

Jiki  na  b'ari  talatu tace  wllh  kawai  sharrin  zuciya ne  innata  ba  dason  raina  hakan  tafaru  ba  duk  tabawa  ce  da  kaka  suka  sakani  a  wannan  hanyar 


Aljana safiya tace  to  yanzu  ina  ita  maman   husna  take  ta  mutu ne ko  sakinta  akayi   ?


Sunkuyar da  kai  talatu tayi  jin  za'a  tono  mata  sirrin ta wanda  ta  jima  tana  boyeshi  

Aljana safiya ta  mata  tsawa  tace  dake  nakefa ina  maman  husna  


Talatu da sauri  tace  bansan  wani  hali  takeciba  tana  raye  kota  mutu  shine  ban  saniba 


Alajana safiya tace  to  ina  kuka  kaita 


Talatu tace  ni  dama  saida  nace  ba  ruwana  tabawa ce  da  kaka  sune  suka  je  wajan  boka  sukace  ya  kasheta  , sai  bokan  yace  bazai yiyuba  saboda  maman  husna  akwai  ibada  , saidai  in  lokacin  da  bata  salla  za'a  iya  kasheta  idan  ankashe ta  zai  dauke  gangar  jikinta  yake  tsafi  dashi  , lokacin  da  tabawa  tazomin  da  maganar  fir  naki  yarda  nace  wllh  babu  ruwana  bazan  iya  daukar rai  ba  saboda  miji  baya  sona  nayiwa  tabawa  rashin mutumci  , amma  dayake  tafini  wayo  sai  cemin  tayi  

Yaya talatu  idan  aka  kashe  maman  husna  kinga  husna  tadawo  wajanki  idan  tagirma  zaki  kwashi  naira  kuma  idan malam  yaga  babu  maman  husna  zaidawo  yana  sonki  kamar  yadda  yakeson  maman  husna  

Da  wannan  gurguwar  shawarar  tabawa  tasaka na  yadda  amma  zuciya  ta  nakasa  samun  nutsuwa  ganin  maman husna  tana  sona  kamar  ba  kiahiyar  taba  duk abinda  takawo  daga  kasar  su  intaje  to  saita  bani  tana  da  kirki sosai  wannan  kirkin  yasa  na  kasa  samun  nutsuwa  da  tabawa  tazomin  da  maganar  nace  ba ruwana  akan  wannan  maganar  daga  karshe  ma  kaka  ya  gida  har  cikin daki  tazo  tayita  min  hudubar  shedan  akan  na  yadda  a  kashe  maman  husna  ni  kuma  naki  yadda  

Kaka ganin  zan  bijire  musu  tacemin  ai  kinsan  wata  bakisan  wata  ba  mahaukaciya  ana  nema  miki y'anci  amma  kina  kaucewa  ni  kaina  na  tsani  maman  husna  ko  kadan  bana  sonta ko  lokacin  aurenta  da  musbahu  ban  soba  baban  shine  yace  tinda  yana  sonta  ya  auri  kayanshi   bayan  auren  saina  kara  tsanar ta  saboda  yadda  musbahu  ke  nuna  mata  soyayya kamar  itace  ta  haifeshi   ko  zaizo  ganinmu  tana  lik'e dashi hakan  yakara  min  tsanar ta  ta  mallakemin  yaro  bashida magana  saita    aliya  hakan  ba  karamin  damuna  yake  to  dan haka  kokiso  ko  karkiso  sai  ankashe  ta  shashasha  kawai  , wata  kishiya  har  wani  tausayin  ta  akeji  marar  hankali  da  bakisan  inda  yake  miki  ciwo  ba  

Dagowa  nayi nace  kaka wllh  nidai  ba  ruwana  akan wannan  lamari  kuje  kuyi  duk  abinda  zakuyi  amma  nidai  bazaayi  kisan  kai  dani  ba  


Kaka tace  kuma  sai  kiyi  mutuwa dai  dole  aliya  tabar  min  gidan  yarona  saboda  bana  kaunar ta  daidai  da  kwayar  zarra  infada  miki  


Na  bude  baki  zanyi  magana  kaka  ta  wankeni  da  mari  har biyu. Tace  kema  saina  raba  soyayyar  dakike mata  kindawo  kina  tsanar  ta  har  diyarta  tafice a  dakin  ina  jin  ta  maman husna na  mata  barka da zuwa  amma  maimakon  ta  amsa  saima  zaginta  data  ringa  yi  harta  bar  gidan  


Fitowa  nayi  maman husna tace  talatu  lfy kike kuka  kuma  bansan lokacin  dana  rumgume maman husna  ina  kukan  tausayin ta  saboda  naga  yau  bata  salla  sosai  nakejin  tausayin ta na  ratsani  


Maman husna tace  talatu  kiyi hakuri  ni  ai  nasaba da  zagin  kaka  wata  rana  sai  labari kinji  kije  ga  abincin kicen  na  zuba  miki  idan  kin  gama  husna  tana  ta  kiranki  bata  ganki  ba  


Jin ta  ambaci  husna  sai  nakara  kuka na  ina  tuna  gobe  zata  kasance  marainiya  , da  kyer  maman  husna  ta  rarrashe ni  nadena  kuka  a  ranar  banyi bacci ba  sai  safa da marwa nakeyi   daga  dakina  zuwa  dakin maman husna  idan  na  leka  sainaga  tana  bacci  saina  koma  har  wajan asuba  banyi  bacci  ba  ina  kukan  wannan  kiyayya  da  kaka  takewa  maman husna  da  ita  da  tabawa  


Malam nadawo  daga salla  yashiga  dakin  maman husna  ya  ga  lafiyarsu  amma  saiga  ga  tamutu  sosai  abin  ta  girgiza malam  yafito  yana  fadin innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un  Allah  yajikanki  da  rahama  aliya  Allah  yasa  kwanciya hutuce  agareki 


Da gudu  tafito  daga  daki  jin  abinda  yake  fada  dakin  na  shiga  naganta  kwance  a  mace  sai  husna  dake  gefenta  tana  bacci  ta  rumgume ta  kuka nasaka  ina  daukar husna  na  rumgume  kar  suma  nayi  saboda  tashin  hankali  fitowa  nayi  naga  malam  shima  kukan  yake  ni kuwa  ina  kallon  husna  saina  kara  sautin kukana  Saboda tsananin  tausayin  ta  danakeji  


Haka  muka  kari  kukanmu  nida  malam  tukun  yafita  yafadawa  malam  salisu  suna  masallaci  yace  maman  husna  ta  rasu  inaji  ina  gani  akwai  yiwa  maman  husna  sutura  aka  kaita  gidanta  na  gaskiya  ,


BAYAN SATI  DAYA 

Yan uwan  maman  husna  zasu  koma  gida  sukace  abasu  husna  amma  kaka  tace  bazai  yiyuba  abar  musu  diyarsu  haka  suka  koma  kasar  su  suna  tausayin  rayuwar  husna  


Haka  naci  gaba  da  kula  da  husna  cikin so  da  kauna   husna  ita  tadauka  nice  mamanta  ba  ruwanta  da  kowa  komai  nice  

Wata  rana  tabawa  tazo  gidana  taga  ina  lallaba  husna  taci  abinci  sai  tsere  mukeyi  ina  binta  , haushi ne  ya  kamata  

Tace  kefa  yaya  talatu  yar  wahala  ce  inbataci  kibar ta  mana  dolene   ni  wllh  kina  bani  mamaki  dan  kishiya har wani  abun sone  banza  na  mata  dan  nasaba  da  wannan  bak'ak'en  maganar  na  tabawa  lokacin  batada  aure   

Bayan ta  tafi  daga gidana taje wajan kaka dayake  kaka  yayar  mahaifiyar muce  dama  auren  gidane  aka  mana  da  musbahu  sai  fada  mata  karya  da  gaskiya  


Nidai  bana  bi  takansu  ina  kula  da  husna  amma  malam  tass  nake  zaginshi  ta  uwa  da  uba  namishi  rashin  kunya  bana  barinsa  ya  kwana  adakina  ko  yazoma  korarshi  nakeyi  , wasa wasa  kawai  saina  zama  mafadaciya   ko  a  unguwar nan  tsoro na  yakeji  daboda  fadana   amma  bana  son  b'acin ran  husna  komai  ninake  mata 



Wata rana  natashi  inajin  zazzabi zazzabi  bayan  nasha  magani  ina  tashi  naji na  tsani  husna  tin daga  ranar  nake  k'untatawa  rayuwar  husna tabawa  na  zugani  ita  da  kaka


Wannan  shine  abinda  nasani  amma  dan  Allah  kiyi  hakuri  karki  kasheni  insha Allah  zan  gyara  halina  nima  bansan  mai  yasa  nadawo  muguwa  ba  dan Allah  innata  kiyi hakuri  zan  nemi  gafarar  malam  da  husna  insha Allah  nadena  komai  tana  kai karshen  maganar  ta  durkusa  a  gaban  aljana safiya tana  kuka  mai  cin  rai


Share hawaye  aljana safiya  tayi  na  tausayin  husna  da  maman ta  har  babanta  

Kallon  talatu tayi  tace  shikenan   yanzu  yaya  kamannin  maman  husna  yake  


Talatu  ya  tsaida  kukanta  tace  farace  tass  doguwa  kyakkyawa  yadda  kika  ga  husna  haka  maman  husna  take  harma  tafi  husna  kyau   


Aljana safiya  afuwa daya zanmiki  tinda  naji  ba  lefinki  bane  asiri suka  miki  kina  dena  kula  da  husna  da  babanta  kika  kina  tsanarsu  duk  wani kulla  kulla  yana  hannun  tabawa  ne  

Talatu tace  wllh  duk  itace  kuma  bazan  taba  yafi  mata  ba  ashe  asiri  sakamin  saboda  ina  aikin  lada  suka  maidani  mafadaciya  marar imani  bazan  yafeba  wllh  sun  cuceni  duniya  da  lahira  sunsaka  ina  zalintar  marainiyar  Allah  da  babanta  babu  abinda  sukamin  Allah  saiya  sakamin  tabawa  keba  yar  uwa  tagari  bace  ke  shedaniya  ce  annamimiya  mai  tarwatsa  farin cikin  gidan aure  Allah  ya  isa  tsakanina  dake  wllh  da  mutum  yasamu  yar uwa  irin ki  gyara  yazauna  bashida  dan  uwa  a  duniya  

Aljana safiya  tayi murmishi tace  ya  isa  haka  ai zata  amshi  hukunci  kema  hukuncin  dana  miki  na  ki gyara  halin kine  kidena  fadawa  mijin ki  maganar da  bata  daceba  kimishi  biyayya  ko  zaki  samu  rabo  a  lahira  


Jiki na rawa talatu tace  insha Allah  nadena  mishi  duk  abinda  nakeyi abaya  zan  mishi  biyayya  kuma  zan  nemi  gafarar shi  harda  husna  tana  goge  hawaye 


Aljana safiya ta  matso kusan talatu  ta  dafa  kafadar talatu  tace  zan  sake  zuwa  na  gani  inkin  gyara  jikin ki  kuma  yanzu  zai  dena  ciwo  dan  kar  mijinki  ya  tambayi  ba'asi nina  tafi  sai  gani  na  biyu  ta  b'ace 


Da sauri  talatu  ta  mike  jin  jikin  rass  yadena  ciwo  zama  tayi  abakin  gado  tana  mai  danasanin  halin  ta tana  tuno  cutar  da  tayiwa husna da  babanta  sosai  take  kuka  tana fadin  Allah  natuba  insha Allah  zan  gyara  halina  nadena  bin  bokaye  da  malamai  dama  ba  halina  bane  ashe  asiri  akai min  Allah  yatsine  miki  tabawa  bazan  yafe  miki  ba  , wata  nutsuwa  taji tana  shigarta  tana  tunano  azabar  da  aljana safiya  tabata  amma  kuma  ta  shafe  mata  ciwon  da  rad'ad'in   bata  jin  komai  sai  nadama  da  danasani da  take  bin  duk  jikinta  , ajiyar zuciya  yasauke  ta nufi toilet  tayo alwala  ta  soma  nafila  tana  mika  tubarta  tana  kuka  



Aljana safiya  ma  tana  fata  taringa  kuka  tace  kai  duniya  ba  tabbas  yanzu  wani  irin  tsana  ne  haka  surukar ka  da  kanwar  kiahiyar ka  su  kasheka  astagafurullah  Allah  ka  shirya  mana  al"ummar  musulmai  baki  daya  , cen kuma  ta  hade rai  tace  lalle  tabawa  kema  zakimin  bayanin  dalilin  dayasa  kika  aikata  haka  saikin  yabawa  aya zakinta
