*51—52*



Acen  gidan  tabawa  kuwa  amina  b'acewa  daga  inda  aljana safiya  ta  sallamesu  bata  tsaya  ko  ina  ba  sai  a  falon  tabawa  ta  bayyana  ganin ba  kowa  yasa  tayi  dakin  ta  , tana  zuwa  a  kan  gado  ta  ganta  tayi  dai-dai  tana  sharar  bacci  kanta  ko  dan  kwali  babu  gashi  ko  kayan  jikinta  basuda  maraba  da  tsirara  , Amina  ta  kalli  gefen  tabawa  amma  bataga  mijin  tabawa  ba  murmishin mugunta  tayi tace  yau  saina  miki  hukunci  wanda  ya  lumka  na  shugaba  sau  goma   dan  ni  nafita  zafi  tana  fadin  haka  ta  shafa  bangon  dakin  ai  tuni  dakin  yadauki  girgiza  


Tabawa  da  tayi  nisa  cikin  baccin  ta  dama  da  tsoro  ta  kwanta  jin  dakin  taji  yana  girgiza  kamar  zai  kife  da  sauri  ta  tashi  tana  waige  waige  , ganin  ba  kowa saiyi  tsoki  tace  aikin banza  ashe  tsoro  ne  naji  babu kowa  na  dauka  ma  baban  rukayya  ne  yadawo  


Amina  ta  hade  rai  kama  bata  tabayin  dariya  ba   abokan  aiki  ta  kira  wato  zomayen  nan  suka  bayyana  adakin  tace  maza  kuje  ki  yayyaga  wacce  muguwar  matar  ku  yagata  yadda  kukeso  , nufar  tabawa  sukayi  wadda  ta  koma  bacci  suka  abka  mata  




Cikin bacci  taji  ana  caka  mata  wani  abu  mai  tsini  ai  da  ihun  dauke  bakinta  ta  tashi  tana  karkade  jikinta  amma   suka  mak'ale   mata  suka  yagonta  tako ina 


Ihu  tabawa  tasoma  tana  fadin  waiyo Allah  nashiga uku   taimako  jama'a  

Amina na  gefe  tana  kallon su  sai  dariyar mugunta  tana fadin  yauwa  yaran albarka  maza  kumata  hukunci  karku  raga  mata  kumata kaca  kaca  


Tabawa  kuwa  sai  ihun  take  wanda  babu  mai  taimakon  ta  babu  kuma  mai  jinta  sai  amina   

Amina  ta  tacigaba  da    dariyar  mugunta  tana  kara  zuga  zomayen  suka  mata  azaba  

Su  kuma  suna  lik'e ajikinta  kamar  cungam  sun  yanceta  kamar  k'udaje  insunga  nama  ko  kayan  tsami  haka  suka  mata  



A cen  bangaren  hafeez  shima  agidan  ladi  ya  bayyana  a  falo  yaganta  da  kwalbar  kodin  a  hannun  ta  dayan  hannun  kuma  sigari  ce  a  gabanta  kuma  kayayyakin  shaye  shaye  birjit  kamar  shago  , wani  mugun  kallon  hafeez  ya  mata  yace  zakici  ubanki  yanzu  baza  wuya  ba  yana  kaiwa  nan  ya  karasa  inda  take  kunenta  ya  murda  da  karfi 


A firgice  ladi  ta  farka  daga  baccin  tana  raba  ido  kamar  maye  yaci  d'a  a  kasuwa   da  karfi  ta  kwarara  ihu  tana  mike  tsaye  ganin  wani  murgujejen  zaki  mai  manya  idanu   a  guje  ta  nufi  dakin ta  danta  buya  tana  shigewa  tasaka  key  a  kofar  tana  mayar  da  numfashi  da  sauri  sauri  , juyowa  tayi  danta  kwanta  aikuwa  tayi  arba  da  da  wani  babban  maciji   kwance  akan  gadon  nata  yana  hanin ta  yatashi  koshiya  yana  tafiya  gareta  


Ba  shiri  ladi  tasaki  fitsari  tana  karkarwa  tasoma  fadin  innahu sulaimanu  wa'innahu  bismillah  rahaman  rashim  tana  zaro ido  ganin  macijin  na  kara  kusantota  tin  kafin  yazo  gareta  ladi  ta  suma  a  bakin  kofar  


Dariya hafeez  yayi yace  kaji  tantiriya  wai  innahu sulaimanu   ance  miki  ni  bansan  Allah  bane  yar  maye  kawai  sakin  gayawa  yan  garinku  karamar  yar  iska  mari  yadauke  mata  a  fuska  wanda  yayi  dalilin  dawowar  ta  daga  sumar  datayi   


Ihu tasoma  tana fadin  waiyo  mamana  waiyo  baba  nashiga tara  yau  ba  uku  bama  tara  nashiga  maciji  jama'a  a  kawomin  dauki  ji  take  an  kece  da  wata  mahaukaciyar dariya wanda  tajita  kamar  zata  tarwatsa  mata  dodon  kunne  ta  rufe  kunan  ta  sai  cen  taji  andena  saita  bude  idon ta  taga  wayam  ba  kowa 



Hafeez ne  yasoma  zane  ladi  yana  daukarta  yayi  sama  da  ita  tukun ya  sakota  kasa  idan  tayi  ihu  yakuma  kara  mata  azaba  



A bangaren aljana safiya  kuwa  data  fito   gidan  umma  ta  nufa  danta  kara  ganin kamannin  husna  da  mamanta  da  talatu  tace  kamar  an  tsaga  kara  haka  suke  kamanni  , da  dakin  ta  bayyana  rufe  idonta  tayi  tana fadin istagafurullah  ya  Allah  dan  batason  taga  abunda  ba  daidai  a  hankali  tabude  idonta  saitaga  husna  kadai  a  gadon  tana  bacci  hankali  kwance  


Karasowa tayi  ta  zauna  kusa  da  husna  tana  kare  mata  kallo dum  taji  gabanta  yafadi  tace  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un  ai  husna  da  maman  aliyu  take  kama  to  mai  hakan  ke  nufi  , kenan  husna  mamansu  daya  da  aliyu  kai  ina  ba  haka  bane  gaskiya  maman  aliyu  tazo  haihuwar  shi  ta  mutu  ita  kuma  maman  husna  tin  tana  karama  ta  mutu  kai  gaskiya  da  sake  ya  rabbi  ka  saitamin  tunanina  duk  na  rikice  


Sauka  tayi  daga  kan  gadon  tana  safa  da  marwa  ta  rasa  mafita  duk  kanta  kalle  saboda  al'ajabi da  tsananin  mamaki  ta  kasa  tantance  shin  maman  aliyu  itace  ta  haifi  husna  ko  kuwa  girgiza kai  tayi  tace  kai  gaskiya  a  a  ba  haka  bane  maman  aliyu  tafadamun  sunan  mijinta  da  babanta  amma  duk  na  manta  sunansu  waiyo Allah  wannan  wacce  irin  cakwakiya  ce  haka  , gaskiya  dole  na  shiga  jikin husna  muje  wajan  baba  don  sanin  wacece  maman  husna  ko  zan  warware  wannan  cakwakiyar  


Dawowa tayi  kusan  husna  ta  shafa  fuskar ta  da  suke  tsananin  kama  da  maman aliyu  

Husna jin  ana  taba  ta  saita  mude  ido  ganin  bataga  kowa  ba  tajuya  taga  alhaji  abdallah  baya  dakin  tsoro  ne  yakama  ta  tuni  jikinta  yadauki  sanyi  tana fadin  subhanallah  kodai  mafarki  nayi  ne  to  amma  waye  yake  shafamin  huska  haka  


Murmishi safiya  tayi  sai  ta  bayyana  agaban ta  tace  nice  nadawo  


Da  sauri  husna  ta  kalleta  tace  la  ummi  kece  jin  da  husna  tayi  aliyu  taya  cewa  aljana safiya  ummi  shine  itama  tafada


Wani sanyi  safiya taji  ta  zauna  kusan  husna  tace  nice  da  fatan  babu  matsala  


Murmishi husna tayi tace  a a  ummi  ba  komai  wllh  


Aljana safiya  tace  to shikenan  bari  natafi  amma  ungo  wannan  kisa  ahannun  ki  idan  kikaga  tayi  haske  ta  yana  nuni  da  ina  kusa  dake  


Amsar  zoben  husna tayi  tasaka  ahannun  ta  tana  murmishin jin dadi  tace  to  ummi  yaushe  zaki  dawo  


Aljana safiya tace  ko  gobe  ma  zaki  ganni  sai  anjima  beyy  harta  juya  zata  b'ace  taji  husna tace  ummi  cikin  muryar  kuka,  da  sauri  tadawo  kusan  husna  tana  fadin  maiya faru  ne  husna 


Rumgume ta  husna tayi tasaka  kuka  tana fadin  ummi  yanzu  a  duniya  dake  da  baba  dasu  umma  da  Alhaji  da  su  sailuba kune  masu  sona  masu   nunamin  kauna   mama  da  kaka da  anty  tabawa  basa  sona 


Shafa kanta  aljana safiya  tayi  tana  kara tausayin  husna  tace  kiyi hakuri  dama  aduniya  bakowa  ne  zaike  sonka  ba   haka  rayuwar  take  kidena  kuka  kinji  ni  ina  sonki  zan  zame  miki  uwa  kinji  


Husna  ta  kuma  mak'ale  safiya tace  nagode sosai ummi  ke  tadabance  ko  a  jinsin  aljannu  da  wuya  asamu  irinki  gaskiya 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ana  samu  husna  kibari  gobe  innazo  saimu  magana  ko  ta  mike  ta  b'ace 



Husna  ta  share  hawayen fuskar ta  tace  Allah yasa  wataran  mama  tasoni   tadena  zagina  



Aljana safiya ta  bayyana  agidan  tabawa  dakin tanufa  jin  ihun  tabawa  , tana  shiga  taga  amina  na  zaneta  da  bulala  ga  zomayen  a  gefe  , dariya  safiya tayi  tace  da  kyau  yarinya Kirki  shiyasa  na  turoki  nan  dan  nasan  kifini  zafin  zuciya  yanzu  barta  zan  mata  tambaya  


Dena  kukan ta  amina  tayi  tana  dariyar itama  tace  shugaba  kinsan  bana  afuwa idan  nazo  yin  hukunci  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  janyomin ita  , hannu amina  tamika  ta  janyo  tabawa  wadda  take  numfarfashi 


Gyar murya  aljana safiya  wadda  tasa tabawa  dawowa  hayyacinta  tana  zare  ido  


Aljana safiya tace  zan  tambayeki  kifadamin gaskiya  idan  kuma  kika  fadamun  abinda  ba  daidai  ba  to  zan  miki  abinda  yafi  wannan  muguwa kawai 


Da sauri  tabawa  tace  insha Allah  zan  fadi  gaskiya  ayi hakuri  


Aljana safiya  tace  inaso  kifadamun  wani  dalili  ne  yasa  kuka  kashe  maman  husna  keda  kakar  husna  , maza  maza  ki  fadamin  gaskiyar  duk  abinda  yafaru  ko  kiga  abinda  zanyi  



Tabawa tace wllh  zan  fadi  gaskiya  bazan  boye  komai  ba  ranki yadade 


Aljana safiya tace inajinki  


Gyara zama  tabawa  tayi  jiki  na  b'ari  tace  



ALOKACIN  DAYA  SHUD'E 

Maman  mu  da  maman  musbahu  yaya  da  kanwa  ce    kaka  ce  babba  maman muce  karama  , kaka  yaranta   uku  Musbahu,, Rabi'u ,, Aminu    sune  yaranta  daga  bata  kuma  haihuwa ba  batada  diya  mace  maman  itama  daga  yaya  talatu  sai  ni  tabawa  

Tin  lokacin  dana  taso  Allah  ya  jarabbaceni  da  son  yaya  musbahu  hakan  yasa  nakoma  gidan  kaka  da  zama  dan  nake  ganinshi  inajin  sanyi  azuciya  ta  

Shi  kuma  musbahu  irin  muskilan  mazan  nan  ne  baya  sakin  fuskarshi  musamman  ma  idan  yazo  gida  ko  su  yaya  aminu  baya  sakin  musu  fuska  bareni  da  nake  shishige  mishi  dan  nasamu  kulawa  daga  gareshi  amma  hakan yaki  yiyuwa 


Alokacin  da  kaka  tacewa  yaya  musbahu  ya  fitar  da  matar  aure  Allah  Allah  nake  yace  ni  yakeso  dan  yanzu  yana  sakin  min  fuska  muna  hira  dashi sosai  amma  abin  bakin  cikin  sai  yacema  kaka   shifa  har  yanzu  baiga  wadda  yakeso  ba  gaskiya  idan  su  aminu sun  samu  mata  ayimusu  auren  shi  ba  yanzu  ba


Kaka  tace  to shikenan  haka  kuwa  akayi    aminu da  rabi'u  suka  fitar  da  matar  aure  asha  biki  suka  tare agidansu  


BAYAN SHEKARA BIYU 

Har yanzu  ina  nan  ina  dakon  soyayyar  yaya  musbahu  a cikin    zuciyar  ta  kuma  nasaka  fada mishi  kwatsam  wata rana  saiga  yaya  musbahu  yazowa  da   kaka  maganar  yasamu  matar  aure  


Kaka  tace  yar  waye  yarinyar  tanuna  farin cikin  ta  sosai  tace  inace  unguwar su  


Musbahu  yace  mama  sunan ta  aliya  ba  yar  Nigeria  bace  yar  kasar  masar  ce  amma  kasuwanci  yadawo  da  baban  ta  Nigeria  suna  nan  bayan  layinmu  , yarinyar  tana  da  hankali   da  nutsuwa  saima  kin  ganta  mama  


Sosai  kaka  ta  fadada  murmishin  ta  tace  Alhamdulillah  masha  Allah  to  saika  kawomin  ita  naganta  ko  kace  balarabiya  kasamo  mana  


Sunkuyar  da  kai  yayi yace  to  mama  zan  kawota  gobe  nan  yatashi  yafice


Ni  kuma Ina  daga  daki  inajin  duk  abinda  suke  fada  a  ranar  kwana  nayi  ina  rusa  kuka  saboda  ganin  yaya  musbahu  zai  bar  rayuwa ta   shi baima  san  inayi  ba  san  maso  wani  koshin  wahala  shi  nakeyi  



WASHE GARI 

Da  yamma  saiga  yaya  musbahu  ya  kawo  aliya  lokacin  dana  ganta  saida  nayi  sumar  tsaye  saboda  tsananin  kyau  irin  na  aliya  


Kaka  ta  amsheta  hannu  biyu  suka  gaisa  tukun yaya  musbahu  yamaida  ta  gidansu  yana  dawowa  yacewa  kaka  baban  aliya  yace  yaturo  da  manyan  shi  

Hakan  yayiwa  kaka  dadi  baba  na  shigowa  tafada  mishi  komai  shima  sosai  yayi farin  ciki  , washe gari  suka  je  nemawa  yaya  musbahu  auren  aliya  , babu  wata  matsala  aka  bashi auren ta   sati  daya  aka  saka  bikinsu  saboda  baban  aliya  yace  zasuje  kasarsu  bayan  wani  lokaci  zasu  dawo  suga  aliya  



Ni  kuma  nakasa  tsaye  nakasa  zaune  abin  duniya  yayi  min  yawa  hakan  yasa  nashiga  neman  malamai  ina  zuwa  akan  a  lalata  auren  yaya  musbahu  da  aliya  , amma  abin  bakin  cikin  duk  inda  naje  sai  ace  min  wannan  auren  ba  fashi  hakan  yasa  na  kura  nasha  alwashin  daga  anyi  auren  zanyiwa aliya  kurciya  tabar  Nigeria  baki  daya  da  wannan  shwarar  na  denne  komai  danake  ji  


Sati  auren  su  yaya  yacika  aka  sha  biki  sosai iyayen  aliya  suka  kashe  mata  kudi  kayan  dakin  ta  nagani  nafada  , danagani  hakan  yakara  min  tsanar  aliya  amma  nayi  shuru  na kyale  komai  sai  bayan  biki  



Bayan aurensu  nasake  komawa  wajan  boka  na  nace  inaso  yayiwa  aliya  kurciya  , amma  yace  wannan  bazai yiyuba  saboda  aliya  mace ce  mai  riko  da  ibada  dan  haka  bazai  iya  wannan  aikin  ba  haka  nadawo  gida  raina  kamar  gawayi  


Kaka  ganin yadda  musbahu  yake  kulawa  da  matarshi  ko  gidanta  tare  suke  zuwa  baya  rabuwa  da  ita  inba  kasuwa  ba  kasuwar  ma  indai   ba  zama  zaiyiba  to  tare suke  tafiya    , nanma  kaka  tasake  kara  tsanar  ta  tin  daga  lokacin  kaka  tace  aliya  ta  asirce  mata  yaro  


Ni  kuma  ganin  kaka  ta  tsani  aliya  saina  goya  mata baya   kullin  sai  munje  gidan  mun  mata  rashin  mutumci  wani lokacin  har  dukanta  mukeyi   haka  muka  ringa  gana  mata  azaba  kullin  da  kalar  rashin mutumcin  da  zamu  mata  , ita  kuka  saidai  tayi shuru  idan  kuma  abin yamata  yawa  saidai  tayita  kuka  


Hakan  ba  karamin  dadi  yakemin  ba  idan  naga  aliya  a  cikin  damuwa  ana  haka  tasamu  ciki   murna  wajan  musbahu  kamar  ya  maida  ta  ciki   haka  sukaci  gaba  da  renon  cikin  har  yakai  lokacin  haihuwa ta  haifi  diyarta  mace  kyakkyawa  kamar  ita  



Sosai  musbahu  da  aliya  sukayi  farin ciki  da  baba nida  kaka  kuwa  ko  munje  saidai  muyita  zaginta  


Lokacin  da  husna  takai  shekara bakwai a  duniya  na  ringa  zuga  kaka  da  tasaka  musbahu  ya  kara aure  


Kaka kuwa  yadda  tinda  dama ta  tsani  aliya  takirashi  tace  ya  kara  aure  tashin hankali  yashiga  yace  shi  bashida  ra'ayin  kara  aure  , kaka  tace  dole  saiyayi  inma  bai  fitar  da  matar  aure  ba  ita  zata nema  mishi  a  dangi  , ganin  yana  neman  bijire  mata tace  zata  tsine  mishi  indai  baibi  umarnin  taba  


Damuwa  sosai  yaya  musbahu  yashiga  sai  yace  wa  kaka  ta  nemo  mishi  a  dangin  shi  bashida  budurwa  


Lokacin  danaji  haka  farin ciki  yakamani  nasan  kaka  ni  zata  aura  mishi   sai  nake  ta  jan  yaya  musbahu  ajiki  idan  yazo  gidan  kaka  nake  tambayar  shi  husna   hakan  yasa  yasakar  min  fuska   


Abin  mamaki  kaka  data  zo  nema  mishi  mata  maimakon  tace  tabashi  ni  amma  sai  tace  musbahu  andaura  maka  aure  da  talatu  ,, ina  jin  haka  a  shige  daki  nayita  kuka  


Haka  inaji  ina  gani  yaya  talatu  ta  tare  agidan   yaya  musbahu  aliya  ta  karbeta  hannu biyu  babu  wani  tashin hankali   sosai  suka  hade  kansu    hakan  yasa  yaya musbahu  jin dadi  ganin  yadda  talatu  keson  husna  da  maman  ta  


Ni kuma  nayita  bin  bokaye  nida  kaka  donmu  shiga  tsakanin  yaya  talatu  da  aliya  daga  zaman  lfy  yadawo  zaman  tashin hankali   amma  bai  yiyuba  kullin  sainaje  nayiwa  aliya  rashin kunya  wani lokacin  ma  da  kaka  muke  zuwa  muyita  zaginta  ta  uwa  ta  uba  

Lokacin  da  mukazowa  da  yaya  talatu  zancen  akashe  aliya  nan tace  bazata  yadda  ahada  baki  da  ita  akaishe  mata  kishiyar  ta  suna  zamansu  na  lfy  ganin  zata  bamu  matsala mukayi  aikin mu  nida  kaka  aka  kaishe  aliya  

Bayan  mutuwar  aliya  yaya talatu  ta  daura  son  duniya  akan  husna  kamar ita  ta  haifeta  hakan  yasa  kaka  tace  muje  asaka  mata tsanar  husna  babu  bata lokaci  mukasa  aka  shiga  tsakanin  yaya  talatu  da  husna   taringa  azabtar  da  husna   


Sosai  tadawo  tana  tsanar husna  wani lokacin  ma  sai  husna  tayi  kwana biyu  bataci  abinci ba   hakan  yayimin  dadi  sosai   

Nasake  komawa  wajan  boka  nace  inaso  a  haukatar min  da  yaya talatu  duk da  lokacin  nayi  aure  harna  haifi  rukayya   boka  yace  bazai yiyuba  haka  na  hakura  amma  kullin  ina  kara  zugata  tana  yiwa  yaya  musbahu  rashin kunya  kuma  kaka  ma  na  daure  mata  gindi  


Wannan  shine  dalilin  dayasa  na  tsani  maman husna da  husna  har  yadawo  kan  yayata  amma  wllh  natuba  bazan  sake  aikata sab'on Allah  ba  insha Allah  kiyi hakuri  ki  yafemin  bazan  sake  ba


Aljana safiya  kuka  yaci  karfin  ta  wanke  tabawa da husna  tace    azzalima  macuciya   wllh  bakida  imani  kun  kashe  rai  hallau  kuna  azabtar  da  bayin  Allah  to  wllh  kin  karbi  hukunci  shedaniya  barin  irinku  aduniya  masifa  ne  


Kallon  amina tayi   tace  amina  muje  gida  gobe  zan  kaiwa  kaka  ziyara  duk da  naji  komai  amma wllh  saina  hukunta  ta  itama  ke  kuma  ta  nuna  tabawa. Yanzu  kika  soma  ganin  azaba  idan  kinga  nadena  azabtar  dake  to  baba  da  husna ne  suka  yafe  miki  da  mijin ki   maci  amanar  sunnar  annabi (S.A.W) da  aurenki  kike  aikata  zina  harda ciki  sau biyu   kinyi  asara  


Zoben hannun ta ta  murza yana  kawo  haske tace  hafeez  maza  kazo  mutafi  gida  zamu  sake dawowa  itama  ladin  sainaji  abinda  yasa abar  iyayen  tazo  tana  kasuwanci  , tana  bude idon ta  hafeez  ya  bayyana   

Yace  yashugaba  nayi  mata  hukunci  kamar  yadda  kikace  , Aljana safiya  tace  yayi kyau  muje  nan  suka  b'ace

*53 —54*



Bayan  su  aljana  safiya  sunbar  gidan  tabawa  ,itama  ta  tashi  tana  tafiya  a  duk'e  saboda  mugun  dukan  nataci  a  hannun  aljana  amina  ga  kuma  ko  ina  na  jikinta  zafi  sosai  yake  mata   inda  zomayen  nan  sukayi  ta  yagonta  suna  yankar ta   


Sai yanzu  tasoma  danasanin  halinta  nan  take badama  ta  shigeta  hannun  biyu ta  daura  akai  tafasa  kuka  tana fadin  waiyo Allah  ni  tabawa  najawa  kaina  bak'in  halina  yajamin  gashi  yanzu  nayi  gamo  da  aljannu  suna  azabtar dani  Allah  ka  yafe  min  nacuceki  husna  kiyimin  aikin  gafara  wllh  nadena  duk  wani  hali  istagafurullah  Allah  natuba  bazan  sake  kaucewa  hanyar  kaba  insha Allah  , haka  tayita  sumbatu  kamar  zararriya  tana  wanke  jikin  ta 


WASHE GARI 


Husna ce  tafito  daga  kitchen  hannun ta  dauke  da  kwanon  abinci  tana  tafiya  tana  ci  sai  lumshe ido  take  

Mimee  tace  kai  mommy  wai  duk dadin  abincin  ne  yasa  kike  wannan  lumshe  to  waima  menene  kika  dafa  nima  zanci  mommy  takai  karshen maganar  tana  fuskar tausayi 


Harara husna  ta  aika  mata  tace  kai  yarinya ta  bafa  wani abu  bane  mai  dadi  garin  kwaki  ne  da  daddawa  yarinya  yasha  magi da  daddawa  kamar  miyar kuka   irin  wadda  umma  take  to  da  daddawa  na  dauko  na  jik'a  kwakin  bayan  ruwan  ya  tsotse  saina  barbad'a  daddawar  daga sama  nasa  mai  na  cakud'a  kinji  yadda  yayi dadi kuwa 


Mimee tace  please mommy  kidan  ban  naji  akwai  dadin  da  kike fada 



Husna  takaraso  ta  zauna  tace  yarinya  baki  isaba  wannan  abinci  ba  irin naki  bane 


Dariya umma tayi tace  kishiyar tawa ko  mai  gidan  nawa  yau  da  kwadayin  kwaki  aka  tashi 


Husna  itama  dariyar tayi  tace  Allah  umma  tin  kafin gari  ya  waye  nake  son  cin  kwaki  da  daddawa   shine  fa  bacci  gaba  dani  ban  fito  da  wuri ma  ina  farkawa  ko  wanka  banyi  ba  nace  sainaci  tukun  


Mustafa yace  mommy  kibamu  muce  koda  kadan  ne  


Husna  ta  sauka  kasa  tace  maza ku  sauko  muci  , da  sauri  suka  sauko  suna  murna  duk  wanda  yakai  bakinsa  sai  yace  wllh  mommy  akwai dadi  gobe  ma  zaki  mana  


Hade rai  husna  tayi  tace  kutashi  kuban  tinda  renani  ta  kara  hade rai  alamar  ba  wasa


Yusuf yace  me  mukayi  mommy  kiyi  hakuri  kwakin  akwai  dadi  karki  koremu  kin  mommy  

Mustafa yace kiyi hakuri mommy  munason  muke  cin  abinci  dake  kinji  mommy 


Husna  tace  bana   ce  muku  ba  idan  ana  cin  abinci  ba'a  magana  shine  yanzu  kuke  cikamin  kunne  da  surutu  ko  so  kuke  saina  zaneku  ne  iyee  tambayar  ku  nake  ?


Da sauri  suka  rumgume husna  suna  fadin  mommy  Allah  yabaki  hakuri  insha Allah  bazamu  sakeba    insha Allah  kinji  sweetheart mommy  


Murmishi tayi  tace to  shikenan  kosa  muci 


Umma ko  cike da burgewa  take  kallon  su  



Cikin dare  yau  su  aljana safiya   dira  gidan  kaka  


Kaka  na  kokarin  kwanciya   taji  ana  buga  kofa  , tashi  tayi  tana  tambayar waye  ne   jin  ba'ayi  magana  ba  saita  mike  ta  nufi  kofar  

Tana  kokarin  budewa taji  an   mintsine  ta  a  wuya  da  sauri  ta  juyo  saitaga  wayam  bakowa  , bude  kofar  tayi  da  sauri  tayi  baya  tana  fadin  subhanallah  ganin  wani  bak'in  kare  kamar  jaki  babba  dashi  

Biyota  Karen yayi  yana  bude  baki  , ai  da  guje  kaka  ta  haye  gado  ta  rufe  kanta  da  bargo tana  rufa  taji  ana  girgiza dakin  kamar  ba'a  kasa  yakeba  

Ihu kaka  tasoma tana fadin  waiyo Allah  yau  menene  yake faruwa  tani  ne  haka  

Daga bayanta  taji ance  abinda  kika  shuka ne  zaki  girba  kingane  

Ai  da sauri  kaka  ta  bude  rufar  datayi  tana  zare ido  ganin  dakin  babu  haske ji  tayi  an  ja  kafarta  , ihu tasoma  tana  fadin  la'ilaha ilallahu   ni Hindatu  waye  wai  yake  jamin  kafa   tana  rufe  bakinta  aka  kama  kafar  duka  biyu  ana  janta  


Kaka  na  salati  tana  rokon  wanda  yake  janta  amma  shuru  ba  mai  bata  amsa  , saida  sukayiwa  kaka  liliss  tukun suka  barta    tana  maida  numfashi 


Aljana safiya tace  amina  dan  jinjiga  min  ita  tukun  saina  mata  tambayar  , Amina tace  angama  shugaba  

Kaka  na  numfarfshi  taji  an shaketa  anyi  sama  da  ita  

Amina nayin  sama  da  kaka  ta  ringa  zane kaka  tako  ina  

Kaka  na  ihun  azaba  tana bada hakuri  amma  kamar iska  mai  wucewa  take  fadawa   


Saida  amina  tawa  kaka  dukan tsiya  tukun  tasakota  kasa  timm  tafado. Aljana safiya  tace  tsohuwa  shin  kozan  san  dalilin  dayasa  kika  tsani  husna da  maman ta

Jiki  na  b'ari  kaka tace  wllh  babu  abinda  tamin  kawai  ina  kishi  ne  ganin yadda  musbahu  yake  mata  kamar  zai  maidata  cikinsa  ko  ni  dana  haifeshi  bai  nuna  min  kulawa  irin  wadda  yake  wa  aliya  ba shiyasa   naji  tsanarta na  nunkuwa  a  raina  kuma  dama  tabawa  take  zugani  nan  fada  mata  komai   

Labarin da  tabawa  ta  fadawa  aljana safiya  shine  kaka  ta  sake  fadawa  aljana safiya  


Kaka tace  wllh  insha Allah  natuba  dama  ita  ke  zugani  kuma  yanzu  nagane  gaskiya  gobe  zanje gidan  husna  na  nemi  gafarar ta  har  gidan  musbahu  shima  na  nemi  gafarar shi  insha Allah  na  canja  halina  daga  yanzu  kiyimin  aikin  gafara  dan Allah  dan  darajar  mahaifiyar ki  da  mahaifinki  kinji  kiyi hakuri  insha Allah  zan  nemi  gafarar su   namiki  alkawari  baza  sake  ba  kaka  na  maganar  tana  kukan  nadama  dan  sai  yanzu  take  ganin  wautar  data  biyewa  tabawa  sukayi  suna  takurawa  bayin Allah  


Aljana safiya tayi ajiyar zuciya  tace  to shikenan  wato  dai  tabawa  tayi  yawo  da  hankalin  ku  ,ko  yar  uwar  tama  bata  bari  ba  saida  ta  mata  asiri  dan  takoma  muguwa  tana  cutar  da  husna  tabawa  batada  imani  

Da  sauri  kaka  tace  wllh  itace  kuma  insha Allah  har  abada  nadena  komai  nagane  gaskiya  zanke  istigifari  ina  neman  yafiya  a  wajan  Allah  dakuma  wanda  na  cuta   insha Allah 


Aljana safiya tace  shikenan  saiki  nemi  gafarar su  tana fadin  haka  tace  amina  muje  nan  take  suka  b'ace 


Kaka  na  ganin sun  b'ace  ta  ringa fadin  astagafurullah  wa'atubu  alaika  Allah  kayafe  min  zunufaina   nayi  danasanin  biyewa  tabawa  nake  cutar  jikata  jinina  haka  ta  kwanta  tana  maimaita  kalmar  neman  gafara  da  haka bacci  yayi  gaba da  ita



Su  aljana safiya  na  b'acewa  amina ta  kallon safiya tace shugaba  wllh  dakin  bari  nasake  casata  tukun  mubarta  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  hakan  ma  ai  taji  ajikinta  yanzu  muje  gidan  ladi   nan  suka nufi  gidan  ladi

Suna  bayyana  a  gidan ladi  suka  sameta  kwance  tana  rawar  sanyi  alamar  dai  batada  lfy  


Tsawa aljana safiya  tamata,, da  sauri  ladi  tamike  tana  waige  waige  wata  kyakkyawar  mace  tagani  a  gaban ta  da sauri  taja  dabaya tana fadin  nashiga  uku  dan Allah kuyi hakuri  wllh  nadena  komai  insha Allah  gidanmu  ma  zankoma 


Aljana safiya tace  dalla  malama  ki  nutsu  magana nakeso  muyi,, Ladi tace  na  nutsu ranki   yadade 

Aljana safiya tace  wani  dalili ne  yasa  kike  zaman  kanki  ?

Ladi tace  dalilin  dayasa  nazama  karuwa  auren  dole  ne  zaayi  min  ni kuma  banason  mijin  iyayena  sukace  saina  aureshi nikuma  inada  wanda  nakeso  , amma  iyayena  sukace  dole  saidai  na  auri  zabinsu  ni  kuma  nace  bazan  iya   auren  zabinsu  ba  koda  na  aureshi  bazan  mishi  biyayya  ba  bare  yaji  dadin  zama  dani   in  haka  tafaro  zan  tashi  a  lahira  yar  wuta  tinda  banyi  biyayyar  aureba, amma  sukace  sam  bazai  yiyuba  ganin  haka  yasa  nagudu  nida saurayina  wanda  nakeso  muka  bar  garin  mu  muka  dawo  kaduna  

Bayan  munzo  gida  yakama  mana  anan  muke  zama  bai  taba  nunamin  iskanci  ba  har  mukakai  shekara biyu  hakan  yasa  na  kwantar da hankalina  nasaki  jiki  dashi  sosai  yake  nuna  kulawarsa  dakaina   baya  bari  wani  yayimin  magana  anan  yamayar  dani  islamiya  nacigaba  da  karatuna  harna  sauke  alqur"ani mai girma  

Ranar  danayi sauka  nayi  kukan  marar  iyaka ina  tuna  iyayena  da  suka  kasa  fashimtata  akan  abinda  nakeso  haka  yaci  gaba  da  kula  dani  wata rana  yace  zaman  damuke  mutanen  gidan  na  mana  kallan  miji  da  mata  dan  haka  zaije  a  daura  mana  aure 

Nan da  nan  natashi  hankali na  ina  tsoron  yaje  gidanmu  su  koreshi  nace  idan  zakaje  neman  aurena  saidai  kaje  kano  wajan  abokin  babana  saiya  daura  mana  aure  

Yayi  na'am  da  shawara ta  washe  gari  ya  shirya  yatafi  kano  neman  aurena   bai  dade  a  hanya  ba  ya  kirani  yace  yazo  kano  namishi  kwatancen  gidan  abokin babana  


Bayan yace  ne  yafadawa  abokin babana  duk  abinda  yake faruwa  yasheda  mishi  yazo  neman  aurena  

Abokin babana  baiji  dadi ba  na  tafiyar  damukayi  yamishi  nasiha  nima  yakirani  awaya  abokin babana  yayita  mana  masiha  yace  zaije  ya  bawa  babana hakuri  insha Allah  zai  amince da  auren mu  haka  saurayina  ya  kamo  hanyar kaduna  ,a  hanya  yan  fashi  suka  taresu  suka  nemi  kudi  yamusu  gardama  hakan  yasa  sukayi  fushi  nan  take  suka  kashe  shi  , yan sanda  suka  shiga  neman  danginsa  

Nember  daya  kira  takarshe  tawace  sai  suka  kirani suka  fadamin  mutuwar  shi  a lokacin  kamar  nayi  hauka  haka  nakasance  gidan  damuke  haya  aka  kawoshi  mazan  gidan  suka mishi  sutura   nasha  kuka  kamar  raina  zai  fita   saboda  ba  karamin  rashi  nayiba  

Bayan  wani lokaci  nakoma  gidanmu  amma  ina  shiga  suka  koreni  danaki  tafiya ma  yara  sukasa  su  koroni  suna  jifana  da  duwatsu kamar  wata  mai  tab'in  hankali  haka  nadawo  Kaduna  zuciya  ta  na  k'una  

Dalilin  shaye shaye na  da  fadawa  karuwanci    saboda  damuwar  dana  shiga  ne hadu  da  wata  kawa  itace  tasakani  a  wannan  harkar  taringa  kawomin  kayan  maye  inasha  duk  lokacin  danaji  damuwa  inasha  shikenan  sainaji  nadena  jin  damuwa  da  wannan  damar  tasaka  harkar  karuwanci  nasha  kuka  da  bakin  ciki  lokacin  dana  zubar  da  kimata  da  mutumci na  naya' mace  amma  haka  kawata  tayita  min huduba  har  nadena  damuwa  a  haka  muka  hadu  da  tabawa  saidai  naga  tabawa  tafini  rashin  mutumci  da  rashin imani   


WANNAN SHINE DALILIN  RAYUWA TA  A  BARIKI


Ajiyar zuciya  aljana safiya ta sauke  tace  gaskiya  iyayenki  basuda  fashimta  da  sukaki  tsayawa  suji  ra'ayinki  bayan  kuma  kin  koma  garesu  kin  tuba  suka  ki  karbarki  suka  kyamace ki  yanzu  inane  garinku  

Ladi  na  kuka  ta  tsaida  kukan  tace  ni  yar  gombe  ce   yanzu  zan  goma  ga  iyayena  koda  zasu  yankani  bazan  je  ko  ina  ba  natuba  insha Allah   da  wannan  kazamar  rayuwar  duk da  dasa  hannuna  amma  suma  iyayena  sun  taka  rawar  gani  wajan  rugujewar  rayuwa ta  amma  ba komai  zan  koma  garesu  


Aljana safiya  tace  to shikenan  kishare  hawayen  ki  insha Allah  zasu  karbeki  ki  shirya  gobe  na kaiki  gidanku  na  roka  miki  su  insha Allah  zasu  yafe  miki  yanzu  kisha magani  ki  kwanta  gobe  zanzo  da  safe  


Godiya sosai  ladi  tayi wa  aljana safiya  tana  kara  sautin kukan ta  na  nadama 


Sallama suka  mata  nan  suka  b'ace  , a  hanya  suna  tafiya  amina  dake  batada  hakuri  tace  shugaba  amma  wllh  wasu  iyayen  su  suke  saka  yaransu  fadawa  halaka  kijifa  labarin  ladi  wllh  tabani  tausayi  lokacin  da  take  cewa  takoma  amma  iyayen ta  suka  hadata  da  yara  suna  jifanta  da  duwatsu  , wannan  ai  ba  adalci  bane  


Aljana safiya tace  hmmm  kedai bari  kawai  wasu  iyayen  basuda  tausayi  , Allah  yashirya  masu  hali  irin su  , suka  amsa  da  ameen  , 


Bayan sunje gida  aljana safiya  tabawa  abba  labarin  komai   , shima  abun  yabashi  tausayi  sosai  yace  Allah  yashirya su  in Allah  yakaimu  goben  sheki  kaita  gidan  , Aljana safiya  tace  insha Allah abbana  


Washe gari  da  wuri  ladi  tashiya  kayanta  tanyi  tana  kuka   zama  gamawa  aljana safiya  da  amina da  hafeez  suka  bayyana  gidanta  gaisawa  sukayi  aljana safiya tace  ki  runtse ido  


Ladi tace to  ta  rufe idonta  cen  sai  aljana safiya tace  bude  idon ki


Budewa ladi tace  da sauri  ta  kallesu  tana  share  hawaye  tace  har munxo  garinmu  


Murmishi  safiya tayi tace  eh  ina  ne  anguwarku  

Kwatance  tamusu  suka  je  har  bakin  kofar  gidansu  

Ladi  maganin  anzo  gidansu  sai  kukan ta  yakaru  tana  tsoro  shiga  gidan  bataso  su  kuma  cin  mata  mutumci  irin  na  shekarun  baya 


Aljana safiya tadafa  ta  tace  ki  daure  ki  dena  kukan  kishiga Da sallama  ladi tace  to  nan tashiga  gidan  bakinta  dauke  da  sallama  

Da sauri  wata  mata  ta  mike  tana  fadin  ladi  ke  nake  gani  ko  gizon  ne  da  idona  yasaba  yi

Ladi  cikin  kuka  tace  nice  mama  na  yarki  ce  tadawo  gareki  ,, da  sauri  maman  ladi  ta  rumgume  ta  suka  kuka  abin  tausayi  inka  gansu  


Mama  tace  ladi  ashe  kina  raye  ashe  zan  sake  ganinki  kafin  mutuwa  ta   Allah nagode  maka  daka  nuna  min wannan  ranar  


Dariya  mai  hade  da  kuka  ladi  takeyi  tace  Alhamdulillah  mama  ina  raye  tin lokacin  da  baba  ya  koreni  ya  hadani  da  yara  suka  ringa  jifana  dana  koma  kano  ban  sake  kallon  koda  hanyar  gombe  ba  mama  dan  Allah  kuyafe  min  natuba  


Mama  tace  na  yafe  miki  ya'ta  tinda  babanki  ya koreki  bansanke  samun  nutsuwa  ba  


Ladi tace  mama  tare  muke  da  bak'i  suna  waje  , da sauri  mama  tace  kai dama  mutane kikazo  ladi  kika  barsu  a waje  jeki  shigo  dasu  


Mikewa  ladi  tayi tafita  bakin  kofar gidansu  fuskar ta  cike  da  farin ciki  tace  anty  kushigo  ciki  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ai  muna  ganin  duk  abinda  kukeyi  , nan  suka  shiga  ciki  da sallama  amsawa  mama  tayi  tana  lale  barhabin  baki  sanunku  da  zuwa  bayin Allah  ga  guro  ku  zauna  , aljana safiya  tace  yauwa  mama  mungode  tukun  suka  zauna


Baban ladi ne  yayi sallama  yana  kutsa  kai  ciki  , amsa  mishi  sukayi  

Turus  ya  tsaya  da  mamaki  yace  ladi  kice  kika  dawo  yana  nunata  da  hannu 

Da sauri  ladi  ta  rumgume  aljana safiya  jikinta  na  kyerma  ta  fashe da  kuka  mai cin  rai  tace  anty  kin  gani  ko  nace  miki  dama  baba  bazai  yafemin ba  yaki  ya  fashimceni  yanzu  ma  kin  gani  ko  wllh  korata  zaiyi  dan Allah  anty  mukoma  karya  kara  sa  yara  suke  jifana  irin  na  mahaukaciya  ni  zan  biki  yankinku  sainayi  rayuwa  ta  acen  ba  dole  sainayi  aure  ba  ta  sake  rumgume  aljana safiya 

Dagota  aljana safiya  tayi  tace  ki nutsu  mana  ladi  insha Allah  bazai  sake  korarki  ba  zai  yafe  miki  kinji  kidena  fadar  haka  ni  zan  musu  magana  bazasu  koreki ba  


Baba  da sauri  ya  karaso  yana fadin  ladi  ashe  zan  sake  ganinki  ya'ta  ashe  kina  raye  dan Allah  ladi  kiyafemin da  rashin  fashimtar ki  danayi  mamanki taso  ta  fashimtar  dani  amma  b'acin  rai  yahanani  ganewa  kiyi  hakuri  kiyafe min kinji  

Ladi  da  sauri  ta  kalli  aljana safiya  , jinjina  mata  kai aljana safiya  tayi  alamar  taje  ga  baban  ta  

Mikewa  tayi  ta  fada  jikin  babanta  tana  kuka  tace  baba  nice  zan  nemi  gafarar ku  dana  kin  bin  umarninku  da  zabin  da  kukamin  kayi hakuri  ko  kamin lefin  ma  na  yafe  maka  nima  ka  yafimin


Shafa kanta  yayi  yace   na  yafe  miki  yarinya  ta  Allah yamiki  albarka  kinji  Allah  ya  baki  miji  na  gari  

Mama  sosai  tayi  farin ciki  matuka  

Ladi tace  mama  wannan  bayin Allah su suka  taimake  ni  tana  nuna  su  aljana safiya 


Baba da  mama  sukace  gaskiya  bamuda  bakin  da  zamu  muku  godiya  Allah  Allah yasaka  muku da  alkhairi  mungode  mungode sosai da  wannan  karamcin


Murmishi aljana safiya  tayi tace  ba komai ai  yiwa  kaine  amma  dan Allah  mama da  baba  idan  yaro  yazo  da  abinda  yake  so  ku  tsaya  ku  fashimci  ya'yanku  kar  kumusu  dole  kuce  shesun  auri  wanda  basaso  to koda  sun  muku biyayyar  sun  auri  wanda  kukeso  bazasu  musu  biyayyar aureba  saboda  basa sonsu  kuma  auren  dole  bashida  fa'ida  ko  kadan babu  komai  a cikinsa  sai  tarin  nadama  da  b'acin rai  yakamata  iyaye  masu  irin wannan  halin  kudena  kuke  bawa  yaranku  damar  tsabar  wanda  sukeso  saboda  a  zauna  lfy 

So  tari  idan  kaga  karuwai  yan shaye shaye  to  idan  ka  bincika  sai  kaji  ta  dalilin  auren  dolene  yasa  suka  fada  harkar  bariki  dan  Allah  kuke  fashimtar  yaranku  don  ceto  rayuwar su  ga  fadawa  halaka  



Baba yace  insha Allah  baiwar Allah  zamu  gyara  mun  gode  sosai  

Mama tace  bamuda  bakin  dazamuyi  godiya  agareku  saboda kun mana  abin  muka  kasa  Allah  yasaka  da alkhairi  

Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ba komai  


Baba yace ladi  to  yanzu  ina  Ibrahim din  yake  ko  kun  rabu ne bayan  kunbar  gari?

Kuka ladi  tasaka tace  baba  ibrahim  ya  rasu   nan  tabasu  labarin  duk  rayuwar  dasukayi  har lokacin  mutuwar shi 

Sosai  baba  da  mama  suka  girgiza  da  jin  mutuwar  ibrahim  , sukace  Allah  yajikanshi  da  rahama  

Da  ameen  suka  amsa 


Dariya ladi tayi  ,, kallon ta  baba da mama  suke  mama  tace  lfy  ladi kike dariya 

Ladi tace  inaso  nafada  muku  wata  magana  amma  kuma  ina  tsoron  ku  kasa  nutsuwa 

Dafe  kirji  sukayi  baba yace  fadi  muji  mana  

Cikin dariya ladi  tace  baba mama  ko kunsan  kuwa  su  anty safiya  ba  mutane  bane  aljannu  ne  

Da sauri suka  kallesu  mama  tuni  tasoma  kyerma  tana fadin  ke  ladi  da  gaske  yanzu da  aljannu  muke  zaune  

Ladi kuwa dariya take  harda  rike  ciki  ganin  iyayen nata  sun  tsorata  tace eh  mama  ba  mutane  bane  su  suka  gyaramin  halina har  naji  inason  dawowa  gareku  anty safiya  dan  nuna  musu  tinda  basu  yadda ba 

Amina tayi dariya tace  ke  bakida  mutumci yanzu  kin  razana  su  

Aljana safiya tace  eh  muba mutane bane  baba  kuma  bama  cutar  da  kowa  hasalima  taimakon  wanda  aka  cuta  muke yi  yanzu zamu tafi 


Baba yace  Allahu akbar  gaskiya  kud'in  na  dabanne  mungode sosai 


Ba komai  sukace  tukun suka  tashi  aljana safiya tace  sai  wani lokacin  ladi  Allah  yabaki  miji  nagari  ni zamu koma  gida  sai  wani lokacin 

Tashi ladi tayi  ta  rumgume  aljana safiya  tukun  ta  rumgume  aljana amina  tace  sai  wata rana  anty safiya  Allah yasaka da alkhairi 

Aljana safiya tace  insha Allah  ma  zaki  sake  ganina  nan  suka  b'ace 


Dawowa tayi  wajan su  mama  nan  suka  soma  hirar  yaushe  gamo  sunata farin ciki 



Su aljana safiya  na  b'acewa  gidan husna  suka  bayyana  bakin  kofar  get  , Aljana safiya tace  nasan  baku  taba  ganin husna  ba  to  kuzo  muje  ku  ganta  dama  kuma  inason  ku  tayani  duba  kamannin husna da aliyu  kaina  ya  daure  wllh  

Amina tace  to  shikenan  shugaba muje  kwankwasawa  sukayi  mai  gadi  yabude  musu  

Kallon su  yayi yace  bayin Allah daga ina, Aljana safiya tace  mu  yan uwan  husna ne  munzo  mata  ziyara ne  bude musu  yayi  suka  shigo nan  suka  nufi  ciki 


Husna  na  dakinta  a  zaune  daga  zoben hannun ta  takawo haske  cike da  farin ciki  tace  waiyo  Allah dadi  ummi na  tazo   nan  tafito  falo  tana  jiran  zuwansu 

Da  sallama  suka  shiga  cikin  falon  umma  ta  amsa  sallamar  tace sannunku  da  zuwa   

Kuzauna  ga  gori  tana  nuna  musu  kujera,  Husna ta  fito  daga  dakin ta  da  sauri  ta  karaso  har tana  hadawa  da  dan  gudu  tace  ga  ummi na  oyoyo  ta  rumgume aljana safiya 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  kai  husna  kibi ahankali  ko  baki  ganin  cikin jikinki  kike  gudu  baki  tsoron  kifadi  ne  banason  wannan  abun  karki  karayi  kinji  ko 

Murmishi husna tayi tace  bazan  sakeba  ummi na  nadena  dagowa  tayi  tana  kallon  su  amina 


Amina  kuwa  banka  wa  husna harara tayi  

Da sauri  husna tace  waiyo afuwan  anty  amina  banganku  bane  wllh  tasaki  aljana safiya  ta  rumgume  amina  


Amina tace  eh  dama  tinda  kin  samu  maman ki ai  dole  kin  manta mu 

Husna tace yi hakuri  yaya  hafeez  barka da warhaka  ykk 

Murmishi yayi  yace lfy lau  yar ummi 

Umma na murmishi tace  toga  abinci aljana safiya tace  wllh  mun  koshi  umma  

Umma tace  haka  dai  ko  ruwane  kusha  

Amina ta  dauki ruwan  ta  a hannun ta  

Umma ta  tashi  tace  husna  nina  shiga  daga  ciki  bacci  nakeji  baki  kigaida gida  ,Aljana safiya tace  insha Allah  umma  mun gode sosai  , murmishi umma tayi  ,,tayi sama



Aljana safiya ta  runtse ido ganin haka yasa  amina da  hafeez  suma  suka  rufe  idonsu  dama  tuni  aljana safiya  ta  saka  husna bacci 



Aljana safiya tace  amina  hafeez  dan Allah  ku  kalli  husna  da  kyau  bata  kama  da  aliyu  

Bude  idon su  sukayi  amina tace  wllh  nagani  shugaba  wannan  ai  inaga  yar  uwar  aliyu ce 

Hafeez yace  wannan  wani  irin  kama ne  haka  tabbas husna  yar  uwar  aliyu ce  amma  shugaba  ina  maman  husna 


Aljana safiya tace  ta  rasu  nan  ta  kwashe tarishin rayuwar husna da  mamanta  tafada  musu 


Sosai amina take kuka jin  cin mutumcin da  aka  yiwa  husna da  maman ta tace  to  shugaba  wannan  lamarin  nima  ya  daure  min  kai   ita  maman ta  tin tana  yar  karama  ta  rasu  aliyu kuwa  anzo  haihuwar shi  mamanshi ta rasu  to  indai  hakane  su  yan uwan junane  kodai  kamanni ne  na  ubangiji 


Hafeez yace  gaskiya  nima  hakan  nake  tunani  amma  shugaba  ki  fada  mata  komai  tinda  dama  batasan  tarishin maman taba  


Aljana safiya tace  banason  tashiga damuwa ne  amma  dole  nafada  mata  , nan  ta  tashi  husna  

Bude idon ta tayi  tace ummi  bacci ne  yayi  gaba  dani  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace ba  komai  ki  nutsu  yau  zan  baki  labarin  mamanki 


Da sauri  husna ta  gyara zama  tace  yauwa ummi  dama  inason  naje  baba  yafada min ina  dangin  mama na suke  to  kifadamun  ummi 


Aljana safiya ta  rumgume husna tukun  tabata labarin mamanta  tace  amma  karki  sawa  kanki damuwa  kinji  

Husna  na   kuka sosai  tace  amma  anty  tabawa bata yimin  adalci ba  data  rabani  da  mama na  

Aljana safiya tace  to  kinji dai  mamanki  yar  kasar  marar ce  idan  kikaje  gidanku saiki  tambayi  baba  yafada  miki  a  kasar masar  inda  kakan ninki  suke  


Husna tace insha Allah ummi  nagode sosai da  taimakon ku  agareni  ke  alkhairi ce  a  rayuwa ta  amma  ummi dan  Allah  idan  yafada mun  zamuje  dake  ko  dankiga  dangin  mama  zamuje  harda  aliyu  kuma  inaso  kibani  shi 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace kai  anya zan  iya  rabuwa  da  aliyu  kuwa  husna  aiko  babanshi  yafito  bazan  iya  rabuwa  dashi  ba  

Husna tace to shikenan  badamuwa  amma  zakike  kawomin shiko  


Aljana safiya tace eh zan kawoshi  yanzu  bari  muwuce  gida  sai  nadawo  ranar  da  zamuje  kasar masar kiga  su  kaka 

Murmishi husna tayi tace to  kigaishe minda abba da aliyu 


Aljana safiya tace insha Allah muntafi  , nan  suka b'ace 



*BABAN KWANA BIYU*



Husna taje  gidansu  talatu  kamar  ta  maidata  ciki  sai  nan nan  take  da  ita  hakan  yayiwa  husna dadi  da  baba  ganin  talatu  ta  shiryu  


Talatu tazo  kusan  husna tace ya'ta dan  Allah ki yafemin  abubuwan dana  miki  nacin mutumci  kinji yar albarka  


Husna cike da farin ciki tace na  yafe miki  har  kaka  duka  nayafe  muku  Allah  ya  yafe  mana  lefukanmu 

Talatu tace ameen  ta  rungume  tana kukan  nadama  tace insha Allah  nadena komai 


Baba ne yashigo  yana ganinsu  yace  iyee  kaga  uwa  da  diyarta


Murmishi husna tayi  tace  yauwa baba  dama  zuwana  wajan  nazo  



Baba yace  to  inajinki  husna yar baba  yana  zama  kusanta 


Husna tace  baba  ance  mama  yar  kasar  masar ce  to  a  wani  bangare  take  sunaso  naje  na  gansu


Nan da  nan  baba  yasoma  hawaye  jin  an  tuno  mishi  da  abin kaunar shi  yace  mahaifiyar ki  yar  gidan  sarauta  ce  babanta  yadawo Nigeria ne  saboda  bayason  sarauta  bayan  auren mu  suka  koma  kasarsu  suka  bani  amanar  mamanki  tin  daga  lokacin  basu  sake  dawowa ba  har wannan  lokacin  da  nake  fada  miki  amma  Alhamdulillah  nima  zamuje  tare dake  


Talatu tace harni  zaa je  


Husna tace  to  insha Allah  zamuje  naga  kakan nina  



Nan sukaci gaba da hira  sai yamma  ta  koma  gida

*57—58*



jirgin  su  husna  na  dagawa  zuwa  kasar  masar  , acikin jirgin  husna  sai  murna  take  yau  itama  zataga  dangin  maman  ta  Alhaji abdallah  sai  kallon ta  yakeyi  yana  murmishi  ganin  sahibar tashi  na  cikin farin ciki 

Yace  sahibaty wai yau  duk  farin cikin  ganin  dangin ne haka  bakinki  yakasa  rufuwa ?

Husna tace  sahiby  bazaka  gane  ba  wllh  ji  nake  kamar  jirgin baya  sauri  na  k'agu  naga  iyayen  mama  ne  shiyasa  Allah sarki  mama Allah yajikanki da rahama 


Murmishi yayi yace ameen  yau  zanga  dawa  kike  kama  a  dangin  mama  

Murmishi husna tayi tace zaka  gani  ai  tinda  baba  yace  kamar  mama  nayi  itama  kamar  babanta  ne  tayo  kaga  kuwa  zakaga  masu  kama  dani sosai makuwa 


Rumgume ta  yayi  yana  shinshinan  wuyarta yace  to  shikenan  yanzu  kiyi  hakuri bacci  kafin  muje  ko  

Shafa  sajenshi  tayi  wanda  kullin  yake matuk'ar burgeta  tace  to  shikenan  ana  Zuwa  katashe  ni  kaji ko  idan  kaki  tashina  tam  nida  kaine 

Rike  baki  yayi yace  ah  wane  ni  ai  dole  na  tasheki  karki  sauke  min  gajiyar  hanya akaina  zan  tasheki   kwanta  abinki  kihuta  farin cikin  abdallah bawan  Allah maraya 


Murmishi tayi tace  to  abin alfaharin  husna  yar  baba  marainiyar Allah  


Dariya  yayi yace wato kin  rama  daiko  , Husna tace  eh  wllh  ai  naji  dadi  ne  Allah yabarmu tare mijina  
Yace ameen  nan  ya  kara rumgume ta,  ita  kuma  ta  rufe  idonta  alamar bacci  



Aljana safiya najinsu  murmishi tayi a fili tace  Alhamdulillah  Allah nagode maka daka bawa husna  miji  nagari  mai  tausayin maraicinta 


Amina tayi yar dariya tace shugaba  to  nima  kimin addu'a Allah  yabani miji nagari  mai  tausayina 


Dafata  safiya tayi tace  ayya  ai  ki  kwantar da hankalin ki  nizan  zab'a miki miji kinji  yar  amana  ta  

Sunkuyar da kai amina tayi tana  rufe fuska sai murmishi take 


Aljana safiya dariya tayi tace a  kaji  rashin  kunya  ana  son  aure  ana  wani daga  hanci  sama  to  muna  dawowa  kema  samu  miki  miji  kowama  ya  huta  kema  kizama  mama  yar  k'anwata  


Amina kuka tasa  tana  rumgume safiya tace  shugaba  banason  nabarki  inason  nazauna  kusa  dake  muke  aikin  lada  dake  nina  hakura da  auren ma  wasa nake  wllh  bazan  iya  rabuwa  dakeba  na  shak'u  dake  fiye  da  yanda  na  shak'u  da  iyayena   Allah ne  gata na  kece  gatana  bazan  iya  aure  a wani  yankin  ba  nabar  yankin mu  inasonki shugaba  kaunarki  ajinina  take  ni banason  auren  zan  zauna  dake  har  abada  muke  taimakon bayin Allah  , takai karshen maganar tana  sake  saka sabon kuka 


Bubbuga bayanta aljana safiya takeyi tace  haba  amina  daga  magana  sai  kuma  ki  rikice  haka  bafa  auren  bane  yazo ba  fadi  ne  nayi  ke  yanzu  bakiso  kiyi  aure  ki  haifi  yara  kema  ake  ce  miki  mama  ko  umma  ai  aure  yazama dole  insha Allah  zaki  samu  miji  nagari  wanda  har  wani  lokacin  zaki  manta  dani  


Amina cikin kuka tace Allah yakiyaye  aure  yasa  na  manta dake  to  ai  gyara  karnayi  auren  indai  zan  manta  dake 


Aljana safiya ganin  amina  ta  saka  mata  rigima  sai  danne  dariyar ta  tace to  shikenan  baza'a miki  auren ba  muzauna  tare ko  


Dagowa amina tayi tace eh wllh muyi zaman mu 


Murmishi safiya tayi kawai  ta  kyale  maganar  shesun  dawo ayi  maganar aurenta 


10:00 AM  jirgin su  yasauka  a  masar  mutanen ciki  suka  soma  fitowa  cen  saigasu  husna  suna  fitowa  daya  bayan daya har  suka gaba  fitowa  baki  daya



Husna ta kalli baba yace  to  baba  ina  zamubi  yanzu 



Baba yayi murmishi yace  motar  haya  duk wanda muka  cewa  gidan  sarauta  zai  kaimu  insha Allah 


Alhaji abdallah yace  to kuzo  mushiga mota uku  zamu dauki  drop suna  fita  bakin  hanya  motoci suka  soma  tsayawa  suna  tambayar su  in  zasuje,  Alhaji abdallah yace  ko  gidan  sarki  sukeso  akaisu  mai  motar yace  to  ku  shigo  kowa  yashiga  sukayi motar uku  suka  kama  hanyar  gidan  sarki



A bakin kofar  shiga  suka  saukesu  Alhaji abdallah  ya  biyasu  kudinsu  suka  tafi. Su  kuma  suka  nufi  bakin kofar shiga  cikin masarautar 


Suna karawa Alhaji abdallah yamusu magana  da  harshen turanci  yace  so  bakin  sarki  ne  sun  kawo  mishi ziyarane 


Masu gadin  wani  daga cikin yace  gaskiya  bazai  yiyuba  mu  bamu  yadda  daku  ba  ku  koma  kawai  tayama  zakuce  wajan  sarki  kukazo  inbanda  ma  kun  renamu,  nan  yaci  gaba da  masifar  kamar dama  jiran  su  tambayeshi  yakeyi  

Ran aljana safiya ne  yasoma  b'aci  kallon ta  abba yayi yace safiya  jeki  musu  magana  yaran  nan  zafin  rana  she  shigarsu  yakeyi 


Tunkarar shi  aljana safiya tayi tace  bawan Allah  mufa  baki ne  tin  daga  Nigeria  mukazo  kuma  saboda  rashin mutumci  irin  naka  kace  wai  mun  rena  maka  hankali  zaka  bamu  waje  mu  ficene  kona  canja  maka  kamanni  tana  wurga miki harara 




Shima cike da masifa  yace  baza'a  matsa  ba  ai  da  ace ku  bakin  sarki ne  to  zaice  mana  baki zasu  shigo  ku wllh  n....bai  ida  abinda  yakeson  fadaba  aljana safiya ta  wanka  mishi  mari  har  biyu  


Cikin fushi tace  kai  ina  wasa  dakai ne  zaka  matsa  ko  saina  kakkarya  ka  anan  marar mutumci  dama  haka  mukewa  mutane  saboda  sarki shamsu  ya  koya  muku  rashin mutumci  to  zan  maganin  ka  idan  baka  bani  waje  muka  fice  ba



Wanda  aljana safiya ta  mara  ji  yayi kamar  ya  kurumce  saboda  tsananin zafin  marin  da sauri  ya  bude  hanya  yace  dan Allah kiyi hakuri  kushiga  amma  gaskiya  kin  iya  bari  



Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ai  wannan  shafar fuskar ka  ne  nayi  inda  marin  ne  ai   da  yanzu  baka  dawo  hayyacinka  ba juyawa tayi tace  kutafo 


Su baba  da  mamaki  suke  kallon  ta  sabanin sauran  da  sukasan   wacece safiya 


Suna  shiga  aljana safiya  taga  zoben hannun ta takawo haske  saurin kallon abba tayi tace  kardai  ace  nan  shine  masautar  sarki adam sulaiman  

Abba yace  indai kinbashi  zobe  to  nan  ne  

Murmishi safiya tayi tace to  ka  ganshi  cen  yana  tunkaro  mu  inaga  fitama  zaiyi  


Da sauri suka  kalli  inda  aljana safiya take  nuna  musu  aikuwa  suka  ganshi  


Husna na  ganinshi  tasoma hawaye  ganin  yana  kama  da  ita  hannun Alhaji abdallah ya rike  tace  sahiby  kaga  mai  kama  dani ko



Murmishi yayi yace nagani sosai kuke kama 


Sarki adam  kuwa  mamaki ne  da  al'ajabi  yakamashi  ganin  zoben hannun shi takawo haske  yace  to  ikon Allah  aljana safiya ce  a  masarautar mu  , nan  yasoma  waige waige  yana  son ganin ta  inda  zata  fito  , gabanshi  ya  kalla  yaga  mutane  sunfi  Goma kara  kallon su  yayi  aikuwa  yagano  aljana safiya tana  mishi murmishi 


Da sauri  yace  wa  daugarar  shi kutsaya  inada  baki  tsayar  da  dokin  sukayi  yasauka    yanufo  su  husna 

Yana karasowa  yace  assalam alaikum  ranki yadade yau kece  a  yankin namu  sannunku da  zuwa  gaskiya  yau  muna  da  manyan  baki 


Murmishi yayi yace eh mune  wani abu  mai  mahimmanci yakawo mu  


Sarki adam yace  to  badamu  mushiga  ciki  , harya  juya  sai  yaji  ankama  hannun shi  


Da sauri yajuyo  aliyu yagani  yana  dariyar  murmishi sarki adam yayi yace  la  yaron ummi  dakai  akazo  inata  cewa  zan  sake  kai  muku  ziyara  amma  ban  samu  dama  ba 


Aliyu na tsalle yace  wana kama  bak'on ummi  shine  katafi  kaki  dawowa  kullin sainace  ummi na  takaini  wajanka  naganka  sai  tace  ba  yanzu  ba  sai  yau na ganka, sarki adam yace ayya  Allah sarki  to muje ciki  aliyu 

Gaba  sarki shamsu  yayi  su  baba  nabinsa  abaya  har  sashen  baki   daki daya suka  zauna  


Sarki adam yasa  aka  kawo musu abinci  da  ruwa  suka ci  suka  koshi    suna  gamawa   aka  dauke  kayan  abincin  


Aljana safiya tace  yauwa  sarki adam  abinda  ya  kawomu  shine  wannan  ta  nuna mishi  husna  da  baba 

Da sauri sarki adam  ya  mike  yana  kallon husna yace  aliya  kece  dama  da  rabon  zan  sake  ganinki  yar  uwata  Alhamdulillah  kai  Allah nagode  maka  


Aljana safiya tace kai  dakata  bafa  aliya bace  yarta  ce  wannan  mijin  tane  ko  bakasan  jin  nata  bane  


Sarki adam yace  Alhamdulillah  dama  aliya  tanada  diya  kamar  wannan  zo  nan  ya'ta  ni  baban  kine  kanin  maman ki  yana  mika  mata  hannu 


Husna  na  share hawaye  ta  mike  taje  wajan sarki adam  da  sauri  ya  rumgume ta  yana  kuka  itama  husna  kukan  take  


Babu  wanda  yahanasu  saida  sukayi  mai  isarsu  tukun  sarki adam ya  dago husna  yana  kallon ta  kamar  an  dauki  fuskar  maman ta  adaura  mata 


Yace  yarinya ya  sunan ki? Tace  sunana husna  , sarki adam yace to  ina  maman  naki  take 


Komawa tayi jikinta  tasaka sabon kuka  cikin kuka tace  uncle mamana  ta  rasu  tin  ina  karama  


Da sauri  ya  sake  dago husna  yace  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un husna  kina  nufin  kicemin  yar uwata  ta  rasu  


Husna tace eh  ta  rasu    



Sarki adam yace wa  wani  dogarinsa  sarkin yaki  kaje  kace  su  baba  suzo  dakin  baki  ina  jiransu 


Cike da girmamawa yace to  ranka yadade nan  yafice ,  babu jimawa sai  gashi yadawo  fina  biye  dashi

Da sallama suka shigo   amsa  musu  sukayi  


Saida  suka  zauna  tukun  sarki adam yace husna  jeki  ga  kakannin ki  cen  ya  nuna  mata  tsofin  da  suke  zaune  kowa  yasaki  baki  yana  kallon  husna 



Kakar husna ce  ta  kasa  hakuri tace  wannan  aliya ce  take min gizo ko kuwa 



Husna  ta  karaso  inda  suke  tace  kaka  yarta ce  ba  ita  bace  , da sauri suka rumgume ta  suna fadin Alhamdulillah  to ina  aliyar take ne 


Sarki adam yace dadda  saidai muyi hakuri  yaya aliya  tarigamu  gidan  gaskiya  


Da sauri dadda  ta  dago  tana  girgiza kai  tace innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un aliya kiyafe mana  rashin  waiwayar ki  damukayi  Allah yajikanki  da  rahama 



Baba  ya  taso  yazo  kusan  su  dadda  yace  dadda  barka da warhaka  dafatan munsameku lfy

Dadda tace  musbahu  kune  kuke  tafe  maiyasa  kukayi  zuwa  bayan  tafowarmu  


Baba yace  dadda  ai  bayan  tafiyar ku  babu  dadewa  aliya tasamu cikin husna   tacemin  sai  bayan  ta  haihu  na  kawota  lokacin  banida  kudin  jirgin  dazamuzo  nan  to  bayan ta  haihu  kuma  tace  ba  yanzu  zatazo  kasarta  ba  saita  haifi  yara  uku  intazo  zakuji  dadin  ganinta  da  iyalinta to  husna nata shekara uku  maman tarasu   kuma  bansamu  dama  zuwa  ba  sai  yanzu  bayan  husna  tayi aure  ta  bukaci  nakawo  ta  wajan  dangin maman  ta   shine  mijinta  yafiya  kudin  jirgi  mukazo  


Ajiyar zuciya baban  aliya yasauke   yace  Allah  baiwar Allah Allah  yajikanta  da  rahama  muna  munso  zuwa  amma  wani  azzalimi hatsabibi  ya  tarwatsa  mana  masarauta  ya  kashe  mana  mutane dayawa      muna  da kyer muka  tsira  yanzu  Alhamdulillah  yaje  yatabo  karshen  kwanan  shi  ya  taba *ALAJANAR JARUMAI*  yar  albarka  ta  takashe  mugu  yanzu  Alhamdulillah  muna  zaman lfy  


Sarki adam zaiyi magana  aljana safiya  ta  nuna  mai  karya ce  itace  , shuru  yayi sai cen tukun yace  gaskiya  Alhamdulillah  yanzu  garinmu  babu  tashin hankali  muna  zaman lfy  



Dadda tace  ina  mai  gidan  namu  yake  ga  sabuwar amarya  


Alhaji abdallah ya  matso  wajan  su  ya  gaishesu  suna  sa  mishi  albarka  


Umma  kuwa  sai  murmishi take  ganin ana  yabon  danta  


Aliyu ne  yasaka  kuka yace  ummi  nima  ina  babana  koya  mutu  irin  na  maman  husna  , tam  kirjin  aljana safiya  ya  buga  amma  saita  rikeshi  tace  kaga  abba  ne  babanka  kaji  nice  mamanka  kadena  kukan  


Aliyu yace to  ummi ke ina  baban ki  koya  mutu ne 


Tinda  suka  fara  maganar  dadda  take  kallon  aliyu  bako  kiftawa  sai  cen  tace  wannan  mai  kama  da  adam  dinfa 


Ajiyar zuciya aljana safiya tasauke ta  kalli  su  sarki adam tace  shin  ko  zaku  fadamun  yadda  aka  tarwatsa  muku  masarauta  ?



Sarki adam yace  a lokacin da  sarki  shamsu  yakawo  mana  hari  babu  wanda  yasan  da  zuwansa  saijin  ihun  mutane  muka  ringa  ji   sarki shamsu mugune  yana  anfani  da  tsafi  ne ya  kwashe mana  dukiyar mu  ya  dauke  mana  mayakan mu  duk  wani  abu  mai  anfani  saida  yayi  gaba  dashi  


Daga karshe ma  masarautar  yadawo  yake  mulkin  zalinci   nida su  dadda  da  baba  da  sauran  yan  uwan  mu  mukabar  masarautar baki daya  ganin  tafi  karfi munfi



Haka  mukaci  gaba  da  bak'ar  rayuwa   cikin kunci  sarki shamsu  hadda  matata  yadauke  da  tsohon ciki  a jikinta   kamar  nayi  hauka  haka  nakeji  alokacin  da  kyer  yasaita  kaina  muka  shiga  duniya  muna  fatauci  da  yar  dukiyar  da  muka  kwasa  dazamu  gudu  daga  masarautar 


Mun dauki  shekaru sunkai  shida  muna  yawo  a  duniya  kamar  wasu  mahaukata   sai  wannan  shekara  ne  mukaji  labarin  *ALJANAR JARUMAI*  ta  kashe  shi  amma  kullin  da  Lateefa  nake  kwana  nake  tashi  araina   bansani ba  ta  mutu ne  ko  tana  raye itada  abinda ke  cikin ta Allah  masani  




Da sauri  aljana safiya  ta  dago tace  yaya  kamanni ta  yake  


Sarki adam yace  kamar  dai  husna  haka  kamannin ta  yake  saboda  auren dangi ne  aka  mana  ita  diyar  kanin  baba ce  shiyasa  muke  kama  sunfi  kama  da  aliya kamar  yan biyu inkika  gansu  


Mikewa aljana safiya tayi  tana  murmishi  tana  share  hawaye  sujjadar godiya ga Allah  tayi  ta  dago  tana  kallon  abba  tace  Alhamdulillah  Alhamdulillah  Alhamdulillah  ya  Allah  abba  ashe  sarki adam  baban  aliyu ne  shiyasa  ranar  daya  ganshi  kullin  saiyayi  kukan  nakaishi  wajan  bak'on dayazo  gidanmu  da kyer  muke  mishi  wayo  ya  hakura  ashe  babanshi ne  yagani  shiyasa  yake  kukan  saiga  zo  wajan shi 


Sarki adam da sauri ya  matso kusan  aliyu  shi sai  yanzu ma  yake  ganin  kamannin sudashi  rungume  aliyu yayi  yana fadin  Alhamdulillah  ashe  kai  jinina  ne  shiyasa  dana  dawo  gida  naketa  tunashi  Allah  abin godiya    

Aljana safiya tace amma  ko  babanshi yafito  bazan  iya  rabuwa da  aliyu ba  





Dadda tace  kai masha Allah  Alhamdulillah  wannan  lamari  yayi  kyau  gaskiya  jikokina  biyu  Allah  yabayyana minsu  yarinya yasunan ki

Aljana safiya tace  ni  sunana safiya  



Dadda tace  masha Allah  Allah yamiki albarka  mungode sosai  


Aljana safiya tace ba komai  ai  yiwa  kaine  

Nan akaci gaba da  hira  aliyu na  mak'ale  da  babanshi  shima  sarki adam  na  kara  rumgume  shi yana  jin  kaunar  yaron  nashi 


Anata  hira  mimee  ta  farka daga  bacci  ganin  babu  wanda  ya  kulata  saita  nufo  husna  , tana  zuwa  ta  fada  kan cinyar husna  tasoma kuka  

Husna tace  a a  yar  mommy  kintashi ne  


Mimee tace  mommy  tin  dazun na  tashi  babu  wanda  yakulani  saina  ganki  a  nan  shine  nazo  kusan  mommy na 


Murmishi husna tayi tace  to  ai  ni  banga  lokacin  da  kika  tashiba  zakici abinci akawo miki?

Mimee tace  mommy  nidai  kizo  muje  muyi  bacci  mun gaji  ko  muhuta  


Husna tace  banajin bacci yanzu  mimee  kwanta  anan  saikiyi akan  cinyata  ko 


Mimee tace mommy  maiyasa  bazaki  kwanta  ki  hutaba  ,Husna tace  zan  kwanta  fara  kwanciya  tukun  


Dadda tace to husna  wannan ma  yarki ce  masha Allah 


Husna tace  eh  yatace  amma  itama mamansu tarasu  wajan  haihuwar ta  su  uku ne  ga  yan uwanta cen 

Dadda tace  Allah sarki  nan  dai  taron  marar  uwa ne Allah  yajikansu  da rahama  


Sukace ameen  



Bayan su  husna  sun  huta  aka  ringa  tsayawa  dasu  masarauta ana  nunasu  itada  aliyu   kowa  inya  gansu   saiyayi hawaye  saboda  tuna  iyayensu  dasukayi  


Satinsu  daya  a  kasar masar suna  zagaya dangi  da  yan abokan arziki  


Yaune  zasu  koma  Nigeria  sunsha  tsaraba  ba  kadan  ba  


Dadda ce  ta  shigo sashensu  tace  yauwa  ina  da  magana  daku   


Zama sukayi baba yace muna jinki  ,Dadda tace  dama  inaso  dama  baban  safiya muna  barar  auren  safiya  inaso  ta  auri  adam  


Da sauri  aljana safiya tadago  ta  kalli  sarki  adam  shimq  ita  yake  kallo  

Sarki adam yace  dadda  safiya fa  ba  mutum  bace  aljana ce 



Da sauri dadda  ta  mike tace  kai  yaron nan  da  gaske  kake  yi  safiya  ba  mutum  bace  


Sarki adam yace  eh  ai  itace *ALJANAR JARUMAI*  kuma  itace  ta  taimakemu  lokacin  da  sarki  shamsu  yaturo  mana  da  abun  cutarwa  


Ajiyar zuciya dadda tasauke tace  lalle  kinci  sunanki *ALJANAR JARUMAI*  Allah  yamiki  albarka  kinji 


Aljana safiya tace ameen  nagode sosai  yanzu  mu  zamu  wuce  amma  inba  damuwa  sarki adam nayi  maka  mata ban  saniba  ko  zaka  sota



Murmishi yayi yace  ai  nasan  duk  abinda  yafito  daga  gareki  mai  kyau  ne  dan  haka  ko  ban  ganki  bama  na  amince  da  auren  


Cike da farin ciki  aljana safiya tace Alhamdulillah  nagode sosai  gaskiya  , sai  kazo  ganin  amarya  muzamu  tafi  


Aliyu ya  kama  hannun  safiya  yace  ummi  mu  koma  gida  ko  nan  gidan  ba  irin  namu  bane  ko 


Sarki adam yace  to  muje  abinku  dama  dai  bakason  zama  damu  shine  kake  cewa  garinmu  bashida kyau 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  ato  kasan dai  garinmu  yafi  naku  kyau  sosai  


Dadda da  baba  sukace to  shikenan  aliyu  amma  kake  zuwa  ganinmu  ko  ,Aliyu yace  eh  zamuzo  da  ummi  na  


Fitowa sukayi  aka  shishiga  motoci  har  airport  sukaje  suna  zuwa  jirginsu  ya  sauka  


Sallama  sukayiwa  su  sarki adam  sosai  dadda  take  jaddadawa  baba  musbahu  idan  husna ta  haihu  ka fada  mana  ga  number  wayar  adam  nan  anbaka  


Baba yace insha Allah dadda  zan  fada  muku  


Sarki adam  kuwa  kamar, zaiyi kuka  ganin  aliyu  yaki  yarda  ya  zauna  dashi  amma  saboda  karamci  irin na  aljana safiya  ya  hakura   badan  ranshi  yaso ba


Aljana safiya  ta  fuskanci haka  saita  kalli aliyu  da  yake  tsokana  mimee  tace  yaron kirki  kazauna  anan  kaji  idan  nadawo  saimu  tafi


Aliyu yasoma kuka  yace  banaso  ummi  mukoma  gida  nidai  bazan  zauna ba 

Aljana safiya  tace  yarona toyi shuru  rufe  idonka  zan  baka  wani  abu  


Da sauri  yarufe  yana  dariya yace mommy na  rufe  , dafa  kanshi  aljana safiya  cen  kuma  tace  to  bude 


Budewa  yayi  yana  kallon ta  murmishi  tamishi tace  yaron kirki  maza  ga  babanka  cen  kuzauna    idan  nadawo  sai mutafi 


Da sauri yasaki hannun safiya  yakoma wajan sarki adam yace  kaine  babana  inji  ummi na  zamu  zauna  ko


Nannauyar ajiyar zuciya sarki adam yasauke yace  eh  yarona  zamu  zauna  


Aliyu ya  soma  dagawa  su  safiya hannu yace  saikun dawo ummi na 


Murmishi safiya tayi  tace Alhamdulillah  yaro  zauna  kaji  dumin babanka  tukun tace  to  saina  dawo  


Nan  suka  sake  musu  sallama  suka  shiga jirgi  , saida  sukaga  tashinsu  tukun  suka  koma gida  


Sarki  adam kuwa  mamaki  yake  ganin  yadda  nan da nan  aliyu  ya  yarda  dashi  cen kuma yace  ai  wannan  aikin  safiya ne  jaruma  Allah  yadafa miki  




Jirgin su husna yasauka  a  airport   suna  fitowa  su  aljana safiya  suka  musu  sallama  


Husna tace  kai  ummi  bazaki  zo  gidanmu  kihuta ba  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  sai  bayan  kwana biyu  kila  zanzo  yanzu  gida  zan  koma  na  huta  tukun 


Husna tace to ummi  fatan alkhairi  nan  suka  shiga mota  suna  musu beyy  


Su aljana safiya ma  suka  b'ace  sai a  dajinsu  suka  bayyana  nan  kowa  yayi  dakinsa  dan ya huta 


Su husna ma  na  zuwa  gida  wanka  tayiwa  yaran  tukun  itama  tayi  suka  kwanta  baccin  gajiya....




*MAMAN NUSAIBA CE ✍🏻*

husna bata  farka  ba  sai  kunsan  magarib  tukun ta farka  toilet  tashiga tayi wanka   bayan tafito shiryawa  tayi cikin doguwar rigar atamfa masha Allah  tayi kyau ta   kashe dauri tukun  ta  fito  falo  



Tin kafin ta  karaso mimee ta  mike  daga kan  cinyar  umma  ta nufeta tana  kuka 


Kama hannun ta husna tayi  tace  waya  tabamin  autar   mommy  ne  yau  agidan nan  mubawa  hamata  iska 


Murmishi umma  tayi  tace  kirabu da ja'ira  tun d'azun  nakebin  kanta  taci abinci amma tak'iya  wai  ita  sai  mommyn ta  zata  mata  abinci 


Da mamaki husna  ta  kalli mimee tace  maiyasa  bakici  abinci yauba  ?


Mimee tace mommy  nidai  kwaki da daddawa irin na ranar nan zakiyi mana  gasu  Yusuf cenma  suna  fushi  basuci  abinci ba  tinda  muka  dawo  tana  nuna mata  su  da  hannu 



Kallon su husna tayi tace  maza kuzo nan  , tayi  sukayi  kowa  sai  turo baki yakeyi    saboda  ba  karamar  yunwa  sukeji  ba  suna  zuwa  zama  sukayi  a  gaban  husna  



Hade rai  husna tayi  ta kallesu tace  ban  hanaku  wannan  halin ba  wato saboda  bakujin  magana ta  shine  tin safe  kuke   zaune  bakuci  abinci  ba  ko  to  daga yau  karna sake  ganin kun  zauna  da  yunwa  idan  kuma  kuka sake  saina  zaneku daya bayan daya  tinda  bakujin magana 


Da sauri sukace mommy kiyi hakuri mu  kwaki da daddawa mukeso  umma  kuma  tace  ita  bata  iyaba  amma  bazamu  sakeba kinji my sweet mommy muje  ki  mana 


Murmishi husna tayi tace  to shikenan  amma in  kuka  sake  irin haka  bamai  amsarku a  hannuna  muje kitchen na  hada  muku  , tashi sukayi  dukkansu  suka  bita kitchen 


Umma tace murmishi tace gaskiya  abdallah  kayi  dacen mata  kalli  fa  yadda  take  kula  maka  da  yara  wannan  ko  uwar data  haifesu  bazata  musu  wannan  kulawar ba Allah  Allah yabar tare  har gidan aljanna 



Alhaji abdallah yace ameen ummana  yana  kallon hayar kitchen  din  sosai  son  husna  ke  nunkuwa  a  zuciyar shi,  nan  sukaci gaba da hirar su





Aljana safiya ce  zaune   ita dasu abba  tace  abba  dama  inason  yin  wata  magana ne 


Abba yace  babu damuwa  yar albarka kiyi  


Murmishi safiya tayi tace dama  abba  inason  a  hada amina da  hafeez aure  saboda  naga  akwai  shakuwa a  tsakanin su  mai  karfi  amma  bazamu  yanke  hukunci ba  sai  anji  ta  bakinsu 


Abba yace masha Allah  to  ku  kunji  abinda  ummin  naku  tafada  kai  hafeez  kana  son  amina  


Sunkuyar da kai hafeez yayi tukun yace  abba  aini na  jima  inason  amina   ban  saniba  ko  ita  tana  sona  ko  akasin  haka  amma  banason  a  tauye  mata  hakkinta  a  tambayeta  tukun  aji  ta  bakinta  


Abba yayi murmishi yace to  amina kinji abinda  dan  uwanki  yafada  kin  yarda  ahadaku  aure 



Amina tarufe fuska  tana murmishi tace  nima  ai  na  jima  inasonshi  na  amince  ayi  mana  aure  tana  fadin  haka  ta  ruga  aguje  cikin  daki



Dariya sukayi  shima  hafeez  yabar  wajan  yana  jin  matsanancin  farin ciki  


Aljana safiya tace abba  asaka  sati daya  kawai  tinda  munada    komai  agida  inaso bikin sarki adam shi kuma ya zama  sati biyu masu  zuwa  


Abba yace  to  masha Allah  satin ma  yayi  shi  sarki adam din har kin mishi  matar  ne 


Aljana safiya tace eh  abba  gobe  nakeson  zanje  gidan su  yarinyar  daga  nan  naje  nagano  su hajiya da bilkisu 



Murmishi  ABB  yayi yace  Allah  yakaimu  goben  ta  amsa da ameen  , nan  sukaci gaba da hirar su 



WASHE GARI 


da  wuri su  aljana safiya  suka  shirya   zuwa  ziyarar  da  sukeson  zuwa  suna  fita  daga  gida  aljana safiya tace  amina kitafi  masar  kikawo  sarki adam  inason  yaga  matar dazai  aura  



Cike da girmama tace to angama shugaba  nan  ta  b'ace  suma  suna  b'acewa  a  kofar  gidan  su  ladi  suka  bayyana 


Ladi ce  tafito  ta  hijab dinta  har  kasa  kallon hannun ta  tayi  ganin  zoben hannun ta  yakawo haske  cike  da  murna  tace  la  anty  safiya  ce  tazo  ji  tayi  andafata  ta  baya 


Juyawa tayi  taga  aljana safiya  cikin matsanancin farin ciki  ta  rumgume  aljana safiya  tana fadin oyoyo  anty safiya 


Murmishi safiya tayi tace  masha kinya  yadda  kikayi  kyau  matuka  


Ladi tace nagode sosai anty muje ciki su  mama  nanan  


Ciki  suka  shiga  da sallama  amsa musu  su  mama  sukayi  



Mama tace  a a  sannunku da  zuwa masha Allah  yau  munada  manyan baki  ashe  kuzauna  , zama  sukayi  

Aljana safiya ta  gaishe da mama  cike  da  kulawa  mama  ta  amsa  tana  fadada  murmishin ta  itada baba


Aljana safiya tace dama  baba  nazo  da  wata magana ne  ban  sani  ba  zaku  amsheta 



Baba yace  ai  nasan  duk  abinda  yafito daga gareki alkhairi  dan  haka munajinki



Aljana safiya tace ngd  dama  nayiwa  ladi  mijin  aure ne  sunanshi  sarki Adam sulaiman  sarkir  kasar masar 


Da mamaki baba  yake  kallon ta  yace  yanzu  yar nan  kina  gani  zai  amince  ya  aure  ta  ahaka?

Aljana safiya tace  insha Allah  gasunan ma  zuwa  

Mama tace Allah ya  tabbatar mana da alkhairi 


Aljana safiya tace ladi  maza kije kiyi wanka  sunzo  mikewa  ladi  tayi  tama  rasa  mai  zatayi  wai  yau  itace  zata yi  aure sarki  , tana  shiga  daki  tashiga  wanka  



Amina da sarki adam ne  suka  bayyana  a  bakin kofar gidansu ladi  , Amina tace  bari  namaka  iso  ko 


Murmishi yayi yace  to  so  nake  naga  matata 


Yar dariya amina tayi tace  hartama  zama  matarka  ko  


Shima dariyar yayi yace  insha Allah  , ficewa  tayi ciki  tacewa  aljana safiya  sarki  adam  na  bakin  kofa 



Aljana safiya tace  baba  inane  dakin  baki  akaishi  cen  kafin  tafito  , nuna  mata  baba  yayi  , kallon amina tayi tace  ki kaishi nan  zankawo  ta 

Da to  amina ta  amsa  ta  fita  takaishi  dakin baki  tace mishi  yanzu  amaryar zatazo  ,, murmishi yayi yace  to  shikenan  saina  ganta 



Aljana safiya tamike tashiga  dakin  ladi  tasameta  tagama  komai  wasu  kaya  masu  kyau  ta  bata  riga da sket da  mayafi  da  takalmi  duk  pink   kwalliya ta  tsarawa ladi  sosai  tayi kyau  


Kayan  ladi  tasaka  masha Allah  tayi kyau sosai kamar  ka  saceta  ka  gudu  dakin  bakin suka  shiga  da sallama 

Amsa  musu  yayi  da  fara'arsa zama  sukayi  nan  suka gaisa 



Aljana safiya tace  to  yakaga  amaryar  tayi  ?


Sarki adam daya  k'urawa  ladi  na  mujiya  ya  juya  kan  aljana safiya  yace  ai  tinkafin  naganta  naji  ina  sonta  dan  haka  kawai  afara  shirin  biki  yan  mata ya  sunanki 


Jin haka aljana safiya tabar musu dakin 


Ladi tace  sunana  Wasila  amma  anfi  sanina  da  ladi  ina  fatan  zaka  rikeni  amana  kazamo  gatana  a  rayuwar aurenmu  ayimin adalci  kaji  tausayi na 


Murmishi yayi yace  insha Allah  nadauka  bazaki  taba  dana sanin  auren  mu ba  yanzu  muje  kiyimin  izinin  ganinsu  baba  


Tashi tayi  tafita  wajansu  mama  tazo  sai murmishi  take  tace  baba  yace  yana  neman  izinin  ganinku  


Baba yace kice  ya  shigo,,  juyawa  ladi tayi   takoma  suka  dawo  a  tare 


Har kasa  sarki Adam  ya  durkusa  ya  gaishe  dasu  baba  , amsawa  baba  yayi  cike da  kulawa  


Sarki adam yace  baba  ina  neman  izinin auren  ladi  kuma  bana  son  asa  lokaci  mai  tsayi  


Murmishi baba yayi yace  to safiya  kamar  yaushe za'a saka  


Gyara zama tayi tace  sati biyu  kawai  ya  isa   , yanzu  idan  ka  koma  gida  katuro  da  manyanka  sukawo  lefe  da  sadaki 



Sosai sarki adam yayi farin ciki yace  insha Allah  godiya sosai yamusu  tukun amina  ta  maidashi  gida  , yana  zuwa  yafadawa  su  dadda  suma  babu  bata  lokaci  suka  ce  bayan  kwana biyu  zasu  biyo  jirgi  akawo  kayan lefe 


Aljana safiya tace to  ladi Allah  yanuna mana ranar bikin  kinga  muma  sati mai  zuwa  za'a  auren  amina hafeez   zanzo  na  daukeku  kedasu  husna  in  lokacin  bikin  yayi 



Cike da  farin ciki ladi tace  masha Allah Allah  yasanya alkhairi  Allah yakaimu lokacin bikin 



Ameen sukace  tukun  suka  musu sallama  , nan  suka  b'ace 



Baba yace gaskiya  safiya  salihar macece  Allah  yadafa  mata  , Mama  tace  ameen  mai  gida  , Ladi kuwa na  daki  sai  kukan  farin ciki takeyi  



Su  aljana safiya  na  b'acewa  gidan su  alhaji da  hajiya  suka  nufa  abakin  get din  suka  bayyana  , kwankwasawa  sukayi  


Mai gadin ne  yabude  musu  yace  malamai  daga ina  haka 


Kallon shi sukayi  aljana safiya tace  hajiya na  nan kuwa  


Ma gadin yace  ai  yau  kwana nan su  uku  a  saudiya  sun  tafi  ummura  


Aljana safiya tace masha Allah to  idan  sunzo  kace  safiya  tazo  basa nan 


Mai gadin yace insha Allah zan  fada  musu 



Aljana safiya tace  muje  nan  suka  yi  gaba  saida  sukaga  mai  gadin  ya  rufe  kofa  suka  b'ace 



A gidan su  bilkisu suka  bayyana  sallama sukayi  aljana safiya ce  agaba  



Da  gudu bilkisu tazo  ta  rungume  safiya tana oyoyo  ashe  dama  zan  sake  ganinku  mutanen  kirki  sannunku da zuwa  



Murmishi safiya tayi tace gamu  kuwa  mun  zagayoku  


Bilkisu tace inna  siyama  maza  kuzo  munyi  baki  , da sauri suka fito 


Suna ido hudu da  aljana safiya ai  da sauri  siyama  ta  mak'ale  a jikin  inna  tace  inna  wllh  sun  dawo  kifada  musu  yanzu  zaman  lfy  mike  babu  abinda  mukewa  bilkisu  


Amina tace  ummi  dan  bari  na  razana  siyama  mana, 

 Dariya  aljana safiya tayi tace kai  amina  bakida  mutumci  ki  kyaketa  kawai 

Dariya itama tayi tace  to shikenan  


Aljana safiya tace mufa  babu  abinda  zamu  muku  ziyara  muka  kawo  muku  kudena  tsoronmu 


Jin  siyama  tasaki inna  tana  shafa  cikin da  daya kereta  sosai  tace  Alhamdulillah  wllh  har  yaron  cikina  yayi  wani  irin  juyi


Dariya amina tayi tace  amma kedai  a  masu  tsoro ma  kece  nember one  matsoraciya kawai 


Siyama tayi dariya  eh  naji dai  , zama  sukayi  aka  kama  hira 


Garbati ne  yashigo gidan  ganinsu  aljana safiya  sosai yayi farin cikin ganinsu  shima  ya  zauna  a  kusan  bilkisu 



Aljana safiya tace  waini  ina  abinda kika haifa ne bilkisu?


Murmishi bilkisu tayi tace  mace  na  haifa  sunanki  aka  saka  mata  bayan ita  nasake  haihuwar  namiji  nasa  mishi  sunan  hafeez  , yanzu  kuma  ina  goyon  amina,  duk  sunanku  nasaka  musu


Amina tace  kai  madallah  ashe anyimin takwara  ina  suke  yaran  mu  gansu  


Yaran bilkisu ta  kira  suka  fito  sosai  aljana safiya  tayi  farin ciki  ganin  bilkisu  bata  wata  matsala  ,basu jimaba  suka  musu  sallama  bilkisu  wannan  karon ma  harda  hawayen ta  

Aljana safiya tace amina  zatazo  daukarki  zuwa  yankin mu  nan  da  kwana shida  za'ayi  auren  su ita  hafeez  


Bilkisu tace  kai  masha Allah to shikenan  Allah  yakaimu  , nan  suka  fice 


Bayan kwana biyu  dangin sarki adam sukawo  lefe  akwati  dozin  biyu  , sosai  su  mama  sukayi  farin  ciki  dangi  sai  zuwa  suke  ganin kaya  kowa  na  gulma  



Husna yau tinda  asuba  take  jin  ciwon mara  kadan kadan  sharewa  tayi  saboda  dama  kullin haka takeji lokacin da  cikin yashigo watan haihuwa  fitowa  tayi daga daki  tana  damk'e baki  jin  wani  azababben ciwo  daya  tafo  mata  daure  take  tana  tafiya  harta  kasa  daurewa  durkushewa tayi  tana fadin  innalillahi wa inna ilaihin raju'un  la'ilaha ilallahu Muhammad rasulullahi S.A.W  umma  bayana  cikina  


Da gudu umma  ta  karaso  inda  take  tana fadin subhanallah   wannan  ai  haihuwa ne  wayar ta ta  dauko  ta  kira  diraban su   dana  kashewa  yashigo da sauri  


Umma tasaka  hijab din ta  tasakawa husna  ta  taimaka mata  har bakin mota  dreban yace hajiya  kayan baby fa  umma tace muje  kawai  daga  baya  kazo  ka  dauka  , jan  motar  yayi  sukabar gidan



Sosai yake  sharara  gudu  hospital din Hassan  suka  nufa  suna  zuwa  aka  dauki  husna  zuwa  emergency room  


Umma tace  kaje  ka  kawo  kayan baby  suna  nan  a  dakin  yara  kacewa  kande ta  dauko  makasu  muna  asibiti  , da  to  ya  amsa  nan  yatafi 


Umma takira  Alhaji abdallah tafada  mishi  hankali a tashe  yazo  asibiti  kamar  wani  zararre  sai  zarya  yake  yana  kai komo  


Bawan minti awa daya   saiga  wata nosee  tafito  , da sauri  umma tace  yaya  ta  haihu ne  


Murmishi tamusu  tace  eh  Alhamdulillah      ta  sauka lfy  ta  yan uku  biyu maza  daya  mace 



Umma tace Alhamdulillah  ala ni"imatullah masha Allah  ,, Alhaji abdallah  kuwa  saboda tsananin farin cikin da  mamaki  kudi  yaciro  a  aljihunsa  bai  san  nawa  bane  ya  mikawa  nosee  din  data  musu albishir 



Godiya sosai tamusu tukun ta  anjima  zaa kaita  dakin  hutu   saiku  ganta  bari  akawo  baby's  din 


Umma tace Allahu akbar  abdallah  kaga ikon Allah ko  husna ta  haifi yan uku  wllh banyi  tsammanin  husna  zata  haifi yan uku ba   kaga  Allah  buwayi gagara misali   Allah yaraya mana su 


Alhaji abdallah baki kamar gonar  auduga  yace  gaskiya umma koni  banyi  tsammanin  husna  yan  cikinta ba  ganinta  karama  saidai  Allah  yafi  gaban  misali  yabamu  har  uku  kuma  Alhamdulillah  ta  haihu lfy  


Umma tace hakane ka kira baban ta  kafada  mishi  


Wayar  dama  a hannun shi take  kiran  baba  yayi  yana dagawa  bayan sun  gaisa  ya  sheda mishi  haihuwar husna  ta  yan uku   baba  yakamu  da  farin ciki  sai  godiya yake  ga Allah  yace  ga  talatu nan  yanzu  zatazo  shi ma  gobe  zaizo  har gida  yaga  baby's 



Alhaji abdallah yace to  haka  yayita  kira  abokanshi  da yan uwa  yana  fadin  husna  ta  haifa  mishi  yan uku  

Nosee din takawo musu  yaran  sosai  umma  ke  kallon  macen  ganin  yadda  take  kama  da  ita  ,tace  masha Allah  wannan  dani  take  kama 


Alhaji abdallah yace kinyi takwara  murmishi umma tayi   


Bayan ankai  husna  dakin  hutu  tana  farkawa  aka  sallamesu  saboda  babu wata matsala   gida  suka  tafi   anan  suka  tarar  da  talatu  tana jiran  zuwansu 


Da sauri  ta  amshi baby tana  kallon  su  masha Allah  tafada  tana  cewa  Allah sarki  Allah yaraya ku  nima  nayi  sabon miji  


Dariya sukayi talatu  ta  mika  yaron  tace  husna  muje  ruwan  wanka  a  toilet  muje  namiki  , ba  muso  husna  ta  mike  talatu  a  rike  suka  shiga  wanka  



Sosai talatu take  kula  da  husna  kullin  agidan  take  yini  bata  komawa  sai  dare  tana  tashi da  safe  take  dawowa  , hakan  yayiwa  husna dadi   ganin  tako  ina  ana  kula  da  ita  




Aljana safiya taji haihuwar husna  amma  hidimar aure  ya  hanata  zuwa  sau  daya  tazo  



Yaune  aka  daura  auren  amina  da  hafeez  , masha Allah  anyi taro sosai  su  bilkisu  anzo  hadda  su  siyama  , sarki adam ma  baa barsu  abaya  ba  da  iyayen shi  , 



Washe gari  sarki adam da  iyalansa  suka  koma  gida  aka  bar  aliyu  anan  yakwana biyu  idan  sunje  bikin  sarki adam  shebar  shi  

Suma su  bilkisu  hafeez takaisu  gida  , sosai  aljana safiya taji  matukar  gashi  gobe  ne  sunan  yaran  husna  tace  zataje  tinda  komai  yalafa 


Washe gari  , yaune  ranar  suna  yan  kasar masar  duk  sun zosu  ga su  kaka  ga  yangin umma  sosai gidan  yacika  da  dangin  ko  wani  bangare  


Husna  nagani  tacaba  kwalliya  kamar  zataje  gasar  kyau  kamar  ba  ita  bace  ta  kaihu  tashi  lessin blu  mai  duwatsu  kayan  sun  amsheta  masha Allah 


Shanu  uku  Alhaji abdallah ya  yankawa  yaranshi  da  raguna  uku  


Yara  sunci  sunnan   khalil da  Mahfud  macen  kuma  husna tace  ummin ta  zatawa  takwara wato  safiya  , hakan  yayiwa  safiya dadi  , 


Kyaututuka  jarirai  sunsha  su  kan  kayan  sakawa  kayan  wasa  kudi  masha Allah  yara  sunyi  goshi  


Haka  akayi  taro  lfy  aka  tashi  lfy  kowa  yakoma  gida  lfy


Dadda tace  ita  zata  dauki  husna  acen  zatayi  wanka  , talatu kuka tasaka tace  dan Allah  kubar min  ita  tazo  namata  wanka  


Da farko  dadda taki  yarda  ganin yadda  talatu  take  rokonsu  saita  bata  tausayi  tace  shikenan  amma  kibari  mutafi  cen  harke  bayan  bikin  adam  saiku  dawo  gida  


Talatu  takira baba tafada mishi  baba  murmishi yayi yace  ba  komai  kuje  Allah yakiyaye hanya  

Sosai umma taji dadi ganin yadda  ake  kula  mata  da  jikokin  ta  , 


Kayansu  suka  hada  aka  shirya yaran  har  husna  alhaji abdallah  yakaisu  har  airport   saida  yaga  tafiyarsu  tukun  yadawo  gida cike  da  kewar  matarshi  da  yaranshi 


BAYAN SATI DAYA 


A yaune  d'umbin  jama'a  suka  sheda  daurin auren  Wasila  ladi  mukhtar da  sarki adam sulaiman  akan sadaki  naira  dubu  dari  , sosai  akaiyi  taro  


Bayan sallar la'asar  aka  shirya  amarya  da  hawayen  ta  ansha  anko  amayar tasha  kyau  iyayen ta  sun  mata  nasiha  sosai  tana  kuka  aka  fito  da  ita  zuwa  mota  da  kawayen ta 



*Nima  ina  sauri  nafito dan  kar  mota  tabrni  sai  naji  ance  maman  nusy  ina  juyawa  naga  tawagar   *ALAJANAR JARUMAI FANS  ko  wacce  tasha  anko  nan  nabisu  muka  shiga  mota  wannan  karar dai  husna tayimin anko💃🏼💃🏼🥰*



Jirgi su  amarya  yazo  suna  zuwa  yasauka  babu  bata  lokaci  suka  shiga  jirgi  yadaga  sai  kasar  masar  


Bayan sunje aka tarbesu  hannu biyu biyu  washe gari akayi  walima  da  dare misalin karfe 10:00 PM    aka  raka  ango dakin  amaryar  


Nima daga nan  nace  asha  amarci  lfy su  ladi  ganin  sarki adam ya  rufe kofa  sainayi baya na  dawo  Kaduna  



Tabawa ce  tazo  asibiti  tace  a  cire  mata  cikin  jikin  da  farko  likitan  yaki  yarda  amma  data  takura  mishi  ta  bashi  dubu dari biyar  ganin wannan  yace  zai  mata  dama cikin  wata  shida  ne  amma  haka  akai  kai  tabawa  wani  daki  aka  mata  allurori  


Saida  akasha  wahala  kafin  cikin ya  fita  bayan  agama  tayi  kwana  guda  tana  bacci  tana  tashi  aka  sallameta  tadawo gida 


Kwanan ta  biyu  da  dawowa  tsananin  ciwo  yazo  mata  tace  ga  garinku  nan  ta  rasu  rukayya  kamar hauka  , Talatu  ma  tasha kuka  ganin  yar uwar  ta  tamutu  bata  tuba  ba  , bayan  kwana bakwai  tacewa  mijin tabawa  zata  tafi  rukayya  baiyi  mata  muso  ba  yace  gata nan  , Rukayya  ta  hada  kayanta  tadawo  gidan  talatu  da  zama  

BAYAN WATA DAYA  
Yau  husna  takoma  gidanta   sosai  Alhaji  abdallah  yakejin  dadin  dawowar ta  bayan sallar isha"i  husna tayi shirin  bacci    yaran  ta  shayar  tukun  ta  kwantar  dasu  Alhaji abdallah  ne  yashigo  shima  yayi  shirin bacci  , yaran  yayiwa addu'a  tukun  suma  suka  kwanta  , janyo husna yayi jikinsa  ,

Daga nan  narufe musu kofa  nafece 

Aljana safiya  na  dakinta  sai  murmishi  take  tana  tuna  gwagwarmayar  datasha  a  rayuwa   murmishi takuma yi tace Alhamdulillah   Allah abin godiya  nan  ta  kwanta 

Tammati bi hamdulillah  anan  nakawo  karshen  wannan  littafina  mai  suna  ALJANAR JARUMAI  


 Kurakuran  danayi  a cikinsa  Allah  kayafemin  darasin  dake  cikinsa  Allah  yasa  bani ladan
